<Application x:Class="CatFileAssistant.Desktop.App"

--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/App.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Pipes;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Installation;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;

public partial class App : System.Windows.Application
{
    private Mutex? instanceMutex;
    private bool ownsMutex, exiting;
    private CancellationTokenSource? pipeCancellation;
    private PetController? pet;
    private TrayIcon? tray;
    private IDisposable? installationServer;
    private string? artifactRoot;
    protected override void OnStartup(StartupEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnStartup(e);
        DispatcherUnhandledException += FatalUiError;
        try
        {
            InstallLifecycle.CleanupOwnedArtifacts = OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup;
            if (InstallLifecycle.HandleCommandLine(e.Args)) { Shutdown(InstallLifecycle.ExitCode); return; }
            // Package verification is isolated from the user's account and background session.
            var verifying = Array.IndexOf(e.Args, "--verify-package") >= 0;
            var normalData = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "CatFileAssistant");
            var identity = Environment.UserDomainName + "\\" + Environment.UserName;
            if (!string.Equals(InstallLifecycle.DataRoot, normalData, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) identity += "|" + InstallLifecycle.DataRoot;
            var session = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(identity)))[..16];
            var pipeName = "CatFileAssistant.V08." + session;
            if (!verifying)
            {
                instanceMutex = new Mutex(false, "Local\\" + pipeName);
                try { ownsMutex = instanceMutex.WaitOne(0); } catch (AbandonedMutexException) { ownsMutex = true; }
                if (!ownsMutex)
                {
                    try { using var client = new NamedPipeClientStream(".", pipeName, PipeDirection.Out); client.Connect(1000); client.WriteByte(1); }
                    catch (IOException) { }
                    catch (TimeoutException) { }
                    Shutdown(); return;
                }
            }
            var verifyDirectory = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "CatFileAssistant-package-" + Environment.ProcessId);
            artifactRoot = verifying ? verifyDirectory : InstallLifecycle.DataRoot;
            OwnedArtifacts.Configure(artifactRoot);
            var model = verifying ? new MainViewModel(settings: new SettingsStore(verifyDirectory), log: new DiagnosticLog(Path.Combine(verifyDirectory,"logs"))) : new MainViewModel();
            var window = new MainWindow(model); MainWindow = window;
            window.ShowHomePage();
            window.Closed += (_, _) => Shutdown();
            window.Show();
            if (!verifying)
            {
                pet = new PetController(model)
                {
                    OpenMainRequested = () => { window.ShowFilePage(); ShowMain(window); },
                    OpenPetPageRequested = tab => { ShowMain(window); window.ShowPetPage(tab); },
                    ExitRequested = () => RequestExitAsync(),
                    MainWindowProvider = () => window
                };
                window.AttachPetController(pet);
                window.BeforeExit = SettleBeforeExit;
                window.HideToBackground = () => { if (!model.PetSettings.Enabled) return false; window.Hide(); return true; };
                tray = new TrayIcon(() => ShowMain(window), () => new (string, Action)[]
                {
                    ("打开软件", () => ShowMain(window)),
                    ("显示 / 隐藏猫咪", () => pet.ToggleVisibility()),
                    ("桌宠设置", () => { ShowMain(window); window.ShowPetPage("settings"); }),
                    ("彻底退出", () => _ = RequestExitAsync())
                });
                pipeCancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
                _ = ListenForActivationAsync(pipeName, window, pipeCancellation.Token);
                installationServer = InstallLifecycle.StartServer(RequestExitAsync);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
            RecordAndExplain(error);
            Shutdown(1);
        }
    }
    private static void ShowMain(MainWindow window)
    { window.Show(); if (window.WindowState == WindowState.Minimized) window.WindowState = WindowState.Normal; window.Activate(); }
    private async Task ListenForActivationAsync(string name, MainWindow window, CancellationToken token)
    {
        while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            try
            {
                using var server = new NamedPipeServerStream(name, PipeDirection.In, 1, PipeTransmissionMode.Byte, PipeOptions.Asynchronous | PipeOptions.CurrentUserOnly);
                await server.WaitForConnectionAsync(token);
                var command = new byte[1];
                await server.ReadExactlyAsync(command, token);
                if (command[0] == 1) await Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() => ShowMain(window));
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { break; }
            catch (IOException) { }
        }
    }
    private async Task<bool> RequestExitAsync()
    {
        if (exiting || MainWindow is not MainWindow window) return false;
        exiting = true;
        try
        {
            if (!window.Model.CanEdit)
            {
                ShowMain(window);
                if (!window.ConfirmApplicationExit()) return false;
                if (window.Model.IsImporting) { window.Model.CancelImport(); await window.Model.ActiveImport; }
                if (window.Model.IsProcessing) { window.Model.Cancel(); await window.Model.ActiveRun; }
            }
            if (!SettleBeforeExit()) return false;
            pet?.Dispose(); pet = null;
            tray?.Dispose(); tray = null;
            window.ApproveExit(); window.Close(); Shutdown();
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception error) { RecordAndExplain(error); Shutdown(1); return true; }
        finally { exiting = false; }
    }
    private bool SettleBeforeExit()
    {
        var result = pet?.SettleWorkForExit();
        if (result is null || result.Succeeded) return true;
        if (MainWindow is MainWindow window) ShowMain(window);
        MessageBox.Show(Locale.TranslateText(result.Message), Locale.T("工作收益尚未保存"), MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Warning);
        return false;
    }
    protected override void OnExit(ExitEventArgs e)
    {
        installationServer?.Dispose();
        pipeCancellation?.Cancel(); pipeCancellation?.Dispose();
        pet?.Dispose(); tray?.Dispose();
        if (ownsMutex) instanceMutex?.ReleaseMutex();
        instanceMutex?.Dispose();
        if (artifactRoot is not null)
            try { OwnedArtifacts.Cleanup(artifactRoot); } catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or System.Text.Json.JsonException) { }
        base.OnExit(e);
    }
    private async void FatalUiError(object sender, DispatcherUnhandledExceptionEventArgs e)
    {
        // Unexpected dispatcher failures cannot be assumed recoverable. Explain the exit.
        e.Handled = true;
        if (MainWindow is MainWindow window)
        {
            if (window.Model.IsImporting) { window.Model.CancelImport(); try { await window.Model.ActiveImport; } catch (Exception) { } }
            if (window.Model.IsProcessing) { window.Model.Cancel(); try { await window.Model.ActiveRun; } catch (Exception) { } }
        }
        RecordAndExplain(e.Exception);
        Shutdown(1);
    }
    private static void RecordAndExplain(Exception error)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(InstallLifecycle.DataRoot, "logs");
        string id;
        try { id = new DiagnosticLog(directory).Write("Fatal desktop error", error.ToString()); }
        catch { id = "未能保存日志"; }
        System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(Locale.F($"程序遇到了无法继续的问题，将安全退出。已完成的文件仍会保留。\n\n请重新启动。诊断记录：{id}\n日志位置：{directory}"), Locale.T("你的猫咪文件助手"), MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/AppVersion.cs ---
namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;

public static class AppVersion
{
    public static string Label
    {
        get
        {
            var version = typeof(AppVersion).Assembly.GetName().Version;
            return version is null ? "V1.0.2" : $"V{version.Major}.{version.Minor}" + (version.Build > 0 ? $".{version.Build}" : "");
        }
    }
    public static string Sidebar => "CAT FILE ASSISTANT · " + Label;
}

--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/BooleanVisibilityConverter.cs ---
using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;
public sealed class BooleanVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture) => value is true ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture) => value is Visibility.Visible;
}
public sealed class InverseBooleanVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture) => value is true ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture) => value is not Visibility.Visible;
}

--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Controls/CatView.xaml.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;

public enum CatPresentation { Full, Compact }

/// <summary>Whole 2D artwork; presentation clocks never drive conversion or queue state.</summary>
public partial class CatView : UserControl
{
    public static readonly DependencyProperty StateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(State), typeof(AssistantState), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(AssistantState.IDLE, PoseChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty CompletionCueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(CompletionCue), typeof(long), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(0L, CueChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty PresentationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(Presentation), typeof(CatPresentation), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(CatPresentation.Full, PoseChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty IsEmptyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(IsEmpty), typeof(bool), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(false, PoseChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty IsHoveredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(IsHovered), typeof(bool), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(false, HoverChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty PointerXProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(PointerX), typeof(double), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(0d, PointerChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty PointerYProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(nameof(PointerY), typeof(double), typeof(CatView), new PropertyMetadata(0d, PointerChanged));
    public AssistantState State { get => (AssistantState)GetValue(StateProperty); set => SetValue(StateProperty, value); }
    public long CompletionCue { get => (long)GetValue(CompletionCueProperty); set => SetValue(CompletionCueProperty, value); }
    public CatPresentation Presentation { get => (CatPresentation)GetValue(PresentationProperty); set => SetValue(PresentationProperty, value); }
    public bool IsEmpty { get => (bool)GetValue(IsEmptyProperty); set => SetValue(IsEmptyProperty, value); }
    public bool IsHovered { get => (bool)GetValue(IsHoveredProperty); set => SetValue(IsHoveredProperty, value); }
    public double PointerX { get => (double)GetValue(PointerXProperty); set => SetValue(PointerXProperty, value); }
    public double PointerY { get => (double)GetValue(PointerYProperty); set => SetValue(PointerYProperty, value); }
    /// <summary>Visible unique batch feedback, including static feedback in reduced mode.</summary>
    public int CelebrationCount { get; private set; }
    public bool HasActiveMotion { get; private set; }
    public bool IsSleeping { get; private set; }
    internal bool IsBlinking { get; private set; }
    internal double ClosedEyesOpacity => ready ? ClosedHead.Opacity : 0;
    internal string? LastPresentationError { get; private set; }

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, BitmapSource> imageCache = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private readonly Dictionary<(DependencyObject Target, DependencyProperty Property), long> clockVersions = new();
    private readonly DispatcherTimer blinkTimer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background);
    private readonly DispatcherTimer idleTimer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background);
    private readonly DispatcherTimer settleTimer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background);
    private readonly DispatcherTimer loopTimer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background);
    private DateTime lastActivity = DateTime.UtcNow;
    private Window? owner;
    private bool attached;
    private bool ready;
    private bool assetFailed;
    private long consumedCue;
    private long motionVersion;
    private long blinkVersion;
    private long nextClockId;
    private long scheduledLoopVersion;
    private AnimationClock? blinkClock;
    private bool CanDisplay => attached && IsVisible && owner?.WindowState != WindowState.Minimized;
    private bool CanMove => CanDisplay && !MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion && !assetFailed;
    private bool CanBlink => CanMove && !IsSleeping && State == AssistantState.IDLE;

    public CatView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        ready = true;
        Loaded += OnLoaded;
        Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
        IsVisibleChanged += OnVisibilityChanged;
        SizeChanged += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (!attached) return;
            var recover = assetFailed;
            Guard(() => { LoadAssets(); if (recover) ApplyPose(false); });
        };
        blinkTimer.Tick += (_, _) => Guard(StartBlink);
        idleTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1);
        idleTimer.Tick += (_, _) => CheckSleep();
        settleTimer.Tick += (_, _) => { settleTimer.Stop(); Guard(() => ApplyPose(false)); };
        loopTimer.Tick += (_, _) =>
        {
            loopTimer.Stop();
            if (scheduledLoopVersion == motionVersion && CanMove) Guard(StartLoops);
        };
    }

    public void Wake()
    {
        lastActivity = DateTime.UtcNow;
        if (!IsSleeping) return;
        IsSleeping = false;
        Guard(() => ApplyPose(true, 180));
    }

    internal void SetIdleElapsedForTesting(TimeSpan elapsed)
    {
        lastActivity = DateTime.UtcNow - elapsed;
        CheckSleep();
    }

    internal void BlinkForTesting() => Guard(StartBlink);

    protected override void OnDpiChanged(DpiScale oldDpi, DpiScale newDpi)
    {
        base.OnDpiChanged(oldDpi, newDpi);
        if (attached) Guard(() => { LoadAssets(); ApplyPose(false); });
    }

    private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args)
    {
        if (attached) return;
        attached = true;
        consumedCue = Math.Max(consumedCue, CompletionCue);
        owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
        if (owner is not null) { owner.StateChanged += OwnerChanged; owner.Closed += OwnerClosed; }
        MotionPreferences.Changed += PreferenceChanged;
        Guard(() => { LoadAssets(); ApplyPose(false); });
    }

    private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Detach();
    private void OwnerClosed(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Detach();

    private void Detach()
    {
        if (!attached) return;
        attached = false;
        MotionPreferences.Changed -= PreferenceChanged;
        if (owner is not null) { owner.StateChanged -= OwnerChanged; owner.Closed -= OwnerClosed; }
        owner = null;
        Guard(() => ApplyPose(false));
    }

    private void OwnerChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Guard(() => ApplyPose(false));
    private void PreferenceChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Guard(() => ApplyPose(false));
    private void OnVisibilityChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    {
        if (ready) Guard(() => ApplyPose(false));
    }

    private static void PoseChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    {
        var cat = (CatView)target;
        if (!cat.ready) return;
        cat.lastActivity = DateTime.UtcNow;
        cat.IsSleeping = false;
        cat.Guard(() =>
        {
            if (args.Property == PresentationProperty && cat.attached) cat.LoadAssets();
            cat.ApplyPose(true);
        });
    }

    private static void HoverChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    {
        var cat = (CatView)target;
        if (!cat.ready) return;
        cat.Wake();
        cat.Guard(cat.UpdateGaze);
    }

    private static void PointerChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    {
        var cat = (CatView)target;
        if (!cat.ready || !cat.IsHovered) return;
        cat.lastActivity = DateTime.UtcNow;
        cat.Guard(cat.UpdateGaze);
    }

    private static void CueChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    {
        var cat = (CatView)target;
        var cue = (long)args.NewValue;
        if (cue <= cat.consumedCue) return;
        // Hidden or minimized controls consume the signal without replaying it on restoration.
        cat.consumedCue = cue;
        if (!cat.ready || !cat.CanDisplay || cat.State != AssistantState.SUCCESS) return;
        cat.CelebrationCount++;
        cat.Guard(cat.Celebrate);
    }

    private void LoadAssets()
    {
        var compact = Presentation == CatPresentation.Compact;
        var width = ActualWidth > 0 ? ActualWidth : double.IsFinite(Width) && Width > 0 ? Width : 160;
        var height = ActualHeight > 0 ? ActualHeight : double.IsFinite(Height) && Height > 0 ? Height : 160;
        var dpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(this);
        var pixels = 160 * Math.Min(width / 160, height / (compact ? 140 : 160)) *
            (compact ? 1.15 : 1) * Math.Max(dpi.DpiScaleX, dpi.DpiScaleY);
        var bucket = pixels <= 128 ? 128 : pixels <= 256 ? 256 : 512;
        OpenHead.Source = GetImage("Cat/cat2d-idle.png", bucket);
        ClosedHead.Source = GetImage("Cat/cat2d-closed.png", bucket);
        SleepImage.Source = GetImage("Cat/cat2d-sleep.png", bucket);
        assetFailed = false;
        LastPresentationError = null;
        FallbackImage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
    }

    private static BitmapSource GetImage(string relative, int width)
    {
        width = width <= 128 ? 128 : width <= 256 ? 256 : 512;
        var key = relative + "@" + width;
        if (imageCache.TryGetValue(key, out var existing)) return existing;
        var bitmap = new BitmapImage();
        bitmap.BeginInit();
        bitmap.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad;
        bitmap.DecodePixelWidth = width;
        bitmap.UriSource = new Uri("pack://application:,,,/YourCatFileAssistant;component/Assets/" + relative, UriKind.Absolute);
        bitmap.EndInit();
        bitmap.Freeze();
        imageCache[key] = bitmap;
        return bitmap;
    }

    private void ApplyPose(bool animate, int duration = 180)
    {
        if (!ready) return;
        // Stop each clock at its current value before handing the whole illustration to a new pose.
        StopMotion();
        var compact = Presentation == CatPresentation.Compact;
        Scene.Height = compact ? 140 : 160;
        PresentationScale.ScaleX = PresentationScale.ScaleY = compact ? 1.15 : 1;
        PresentationOffset.Y = compact ? 8 : 0;
        OpenHead.Opacity = 1;
        ClosedHead.Opacity = State == AssistantState.SUCCESS ? 1 : 0;
        StatusBadge.Visibility = !compact && State is AssistantState.SUCCESS or AssistantState.ERROR ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        SuccessMark.Visibility = State == AssistantState.SUCCESS ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        ErrorMark.Visibility = State == AssistantState.ERROR ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        BadgeSurface.SetResourceReference(System.Windows.Shapes.Shape.FillProperty, State == AssistantState.ERROR ? "WarningTint" : "SuccessTint");
        LoadingRing.Visibility = !compact && State == AssistantState.PROCESSING ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        var milliseconds = State == AssistantState.DRAGGING ? 350 : State == AssistantState.ERROR ? 400 : duration;
        var move = animate && CanMove;
        var dragging = CanMove && State == AssistantState.DRAGGING;
        var bodyY = dragging ? -2 : CanMove && State == AssistantState.ERROR ? 2 : 0;
        Set(BodyOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, bodyY, milliseconds, move);
        Set(DragScale, ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, dragging ? 1.015 : 1, milliseconds, move);
        Set(DragScale, ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, dragging ? 1.015 : 1, milliseconds, move);
        Set(BreathOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, 0, milliseconds, move);
        Set(WorkRotation, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, 0, milliseconds, move);
        Set(SleepBreath, TranslateTransform.YProperty, 0, duration, move);
        Set(LoadingRotation, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, 0, milliseconds, false);
        var sleeping = IsSleeping && !compact;
        SleepingRig.Visibility = !assetFailed && (sleeping || move && SleepingRig.Opacity > .001) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        Rig.Visibility = assetFailed ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        // Both pictures crossfade in place, with an opaque awake picture under the eye overlay.
        Set(SleepingRig, OpacityProperty, sleeping ? 1 : 0, duration, move);
        Set(Rig, OpacityProperty, sleeping ? 0 : 1, duration, move);
        AutomationProperties.SetName(this, sleeping ? "猫咪正在休息" : State switch
        {
            AssistantState.DRAGGING => "猫咪准备接收文件", AssistantState.PROCESSING => "猫咪正在处理文件",
            AssistantState.SUCCESS => "猫咪提示文件处理成功", AssistantState.ERROR => "猫咪提示文件处理遇到问题", _ => "猫咪等待文件"
        });
        UpdateGaze();
        if (CanDisplay) idleTimer.Start();
        if (!CanMove) return;
        HasActiveMotion = true;
        if (move)
        {
            scheduledLoopVersion = motionVersion;
            loopTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(Math.Max(duration, milliseconds) + 1);
            loopTimer.Start();
        }
        else StartLoops();
    }

    private void StartLoops()
    {
        if (!CanMove) return;
        if (IsSleeping)
        {
            Loop(SleepBreath, TranslateTransform.YProperty, 0, -.7, 2000);
            return;
        }
        if (SleepingRig.Opacity <= .001) SleepingRig.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
        if (State == AssistantState.PROCESSING)
        {
            Loop(BreathOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, 0, -.8, 1200);
            Loop(WorkRotation, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, -.35, .35, 1200);
            if (Presentation == CatPresentation.Full)
            {
                var rotation = new DoubleAnimation(0, 360, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1600)) { RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever };
                LoadingRotation.BeginAnimation(RotateTransform.AngleProperty,
                    rotation,
                    HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
            }
        }
        else Loop(BreathOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, 0, -1, 2000);
        if (CanBlink) ScheduleBlink();
    }

    private void UpdateGaze()
    {
        if (!ready) return;
        var hover = CanMove && IsHovered && IsEmpty && State == AssistantState.IDLE && !IsSleeping;
        var x = hover ? Math.Clamp(PointerX, -1, 1) * 1.5 : 0;
        var y = hover ? -1.5 + Math.Clamp(PointerY, -1, 1) * .5 : 0;
        Set(LookOffset, TranslateTransform.XProperty, x, 200, CanMove);
        Set(LookOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, y, 200, CanMove);
    }

    private void Celebrate()
    {
        ApplyPose(true);
        if (!CanMove) return;
        loopTimer.Stop();
        StopClock(BreathOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty);
        Set(BreathOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, 0, 180, true);
        var duration = MotionInt("MotionSuccessDurationMs", 600);
        Keyframes(BodyOffset, TranslateTransform.YProperty, -4, duration);
        settleTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(duration + 20);
        settleTimer.Start();
    }

    private void CheckSleep()
    {
        if (!CanDisplay || !IsEmpty || State != AssistantState.IDLE || Presentation != CatPresentation.Full) return;
        if (IsSleeping || DateTime.UtcNow - lastActivity < TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(MotionInt("MotionSleepAfterMs", 120000))) return;
        IsSleeping = true;
        Guard(() => ApplyPose(true, 800));
    }

    private void ScheduleBlink()
    {
        if (!CanBlink) return;
        var min = MotionInt("MotionIdleBlinkMinMs", 4000);
        var max = Math.Max(min + 1, MotionInt("MotionIdleBlinkMaxMs", 7000));
        blinkTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(Random.Shared.Next(min, max));
        blinkTimer.Start();
    }

    private void StartBlink()
    {
        blinkTimer.Stop();
        if (!CanBlink || IsBlinking) return;
        var version = ++blinkVersion;
        var poseVersion = motionVersion;
        IsBlinking = true;
        ClosedHead.BeginAnimation(OpacityProperty, null);
        ClosedHead.Opacity = 0;
        var animation = new DoubleAnimationUsingKeyFrames
        {
            Duration = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(160), FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop
        };
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new LinearDoubleKeyFrame(0, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.Zero)));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new LinearDoubleKeyFrame(1, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(45))));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new DiscreteDoubleKeyFrame(1, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(90))));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new LinearDoubleKeyFrame(0, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(160))));
        Timeline.SetDesiredFrameRate(animation, 60);
        var clock = (AnimationClock)animation.CreateClock(true);
        blinkClock = clock;
        clock.Completed += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (version != blinkVersion || poseVersion != motionVersion || !IsBlinking) return;
            Guard(() =>
            {
                ClosedHead.BeginAnimation(OpacityProperty, null);
                clock.Controller?.Remove();
                blinkClock = null;
                ClosedHead.Opacity = State == AssistantState.SUCCESS ? 1 : 0;
                IsBlinking = false;
                ScheduleBlink();
            });
        };
        ClosedHead.ApplyAnimationClock(UIElement.OpacityProperty, clock, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
        clock.Controller?.Begin();
        clock.Controller?.SeekAlignedToLastTick(TimeSpan.Zero, TimeSeekOrigin.BeginTime);
    }

    private void Set(Animatable target, DependencyProperty property, double goal, int milliseconds, bool animate)
    {
        var current = (double)target.GetValue(property);
        target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
        target.SetValue(property, goal);
        clockVersions.Remove((target, property));
        if (!animate || Math.Abs(current - goal) <= .001) return;
        var version = motionVersion;
        var clock = ++nextClockId;
        clockVersions[(target, property)] = clock;
        var animation = new DoubleAnimation(current, goal, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(milliseconds))
        {
            EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop
        };
        animation.Completed += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (!OwnsClock(target, property, version, clock)) return;
            Guard(() => { target.BeginAnimation(property, null); target.SetValue(property, goal); clockVersions.Remove((target, property)); });
        };
        target.BeginAnimation(property, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
    }

    private void Set(UIElement target, DependencyProperty property, double goal, int milliseconds, bool animate)
    {
        var current = (double)target.GetValue(property);
        target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
        target.SetValue(property, goal);
        clockVersions.Remove((target, property));
        if (!animate || Math.Abs(current - goal) <= .001) return;
        var version = motionVersion;
        var clock = ++nextClockId;
        clockVersions[(target, property)] = clock;
        var animation = new DoubleAnimation(current, goal, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(milliseconds)) { FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop };
        animation.Completed += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (!OwnsClock(target, property, version, clock)) return;
            Guard(() => { target.BeginAnimation(property, null); target.SetValue(property, goal); clockVersions.Remove((target, property)); });
        };
        target.BeginAnimation(property, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
    }

    private bool OwnsClock(DependencyObject target, DependencyProperty property, long version, long clock)
        => version == motionVersion && clockVersions.TryGetValue((target, property), out var active) && active == clock;

    private void Loop(Animatable target, DependencyProperty property, double from, double to, int halfPeriod)
    {
        clockVersions.Remove((target, property));
        var current = (double)target.GetValue(property);
        target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
        target.SetValue(property, from);
        var animation = new DoubleAnimationUsingKeyFrames
        {
            Duration = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(halfPeriod * 2), RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever
        };
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(from, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.Zero)));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(to, KeyTime.FromPercent(.5), new SineEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseInOut }));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(from, KeyTime.FromPercent(1), new SineEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseInOut }));
        if (Math.Abs(current - from) <= .001)
        {
            target.BeginAnimation(property, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
            return;
        }
        // Blend to the loop baseline once; repeat only the settled loop so it cannot drift or jump.
        var version = motionVersion;
        var clock = ++nextClockId;
        clockVersions[(target, property)] = clock;
        var transition = new DoubleAnimation(current, from, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(180))
        {
            EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop
        };
        transition.Completed += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (!OwnsClock(target, property, version, clock) || !CanMove) return;
            Guard(() =>
            {
                target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
                target.SetValue(property, from);
                clockVersions.Remove((target, property));
                target.BeginAnimation(property, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
            });
        };
        target.BeginAnimation(property, transition, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
    }

    private void Keyframes(Animatable target, DependencyProperty property, double peak, int milliseconds)
    {
        var current = (double)target.GetValue(property);
        target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
        target.SetValue(property, 0d);
        var version = motionVersion;
        var clock = ++nextClockId;
        clockVersions[(target, property)] = clock;
        var animation = new DoubleAnimationUsingKeyFrames { Duration = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(milliseconds), FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop };
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(current, KeyTime.FromPercent(0)));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(peak, KeyTime.FromPercent(.38), new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }));
        animation.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(0, KeyTime.FromPercent(1), new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseInOut }));
        animation.Completed += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (!OwnsClock(target, property, version, clock)) return;
            Guard(() => { target.BeginAnimation(property, null); target.SetValue(property, 0d); clockVersions.Remove((target, property)); });
        };
        target.BeginAnimation(property, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
    }

    private void StopMotion()
    {
        ++motionVersion;
        ++blinkVersion;
        blinkClock?.Controller?.Remove();
        blinkClock = null;
        blinkTimer.Stop(); idleTimer.Stop(); settleTimer.Stop(); loopTimer.Stop();
        clockVersions.Clear();
        HasActiveMotion = false;
        IsBlinking = false;
        if (!ready) return;
        foreach (var transform in new[] { PresentationOffset, BodyOffset, BreathOffset, LookOffset, SleepBreath })
        {
            StopClock(transform, TranslateTransform.XProperty);
            StopClock(transform, TranslateTransform.YProperty);
        }
        foreach (var transform in new[] { WorkRotation, LoadingRotation }) StopClock(transform, RotateTransform.AngleProperty);
        foreach (var transform in new[] { PresentationScale, DragScale })
        {
            StopClock(transform, ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty);
            StopClock(transform, ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty);
        }
        foreach (var visual in new UIElement[] { Rig, SleepingRig, OpenHead, ClosedHead, FallbackImage }) StopClock(visual, OpacityProperty);
        // A cancelled blink cannot survive a business state, policy or visibility change.
        OpenHead.Opacity = 1;
        ClosedHead.Opacity = State == AssistantState.SUCCESS ? 1 : 0;
    }

    private static void StopClock(Animatable target, DependencyProperty property)
    {
        try
        {
            var current = target.GetValue(property);
            target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
            target.SetValue(property, current);
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Cat clock cleanup: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private static void StopClock(UIElement target, DependencyProperty property)
    {
        try
        {
            var current = target.GetValue(property);
            target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
            target.SetValue(property, current);
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Cat visual cleanup: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private int MotionInt(string key, int fallback)
        => TryFindResource(key) is int value && value > 0 ? value : fallback;

    private void Guard(Action action)
    {
        try { action(); }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
            LastPresentationError = error.ToString();
            Trace.TraceWarning("Cat presentation fallback: {0}", error);
            StopMotion();
            assetFailed = true;
            Rig.Visibility = SleepingRig.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
            try
            {
                FallbackImage.Source = OpenHead.Source ?? GetImage("Cat/cat2d-idle.png", 256);
                FallbackImage.Opacity = 1;
                FallbackImage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
            }
            catch (Exception fallbackError)
            {
                Trace.TraceWarning("Cat fallback asset: {0}", fallbackError.Message);
                try { FallbackImage.Source = GetImage("app.png", 256); FallbackImage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible; }
                catch (Exception iconError) { Trace.TraceWarning("Cat fallback icon: {0}", iconError.Message); }
            }
        }
    }
}




--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Controls/FileActionPicker.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;

/// <summary>Selects a local operation only. Import and execution remain owned by MainViewModel.</summary>
public static class FileActionPicker
{
    public static FileProcessingAction? Show(Window? owner, IReadOnlyList<FileActionChoice> choices)
    {
        ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
        var area = SystemParameters.WorkArea;
        var dialog = new Window { Title = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("选择处理用途"), Owner = owner, Width = Math.Min(500, area.Width - 24), Height = Math.Min(620, area.Height - 24), MinWidth = Math.Min(380, area.Width - 24), MinHeight = Math.Min(320, area.Height - 24), WindowStartupLocation = owner is null ? WindowStartupLocation.CenterScreen : WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner, UseLayoutRounding = true };
        dialog.SetResourceReference(Window.BackgroundProperty, "Background"); dialog.SetResourceReference(Window.ForegroundProperty, "Text"); dialog.SetResourceReference(Window.FontFamilyProperty, "UiFont");
        ThemedWindowFrame.Attach(dialog);
        var panel = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(24) };
        panel.Children.Add(new TextBlock { Text = "想怎样处理这些文件？", FontSize = 20, FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold });
        var hint = new TextBlock { Text = "选择用途后加入队列，还可以调整参数。可接收数量为初步检查，导入时会验证文件内容。", TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 20), FontSize = 12 }; hint.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "Muted"); panel.Children.Add(hint);
        FileProcessingAction? selected = null;
        foreach (var choice in choices)
        {
            var button = new Button { Content = Locale.F($"{Locale.T(choice.Title)}    可接收 {choice.CompatiblePaths.Count} / {choice.TotalCount} 项"), Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8), HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, IsEnabled = choice.CompatiblePaths.Count > 0 };
            button.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, "SoftButton"); button.Click += (_, _) => { selected = choice.Action; dialog.DialogResult = true; }; panel.Children.Add(button);
        }
        var cancel = new Button { Content = "取消", IsCancel = true, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right, Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 0) }; cancel.SetResourceReference(FrameworkElement.StyleProperty, "SoftButton"); cancel.Click += (_, _) => dialog.Close(); panel.Children.Add(cancel);
        dialog.Content = new ScrollViewer { Content = panel, VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto };
        dialog.Loaded += (_, _) => UiMotion.Reveal(panel, 4, 200);
        var scrim = owner?.FindName("ModalScrim") as Border;
        try
        {
            if (scrim is not null) scrim.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
            return dialog.ShowDialog() == true ? selected : null;
        }
        finally { if (scrim is not null) scrim.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Controls/FunctionalIcon.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;

public enum FunctionalIconKind
{
    Home, Tasks, Video, Audio, Image, Extract, Add, Lock, Settings, Info, Folder,
    Open, Remove, Clear, Start, Stop, Retry, Check, Warning, ChevronDown, Compress, Gif
}

/// <summary>A shared 24-unit icon grid with rounded 1.8-unit strokes.</summary>
public sealed class FunctionalIcon : Control
{
    public static readonly DependencyProperty KindProperty = DependencyProperty.Register(
        nameof(Kind), typeof(FunctionalIconKind), typeof(FunctionalIcon),
        new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(FunctionalIconKind.Folder, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));

    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<FunctionalIconKind, Geometry> Drawings = CreateDrawings();

    static FunctionalIcon()
    {
        WidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(FunctionalIcon), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(20d));
        HeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(FunctionalIcon), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(20d));
        FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(FunctionalIcon), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
        IsHitTestVisibleProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(FunctionalIcon), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
        ForegroundProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(FunctionalIcon), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(
            Brushes.Black, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
    }

    public FunctionalIconKind Kind
    {
        get => (FunctionalIconKind)GetValue(KindProperty);
        set => SetValue(KindProperty, value);
    }

    protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawingContext)
    {
        base.OnRender(drawingContext);
        var side = Math.Min(ActualWidth, ActualHeight);
        if (side <= 0) return;
        var pen = new Pen(Foreground, 1.8)
        {
            StartLineCap = PenLineCap.Round,
            EndLineCap = PenLineCap.Round,
            LineJoin = PenLineJoin.Round
        };
        drawingContext.PushTransform(new TranslateTransform((ActualWidth - side) / 2, (ActualHeight - side) / 2));
        drawingContext.PushTransform(new ScaleTransform(side / 24, side / 24));
        drawingContext.DrawGeometry(null, pen, Drawings[Kind]);
        drawingContext.Pop();
        drawingContext.Pop();
    }

    private static IReadOnlyDictionary<FunctionalIconKind, Geometry> CreateDrawings()
    {
        var paths = new Dictionary<FunctionalIconKind, string>
        {
            [FunctionalIconKind.Home] = "M 3,11 L 12,3 L 21,11 M 5,10 V 21 H 10 V 15 H 14 V 21 H 19 V 10",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Tasks] = "M 9,5 H 21 M 9,12 H 21 M 9,19 H 21 M 3,5 L 4,6 L 6,3 M 3,12 L 4,13 L 6,10 M 3,19 L 4,20 L 6,17",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Video] = "M 5,4 H 19 Q 21,4 21,6 V 18 Q 21,20 19,20 H 5 Q 3,20 3,18 V 6 Q 3,4 5,4 Z M 10,8 L 16,12 L 10,16 Z",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Gif] = "M 5,4 H 16 Q 18,4 18,6 V 16 Q 18,18 16,18 H 5 Q 3,18 3,16 V 6 Q 3,4 5,4 Z M 21,7 V 19 Q 21,21 19,21 H 8 M 9,8 L 14,11 L 9,14 Z",

            [FunctionalIconKind.Image] = "M 5,4 H 19 Q 21,4 21,6 V 18 Q 21,20 19,20 H 5 Q 3,20 3,18 V 6 Q 3,4 5,4 Z M 3,16 L 9,10 L 15,16 L 18,13 L 21,16 M 15,8 A 1.5,1.5 0 1 0 18,8 A 1.5,1.5 0 1 0 15,8",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Extract] = "M 11,19 H 5 Q 3,19 3,17 V 6 Q 3,4 5,4 H 17 Q 19,4 19,6 V 10 M 8,8 L 13,11 L 8,14 Z M 16,13 V 19 M 19,11 V 21 M 22,14 V 18",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Add] = "M 12,5 V 19 M 5,12 H 19",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Lock] = "M 6,10 H 18 Q 20,10 20,12 V 19 Q 20,21 18,21 H 6 Q 4,21 4,19 V 12 Q 4,10 6,10 Z M 8,10 V 7 A 4,4 0 0 1 16,7 V 10 M 12,14 V 17",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Settings] = "M 9,3 H 15 L 16,6 L 19,7 L 21,10 L 19,13 L 19,16 L 16,19 L 13,19 L 10,21 L 7,19 L 6,16 L 3,15 V 9 L 6,8 L 7,5 Z M 8,12 A 4,4 0 1 0 16,12 A 4,4 0 1 0 8,12",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Info] = "M 3,12 A 9,9 0 1 0 21,12 A 9,9 0 1 0 3,12 M 12,11 V 17 M 12,7 V 7.2",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Folder] = "M 3,7 V 18 Q 3,20 5,20 H 19 Q 21,20 21,18 V 9 Q 21,7 19,7 H 11 L 9,4 H 5 Q 3,4 3,6 Z",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Open] = "M 14,4 H 20 V 10 M 20,4 L 11,13 M 10,4 H 5 Q 3,4 3,6 V 19 Q 3,21 5,21 H 18 Q 20,21 20,19 V 14",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Remove] = "M 6,6 L 18,18 M 18,6 L 6,18",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Clear] = "M 4,6 H 20 M 9,6 V 3 H 15 V 6 M 6,6 L 7,20 H 17 L 18,6 M 10,10 V 16 M 14,10 V 16",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Start] = "M 7,4 L 20,12 L 7,20 Z",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Stop] = "M 6,5 H 18 Q 19,5 19,6 V 18 Q 19,19 18,19 H 6 Q 5,19 5,18 V 6 Q 5,5 6,5 Z",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Retry] = "M 20,8 A 9,9 0 1 0 21,14 M 20,3 V 8 H 15",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Check] = "M 4,12 L 9,17 L 20,6",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Warning] = "M 10.5,4 Q 12,1.5 13.5,4 L 22,19 Q 23,21 20,21 H 4 Q 1,21 2,19 Z M 12,9 V 14 M 12,17 V 17.2",
            [FunctionalIconKind.ChevronDown] = "M 6,9 L 12,15 L 18,9",
            [FunctionalIconKind.Compress] = "M 3,3 L 9,9 M 4,9 H 9 V 4 M 21,3 L 15,9 M 15,4 V 9 H 20 M 3,21 L 9,15 M 4,15 H 9 V 20 M 21,21 L 15,15 M 15,20 V 15 H 20"
        };
        var result = new Dictionary<FunctionalIconKind, Geometry>();
        foreach (var pair in paths)
        {
            var geometry = Geometry.Parse(pair.Value);
            geometry.Freeze();
            result.Add(pair.Key, geometry);
        }
        return result;
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Controls/ResultFileActions.cs ---
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;

/// <summary>Uses shell item identifiers, so Unicode, spaces and commas never become command syntax.</summary>
internal static class ResultFileActions
{
    internal static IntPtr ParseItem(string path)
    {
        Marshal.ThrowExceptionForHR(SHParseDisplayName(path, IntPtr.Zero, out var item, 0, out _));
        return item;
    }

    public static void Reveal(string path)
    {
        var item = ParseItem(path);
        try { Marshal.ThrowExceptionForHR(SHOpenFolderAndSelectItems(item, 0, IntPtr.Zero, 0)); }
        finally { Marshal.FreeCoTaskMem(item); }
    }

    [DllImport("shell32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, ExactSpelling = true)]
    private static extern int SHParseDisplayName(string name, IntPtr bindingContext, out IntPtr item, uint attributes, out uint actualAttributes);
    [DllImport("shell32.dll", ExactSpelling = true)]
    private static extern int SHOpenFolderAndSelectItems(IntPtr item, uint count, IntPtr children, uint flags);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Controls/ThemedWindowFrame.cs ---
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Shapes;
using System.Windows.Shell;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;

/// <summary>Themeable caption; WindowChrome retains native moving, sizing and system commands.</summary>
internal sealed class ThemedWindowFrame
{
    private readonly Window window;
    private readonly Border frame = new();
    private Button? maximize;
    private Path? maximizeGlyph;
    private HwndSource? source;
    private ThemedWindowFrame(Window window)
    {
        this.window = window;
        window.SourceInitialized += Initialize;
        window.StateChanged += StateChanged;
        window.Closed += Closed;
    }
    public static void Attach(Window window) => _ = new ThemedWindowFrame(window);

    private void Initialize(object? sender, EventArgs args)
    {
        if (window.Content is not UIElement body) return;
        window.Content = null;
        var canResize = window.ResizeMode is ResizeMode.CanResize or ResizeMode.CanResizeWithGrip;
        WindowChrome.SetWindowChrome(window, new WindowChrome
        {
            CaptionHeight = 32, ResizeBorderThickness = new Thickness(canResize ? 6 : 0),
            GlassFrameThickness = new Thickness(0), CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(0),
            UseAeroCaptionButtons = false, NonClientFrameEdges = NonClientFrameEdges.None
        });
        var layout = new DockPanel();
        var caption = new DockPanel { Height = 32, LastChildFill = true };
        caption.SetResourceReference(Panel.BackgroundProperty, "Surface");
        var buttons = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal };
        DockPanel.SetDock(buttons, Dock.Right);
        if (window.ResizeMode != ResizeMode.NoResize)
        {
            buttons.Children.Add(CaptionButton("最小化", "M0 5 H10", () => SystemCommands.MinimizeWindow(window)));
            maximize = CaptionButton("最大化", "M0 0 H10 V10 H0 Z", ToggleMaximize);
            maximize.IsEnabled = canResize;
            maximizeGlyph = (Path)maximize.Content;
            buttons.Children.Add(maximize);
        }
        buttons.Children.Add(CaptionButton("关闭", "M0 0 L10 10 M10 0 L0 10", () => SystemCommands.CloseWindow(window), true));
        caption.Children.Add(buttons);
        var identity = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(12, 0, 12, 0) };
        var icon = new Image { Width = 16, Height = 16, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 8, 0) };
        icon.SetBinding(Image.SourceProperty, new Binding(nameof(Window.Icon)) { Source = window });
        identity.Children.Add(icon);
        var title = new TextBlock { FontSize = 12, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis };
        title.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "Text");
        title.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new Binding(nameof(Window.Title)) { Source = window });
        identity.Children.Add(title); caption.Children.Add(identity);
        DockPanel.SetDock(caption, Dock.Top);
        layout.Children.Add(caption); layout.Children.Add(body);
        frame.Name = "ThemedWindowBorder"; frame.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1);
        frame.SetResourceReference(Border.BorderBrushProperty, "Border");
        frame.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "Background");
        frame.Child = layout; window.Content = frame;
        source = HwndSource.FromHwnd(new WindowInteropHelper(window).Handle);
        source?.AddHook(WindowMessage);
        StateChanged(null, EventArgs.Empty);
    }

    private Button CaptionButton(string label, string geometry, Action action, bool close = false)
    {
        var glyph = new Path { Data = Geometry.Parse(geometry), Width = 10, Height = 10, Stretch = Stretch.None,
            StrokeThickness = 1, SnapsToDevicePixels = true, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center };
        glyph.SetResourceReference(Shape.StrokeProperty, "Text");
        var button = new Button { Content = glyph, Style = (Style)window.FindResource(close ? "CaptionCloseButton" : "CaptionButton"), ToolTip = label };
        AutomationProperties.SetName(button, label);
        WindowChrome.SetIsHitTestVisibleInChrome(button, true);
        button.Click += (_, _) => action();
        return button;
    }

    private void ToggleMaximize()
    {
        if (window.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized) SystemCommands.RestoreWindow(window);
        else SystemCommands.MaximizeWindow(window);
    }
    private void StateChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args)
    {
        frame.Padding = window.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized ? SystemParameters.WindowResizeBorderThickness : new Thickness(0);
        if (maximize is null || maximizeGlyph is null) return;
        var label = window.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized ? "向下还原" : "最大化";
        maximize.ToolTip = label; AutomationProperties.SetName(maximize, label);
        maximizeGlyph.Data = Geometry.Parse(window.WindowState == WindowState.Maximized ? "M2 0 H10 V8 M0 2 H8 V10 H0 Z" : "M0 0 H10 V10 H0 Z");
    }
    private IntPtr WindowMessage(IntPtr hwnd, int message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam, ref bool handled)
    {
        // A native maximize hit target also exposes Windows 11's snap-layout interaction.
        if (maximize?.IsEnabled == true && message == 0x0084)
        {
            var packed = lParam.ToInt64(); var point = new Point((short)packed, (short)(packed >> 16));
            var bounds = new Rect(maximize.PointToScreen(new Point()), maximize.PointToScreen(new Point(maximize.ActualWidth, maximize.ActualHeight)));
            if (bounds.Contains(point)) { maximize.Tag = "Hover"; handled = true; return new IntPtr(9); }
            maximize.Tag = null;
        }
        if (maximize?.IsEnabled == true && wParam.ToInt64() == 9)
        {
            if (message == 0x00A1) { maximize.Tag = "Pressed"; handled = true; }
            if (message == 0x00A2) { maximize.Tag = "Hover"; ToggleMaximize(); handled = true; }
        }
        if (message == 0x02A2 && maximize is not null) maximize.Tag = null;
        return IntPtr.Zero;
    }
    private void Closed(object? sender, EventArgs args)
    {
        source?.RemoveHook(WindowMessage); source = null;
        window.SourceInitialized -= Initialize; window.StateChanged -= StateChanged; window.Closed -= Closed;
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Installation/InstallLifecycle.cs ---
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Pipes;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Installation;

/// <summary>Installation commands run before normal startup and never create a wallet or settings.</summary>
public static class InstallLifecycle
{
    public static int ExitCode { get; private set; }
    public static Func<string, IReadOnlyList<string>>? CleanupOwnedArtifacts { get; set; }
    public static string InstallRoot => Path.GetFileName(Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(AppContext.BaseDirectory)).Equals("app", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        ? Directory.GetParent(Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(AppContext.BaseDirectory))!.FullName
        : Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
    private static bool IsTestInstall => File.Exists(Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "installer-test-mode"));
    public static string DataRoot => IsTestInstall ? Path.Combine(InstallRoot, "installer-test-data")
        : Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "CatFileAssistant");
    private static string UserIdentity => Environment.UserDomainName + "\\" + Environment.UserName;
    private static string Hash(string value) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(value)))[..24];
    private static string PipeName => "CatFileAssistant.Install." + Hash(UserIdentity + "|" + InstallRoot.ToUpperInvariant());

    public static string? ReadInstalledLanguage()
    {
        try
        {
            using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "installed-language.json")));
            var value = document.RootElement.GetProperty("language").GetString();
            return value is "en-US" or "zh-CN" ? value : null;
        }
        catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or JsonException or InvalidOperationException or KeyNotFoundException) { return null; }
    }

    private static bool UseEnglishMessages()
    {
        var settings = Path.Combine(DataRoot, "settings.json");
        try
        {
            if (!File.Exists(settings)) return ReadInstalledLanguage() == "en-US";
            using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(settings));
            return document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("Language", out var language) && language.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String && language.GetString() == "en-US";
        }
        catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or JsonException or InvalidOperationException) { return ReadInstalledLanguage() == "en-US"; }
    }

    public static bool HandleCommandLine(string[] args)
    {
        var uninstall = Array.IndexOf(args, "--uninstall") >= 0 || string.Equals(Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(Environment.ProcessPath), "卸载程序", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        var requestExit = Array.IndexOf(args, "--installer-exit") >= 0;
        var clean = Array.IndexOf(args, "--installer-clean-data") >= 0;
        if (!uninstall && !requestExit && !clean) return false;
        try
        {
            if (requestExit) ExitCode = RequestExit();
            else if (clean) ExitCode = CleanData(Array.IndexOf(args, "--keep-pet-save") >= 0);
            else
            {
                var uninstaller = Path.Combine(InstallRoot, "app", "uninstall", "unins000.exe");
                if (!File.Exists(uninstaller))
                {
                    ExitCode = 2;
                    if (!args.Any(argument => argument.Equals("/SILENT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || argument.Equals("/VERYSILENT", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                    {
                        var english = UseEnglishMessages();
                        MessageBox.Show(english
                            ? "This copy was extracted without running Setup, so an installed uninstaller is unavailable. Install the app using the Setup program."
                            : "这是解压版本，尚未通过安装程序安装，因此没有可用的卸载器。请使用安装程序完成安装。",
                            english ? "Your Cat File Assistant" : "你的猫咪文件助手", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    var start = new ProcessStartInfo(uninstaller) { UseShellExecute = true, WorkingDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(uninstaller)! };
                    foreach (var argument in args) if (argument != "--uninstall") start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
                    Process.Start(start); ExitCode = 0;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or OperationCanceledException or TimeoutException or ArgumentException or JsonException)
        { ExitCode = 3; }
        return true;
    }

    public static IDisposable StartServer(Func<Task<bool>> requestExit)
    {
        var cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        _ = ListenAsync(requestExit, cancellation.Token);
        return new ServerLifetime(cancellation);
    }

    private static async Task ListenAsync(Func<Task<bool>> requestExit, CancellationToken token)
    {
        while (!token.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            try
            {
                using var server = new NamedPipeServerStream(PipeName, PipeDirection.InOut, 1, PipeTransmissionMode.Byte, PipeOptions.Asynchronous | PipeOptions.CurrentUserOnly);
                await server.WaitForConnectionAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                var request = new byte[1];
                await server.ReadExactlyAsync(request, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (request[0] != 23) continue;
                await server.WriteAsync(BitConverter.GetBytes(Environment.ProcessId), token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                await server.FlushAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                // The client must retain a process handle before shutdown starts, so it can
                // reliably inspect the exit code even when the application exits immediately.
                using var acknowledgementTimeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(token);
                acknowledgementTimeout.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
                await server.ReadExactlyAsync(request, acknowledgementTimeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (request[0] != 24) continue;
                var accepted = await Application.Current.Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(requestExit).Task.Unwrap().ConfigureAwait(false);
                if (!accepted)
                {
                    await server.WriteAsync(new byte[] { 25 }, token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                    await server.FlushAsync(token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                }
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) when (!token.IsCancellationRequested) { }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) { break; }
            catch (IOException) { }
            catch (ObjectDisposedException) { break; }
        }
    }

    private static int RequestExit()
    {
        using var client = new NamedPipeClientStream(".", PipeName, PipeDirection.InOut, PipeOptions.Asynchronous);
        try { client.Connect(1000); }
        catch (TimeoutException) { return HasLegacyInstance() ? 11 : 0; }
        client.WriteByte(23); client.Flush();
        var pidBytes = new byte[4];
        using var timeout = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));
        client.ReadExactlyAsync(pidBytes, timeout.Token).AsTask().GetAwaiter().GetResult();
        var pid = BitConverter.ToInt32(pidBytes);
        using var process = Process.GetProcessById(pid);
        _ = process.SafeHandle;
        client.WriteByte(24); client.Flush();
        var shutdownClock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var response = new byte[1];
        using var shutdownTimeout = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60));
        int responseLength;
        try { responseLength = client.ReadAsync(response, shutdownTimeout.Token).AsTask().GetAwaiter().GetResult(); }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { return 12; }
        if (responseLength != 0) return response[0] == 25 ? 14 : 15;
        var remainingMilliseconds = Math.Max(0, 60_000 - (int)shutdownClock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds);
        if (!process.WaitForExit(remainingMilliseconds)) return 12;
        return process.ExitCode == 0 ? 0 : 13;
    }

    private static bool HasLegacyInstance()
    {
        if (IsTestInstall) return false;
        var session = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(UserIdentity)))[..16];
        try
        {
            using var mutex = Mutex.OpenExisting("Local\\CatFileAssistant.V08." + session);
            try { if (!mutex.WaitOne(0)) return true; mutex.ReleaseMutex(); return false; }
            catch (AbandonedMutexException) { mutex.ReleaseMutex(); return false; }
        }
        catch (WaitHandleCannotBeOpenedException) { return false; }
    }

    private static int CleanData(bool keepSave)
    {
        var root = Path.GetFullPath(DataRoot);
        if (!Directory.Exists(root)) return 0;
        if (HasReparseAncestor(root)) return 21;
        // A retained receipt means an owned artifact could not be safely removed.
        // Report failure before Inno removes the program, so the user can retry.
        if (CleanupOwnedArtifacts?.Invoke(root).Count > 0) return 22;
        var files = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();
        if (!keepSave) files.AddRange(["settings.json", "settings.json.bak", "pet-state.json", "pet-state.json.bak"]);
        files.AddRange(["logs/diagnostics.log", "logs/diagnostics.previous.log", "owned-temporary-files.json.tmp"]);
        var paths = files.Select(relative => Path.Combine(root, relative.Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar))).ToArray();
        if (paths.Any(HasReparseAncestor)) return 21;
        foreach (var path in paths)
        {
            if (File.Exists(path)) File.Delete(path);
        }
        foreach (var directory in new[] { Path.Combine(root, "logs"), Path.Combine(root, "cache"), root })
            if (Directory.Exists(directory) && !HasReparseAncestor(directory) && !Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(directory).Any()) Directory.Delete(directory, false);
        return 0;
    }

    private static bool HasReparseAncestor(string path)
    {
        for (string? current = path; !string.IsNullOrEmpty(current); current = Path.GetDirectoryName(current))
            if ((Directory.Exists(current) || File.Exists(current)) && (File.GetAttributes(current) & FileAttributes.ReparsePoint) != 0) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private sealed class ServerLifetime(CancellationTokenSource cancellation) : IDisposable
    {
        public void Dispose() { cancellation.Cancel(); cancellation.Dispose(); }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Localization/LocalizedUi.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Documents;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Localization;

/// <summary>
/// Localizes presentation properties without recreating controls, changing their data,
/// or replacing bindings. Editable text, user filenames and diagnostic documents stay verbatim.
/// Loaded/unloaded scopes also cover popup menus and virtualized task rows.
/// </summary>
public static class LocalizedUi
{
    private static bool initialized;
    private static readonly ConditionalWeakTable<DependencyObject, Scope> scopes = new();
    private static readonly List<WeakReference<Scope>> live = new();
    public static readonly DependencyProperty PreserveTextProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "PreserveText", typeof(bool), typeof(LocalizedUi), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
    public static void SetPreserveText(DependencyObject owner, bool value) => owner.SetValue(PreserveTextProperty, value);
    public static bool GetPreserveText(DependencyObject owner) => (bool)owner.GetValue(PreserveTextProperty);

    public static void Initialize()
    {
        if (initialized) return;
        initialized = true;
        EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(FrameworkElement), FrameworkElement.LoadedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(Loaded), true);
        EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(FrameworkContentElement), FrameworkContentElement.LoadedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(Loaded), true);
        EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ContextMenu), ContextMenu.OpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler((sender, _) => ScheduleTree((DependencyObject)sender)), true);
        EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(ToolTip), ToolTip.OpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler((sender, _) => ScheduleTree((DependencyObject)sender)), true);
        Locale.Changed += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (Application.Current?.Dispatcher is { } dispatcher && !dispatcher.CheckAccess())
                dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(Refresh), DispatcherPriority.DataBind);
            else Refresh();
        };
    }

    private static void Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args)
    {
        // Loaded bubbles: each element owns exactly one presentation scope.
        if (sender is not DependencyObject owner || GetPreserveText(owner)) return;
        Attach(owner);
        if (owner is Window || owner is UserControl) AttachTree(owner);
        if (owner is DataGrid grid)
            foreach (var column in grid.Columns)
            {
                Attach(column);
            }
    }
    public static void ScheduleTree(DependencyObject root)
    {
        root.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() => AttachTree(root)), DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
    }
    public static void AttachTree(DependencyObject root)
    {
        var seen = new HashSet<DependencyObject>();
        var queue = new Queue<DependencyObject>(); queue.Enqueue(root);
        while (queue.Count > 0)
        {
            var owner = queue.Dequeue(); if (!seen.Add(owner) || GetPreserveText(owner)) continue;
            // Gather children before translating Content, which may replace a string's presenter.
            if (owner is Visual)
                for (var i = 0; i < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(owner); i++) queue.Enqueue(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(owner, i));
            foreach (var child in LogicalTreeHelper.GetChildren(owner)) if (child is DependencyObject dependency) queue.Enqueue(dependency);
            Attach(owner);
            if (owner is DataGrid grid && grid.IsLoaded) foreach (var column in grid.Columns) Attach(column);
        }
    }
    public static void Attach(DependencyObject owner)
    {
        if (GetPreserveText(owner)) return;
        // Artwork and layout nodes usually have no text. In particular, observing
        // their default, empty accessibility name and tooltip creates WPF property
        // descriptors for every sprite without providing anything to translate.
        if (!scopes.TryGetValue(owner, out _) && !NeedsScope(owner)) return;
        var scope = scopes.GetValue(owner, item =>
        {
            var value = new Scope(item);
            live.Add(new WeakReference<Scope>(value));
            if (live.Count > 2048) live.RemoveAll(reference => !reference.TryGetTarget(out _));
            return value;
        });
        scope.Start();
    }
    private static bool HasMetadata(DependencyObject owner, DependencyProperty property) =>
        BindingOperations.IsDataBound(owner, property) || owner.ReadLocalValue(property) != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue ||
        owner.GetValue(property) is string { Length: > 0 };
    private static bool NeedsScope(DependencyObject owner) =>
        owner is TextBlock or Run or Window or ContentControl or HeaderedItemsControl or DataGridColumn or ItemsControl ||
        (owner is FrameworkElement or FrameworkContentElement &&
         (HasMetadata(owner, ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty) || HasMetadata(owner, AutomationProperties.NameProperty)));
    public static void Refresh()
    {
        for (var i = live.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        {
            if (!live[i].TryGetTarget(out var scope)) { live.RemoveAt(i); continue; }
            scope.Refresh();
        }
    }

    private sealed class Scope
    {
        private readonly DependencyObject owner;
        private readonly List<TextProperty> properties = new();
        private bool active;
        public Scope(DependencyObject owner)
        {
            this.owner = owner;
            if (owner is TextBlock) Add(TextBlock.TextProperty);
            if (owner is Run) Add(Run.TextProperty);
            if (owner is Window) Add(Window.TitleProperty);
            if (owner is ContentControl && owner is not TextBox && owner is not PasswordBox) Add(ContentControl.ContentProperty);
            if (owner is HeaderedContentControl) Add(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty);
            if (owner is HeaderedItemsControl) Add(HeaderedItemsControl.HeaderProperty);
            if (owner is DataGridColumn) Add(DataGridColumn.HeaderProperty);
            EnsureMetadata();
            if (owner is FrameworkElement element) element.Unloaded += (_, _) => Stop();
            if (owner is FrameworkContentElement content) content.Unloaded += (_, _) => Stop();
            if (owner is Expander expander) expander.Expanded += (_, _) => ScheduleTree(expander);
            if (owner is ItemsControl items)
                items.ItemContainerGenerator.StatusChanged += (_, _) =>
                {
                    if (items.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated) ScheduleTree(items);
                };
            if (owner is DataGrid grid)
            {
                grid.LoadingRow += (_, args) => ScheduleTree(args.Row);
                grid.Unloaded += (_, _) => { foreach (var column in grid.Columns) if (scopes.TryGetValue(column, out var scope)) scope.Stop(); };
            }
        }
        private void Add(DependencyProperty property)
        {
            foreach (var existing in properties) if (existing.Property == property) return;
            var binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(owner, property);
            if (binding?.Mode is BindingMode.TwoWay or BindingMode.OneWayToSource) return;
            var path = binding?.Path?.Path;
            if (path is "SourcePath" or "OutputPath" or "OutputDirectory" or "EngineDirectory" or "ArchiveEngineDllPath" or "TechnicalDetails") return;
            if (path == "Name" && owner is FrameworkElement element && element.DataContext is ConversionJobViewModel or ArchiveJobViewModel or RecoveryJobViewModel or ImageObfuscationJobViewModel or HomeImportItem or TaskCenterItem) return;
            var textProperty = new TextProperty(owner, property);
            properties.Add(textProperty);
            if (active) textProperty.Start();
        }
        private void EnsureMetadata()
        {
            if (owner is not FrameworkElement && owner is not FrameworkContentElement) return;
            if (HasMetadata(owner, ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty)) Add(ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty);
            if (HasMetadata(owner, AutomationProperties.NameProperty)) Add(AutomationProperties.NameProperty);
        }
        public void Start()
        {
            EnsureMetadata();
            // Unexpanded logical content may never enter the visual tree. Do not
            // install descriptor handlers until loaded: otherwise that content could
            // be retained after navigating away without ever receiving Unloaded.
            var loaded = owner switch { FrameworkElement element => element.IsLoaded, FrameworkContentElement content => content.IsLoaded, _ => true };
            if (!active && loaded) { active = true; foreach (var property in properties) property.Start(); }
            foreach (var property in properties) property.Render();
        }
        public void Stop()
        {
            if (!active) return;
            active = false;
            foreach (var property in properties) property.Stop();
        }
        public void Refresh()
        {
            if (!active) return;
            EnsureMetadata();
            foreach (var property in properties) property.Render();
        }
    }

    private sealed class TextProperty
    {
        private readonly DependencyObject owner;
        private readonly DependencyProperty property;
        private readonly DependencyPropertyDescriptor? descriptor;
        private string? source, rendered;
        private bool updating;
        public DependencyProperty Property => property;
        public TextProperty(DependencyObject owner, DependencyProperty property)
        { this.owner = owner; this.property = property; descriptor = DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(property, owner.GetType()); }
        public void Start() => descriptor?.AddValueChanged(owner, Changed);
        public void Stop() => descriptor?.RemoveValueChanged(owner, Changed);
        private void Changed(object? sender, EventArgs args) { if (!updating) Render(); }
        public void Render()
        {
            if (updating || GetPreserveText(owner) || owner.GetValue(property) is not string text) return;
            if (source is null || text != rendered) source = text;
            var next = Locale.TranslateText(source);
            rendered = next;
            if (text == next) return;
            updating = true;
            try { owner.SetCurrentValue(property, next); }
            finally { updating = false; }
        }
    }
}
