    private sealed record ArchiveSettingsEditor(FrameworkElement Panel, Func<bool> Apply);
    private ArchiveSettingsEditor CreateArchiveSettingsEditor(Window owner)
    {
        var body = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 19, 0, 0) };
        body.Children.Add(Text("压缩包设置", 13, FontWeights.SemiBold));
        body.Children.Add(Text(Model.ArchiveEngineStatus, 11, margin: new Thickness(0, 7, 0, 9)));
        var advanced = new Expander { Header = "高级：压缩包引擎与解压保护", FontSize = 12, IsEnabled = Model.CanEdit };
        var panel = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 0) };
        panel.Children.Add(Text("7-Zip 引擎文件（7z.dll · Windows x64）", 11));
        var path = new TextBox { Text = Model.ArchiveEngineDllPath, Padding = new Thickness(8), FontSize = 14, Margin = new Thickness(0, 7, 0, 7) };
        panel.Children.Add(path);
        var buttons = new WrapPanel();
        var choose = MakeButton("选择 DLL"); choose.Click += (_, _) =>
        { var picker = new OpenFileDialog { Title = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("选择可信的 Windows x64 7z.dll"), Filter = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("7-Zip 引擎|7z.dll|DLL 文件|*.dll"), CheckFileExists = true }; if (picker.ShowDialog(owner) == true) path.Text = picker.FileName; };
        var restore = MakeButton("恢复内置引擎"); restore.Margin = new Thickness(9, 0, 0, 0); restore.Click += (_, _) => path.Text = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll");
        buttons.Children.Add(choose); buttons.Children.Add(restore); panel.Children.Add(buttons);
        panel.Children.Add(Text("引擎随软件提供。替换时选择来源可信、接口兼容的 x64 版本。", 10, margin: new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 16)));
        advanced.SetBinding(IsEnabledProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("CanEdit") { Source = Model });
        var limits = Model.ArchiveExtractionLimits;
        TextBox AddLimit(string label, string value)
        {
            var grid = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(0, 5, 0, 0) };
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(160) });
            grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });
            var name = Text(label, 11); name.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center; grid.Children.Add(name);
            var input = new TextBox { Text = value, Padding = new Thickness(7, 5, 7, 5), FontSize = 14 };
            Grid.SetColumn(input, 1); grid.Children.Add(input); panel.Children.Add(grid); return input;
        }
        var entries = AddLimit("最大解压条目数", limits.MaximumEntries.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        var depth = AddLimit("最大目录层数", limits.MaximumDepth.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        var size = AddLimit("最大解压总量（GiB）", (limits.MaximumBytes / 1073741824m).ToString("0.####################", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        panel.Children.Add(Text("超过任一上限会停止解压并清理本次临时内容；1 GiB = 1,073,741,824 字节。", 10, margin: new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)));
        advanced.Content = panel; body.Children.Add(advanced);
        return new(body, () =>
        {
            if (!int.TryParse(entries.Text, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var entryLimit) || entryLimit < 1 ||
                !int.TryParse(depth.Text, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var depthLimit) || depthLimit < 1 ||
                !decimal.TryParse(size.Text, NumberStyles.AllowDecimalPoint, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var gib) || gib <= 0 || gib > long.MaxValue / 1073741824m || decimal.Floor(gib * 1073741824m) < 1)
            { ShowInformation("解压保护设置", "请填写大于 0 的条目数、目录层数和解压总量。"); return false; }
            var bundled = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll");
            try
            {
                var full = Path.GetFullPath(path.Text);
                if (!string.Equals(full, Model.ArchiveEngineDllPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && !File.Exists(full))
                { ShowInformation("压缩包引擎不完整", "请选择有效的 7z.dll 文件。"); return false; }
                Model.SetArchiveEngineDllPath(string.Equals(full, bundled, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? null : full);
                Model.SetArchiveLimits(new(entryLimit, depthLimit, (long)decimal.Floor(gib * 1073741824m)));
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException or IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
            { ShowInformation("压缩包引擎设置", "引擎路径无效，请重新选择 7z.dll。"); return false; }
        });
    }
}

using System;
using System.Windows;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;

public partial class MainWindow
{
    private HomeCompanionView? homeCompanion;
    public HomeCompanionView? HomeCompanion => homeCompanion;
    public int HomeGreetingCount { get; private set; }
    public string HomeGreetingText { get; private set; } = "";
    public bool HomeGreetingConsidered { get; private set; }

    private void AttachHomeCompanion(PetController controller)
    {
        DisposeHomeCompanion();
        homeCompanion = new HomeCompanionView(controller);
        homeCompanion.BecameActive += HomeCompanionBecameActive;
        if (shellPage == "home") ShowHomePage();
    }
    private FrameworkElement? HomeCompanionRegion()
    {
        if (homeCompanion is null) return null;
        Detach(homeCompanion);
        return homeCompanion;
    }
    private void HomeCompanionBecameActive(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        if (HomeGreetingConsidered || homeCompanion is null) return;
        HomeGreetingConsidered = true;
        var greeting = HomePetGreeting.Choose(DateTime.Now, Random.Shared);
        if (!homeCompanion.Controller.TryStartupGreeting(greeting)) return;
        HomeGreetingText = greeting;
        HomeGreetingCount++;
    }
    private void DisposeHomeCompanion()
    {
        if (homeCompanion is null) return;
        homeCompanion.BecameActive -= HomeCompanionBecameActive;
        homeCompanion.Dispose();
        homeCompanion = null;
    }
}

using System;
using System.Windows;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;

public partial class MainWindow
{
    private PetController? petController;
    private PetPage? petPage;
    public Func<bool>? HideToBackground { get; set; }
    public bool IsPetPageOpen => PetPageHost.Visibility == Visibility.Visible;
    public void AttachPetController(PetController controller)
    {
        petPage?.Dispose();
        petPage = null;
        PetPageHost.Child = null;
        petController=controller;
        AttachHomeCompanion(controller);
    }
    public void ShowPetPage(string tab="companion")
    {
        if(petController is null || !Model.CanNavigate)return;
        // Home companionship does not need the shop, inventory or settings visual tree.
        if (petPage is null) { petPage = new PetPage(petController); PetPageHost.Child = petPage; }
        shellPage = "pet";
        homeCompanion?.SetHomeVisible(false);
        ShellPageHost.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
        ShellPageHost.Child = null;
        ContentGrid.Visibility=Visibility.Collapsed;
        PetPageHost.Visibility=Visibility.Visible;
        petPage.SelectTab(tab);
        CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Localization.LocalizedUi.ScheduleTree(petPage);
        UpdateNavigationSelection();
        UpdateActiveBatchVisibility();
    }
    public void ShowFilePage()
    {
        if (!Model.CanNavigate) return;
        shellPage = "files";
        homeCompanion?.SetHomeVisible(false);
        ShellPageHost.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
        ShellPageHost.Child = null;
        PetPageHost.Visibility=Visibility.Collapsed;
        ContentGrid.Visibility=Visibility.Visible;
        CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Localization.LocalizedUi.ScheduleTree(ContentGrid);
        UpdateNavigationSelection();
        ApplyShellLayout();
        UpdateActiveBatchVisibility();
    }
    private void Pet_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e) => ShowPetPage();
    public void ApproveExit() => exitApproved=true;
    public bool ConfirmApplicationExit() => ConfirmStopAndExit();
}

--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/MainWindow.Shell.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;

public sealed class HomeImportItem : ObservableObject
{
    public string Path { get; }
    public string Name => System.IO.Path.GetFileName(Path.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
    public string Information { get; }
    private string reason = "";
    public string Reason { get => reason; set => Set(ref reason, value); }
    public HomeImportItem(string path)
    {
        Path = path;
        try
        {
            if (Directory.Exists(path)) Information = "文件夹 · 打包时包含子文件夹";
            else if (File.Exists(path)) Information = System.IO.Path.GetExtension(path).TrimStart('.').ToUpperInvariant() + " · " + ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(new FileInfo(path).Length);
            else { Information = "无法读取"; Reason = "文件已移动或不存在，请重新选择。"; }
        }
        catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or ArgumentException)
        { Information = "无法读取"; Reason = "无法读取文件信息，请检查访问权限。"; }
    }
}

    private RadioButton[] FileNavigation => new[] { NavVideo, NavAudio, NavImage, NavArchiveCreate, NavArchiveExtract, NavRecovery, NavGif, NavCompression, NavExtract, NavImageObfuscate, NavImageDeobfuscate };
    private void UpdateNavigationSelection()
    {
        var selected = shellPage == "files" ? Model.IsArchiveCreatePage ? NavArchiveCreate : Model.IsArchiveExtractPage ? NavArchiveExtract : Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? NavRecovery : Model.IsImageObfuscatePage ? NavImageObfuscate : Model.IsImageDeobfuscatePage ? NavImageDeobfuscate : Model.IsCompressionPage ? NavCompression : Model.IsGifPage ? NavGif : Model.Mode == ConversionMode.Audio ? NavAudio : Model.Mode == ConversionMode.Image ? NavImage : Model.Mode == ConversionMode.Extract ? NavExtract : NavVideo : shellPage == "home" ? NavHome : shellPage == "tasks" ? NavTasks : null;
        foreach (var nav in FileNavigation.Concat(new[] { NavHome, NavTasks }))
        {
            nav.SetCurrentValue(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty, nav == selected);
            UpdateNavigationSurface(nav, false);
        }
    }
    private void UpdateShellState(string? property)
    {
        if (property is "HasActiveBatch" or "ActiveBatchSource" or "CurrentFileAction" or "IsProcessing") UpdateActiveBatchVisibility();
        if (property is "CanNavigate" or "IsProcessing" or "IsImporting" or "IsMediaPage" or "VideoSelected" or "AudioSelected" or "ImageSelected" or "ExtractSelected" or "IsArchivePage" or "IsImageObfuscationPage" or "IsPasswordRecoveryPage" or "IsCompressionPage" or "IsGifPage") UpdateNavigationSelection();
        if (property == "ThemePreference" && themePicker is not null)
            themePicker.SelectedItem = themePicker.Items.OfType<ComboBoxItem>().FirstOrDefault(i => i.Tag as string == Model.ThemePreference);
        if (shellPage == "tasks" && property is "IsProcessing" or "IsImporting" or "ActiveBatchStatus" or "HasActiveBatch") taskView?.Refresh();
        if (property is "IsArchivePage" or "IsArchiveCreatePage") ApplyShellLayout();
    }
    private void UpdateActiveBatchVisibility()
    {
        if (ActiveBatchBar is null) return;
        static bool SharedConversion(FileProcessingAction action) => action is FileProcessingAction.Video or FileProcessingAction.Audio or FileProcessingAction.Image or FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio;
        var sourceVisible = shellPage == "files" && Model.ActiveBatchSource is { } action &&
            (Model.CurrentFileAction == action || SharedConversion(action) && SharedConversion(Model.CurrentFileAction));
        ActiveBatchBar.Visibility = Model.HasActiveBatch && !sourceVisible ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
    }

    private void ApplyShellLayout()
    {
        CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Localization.LocalizedUi.ScheduleTree(SidebarLayout);
        var compact = ActualWidth > 0 && ActualWidth < 900;
        SidebarColumn.Width = new GridLength(compact ? 72 : 216);
        SidebarLayout.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(7, 16, 0, 12) : new Thickness(16, 24, 0, 16);
        BrandHeading.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        foreach (var nav in FileNavigation.Concat(new[] { NavHome, NavTasks }))
        {
            if (nav.Content is not StackPanel panel) continue;
            var label = panel.Children.OfType<TextBlock>().FirstOrDefault();
            if (label is not null)
            { nav.ToolTip = label.Text; AutomationProperties.SetName(nav, label.Text); label.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible; }
            panel.HorizontalAlignment = compact ? HorizontalAlignment.Center : HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
            foreach (var icon in panel.Children.OfType<FunctionalIcon>()) icon.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0, 0, 10, 0);
            nav.Padding = compact ? new Thickness(9, 12, 9, 12) : new Thickness(12, 10, 12, 10);
        }
        if (SidebarNavigationScroll.Content is StackPanel navigation)
            foreach (var label in navigation.Children.OfType<TextBlock>()) label.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        AddFilesSide.ToolTip = "添加文件";
        if (AddFilesSide.Content is StackPanel add)
        {
            foreach (var label in add.Children.OfType<TextBlock>()) label.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
            foreach (var icon in add.Children.OfType<FunctionalIcon>()) icon.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0, 0, 6, 0);
        }
        AddFilesSide.Padding = compact ? new Thickness(6, 9, 6, 9) : new Thickness(14, 9, 14, 9);
        AddFoldersSide.Visibility = Model.IsArchiveCreatePage ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        AddFoldersSide.Content = compact ? "目录" : "添加文件夹";
        AddFoldersSide.ToolTip = "添加文件夹";
        PetEntry.Content = compact ? "猫咪" : "猫咪桌宠";
        PetEntry.ToolTip = "猫咪桌宠 · 陪伴、商店与背包";
        PetEntry.Padding = compact ? new Thickness(3, 8, 3, 8) : new Thickness(8);
        if (SidebarCommands is not null)
        {
            SidebarCommands.Columns = compact ? 1 : 2;
            foreach (var button in SidebarCommands.Children.OfType<Button>())
            {
                if (button.Content is not StackPanel panel) continue;
                foreach (var label in panel.Children.OfType<TextBlock>()) { button.ToolTip = label.Text; label.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible; }
                foreach (var icon in panel.Children.OfType<FunctionalIcon>()) icon.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0, 0, 5, 0);
            }
        }
        SidePrivacy.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Model.HasJobs ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        // Compact navigation still exposes archive folder selection through a context menu.
        if (compact && Model.IsArchiveCreatePage)
        {
            var menu = new ContextMenu(); var folder = new MenuItem { Header = "添加文件夹" };
            folder.SetBinding(IsEnabledProperty, new Binding("CanEdit") { Source = Model }); folder.Click += AddFolders_Click; menu.Items.Add(folder); AddFilesSide.ContextMenu = menu;
        }
        else AddFilesSide.ContextMenu = null;
    }

        buttons.Children.Add(ActionButton("移除选择", () => { foreach (var item in staging.SelectedItems.Cast<HomeImportItem>().ToArray()) homeItems.Remove(item); }, editing: true));

    private static void Detach(FrameworkElement element) { if (element.Parent is Panel old) old.Children.Remove(element); }
    private void SelectHomeFiles()
    {
        if (!Model.CanEdit) return;
        var picker = new OpenFileDialog { Multiselect = true, Title = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("选择待处理文件"), Filter = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("所有文件|*.*") };
        if (picker.ShowDialog(this) == true) StageHomeFiles(picker.FileNames);
    }
    public void StageHomeFiles(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        if (!Model.CanEdit) return;
        foreach (var path in paths)
        {
            try
            {
                var full = Path.GetFullPath(path);
                if (!homeItems.Any(item => string.Equals(item.Path, full, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))) homeItems.Add(new HomeImportItem(full));
            }
            catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or PathTooLongException) { homeFeedback.Text = "部分路径无法读取，请重新选择。"; }
        }
        homeFeedback.Text = $"已接住 {homeItems.Count} 项。选择用途后加入队列，再调整参数并开始。";
    }
    private async void ChooseHomeAction()
    {
        if (!Model.CanEdit || homeItems.Count == 0) { homeFeedback.Text = "先添加文件，再选择处理用途。"; return; }
        var action = FileActionPicker.Show(this, Model.GetFileActionChoices(homeItems.Select(item => item.Path)));
        if (action is not null) await ImportHomeFilesAsync(action.Value);
    }
    public async Task ImportHomeFilesAsync(FileProcessingAction action)
    {
        if (!Model.CanEdit || homeItems.Count == 0) return;
        try
        {
            var result = await Model.ImportForActionAsync(action, homeItems.Select(item => item.Path).ToArray());
            foreach (var accepted in result.AcceptedPaths)
            {
                var item = homeItems.FirstOrDefault(i => string.Equals(i.Path, accepted, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                if (item is not null) homeItems.Remove(item);
            }
            foreach (var rejection in result.RejectedPaths)
            {
                var item = homeItems.FirstOrDefault(i => string.Equals(i.Path, rejection.SourcePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                if (item is not null) item.Reason = rejection.Reason;
            }
            homeFeedback.Text = homeItems.Count == 0 ? "文件已加入队列，原文件不会改变。" : $"还有 {homeItems.Count} 项留在首页，可选择其他用途或查看原因。";
            if (result.AcceptedPaths.Count > 0) { ShowFilePage(); UpdateNavigationSelection(); }
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); homeFeedback.Text = "导入遇到问题，请查看原因或重新选择。"; }
    }

    private FrameworkElement BuildTaskPage()
    {
        var grid = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(24) };
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto }); grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto }); grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition());
        grid.Children.Add(PageHeading("任务中心", "查看本次会话的任务、处理状态与结果。"));
        var tools = new WrapPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 16) };
        var filter = new ComboBox { Width = 132, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 12, 8) }; AutomationProperties.SetName(filter, "任务状态筛选");
        filter.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = "全部状态" });
        foreach (var pair in new[] { (JobStatus.Pending, "等待"), (JobStatus.Processing, "处理中"), (JobStatus.Completed, "完成"), (JobStatus.Failed, "失败"), (JobStatus.Cancelled, "已取消") }) filter.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = pair.Item2, Tag = pair.Item1 });
        filter.SelectedItem = filter.Items.OfType<ComboBoxItem>().FirstOrDefault(i => Equals(i.Tag, taskStatusFilter)) ?? filter.Items[0];
        taskView = CollectionViewSource.GetDefaultView(Model.TaskCenterItems);
        taskView.Filter = item => item is TaskCenterItem task && (!taskStatusFilter.HasValue || task.Status == taskStatusFilter) && (taskSearch.Length == 0 || task.Name.Contains(taskSearch, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) || task.Information.Contains(taskSearch, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase));
        filter.SelectionChanged += (_, _) => { taskStatusFilter = (filter.SelectedItem as ComboBoxItem)?.Tag as JobStatus?; taskView.Refresh(); };
        tools.Children.Add(filter);
        var search = new TextBox { Width = 200, Text = taskSearch, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 8), ToolTip = "按文件名或文件信息查找" }; AutomationProperties.SetName(search, "搜索任务");
        search.TextChanged += (_, _) => { taskSearch = search.Text.Trim(); taskView.Refresh(); }; tools.Children.Add(search); Grid.SetRow(tools, 1); grid.Children.Add(tools);
        var list = new DataGrid { ItemsSource = taskView, AutoGenerateColumns = false, MinRowHeight = 80, RowHeight = double.NaN, RowDetailsVisibilityMode = DataGridRowDetailsVisibilityMode.VisibleWhenSelected };
        AutomationProperties.SetName(list, "全部会话任务列表");
        list.Columns.Add(TemplateColumn("文件 / 功能", "Name", "SourceTitle", 1.4));
        list.Columns.Add(TemplateColumn("方案 / 文件信息", "Format", "Information", 1));
        var status = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(StackPanel)); status.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 8));
        var text = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(TextBlock)); text.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new Binding("StatusText")); text.SetValue(TextBlock.TextWrappingProperty, TextWrapping.Wrap); status.AppendChild(text);
        var progress = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ProgressBar)); progress.SetValue(ProgressBar.MaximumProperty, 100d); progress.SetValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, 6d); progress.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)); progress.SetBinding(ProgressBar.ValueProperty, new Binding("Progress") { Mode = BindingMode.OneWay }); progress.SetBinding(ProgressBar.IsIndeterminateProperty, new Binding("IsIndeterminate"));
        var progressStyle = new Style(typeof(ProgressBar), (Style)FindResource("RoundedProgress")); progressStyle.Setters.Add(new Setter(VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Collapsed)); var processing = new DataTrigger { Binding = new Binding("Status"), Value = JobStatus.Processing }; processing.Setters.Add(new Setter(VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Visible)); progressStyle.Triggers.Add(processing); progress.SetValue(StyleProperty, progressStyle); status.AppendChild(progress);
        list.Columns.Add(new DataGridTemplateColumn { Header = "状态", Width = new DataGridLength(1, DataGridLengthUnitType.Star), MinWidth = 115, CellTemplate = new DataTemplate { VisualTree = status } });
        var actions = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(StackPanel)); actions.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 6));
        AddTaskButton(actions, "查看来源", TaskSource_Click, null);
        AddTaskButton(actions, "打开结果", TaskResult_Click, "HasOutput");
        AddTaskButton(actions, "打开所在位置", TaskFolder_Click, "HasOutput");
        AddTaskButton(actions, "查看原因", TaskError_Click, "HasError");
        list.Columns.Add(new DataGridTemplateColumn { Header = "操作", Width = 136, CellTemplate = new DataTemplate { VisualTree = actions } });
        var details = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ItemsControl)); details.SetBinding(ItemsControl.ItemsSourceProperty, new Binding("Inputs")); details.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(16, 8, 16, 8)); var input = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(TextBlock)); input.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new Binding()); input.SetValue(TextBlock.TextWrappingProperty, TextWrapping.Wrap); details.SetValue(ItemsControl.ItemTemplateProperty, new DataTemplate { VisualTree = input });
        var detailScroll = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ScrollViewer)); detailScroll.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MaxHeightProperty, 120d); detailScroll.SetValue(ScrollViewer.VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty, ScrollBarVisibility.Auto); detailScroll.AppendChild(details); list.RowDetailsTemplate = new DataTemplate { VisualTree = detailScroll };
        var card = ShellCard(list, new Thickness(8)); card.Margin = new Thickness(0); Grid.SetRow(card, 2); grid.Children.Add(card);
        return grid;
    }
    private DataGridTemplateColumn TemplateColumn(string heading, string primary, string? secondary, double width)
    {
        var panel = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(StackPanel)); panel.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 8));
        var title = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(TextBlock)); title.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new Binding(primary)); title.SetBinding(ToolTipProperty, new Binding(primary)); title.SetValue(TextBlock.TextTrimmingProperty, TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis); panel.AppendChild(title);
        if (secondary is not null)
        {
            var caption = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(TextBlock)); caption.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new Binding(secondary)); caption.SetBinding(ToolTipProperty, new Binding(secondary)); caption.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "Muted"); caption.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, "CaptionSize"); caption.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0)); caption.SetValue(TextBlock.TextWrappingProperty, TextWrapping.Wrap); panel.AppendChild(caption);
        }
        return new DataGridTemplateColumn { Header = heading, Width = new DataGridLength(width, DataGridLengthUnitType.Star), MinWidth = 100, CellTemplate = new DataTemplate { VisualTree = panel } };
    }
    private void AddTaskButton(FrameworkElementFactory panel, string label, RoutedEventHandler handler, string? visibility)
    {
        var button = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Button)); button.SetValue(ContentControl.ContentProperty, label); button.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "ResultActionButton"); button.SetValue(Control.PaddingProperty, new Thickness(6, 3, 6, 3)); button.SetValue(Control.FontSizeProperty, 12d); button.SetValue(FrameworkElement.MarginProperty, new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 2)); button.SetValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, label); button.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.TagProperty, new Binding()); button.AddHandler(Button.ClickEvent, handler);
        if (visibility is not null) button.SetBinding(VisibilityProperty, new Binding(visibility) { Converter = (IValueConverter)FindResource("BoolVisibility") });
        panel.AppendChild(button);
    }

    { if (sender is Button { Tag: TaskCenterItem { HasOutput: true } item }) OpenResultPath(item.OutputPath, false); }

    { if (sender is Button { Tag: TaskCenterItem { HasOutput: true } item }) OpenResultPath(item.OutputPath, true); }

    { if (sender is Button { Tag: TaskCenterItem { Error: { } error } }) ShowError(error); }
    private void ConversionResult_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { if (sender is Button { Tag: ConversionJobViewModel { HasOutput: true, OutputPath: { } path } }) OpenResultPath(path, false); }
    private void ConversionLocation_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { if (sender is Button { Tag: ConversionJobViewModel { HasOutput: true, OutputPath: { } path } }) OpenResultPath(path, true); }
    private void OpenResultPath(string? path, bool folder)
    {
        if (path is null) return;
        try
        {
            if (!File.Exists(path) && !Directory.Exists(path)) { ShowInformation("找不到结果", "文件可能已移动或删除，请检查输出位置。"); return; }
            if (folder) ResultFileActions.Reveal(path);
            else Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
    }

    private void AddAppearanceSettings(StackPanel body)
    {
        Detach(settingsFeedback);
        body.Children.Add(Text("外观", 18, FontWeights.SemiBold, new Thickness(0, 24, 0, 8)));
        themePicker = new ComboBox { MaxWidth = 320, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 240 };
        AutomationProperties.SetName(themePicker, "主题模式");
        foreach (var pair in new[] { ("System", "跟随系统"), ("Light", "浅色"), ("Dark", "深色") }) themePicker.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = pair.Item2, Tag = pair.Item1 });
        themePicker.SelectedItem = themePicker.Items.OfType<ComboBoxItem>().FirstOrDefault(i => i.Tag as string == Model.ThemePreference);
        themePicker.SelectionChanged += (_, _) => { if (themePicker.SelectedItem is ComboBoxItem { Tag: string preference }) Model.ThemePreference = preference; };
        body.Children.Add(themePicker);
        body.Children.Add(Text("外观与动画偏好立即生效，不影响正在处理的文件。", 12, margin: new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)));
    }
    private void ShowInformation(string title, string message)
    {
        var dialog = CreateDialog(title, 440, 240); dialog.SizeToContent = SizeToContent.Height;
        var panel = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(24) }; panel.Children.Add(Text(title, 18, FontWeights.SemiBold)); panel.Children.Add(Text(message, margin: new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 20)));
        var close = MakeButton("知道了", true); close.IsDefault = true; close.IsCancel = true; close.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right; close.Click += (_, _) => dialog.Close(); panel.Children.Add(close); dialog.Content = panel; dialog.ShowDialog();
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

using System.Linq;

using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using System.Windows.Documents;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Localization;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop;

public partial class MainWindow : Window
{
    private static readonly DependencyProperty SurfaceAnimationVersionProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "SurfaceAnimationVersion", typeof(long), typeof(MainWindow), new PropertyMetadata(0L));
    public MainViewModel Model { get; }
    private readonly Func<bool>? confirmStopAndExit;
    private bool exitApproved;
    private bool closingAfterStop;
    public Func<bool>? BeforeExit { get; set; }
    private readonly HashSet<ConversionJobViewModel> newQueueItems = new();
    private readonly HashSet<Border> animatedSurfaces = new();
    private bool rowRevealScheduled;
    private bool navigationScrollScheduled;
    private int hoverDuration = 140;
    private int selectionDuration = 160;
    private int rowEntranceDuration = 160;
    private int dialogDuration = 180;
    public MainWindow(MainViewModel? model = null, Func<bool>? confirmStopAndExit = null)
    {
        Model = model ?? new MainViewModel();
        this.confirmStopAndExit = confirmStopAndExit;
        ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
        ThemeManager.SetPreference(Model.ThemePreference);
        LocalizedUi.Initialize();
        InitializeComponent();
        Loaded += (_, _) => LocalizedUi.AttachTree(this);
        ThemedWindowFrame.Attach(this);
        var workArea = SystemParameters.WorkArea;
        MinWidth = Math.Min(620, Math.Max(500, workArea.Width - 24));
        MinHeight = Math.Min(480, Math.Max(320, workArea.Height - 24));
        Width = Math.Max(MinWidth, Math.Min(1200, workArea.Width - 24));
        Height = Math.Max(MinHeight, Math.Min(820, workArea.Height - 24));
        ApplyDesignTokens();
        DataContext = Model;
        InitializeShell();
        SizeChanged += (_, _) =>
        {
            SummaryCards.Visibility = ActualHeight < 740 ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
            PageSubtitle.Visibility = ActualHeight < 620 ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
            HeadingPanel.Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, ActualHeight < 620 ? 10 : 19);
            var compactEmpty = ActualHeight < 620;
            EmptyLayout.Orientation = compactEmpty ? Orientation.Horizontal : Orientation.Vertical;
            EmptyCat.Width = compactEmpty ? 56 : 144;
            EmptyCat.Height = compactEmpty ? 56 : 144;
            EmptyCat.Presentation = compactEmpty ? CatPresentation.Compact : CatPresentation.Full;
            EmptyTextLayout.Margin = compactEmpty ? new Thickness(12, 0, 0, 0) : new Thickness(0);
            EmptyAssistantText.FontSize = compactEmpty ? 14 : 17;
            EmptyAssistantText.MaxWidth = compactEmpty ? 190 : 420;
            EmptyAssistantText.Margin = compactEmpty ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0);
            EmptyDragInstruction.Visibility = compactEmpty ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
            EmptyFormats.Visibility = compactEmpty ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
            EmptyPrivacy.Margin = new Thickness(0, compactEmpty ? 7 : 10, 0, 0);
            ApplyShellLayout();
            ApplyCompressionLayout();
            ScheduleSelectedNavigationReveal();
        };
        Model.PropertyChanged += Model_PropertyChanged;
        Model.ArchivePasswordCleared += Model_ArchivePasswordCleared;
        SyncArchiveSplitPreset();
        Model.Queue.CollectionChanged += Queue_CollectionChanged;
        MotionPreferences.Changed += MotionPreference_Changed;
        Deactivated += Window_Deactivated;
        StateChanged += Window_StateChanged;
        IsVisibleChanged += Window_IsVisibleChanged;
        Closed += Window_Closed;
        FormatPicker.DropDownOpened += FormatPicker_DropDownOpened;
        LocalizedUi.ScheduleTree(this);
    }

    private void ApplyDesignTokens()
    {
        ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
        SetResourceReference(BackgroundProperty, "Background");
        SetResourceReference(FontFamilyProperty, "UiFont");
        int Read(string key, int fallback) => TryFindResource(key) is int value ? value : fallback;
        hoverDuration = Read("MotionHoverDurationMs", 140);
        selectionDuration = Read("MotionSelectionDurationMs", 160);
        rowEntranceDuration = Read("MotionRowEntranceDurationMs", 160);
        dialogDuration = Read("MotionDialogDurationMs", 200);
    }

    private void Nav_SurfaceChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (sender is RadioButton navigation)
        {
            UpdateNavigationSurface(navigation);
            if (navigation.IsLoaded && navigation.IsChecked == true && (navigation == NavImageObfuscate || navigation == NavImageDeobfuscate || navigation == NavArchiveCreate || navigation == NavArchiveExtract || navigation == NavRecovery))
                Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(navigation.BringIntoView), DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
        }
    }

    private void ScheduleSelectedNavigationReveal()
    {
        if (navigationScrollScheduled) return;
        navigationScrollScheduled = true;
        Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() =>
        {
            navigationScrollScheduled = false;
            if (!IsLoaded) return;
            foreach (var navigation in new[] { NavVideo, NavAudio, NavImage, NavImageObfuscate, NavImageDeobfuscate, NavExtract, NavCompression, NavGif, NavArchiveCreate, NavArchiveExtract, NavRecovery })
                if (navigation.IsChecked == true) { navigation.BringIntoView(); break; }
        }), DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
    }

    private void UpdateNavigationSurface(RadioButton navigation, bool animate = true)
    {
        if (navigation.Template?.FindName("Surface", navigation) is not Border surface) return;
        var selected = navigation.IsChecked == true;
        AnimateSurface(surface, selected ? ResourceColor("AccentTint") : navigation.IsMouseOver ? ResourceColor("HoverSurface") : Colors.Transparent,
            navigation.IsKeyboardFocused ? ResourceColor("Accent") : Colors.Transparent, selected ? selectionDuration : hoverDuration, animate);
    }

    private Color ResourceColor(string key) => (TryFindResource(key) as SolidColorBrush)?.Color ?? Colors.Transparent;

    private static void AnimateBrush(SolidColorBrush brush, Color target, int duration, bool animate)
    {
        var current = brush.Color;
        var version = (long)brush.GetValue(SurfaceAnimationVersionProperty) + 1;
        brush.SetValue(SurfaceAnimationVersionProperty, version);
        brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, null);
        brush.Color = target;
        if (animate && !MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion && current != target)
        {
            var animation = new ColorAnimation(current, target, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(duration))
            { EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop };
            animation.Completed += (_, _) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    if ((long)brush.GetValue(SurfaceAnimationVersionProperty) == version)
                        brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, null);
                }
                catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Completed surface clock cleanup fallback: {0}", error.Message); }
            };
            brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
        }
    }

    private void AnimateSurface(Border surface, Color background, Color border, int duration, bool animate)
    {
        try
        {
            var initialize = animatedSurfaces.Add(surface);
            if (surface.Background is not SolidColorBrush fill || fill.IsFrozen || initialize)
            {
                fill = new SolidColorBrush((surface.Background as SolidColorBrush)?.Color ?? Colors.Transparent);
                surface.Background = fill;
            }
            if (surface.BorderBrush is not SolidColorBrush outline || outline.IsFrozen || initialize)
            {
                outline = new SolidColorBrush((surface.BorderBrush as SolidColorBrush)?.Color ?? Colors.Transparent);
                surface.BorderBrush = outline;
            }
            var canAnimate = animate && IsActive && IsLoaded && surface.IsVisible && WindowState != WindowState.Minimized;
            AnimateBrush(fill, background, duration, canAnimate);
            AnimateBrush(outline, border, duration, canAnimate);
        }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
            Trace.TraceWarning("Surface animation fallback: {0}", error.Message);
            RestoreStaticSurfaceBrush(surface, Border.BackgroundProperty, background);
            RestoreStaticSurfaceBrush(surface, Border.BorderBrushProperty, border);
        }
    }

    private static void RestoreStaticSurfaceBrush(Border surface, DependencyProperty property, Color target)
    {
        try
        {
            if (surface.GetValue(property) is SolidColorBrush brush && !brush.IsFrozen)
            {
                brush.SetValue(SurfaceAnimationVersionProperty, (long)brush.GetValue(SurfaceAnimationVersionProperty) + 1);
                brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, null);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Surface clock cleanup fallback: {0}", error.Message); }
        try { surface.SetCurrentValue(property, new SolidColorBrush(target)); }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Static surface fallback: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private void Drop_SurfaceChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => UpdateDropSurface();

    private void UpdateDropSurface(bool animate = true)
    {
        if (DropArea?.Template?.FindName("DropSurface", DropArea) is not Border surface) return;
        var dragging = Model.IsDragging;
        AnimateSurface(surface, ResourceColor(dragging ? "AccentTint" : DropArea.IsMouseOver ? "HoverSurface" : "Surface"),
            DropArea.IsKeyboardFocused ? ResourceColor("Accent") : dragging ? ResourceColor("AccentBorder") : DropArea.IsMouseOver ? ResourceColor("Border") : Colors.Transparent,
            hoverDuration, animate);
    }

    private void DropArea_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        UpdateDropSurface();
        UpdateCatPointer(e);
    }

    private void DropArea_MouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e) => UpdateCatPointer(e);

    private void UpdateCatPointer(MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        if (!Model.IsEmpty || !DropArea.IsEnabled) return;
        var position = e.GetPosition(DropArea);
        EmptyCat.IsHovered = true;
        EmptyCat.PointerX = Math.Clamp(position.X / Math.Max(1, DropArea.ActualWidth) * 2 - 1, -1, 1);
        EmptyCat.PointerY = Math.Clamp(position.Y / Math.Max(1, DropArea.ActualHeight) * 2 - 1, -1, 1);
        EmptyCat.Wake();
    }

    private void DropArea_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        ResetCatPointer();
        UpdateDropSurface();
    }

    private void ResetCatPointer()
    {
        EmptyCat.IsHovered = false;
        EmptyCat.PointerX = 0;
        EmptyCat.PointerY = 0;
    }

    private void Window_PreviewInput(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    { EmptyCat.Wake(); if (e is KeyEventArgs { Key: Key.Escape }) { Model.SetDragging(false); DragOverlay.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; } }

    private void Model_PropertyChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.PropertyName) || e.PropertyName == nameof(MainViewModel.Language)) LocalizedUi.ScheduleTree(this);
        UpdateShellState(e.PropertyName);
        if (e.PropertyName is nameof(MainViewModel.IsDragging) or nameof(MainViewModel.IsEmpty) or nameof(MainViewModel.CanEdit))
        {
            UpdateDropSurface();
            if (!Model.IsEmpty || !Model.CanEdit) ResetCatPointer();
        }
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(MainViewModel.IsImporting) && !Model.IsImporting) ScheduleQueueReveal();
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(MainViewModel.ArchiveSplitMiB)) SyncArchiveSplitPreset();
        if (e.PropertyName is nameof(MainViewModel.IsImageObfuscationPage) or nameof(MainViewModel.IsCompressionPage) or nameof(MainViewModel.IsGifPage) or nameof(MainViewModel.IsArchivePage) or nameof(MainViewModel.IsPasswordRecoveryPage) or nameof(MainViewModel.HasJobs)) ApplyCompressionLayout();
    }

    private void ApplyCompressionLayout()
    {
        var compact = ActualHeight < 620;
        var toolPage = Model.IsImageObfuscationPage || Model.IsCompressionPage || Model.IsGifPage || Model.IsArchivePage || Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage;
        // Leave the task viewport room for percentage and candidate counts at the minimum height.
        RecoveryOptionsScroll.MaxHeight = Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage && ActualHeight < 520 ? Model.HasJobs ? 40 : 70 : compact ? 104 : ActualHeight < 740 ? 158 : 230;
        ArchiveOptionsScroll.MaxHeight = compact ? 96 : ActualHeight < 740 ? 180 : 245;
        CompressionOptionsScroll.MaxHeight = compact ? 104 : ActualHeight < 740 ? 156 : 220;
        GifOptionsScroll.MaxHeight = compact ? 80 : ActualHeight < 740 ? 138 : 180;
        ImageObfuscationOptionsScroll.MaxHeight = ActualHeight < 520 ? 70 : compact ? 100 : ActualHeight < 740 ? 145 : 190;
        GifDescriptionText.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        GifScopeText.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        CompressionDescriptionText.Visibility = ActualHeight < 700 ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        foreach (var hint in new[] { CompressionLargeHint, CompressionMediumHint, CompressionSmallHint, CompressionTinyHint }) hint.Visibility = ActualHeight < 700 ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        CompressionScopeText.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        SummaryCards.Visibility = toolPage || ActualHeight < 740 ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        QueueAssistantBanner.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        QueueSummaryText.SetCurrentValue(VisibilityProperty, Model.HasJobs && !compact ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed);
        QueueHeading.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(15, 7, 15, 7) : new Thickness(18, 12, 18, 11);
        QueueHeading.Visibility = toolPage && compact && Model.IsEmpty ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        QueueGrid.RowHeight = double.NaN;
        QueueGrid.MinRowHeight = Model.IsCompressionPage ? 88 : 72;
        var compressionCompact = compact;
        ContentGrid.Margin = compressionCompact ? new Thickness(16, 12, 16, 12) : new Thickness(25, 24, 25, 20);
        SettingsCard.Padding = compressionCompact ? new Thickness(10, 8, 10, 8) : new Thickness(13, 11, 13, 11);
        SettingsCard.Margin = compressionCompact ? new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10) : new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 15);
        QueueFooter.Padding = compressionCompact ? new Thickness(13, 8, 13, 8) : new Thickness(15, 12, 15, 12);
        StatusText.Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, compact ? 4 : 9);
        AppFooter.Visibility = compressionCompact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        PageTitleText.FontSize = compressionCompact ? 21 : 24;
        HeadingPanel.Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, compressionCompact ? 8 : compact ? 10 : 19);
        var horizontalEmpty = toolPage ? ActualHeight < 740 : compact;
        EmptyLayout.Orientation = horizontalEmpty ? Orientation.Horizontal : Orientation.Vertical;
        EmptyCat.Width = horizontalEmpty ? 56 : toolPage ? 112 : 144;
        EmptyCat.Height = EmptyCat.Width;
        EmptyCat.Presentation = horizontalEmpty ? CatPresentation.Compact : CatPresentation.Full;
        EmptyTextLayout.Margin = horizontalEmpty ? new Thickness(12, 0, 0, 0) : new Thickness(0);
        EmptyAssistantText.FontSize = horizontalEmpty ? 14 : 17;
        EmptyAssistantText.MaxWidth = horizontalEmpty ? 230 : 420;
        EmptyAssistantText.Margin = horizontalEmpty ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0);
        EmptyDragInstruction.Visibility = toolPage || !compact ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
        EmptyDragInstruction.Margin = new Thickness(0, horizontalEmpty ? 5 : 8, 0, 0);
        EmptyFormats.Visibility = horizontalEmpty ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        EmptyPrivacy.Visibility = compressionCompact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        EmptyPrivacy.Margin = new Thickness(0, horizontalEmpty ? 7 : 10, 0, 0);
    }

    private void Queue_CollectionChanged(object? sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset) newQueueItems.Clear();
        if (e.NewItems is not null)
            foreach (var item in e.NewItems.OfType<ConversionJobViewModel>()) newQueueItems.Add(item);
        if (!Model.IsImporting) ScheduleQueueReveal();
    }

    private void ScheduleQueueReveal()
    {
        if (rowRevealScheduled || newQueueItems.Count == 0) return;
        rowRevealScheduled = true;
        Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, new Action(() =>
        {
            rowRevealScheduled = false;
            var revealed = 0;
            foreach (var item in newQueueItems)
                if (revealed < 8 && QueueGrid.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(item) is DataGridRow row && row.IsVisible)
                { UiMotion.Reveal(row, 4, rowEntranceDuration); revealed++; }
            newQueueItems.Clear();
        }));
    }

    private void QueueGrid_UnloadingRow(object sender, DataGridRowEventArgs e) => UiMotion.Stop(e.Row);

    private void FormatPicker_DropDownOpened(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        if (FormatPicker.Template?.FindName("PART_Popup", FormatPicker) is Popup { Child: FrameworkElement content })
            content.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, new Action(() => UiMotion.Reveal(content, 4, dialogDuration)));
    }

    private void FinishSurfaceAnimations()
    {
        foreach (var navigation in FileNavigation.Concat(new[] { NavHome, NavTasks })) UpdateNavigationSurface(navigation, false);
        UpdateDropSurface(false);
    }

    private void MotionPreference_Changed(object? sender, EventArgs e) => FinishSurfaceAnimations();
    private void Window_Deactivated(object? sender, EventArgs e) { ResetCatPointer(); FinishSurfaceAnimations(); DragOverlay.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; }
    private void Window_StateChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e) { if (WindowState == WindowState.Minimized) FinishSurfaceAnimations(); }
    private void Window_IsVisibleChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e) { if (!(bool)e.NewValue) FinishSurfaceAnimations(); }

    private void Window_Closed(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        DisposeShell();
        petPage?.Dispose();
        Model.ClearArchivePassword();
        Model.ClearRecoverySecrets();
        Model.ArchivePasswordCleared -= Model_ArchivePasswordCleared;
        Model.PropertyChanged -= Model_PropertyChanged;
        Model.Queue.CollectionChanged -= Queue_CollectionChanged;
        MotionPreferences.Changed -= MotionPreference_Changed;
        FormatPicker.DropDownOpened -= FormatPicker_DropDownOpened;
        newQueueItems.Clear();
        FinishSurfaceAnimations();
        animatedSurfaces.Clear();
    }

    private async void AddFiles_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (!Model.CanEdit) return;
        if (shellPage == "home") { SelectHomeFiles(); return; }
        try
        {
            var extensions = Model.IsImageObfuscationPage ? MainViewModel.ImageObfuscationExtensions : Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? new[] { ".zip", ".7z", ".rar", ".7z.*", ".zip.*", ".r??", ".z??" } : Model.IsArchiveExtractPage ? ArchiveImportService.ExtensionsForExtraction : Model.IsCompressionPage ? new[] { ConversionMode.Video, ConversionMode.Audio, ConversionMode.Image }.SelectMany(ImportService.ExtensionsFor).Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) : ImportService.ExtensionsFor(Model.Mode);
            var patterns = string.Join(";", extensions.Select(ext => "*" + ext));
            var dialog = new OpenFileDialog { Multiselect = true, Title = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("把文件交给猫咪"), Filter = Model.IsArchiveCreatePage ? "所有文件|*.*" : $"支持的文件|{patterns}|所有文件|*.*", CheckFileExists = true };
            if (dialog.ShowDialog(this) == true) await Model.AddFilesAsync(dialog.FileNames);
        }
        catch (Exception error) { if (Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage) Model.HandleRecoveryUnexpectedError(); else Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
    }
    private void Output_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        try
        {
            var dialog = new OpenFolderDialog { Title = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("选择处理后的保存位置"), Multiselect = false };
            if (Directory.Exists(Model.OutputDirectory)) dialog.InitialDirectory = Model.OutputDirectory;
            if (dialog.ShowDialog(this) == true) Model.OutputDirectory = dialog.FolderName;
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
    }
    private void OpenOutput_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        try { Directory.CreateDirectory(Model.OutputDirectory); Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo("explorer.exe") { ArgumentList = { Model.OutputDirectory }, UseShellExecute = true }); }
        catch (Exception error) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
    }
    private void NavVideo_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowFilePage(); Model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); }
    private void NavAudio_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowFilePage(); Model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); }
    private void NavImage_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowFilePage(); Model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); }
    private void NavExtract_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowFilePage(); Model.SetMode(ConversionMode.Extract); }
    private void NavCompression_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowFilePage(); Model.SetCompressionPage(); }
    private void NavGif_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { ShowFilePage(); Model.SetGifPage(); }
    private void GifQuality_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (sender is RadioButton { Tag: string quality } && Enum.TryParse<GifQualityLevel>(quality, out var value)) Model.SelectedGifQuality = value;
    }
    private void StandardCompression_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => Model.SelectedCompressionMode = CompressionMode.Standard;
    private void TargetSizeCompression_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => Model.SelectedCompressionMode = CompressionMode.TargetSize;
    private void CompressionLevel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (sender is RadioButton { Tag: string level } && Enum.TryParse<CompressionLevel>(level, out var value)) Model.SelectedCompressionLevel = value;
    }
    private void Remove_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (Model.IsImageObfuscationPage) Model.RemoveImageObfuscationJobs(ImageObfuscationQueueGrid.SelectedItems.Cast<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>());
        else if (Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage) Model.RemoveRecoveryJobs(RecoveryQueueGrid.SelectedItems.Cast<RecoveryJobViewModel>());
        else if (Model.IsArchivePage) Model.RemoveArchiveJobs(ArchiveQueueGrid.SelectedItems.Cast<ArchiveJobViewModel>());
        else Model.RemoveJobs(QueueGrid.SelectedItems.Cast<ConversionJobViewModel>());
    }
    private void Queue_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e) => Model.SelectedJobs = QueueGrid.SelectedItems.Cast<ConversionJobViewModel>().ToArray();
    private void JobStatus_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (sender is Button { Tag: ConversionJobViewModel row } && row.Error is not null) ShowError(row.Error);
    }
    private void Error_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) { if (Model.LastError is not null) ShowError(Model.LastError); }

    private bool AcceptsDrag(DragEventArgs e)
    {
        if (!Model.CanEdit || !e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop)) return false;
        if (e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) is string[] imagePaths && Model.IsImageObfuscationPage)
            return imagePaths.Any(path => File.Exists(path) && MainViewModel.IsImageObfuscationSource(path));
        if (e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) is string[] recoveryPaths && Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage)
            return recoveryPaths.Any(path => File.Exists(path) && MainViewModel.IsRecoverySource(path));
        if (e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) is string[] archivePaths && Model.IsArchivePage)
            return archivePaths.Any(path => Model.IsArchiveCreatePage ? File.Exists(path) || Directory.Exists(path) : File.Exists(path) && ArchiveImportService.IsSupportedArchive(path));
        return e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) is string[] paths && paths.Any(path => File.Exists(path) && (Model.IsCompressionPage ? ImportService.Classify(path) is not null : ImportService.ExtensionsFor(Model.Mode).Contains(Path.GetExtension(path), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));
    }
    private void Window_DragOver(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
    {
        if (!e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop)) { e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None; e.Handled = true; return; }
        var paths = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) as string[] ?? Array.Empty<string>();
        var accepts = IsPetPageOpen || (shellPage == "home" ? Model.CanEdit && paths.Any(p => File.Exists(p) || Directory.Exists(p)) : shellPage == "files" && AcceptsDrag(e));
        e.Effects = accepts ? DragDropEffects.Copy : DragDropEffects.None;
        if (shellPage == "files") Model.SetDragging(accepts);
        DragOverlayText.Text = accepts ? shellPage == "home" ? "松开以接住文件，再选择处理用途" : "松开以添加文件 · 不适合的项目会说明原因" : !Model.CanEdit ? "当前正在处理文件，请完成后再添加" : "这些文件不适合当前页面，请从首页选择用途";
        DragOverlay.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
        e.Handled = true;
    }
    private void Window_DragLeave(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
    {
        var point = e.GetPosition(this);
        if (point.X <= 0 || point.Y <= 0 || point.X >= ActualWidth || point.Y >= ActualHeight)
        { Model.SetDragging(false); DragOverlay.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; }
    }
    private async void Window_Drop(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
    {
        DragOverlay.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
        Model.SetDragging(false);
        if (!e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop)) return;
        e.Handled = true;
        if (e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) is not string[] paths) return;
        if (IsPetPageOpen && petController is not null) { petController.ReceiveFiles(paths); return; }
        if (!Model.CanEdit) return;
        if (shellPage == "home") { StageHomeFiles(paths); return; }
        if (shellPage != "files") return;
        try { await Model.AddFilesAsync(paths); } catch (Exception error) { if (Model.IsPasswordRecoveryPage) Model.HandleRecoveryUnexpectedError(); else Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
    }

    private async void Window_Closing(object? sender, CancelEventArgs e)
    {
        if (!exitApproved && HideToBackground?.Invoke() == true) { e.Cancel = true; return; }
        if (exitApproved || (!Model.IsProcessing && !Model.IsImporting)) { if (BeforeExit?.Invoke() == false) e.Cancel = true; return; }
        e.Cancel = true;
        if (closingAfterStop) return;
        var stop = Model.IsImporting || (confirmStopAndExit?.Invoke() ?? ConfirmStopAndExit());
        if (!stop) return;
        closingAfterStop = true;
        IsEnabled = false;
        try
        {
            if (Model.IsImporting) { Model.CancelImport(); await Model.ActiveImport; }
            if (Model.IsProcessing) { Model.Cancel(); await Model.ActiveRun; }
            exitApproved = true;
            Close();
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); IsEnabled = true; closingAfterStop = false; }
    }
    private bool ConfirmStopAndExit()
    {
        var dialog = CreateDialog("还有文件正在处理", 410, 220);
        var body = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(25) };
        body.Children.Add(Text("猫咪还在处理文件。", 17, FontWeights.SemiBold));
        body.Children.Add(Text("继续处理，或停止当前任务后退出。原文件会保留，未完成的临时文件会清理。", 12, margin: new Thickness(0, 13, 0, 22)));
        var buttons = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right };
        var stop = MakeButton("停止并退出", false); stop.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "DangerButton"); stop.Click += (_, _) => dialog.DialogResult = true;
        var stay = MakeButton("继续处理", true); stay.IsDefault = true; stay.IsCancel = true; stay.Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0); stay.Click += (_, _) => dialog.DialogResult = false;
        buttons.Children.Add(stop); buttons.Children.Add(stay); body.Children.Add(buttons); dialog.Content = body;
        return dialog.ShowDialog() == true;
    }

    private Window CreateDialog(string title, double width, double height)
    {
        var dialog = new Window { Title = title, Width = width, Height = height, Owner = this, WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner, ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize, FontFamily = FontFamily, Foreground = Foreground, Icon = Icon };
        ThemedWindowFrame.Attach(dialog);
        dialog.SetResourceReference(FontFamilyProperty, "UiFont");
        dialog.SetResourceReference(ForegroundProperty, "Text");
        dialog.UseLayoutRounding = true;
        dialog.Closed += (_, _) => { modalCount = Math.Max(0, modalCount - 1); if (modalCount == 0) ModalScrim.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; };
        dialog.SetResourceReference(BackgroundProperty, "Surface");
        dialog.Loaded += (_, _) => { modalCount++; ModalScrim.Visibility = Visibility.Visible; if (dialog.Content is FrameworkElement content) UiMotion.Reveal(content, 4, dialogDuration); };
        return dialog;
    }
    private TextBlock Text(string content, double size = 14, FontWeight? weight = null, Thickness? margin = null) => new() { Text = content, FontSize = size <= 12 ? 12 : size < 17 ? 14 : size < 22 ? 18 : 24, FontWeight = weight ?? FontWeights.Normal, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap, LineHeight = size >= 22 ? 32 : size >= 17 ? 26 : size <= 12 ? 18 : 22, Margin = margin ?? new Thickness(0) };
    private Button MakeButton(string content, bool primary = false) => new() { Content = content, Style = (Style)FindResource(primary ? "PrimaryButton" : "SoftButton"), HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center };
    private void ShowError(ConversionError error)
    {
        var dialog = CreateDialog("看看哪里出了问题", 550, 470);
        dialog.ResizeMode = ResizeMode.CanResize;
        dialog.MinHeight = 350; dialog.MinWidth = 430;
        var body = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(25) };
        body.Children.Add(Text("这个文件好像遇到了一点问题。", 17, FontWeights.SemiBold));
        body.Children.Add(Text(error.Message, margin: new Thickness(0, 14, 0, 0)));
        body.Children.Add(Text(error.Suggestion, margin: new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 18)));
        var details = $"错误代码：{error.Code}\n诊断记录：{error.DiagnosticId ?? "无"}\n\n{error.TechnicalDetails ?? "没有额外技术详情。"}";
        var expander = new Expander { Header = "高级详情", FontSize = 12, IsExpanded = false };
        var advanced = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 0) };
        advanced.Children.Add(ReadOnlyDetails(details, 150, true));
        var copy = MakeButton("复制诊断信息"); copy.Margin = new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 0); copy.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
        copy.Click += (_, _) => { try { Clipboard.SetText(error.Message + "\n" + error.Suggestion + "\n\n" + details); copy.Content = "已复制"; } catch { copy.Content = "复制失败，请重试"; } };
        advanced.Children.Add(copy); expander.Content = advanced; body.Children.Add(expander);
        var close = MakeButton("知道了", true); close.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right; close.Margin = new Thickness(0, 22, 0, 0); close.IsDefault = true; close.IsCancel = true; close.Click += (_, _) => dialog.Close(); body.Children.Add(close);
        dialog.Content = new ScrollViewer { Content = body, VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto }; dialog.ShowDialog();
    }
    private void Settings_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => ShowSettingsPage();
    private FrameworkElement BuildSettingsContent()
    {
        var dialog = this;
        var body = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(25) };
        body.Children.Add(Text("设置", 24, FontWeights.SemiBold));
        body.Children.Add(Text("界面语言 / Language", 13, FontWeights.SemiBold, new Thickness(0, 20, 0, 8)));
        var language = new ComboBox { MinWidth = 240, MaxWidth = 320, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
        language.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = "简体中文", Tag = "zh-CN" });
        language.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = "English", Tag = "en-US" });
        LocalizedUi.SetPreserveText(language, true);
        language.SelectedIndex = Model.Language == "en-US" ? 1 : 0;
        language.SelectionChanged += (_, _) => { if (language.SelectedItem is ComboBoxItem { Tag: string code }) Model.Language = code; };
        body.Children.Add(language);
        body.Children.Add(Text("语言切换立即生效，正在运行的任务不会中断。", 12, margin: new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 12)));
        AddAppearanceSettings(body);
        body.Children.Add(Text("本地处理引擎", 18, FontWeights.SemiBold, new Thickness(0, 24, 0, 0)));
        body.Children.Add(Text(Model.EngineStatus, margin: new Thickness(0, 13, 0, 0)));
        body.Children.Add(Text("软件已经包含转换引擎，通常无需修改。", margin: new Thickness(0, 7, 0, 18)));
        var advanced = new Expander { Header = "高级：转换引擎位置", FontSize = 12, IsEnabled = Model.CanEdit };
        var enginePanel = new StackPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 0) };
        var path = new TextBox { Text = Model.EngineDirectory, Padding = new Thickness(8), FontSize = 12 };
        enginePanel.Children.Add(path);
        var buttons = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Margin = new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 0) };
        var choose = MakeButton("选择文件夹"); choose.Click += (_, _) => { var folder = new OpenFolderDialog { Title = CatFileAssistant.Core.Locale.T("选择包含 ffmpeg.exe 和 ffprobe.exe 的文件夹") }; if (folder.ShowDialog(dialog) == true) path.Text = folder.FolderName; };
        var restore = MakeButton("恢复内置引擎"); restore.Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0); restore.Click += (_, _) => path.Text = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "tools", "ffmpeg", "bin");
        buttons.Children.Add(choose); buttons.Children.Add(restore); enginePanel.Children.Add(buttons); advanced.Content = enginePanel; body.Children.Add(advanced);
        var archiveSettings = CreateArchiveSettingsEditor(dialog);
        body.Children.Add(archiveSettings.Panel);
        body.Children.Add(Text("动画偏好", 13, FontWeights.SemiBold, new Thickness(0, 22, 0, 8)));
        var motionChoice = new ComboBox { FontSize = 12, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, MinWidth = 240, MaxWidth = 320, HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
        motionChoice.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = "跟随系统", Tag = MotionPreference.System });
        motionChoice.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem { Content = "减少动效", Tag = MotionPreference.Reduced });
        motionChoice.SelectedIndex = Model.MotionPreference == MotionPreference.Reduced ? 1 : 0;
        motionChoice.SelectionChanged += (_, _) => { if (motionChoice.SelectedItem is ComboBoxItem { Tag: MotionPreference preference }) Model.MotionPreference = preference; };
        body.Children.Add(motionChoice);
        body.Children.Add(Text("选择减少动效后，猫咪和界面会立即切换为静态反馈。", 11, margin: new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0)));
        body.Children.Add(Text("格式、图片混淆模式和次数、压缩方案、GIF 档位、打包设置与保存位置会自动记住；密码不会保存。", 11, margin: new Thickness(0, 20, 0, 0)));
        var save = MakeButton("完成", true); save.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right; save.Margin = new Thickness(0, 20, 0, 0); save.IsDefault = true;
        save.Click += (_, _) =>
        {
            if (!Model.CanEdit) { settingsFeedback.Text = "当前任务完成后可修改引擎与解压保护。"; return; }
            if (!File.Exists(Path.Combine(path.Text, "ffmpeg.exe")) || !File.Exists(Path.Combine(path.Text, "ffprobe.exe")))
            { ShowInformation("引擎文件不完整", "请选择包含 ffmpeg.exe 和 ffprobe.exe 的引擎文件夹。"); return; }
            var bundled = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "tools", "ffmpeg", "bin");
            if (!archiveSettings.Apply()) return;
            Model.SetEngineDirectory(string.Equals(Path.GetFullPath(path.Text), bundled, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? null : path.Text);
            settingsFeedback.Text = "设置已保存。";
        };
        save.Content = "保存引擎与保护设置"; save.SetBinding(IsEnabledProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("CanEdit"));
        advanced.SetBinding(IsEnabledProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("CanEdit"));
        body.Children.Add(save); body.Children.Add(settingsFeedback);
        return ReadingPage(body);
    }

        var licensePath = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "tools", "ffmpeg", "licenses", "COPYING.GPLv3");
        var noticeText = "FFmpeg / ffprobe 随附引擎按 GPL-3.0-or-later 提供；完整许可、组件来源和对应源码取得方式见 THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md 与 tools/ffmpeg/source/README.md。7-Zip 对应源码随包附带。";
        try { if (File.Exists(noticePath)) noticeText = File.ReadAllText(noticePath); if (File.Exists(licensePath)) noticeText += "\n\nFFmpeg / ffprobe 随附引擎的 GPL 许可全文：\n" + File.ReadAllText(licensePath); } catch (IOException) { } catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { }
        notices.Content = ReadOnlyDetails(noticeText, 220);
        body.Children.Add(notices);
        return ReadingPage(body);
    }

    private TextBlock ContactLink(string label, string caption, string uri)
    {
        var text = Text("", 12, margin: new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0));
        text.Inlines.Add(new Run(label));
        var link = new Hyperlink(new Run(caption)) { NavigateUri = new Uri(uri) };
        link.RequestNavigate += (_, e) =>
        {
            try { Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(e.Uri.AbsoluteUri) { UseShellExecute = true }); }
            catch (Exception error) when (error is System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or InvalidOperationException) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
            e.Handled = true;
        };
        text.Inlines.Add(link);
        LocalizedUi.SetPreserveText(text, true);
        // The label alone is translated; the destination and visible address are preserved.
        var prefix = (Run)text.Inlines.FirstInline;
        prefix.Text = Locale.T(label);
        Locale.Changed += UpdateLabel;
        text.Unloaded += (_, _) => Locale.Changed -= UpdateLabel;
        void UpdateLabel(object? sender, EventArgs e) => prefix.Text = Locale.T(label);
        return text;
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Motion/MotionPreferences.cs ---
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;

/// <summary>One application-wide motion policy, updated when Windows preferences change.</summary>
public static class MotionPreferences
{
    private static MotionPreference preference = MotionPreference.System;
    private static bool? testAllowsMotion;
    private static volatile bool reduceMotion = true;
    private static volatile bool systemAllowsMotion;
    public static bool ReduceMotion => reduceMotion;
    /// <summary>Windows and rendering capability only; independent of the application's page animation preference.</summary>
    public static bool SystemAllowsMotion => systemAllowsMotion;
    /// <summary>The pet's explicit Gentle mode keeps its capped animation independent of page and Windows effects.</summary>
    public static bool ShouldReducePetMotion(PetMotionMode mode) => mode switch
    {
        PetMotionMode.Gentle => false,
        PetMotionMode.System => !systemAllowsMotion,
        _ => true
    };
    public static event EventHandler? Changed;

    static MotionPreferences()
    {
        try
        {
            SystemParameters.StaticPropertyChanged += SystemPreferenceChanged;
            RenderCapability.TierChanged += RenderingTierChanged;
            systemAllowsMotion = EvaluateSystemMotion();
            reduceMotion = preference == MotionPreference.Reduced || !systemAllowsMotion;
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Motion policy initialization: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    public static void SetPreference(MotionPreference value)
    {
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(value)) value = MotionPreference.System;
        var next = value;
        OnUiThread(() => { preference = next; Refresh(); });
    }

    /// <summary>Exercises both presentation policies without changing Windows settings.</summary>
    internal static IDisposable OverrideSystemMotionForTesting(bool allow)
    {
        var dispatcher = Application.Current?.Dispatcher;
        if (dispatcher is not null && !dispatcher.CheckAccess())
            return dispatcher.Invoke(() => OverrideSystemMotionForTesting(allow));
        var scope = new SystemMotionOverride(testAllowsMotion);
        testAllowsMotion = allow;
        Refresh();
        return scope;
    }

    private static void SystemPreferenceChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(args.PropertyName) || args.PropertyName == nameof(SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation))
            OnUiThread(Refresh);
    }

    private static void RenderingTierChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => OnUiThread(Refresh);

    private static bool EvaluateSystemMotion()
    {
        try
        {
            return testAllowsMotion ?? (SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation && RenderCapability.Tier > 0);
        }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
            Trace.TraceWarning("Motion policy evaluation: {0}", error.Message);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static void Refresh()
    {
        var nextSystem = EvaluateSystemMotion();
        var next = preference == MotionPreference.Reduced || !nextSystem;
        if (next == reduceMotion && nextSystem == systemAllowsMotion) return;
        systemAllowsMotion = nextSystem;
        reduceMotion = next;
        if (Changed is not { } handlers) return;
        foreach (EventHandler handler in handlers.GetInvocationList())
        {
            try { handler(null, EventArgs.Empty); }
            catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Motion policy observer: {0}", error.Message); }
        }
    }

    private static void OnUiThread(Action action)
    {
        try
        {
            var dispatcher = Application.Current?.Dispatcher;
            if (dispatcher is null || dispatcher.CheckAccess()) action();
            else if (!dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) dispatcher.BeginInvoke(action);
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Motion policy dispatch: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private sealed class SystemMotionOverride(bool? previous) : IDisposable
    {
        private int disposed;
        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (System.Threading.Interlocked.Exchange(ref disposed, 1) != 0) return;
            void Restore() { testAllowsMotion = previous; Refresh(); }
            var dispatcher = Application.Current?.Dispatcher;
            if (dispatcher is null || dispatcher.CheckAccess()) Restore();
            else if (!dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) dispatcher.Invoke(Restore);
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Motion/UiMotion.Controls.cs ---
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;

public enum ButtonTone { Standard, Primary, Danger }

public static partial class UiMotion
{
    public static readonly DependencyProperty ToneProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "Tone", typeof(ButtonTone), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(ButtonTone.Standard));
    public static ButtonTone GetTone(DependencyObject element) => (ButtonTone)element.GetValue(ToneProperty);
    public static void SetTone(DependencyObject element, ButtonTone tone) => element.SetValue(ToneProperty, tone);
    public static readonly DependencyProperty PopupMotionEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "PopupMotionEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(false, PopupMotionChanged));
    private static readonly DependencyProperty PopupStateProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "PopupState", typeof(PopupMotionState), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(null));
    public static bool GetPopupMotionEnabled(DependencyObject element) => (bool)element.GetValue(PopupMotionEnabledProperty);
    public static void SetPopupMotionEnabled(DependencyObject element, bool value) => element.SetValue(PopupMotionEnabledProperty, value);

    public static readonly DependencyProperty SwitchMotionEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "SwitchMotionEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(false, SwitchMotionChanged));
    private static readonly DependencyProperty SwitchStateProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "SwitchState", typeof(SwitchMotionState), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(null));
    public static bool GetSwitchMotionEnabled(DependencyObject element) => (bool)element.GetValue(SwitchMotionEnabledProperty);
    public static void SetSwitchMotionEnabled(DependencyObject element, bool value) => element.SetValue(SwitchMotionEnabledProperty, value);

    public static readonly DependencyProperty ProgressPolicyEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "ProgressPolicyEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(false, ProgressPolicyChanged));
    private static readonly DependencyProperty ProgressPolicyStateProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "ProgressPolicyState", typeof(ProgressPolicyState), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(null));
    private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey MotionAllowedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttachedReadOnly(
        "MotionAllowed", typeof(bool), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(false));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty MotionAllowedProperty = MotionAllowedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
    public static bool GetProgressPolicyEnabled(DependencyObject element) => (bool)element.GetValue(ProgressPolicyEnabledProperty);
    public static void SetProgressPolicyEnabled(DependencyObject element, bool value) => element.SetValue(ProgressPolicyEnabledProperty, value);
    public static bool GetMotionAllowed(DependencyObject element) => (bool)element.GetValue(MotionAllowedProperty);

    private static void PopupMotionChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
    {
        if (target is not ComboBox combo) return;
        if ((bool)args.NewValue && combo.GetValue(PopupStateProperty) is null)
            combo.SetValue(PopupStateProperty, new PopupMotionState(combo));
        else if (!(bool)args.NewValue && combo.GetValue(PopupStateProperty) is PopupMotionState state)
        { state.Dispose(); combo.ClearValue(PopupStateProperty); }
    });
    private static void SwitchMotionChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
    {
        if (target is not CheckBox toggle) return;
        if ((bool)args.NewValue && toggle.GetValue(SwitchStateProperty) is null)
            toggle.SetValue(SwitchStateProperty, new SwitchMotionState(toggle));
        else if (!(bool)args.NewValue && toggle.GetValue(SwitchStateProperty) is SwitchMotionState state)
        { state.Dispose(); toggle.ClearValue(SwitchStateProperty); }
    });
    private static void ProgressPolicyChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
    {
        if (target is not ProgressBar bar) return;
        if ((bool)args.NewValue && bar.GetValue(ProgressPolicyStateProperty) is null)
            bar.SetValue(ProgressPolicyStateProperty, new ProgressPolicyState(bar));
        else if (!(bool)args.NewValue && bar.GetValue(ProgressPolicyStateProperty) is ProgressPolicyState state)
        { state.Dispose(); bar.ClearValue(ProgressPolicyStateProperty); }
    });

    private sealed class PopupMotionState : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly ComboBox combo;
        private FrameworkElement? content;
        public PopupMotionState(ComboBox combo)
        {
