            this.combo = combo;
            combo.DropDownOpened += Opened;
            combo.DropDownClosed += Closed;
            combo.Unloaded += Unloaded;
        }
        private void Opened(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
        {
            if (combo.Template?.FindName("PART_Popup", combo) is not Popup { Child: FrameworkElement child }) return;
            content = child;
            child.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() =>
            {
                if (!combo.IsDropDownOpen || !ReferenceEquals(content, child)) return;
                var duration = combo.TryFindResource("MotionMenuDurationMs") is int value ? value : 180;
                Reveal(child, 3, duration);
            }), System.Windows.Threading.DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
        });
        private void Closed(object? sender, EventArgs args) => StopPopup();
        private void Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => StopPopup();
        private void StopPopup() { if (content is not null) Stop(content); content = null; }
        public void Dispose()
        {
            StopPopup(); combo.DropDownOpened -= Opened; combo.DropDownClosed -= Closed; combo.Unloaded -= Unloaded;
        }
    }

    private sealed class SwitchMotionState : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly CheckBox toggle;
        private bool attached;
        private bool previousChecked;
        private TranslateTransform? transform;
        public SwitchMotionState(CheckBox toggle)
        {
            this.toggle = toggle;
            previousChecked = toggle.IsChecked == true;
            toggle.Loaded += Loaded; toggle.Unloaded += Unloaded; toggle.IsVisibleChanged += VisibilityChanged;
            if (toggle.IsLoaded) Attach();
        }
        private TranslateTransform? FindTransform() => toggle.Template?.FindName("SwitchOffset", toggle) as TranslateTransform;
        private void Attach()
        {
            if (attached || !toggle.IsLoaded || !toggle.IsVisible) return;
            attached = true; previousChecked = toggle.IsChecked == true;
            toggle.Checked += Toggled; toggle.Unchecked += Toggled;
            MotionPreferences.Changed += PreferenceChanged;
        }
        private void Toggled(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
        {
            transform = FindTransform();
            var next = toggle.IsChecked == true;
            if (transform is not null)
            {
                var from = transform.HasAnimatedProperties ? transform.X : previousChecked ? 18d : 0d;
                transform.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.XProperty, null);
                if (!MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion && toggle.IsLoaded && toggle.IsVisible)
                {
                    var animation = Animation(from, next ? 18 : 0, 140);
                    transform.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.XProperty, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
                }
            }
            previousChecked = next;
        });
        private void StopSwitch()
        {
            transform ??= FindTransform();
            transform?.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.XProperty, null);
            previousChecked = toggle.IsChecked == true;
        }
        private void PreferenceChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(StopSwitch);
        private void Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Attach();
        private void Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) { Detach(); StopSwitch(); }
        private void VisibilityChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
        { if ((bool)args.NewValue) Attach(); else { Detach(); StopSwitch(); } }
        private void Detach()
        {
            if (!attached) return; attached = false;
            toggle.Checked -= Toggled; toggle.Unchecked -= Toggled; MotionPreferences.Changed -= PreferenceChanged;
        }
        public void Dispose()
        {
            Detach(); StopSwitch(); toggle.Loaded -= Loaded; toggle.Unloaded -= Unloaded; toggle.IsVisibleChanged -= VisibilityChanged;
        }
    }

    private sealed class ProgressPolicyState : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly ProgressBar bar;
        private bool attached;
        private Window? window;
        public ProgressPolicyState(ProgressBar bar)
        {
            this.bar = bar;
            bar.Loaded += Loaded; bar.Unloaded += Unloaded; bar.IsVisibleChanged += VisibilityChanged;
            if (bar.IsLoaded) Attach();
        }
        private void Attach()
        {
            if (attached || !bar.IsLoaded || !bar.IsVisible) return;
            attached = true;
            MotionPreferences.Changed += Changed;
            window = Window.GetWindow(bar);
            if (window is not null) window.StateChanged += Changed;
            Refresh();
        }
        private void Refresh() => bar.SetValue(MotionAllowedPropertyKey,
            attached && bar.IsVisible && !MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion && window?.WindowState != WindowState.Minimized);
        private void Changed(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(Refresh);
        private void Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Attach();
        private void Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Detach();
        private void VisibilityChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
        { if ((bool)args.NewValue) Attach(); else Detach(); }
        private void Detach()
        {
            if (attached)
            {
                attached = false; MotionPreferences.Changed -= Changed;
                if (window is not null) window.StateChanged -= Changed;
                window = null;
            }
            bar.SetValue(MotionAllowedPropertyKey, false);
        }
        public void Dispose()
        {
            Detach(); bar.Loaded -= Loaded; bar.Unloaded -= Unloaded; bar.IsVisibleChanged -= VisibilityChanged;
        }
    }
}



--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Motion/UiMotion.cs ---
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;

/// <summary>Finite presentation animations. No timers, task results, or file operations.</summary>
public static partial class UiMotion
{
    public static readonly DependencyProperty EnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "Enabled", typeof(bool), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(false, EnabledChanged));
    public static readonly DependencyProperty SmoothProgressProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "SmoothProgress", typeof(double), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(0d, ProgressChanged));
    private static readonly DependencyProperty StateProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached(
        "State", typeof(MotionState), typeof(UiMotion), new PropertyMetadata(null));
    private static readonly DependencyPropertyDescriptor? PressedDescriptor =
        DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(ButtonBase.IsPressedProperty, typeof(ButtonBase));
    private static int hoverDuration = 140;
    private static int pressDuration = 100;
    private static int releaseDuration = 160;
    private static int progressDuration = 150;

    public static bool GetEnabled(DependencyObject element) => (bool)element.GetValue(EnabledProperty);
    public static void SetEnabled(DependencyObject element, bool value) => element.SetValue(EnabledProperty, value);
    public static double GetSmoothProgress(DependencyObject element) => (double)element.GetValue(SmoothProgressProperty);
    public static void SetSmoothProgress(DependencyObject element, double value) => element.SetValue(SmoothProgressProperty, value);

    public static void ConfigureDurations(int hoverMs, int pressMs, int releaseMs, int progressMs = 150)
    {
        hoverDuration = DurationMs(hoverMs);
        pressDuration = DurationMs(pressMs);
        releaseDuration = DurationMs(releaseMs);
        progressDuration = DurationMs(progressMs);
    }

    public static void Reveal(FrameworkElement element, double offset = 4, int durationMs = 180) => OnUiThread(element, () =>
    {
        if (!element.IsLoaded || !element.IsVisible) return;
        StateFor(element).Reveal(double.IsFinite(offset) ? Math.Clamp(offset, -12, 12) : 0, DurationMs(durationMs));
    });

    /// <summary>Stops only animations owned by this helper, restoring the current underlying values.</summary>
    public static void Stop(FrameworkElement element) => OnUiThread(element, () =>
    {
        if (element.GetValue(StateProperty) is MotionState state) state.Stop();
    });

    private static void EnabledChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
    {
        if (target is not ButtonBase button) return;
        if ((bool)args.NewValue) StateFor(button).EnableButton();
        else if (button.GetValue(StateProperty) is MotionState state) state.DisableButton();
    });

    private static void ProgressChanged(DependencyObject target, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
    {
        if (target is ProgressBar bar) StateFor(bar).SetProgress((double)args.NewValue);
    });

    private static MotionState StateFor(FrameworkElement element)
    {
        if (element.GetValue(StateProperty) is MotionState existing) return existing;
        var state = new MotionState(element);
        element.SetValue(StateProperty, state);
        return state;
    }

    private static int DurationMs(int value) => Math.Clamp(value, 1, 1000);

    private static DoubleAnimation Animation(double from, double to, int duration)
    {
        var animation = new DoubleAnimation(from, to, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(duration))
        { EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop };
        return animation;
    }

    private static void OnUiThread(FrameworkElement element, Action action) => Safe(() =>
    {
        if (element.Dispatcher.CheckAccess()) action();
        else if (!element.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) element.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() => Safe(action)));
    });

    private static void Safe(Action action)
    {
        try { action(); }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("UI motion fallback: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private sealed class MotionState
    {
        private readonly FrameworkElement element;
        private bool buttonEnabled;
        private bool progressEnabled;
        private bool subscribed;
        private bool ownsOpacity;
        private bool ownsProgress;
        private long visualVersion;
        private long progressVersion;
        private double targetProgress;
        private Window? window;
        private Transform? originalTransform;
        private TransformGroup? ownedTransform;
        private ScaleTransform? scale;
        private TranslateTransform? translation;
        private Border? buttonSurface;
        private Brush? originalSurfaceBackground;
        private SolidColorBrush? ownedBackground;
        private long colorVersion;

        public MotionState(FrameworkElement element)
        {
            this.element = element;
            element.Loaded += Loaded;
            element.Unloaded += Unloaded;
            element.IsVisibleChanged += VisibilityChanged;
        }

        private bool CanAnimate => element.IsLoaded && element.IsVisible && element.IsEnabled &&
            window?.WindowState != WindowState.Minimized && !element.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted;

        public void EnableButton()
        {
            if (buttonEnabled) return;
            DetachActive();
            buttonEnabled = true;
            AttachActive();
        }

        public void DisableButton()
        {
            Safe(DetachActive);
            buttonEnabled = false;
            Safe(StopVisuals);
            if (progressEnabled) AttachActive();
            else Dispose();
        }

        public void Reveal(double offset, int duration)
        {
            AttachActive();
            var interruptedOpacity = ownsOpacity ? element.Opacity : (double?)null;
            var interruptedOffset = ownedTransform is not null && ReferenceEquals(element.RenderTransform, ownedTransform)
                ? translation?.Y : null;
            StopVisuals();
            if (!CanAnimate) { ReleaseTransient(); return; }
            if (MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion) { ReleaseTransient(); return; }
            var version = ++visualVersion;
            var baseOpacity = (double)element.GetAnimationBaseValue(UIElement.OpacityProperty);
            if (!MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion)
            {
                EnsureTransform();
                AnimateTransform(translation!, TranslateTransform.YProperty, interruptedOffset ?? offset, 0, duration, version);
            }
            AnimateOpacity(MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion ? baseOpacity * 0.94 : interruptedOpacity ?? 0, baseOpacity,
                MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion ? Math.Min(90, duration) : duration, version);
        }

        public void SetProgress(double value)
        {
            if (element is not ProgressBar bar) return;
            progressEnabled = true;
            targetProgress = double.IsFinite(value) ? Math.Clamp(value, bar.Minimum, bar.Maximum) : bar.Minimum;
            AttachActive();
            var from = bar.Value;
            var version = ++progressVersion;
            bar.BeginAnimation(RangeBase.ValueProperty, null);
            ownsProgress = false;
            // SmoothProgress owns Value; commit its base value before applying a transient clock.
            bar.SetValue(RangeBase.ValueProperty, targetProgress);
            // Reset/retry snaps down immediately; only confirmed increases are interpolated.
            if (!CanAnimate || MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion || targetProgress <= from) return;
            var animation = Animation(from, targetProgress, progressDuration);
            animation.Completed += (_, _) => Safe(() =>
            {
                if (version != progressVersion) return;
                bar.BeginAnimation(RangeBase.ValueProperty, null);
                bar.SetValue(RangeBase.ValueProperty, targetProgress);
                ownsProgress = false;
            });
            ownsProgress = true;
            bar.BeginAnimation(RangeBase.ValueProperty, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
        }

        public void Stop()
        {
            Safe(StopVisuals);
            Safe(SyncProgress);
            ReleaseTransient();
        }

        private void EnsureTransform()
        {
            if (ownedTransform is not null && ReferenceEquals(element.RenderTransform, ownedTransform)) return;
            originalTransform = element.RenderTransform;
            scale = new ScaleTransform(1, 1,
                element.ActualWidth * (0.5 - element.RenderTransformOrigin.X),
                element.ActualHeight * (0.5 - element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y));
            translation = new TranslateTransform();
            ownedTransform = new TransformGroup();
            if (originalTransform is not null) ownedTransform.Children.Add(originalTransform.CloneCurrentValue());
            ownedTransform.Children.Add(scale);
            ownedTransform.Children.Add(translation);
            element.SetCurrentValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty, ownedTransform);
        }

        private void UpdateButton() => Safe(() =>
        {
            if (!buttonEnabled || element is not ButtonBase button || !CanAnimate) { StopVisuals(); return; }
            var pressed = button.IsPressed;
            var duration = pressed ? pressDuration : button.IsMouseOver ? hoverDuration : releaseDuration;
            if (ownsOpacity) element.BeginAnimation(UIElement.OpacityProperty, null);
            ownsOpacity = false;
            var version = ++visualVersion;
            if (MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion)
            {
                StopVisuals();
                UpdateButtonColor(duration, false);
                return;
            }
            UpdateButtonColor(duration, true);
            EnsureTransform();
            AnimateTransform(scale!, ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, scale!.ScaleX, pressed ? 0.98 : 1, duration, version);
            AnimateTransform(scale!, ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, scale!.ScaleY, pressed ? 0.98 : 1, duration, version);
            AnimateTransform(translation!, TranslateTransform.YProperty, translation!.Y,
                pressed ? 0.5 : button.IsMouseOver ? -1 : 0, duration, version);
        });

        private void UpdateButtonColor(int duration, bool animate) => Safe(() =>
        {
            // Navigation and the drop area manage their own differently named surfaces.
            if (!buttonEnabled || element is not Button button ||
                button.Template?.FindName("Surface", button) is not Border surface ||
                button.Background is not SolidColorBrush source) return;
            if (!ReferenceEquals(buttonSurface, surface) || ownedBackground is null ||
                !ReferenceEquals(surface.Background, ownedBackground))
            {
                StopButtonColor();
                buttonSurface = surface;
                originalSurfaceBackground = surface.Background;
                ownedBackground = source.CloneCurrentValue();
                surface.SetCurrentValue(Border.BackgroundProperty, ownedBackground);
            }
            var tone = GetTone(button);
            var target = source.Color;
            if (button.IsEnabled && (button.IsMouseOver || button.IsPressed))
            {
                var key = tone == ButtonTone.Primary ? (button.IsPressed ? "AccentPressed" : "AccentHover") :
                    tone == ButtonTone.Danger ? (button.IsPressed ? "DangerPressed" : "DangerHover") :
                    button.IsPressed ? "PressedSurface" : "HoverSurface";
                if (button.TryFindResource(key) is SolidColorBrush stateBrush) target = stateBrush.Color;
            }
            var brush = ownedBackground!;
            var current = brush.Color;
            var version = ++colorVersion;
            brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, null);
            brush.Color = target;
            if (!animate || !CanAnimate || MotionPreferences.ReduceMotion || current == target) return;
            var animation = new ColorAnimation(current, target, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(duration))
            { EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop };
            animation.Completed += (_, _) => Safe(() =>
            {
                if (version == colorVersion && ReferenceEquals(ownedBackground, brush))
                    brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, null);
            });
            brush.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
        });

        private void StopButtonColor()
        {
            colorVersion++;
            try
            {
                if (ownedBackground is not null) Safe(() => ownedBackground.BeginAnimation(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, null));
                if (buttonSurface is not null && ReferenceEquals(buttonSurface.Background, ownedBackground))
                    // A theme may have changed while the transient brush was in use.
                    // Restore the control's current resource value instead of its old palette snapshot.
                    Safe(() => buttonSurface.SetCurrentValue(Border.BackgroundProperty,
                        element is Button button ? button.Background : originalSurfaceBackground));
            }
            finally { buttonSurface = null; originalSurfaceBackground = null; ownedBackground = null; }
        }

        private void AnimateTransform(Animatable target, DependencyProperty property, double from, double to, int duration, long version)
        {
            target.SetValue(property, to);
            var animation = Animation(from, to, duration);
            animation.Completed += (_, _) => Safe(() =>
            {
                if (version != visualVersion) return;
                target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
                target.SetValue(property, to);
            });
            target.BeginAnimation(property, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
        }

        private void AnimateOpacity(double from, double to, int duration, long version)
        {
            var animation = Animation(from, to, duration);
            animation.Completed += (_, _) => Safe(() =>
            {
                if (version != visualVersion) return;
                element.BeginAnimation(UIElement.OpacityProperty, null);
                ownsOpacity = false;
                ReleaseTransient();
            });
            ownsOpacity = true;
            element.BeginAnimation(UIElement.OpacityProperty, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
        }

        private void StopVisuals()
        {
            visualVersion++;
            if (ownsOpacity) Safe(() => element.BeginAnimation(UIElement.OpacityProperty, null));
            ownsOpacity = false;
            if (scale is not null)
            {
                Safe(() => scale.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, null));
                Safe(() => scale.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, null));
            }
            if (translation is not null) Safe(() => translation.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.YProperty, null));
            if (ownedTransform is not null && ReferenceEquals(element.RenderTransform, ownedTransform))
                Safe(() => element.SetCurrentValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty, originalTransform ?? Transform.Identity));
            originalTransform = null;
            ownedTransform = null;
            scale = null;
            translation = null;
            StopButtonColor();
        }

        private void SyncProgress()
        {
            progressVersion++;
            if (element is not ProgressBar bar || !progressEnabled) return;
            if (ownsProgress) bar.BeginAnimation(RangeBase.ValueProperty, null);
            ownsProgress = false;
            bar.SetValue(RangeBase.ValueProperty, targetProgress);
        }

        private void Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Safe(() => { AttachActive(); Safe(SyncProgress); });
        private void Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
        {
            Safe(DetachActive); Safe(StopVisuals); Safe(SyncProgress); ReleaseTransient();
        });
        private void VisibilityChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
        {
            var visible = (bool)args.NewValue;
            if (!visible) Safe(DetachActive);
            Safe(StopVisuals); Safe(SyncProgress);
            if (visible) AttachActive();
            else ReleaseTransient();
        });
        private void PreferenceChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
        {
            Stop();
            UpdateButtonColor(hoverDuration, false);
        });
        private void ThemeChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(() =>
        {
            StopVisuals();
            UpdateButtonColor(hoverDuration, false);
        });
        private void WindowChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(Stop);
        private void WindowClosed(object? sender, EventArgs args) => Safe(() => { Safe(DetachActive); Stop(); });
        private void PointerChanged(object sender, MouseEventArgs args) => UpdateButton();
        private void PressedChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => UpdateButton();
        private void EnabledChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args) => UpdateButton();

        private void AttachActive()
        {
            if (subscribed || !element.IsLoaded || !element.IsVisible) return;
            subscribed = true;
            MotionPreferences.Changed += PreferenceChanged;
            ThemeManager.Changed += ThemeChanged;
            window = Window.GetWindow(element);
            if (window is not null)
            {
                window.StateChanged += WindowChanged;
                window.Deactivated += WindowChanged;
                window.Closed += WindowClosed;
            }
            if (buttonEnabled && element is ButtonBase button)
            {
                button.MouseEnter += PointerChanged;
                button.MouseLeave += PointerChanged;
                button.IsEnabledChanged += EnabledChanged;
                PressedDescriptor?.AddValueChanged(button, PressedChanged);
            }
        }

        private void DetachActive()
        {
            if (!subscribed) return;
            subscribed = false;
            MotionPreferences.Changed -= PreferenceChanged;
            ThemeManager.Changed -= ThemeChanged;
            if (window is not null)
            {
                window.StateChanged -= WindowChanged;
                window.Deactivated -= WindowChanged;
                window.Closed -= WindowClosed;
                window = null;
            }
            if (buttonEnabled && element is ButtonBase button)
            {
                button.MouseEnter -= PointerChanged;
                button.MouseLeave -= PointerChanged;
                button.IsEnabledChanged -= EnabledChanged;
                Safe(() => PressedDescriptor?.RemoveValueChanged(button, PressedChanged));
            }
        }

        private void ReleaseTransient()
        {
            if (!buttonEnabled && !progressEnabled) Dispose();
        }

        private void Dispose()
        {
            try
            {
                Safe(DetachActive);
                Safe(StopVisuals);
                Safe(SyncProgress);
            }
            finally
            {
                element.Loaded -= Loaded;
                element.Unloaded -= Unloaded;
                element.IsVisibleChanged -= VisibilityChanged;
                Safe(() => { if (ReferenceEquals(element.GetValue(StateProperty), this)) element.ClearValue(StateProperty); });
            }
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Mvvm/ObservableObject.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;

public abstract class ObservableObject : INotifyPropertyChanged
{
    public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;
    public void RefreshLocalizedProperties() => OnPropertyChanged(string.Empty);
    protected bool Set<T>(ref T field, T value, [CallerMemberName] string? name = null)
    {
        if (EqualityComparer<T>.Default.Equals(field, value)) return false;
        field = value;
        OnPropertyChanged(name);
        return true;
    }
    protected void OnPropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string? name = null) =>
        PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
}

public sealed class RelayCommand : ICommand
{
    private readonly Action execute;
    private readonly Func<bool>? canExecute;
    public RelayCommand(Action execute, Func<bool>? canExecute = null) { this.execute = execute; this.canExecute = canExecute; }
    public event EventHandler? CanExecuteChanged;
    public bool CanExecute(object? parameter) => canExecute?.Invoke() ?? true;
    public void Execute(object? parameter) { if (CanExecute(parameter)) execute(); }
    public void Refresh() => CanExecuteChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
}

public sealed class AsyncCommand : ICommand
{
    private readonly Func<Task> execute;
    private readonly Func<bool>? canExecute;
    private readonly Action<Exception> onError;
    private bool executing;
    public AsyncCommand(Func<Task> execute, Action<Exception> onError, Func<bool>? canExecute = null)
    { this.execute = execute; this.onError = onError; this.canExecute = canExecute; }
    public event EventHandler? CanExecuteChanged;
    public bool CanExecute(object? parameter) => !executing && (canExecute?.Invoke() ?? true);
    public async void Execute(object? parameter)
    {
        if (!CanExecute(parameter)) return;
        executing = true;
        Refresh();
        try { await execute(); }
        catch (Exception error) { onError(error); }
        finally { executing = false; Refresh(); }
    }
    public void Refresh() => CanExecuteChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/DesktopEnvironment.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

public sealed record PetPosition(double X, double Y, string MonitorId);
public sealed record DesktopMonitor(string Id, Rect Bounds, Rect WorkArea, double DpiScaleX, double DpiScaleY, bool Primary);
internal sealed record DesktopEnvironmentObservation(int? NotificationState, bool IsOwnProcess, string? Executable,
    bool ShellWindow, bool Visible, bool Minimized, bool Captioned, Rect WindowBounds, Rect MonitorBounds);

/// <summary>Low-frequency foreground observation; never injects hooks into a game.</summary>
public sealed class DesktopEnvironment : IDisposable
{
    private readonly Action<bool> blockedChanged;
    private readonly Func<IReadOnlyList<string>> customExecutableNames;
    private readonly Func<bool> detectFullscreen;
    private readonly Func<DesktopEnvironmentObservation> observe;
    private readonly Dispatcher dispatcher;
    private readonly DispatcherTimer timer;
    private readonly NativeMethods.WinEventCallback foregroundCallback;
    private readonly GCHandle callbackHandle;
    private IntPtr foregroundHook;
    private bool disposed;
    public bool IsBlocked { get; private set; }
    public string BlockReason { get; private set; } = "";
    internal int ActiveTimerCount => timer.IsEnabled ? 1 : 0;
    internal bool HasNativeForegroundHook => foregroundHook != IntPtr.Zero;

    public DesktopEnvironment(Action<bool> blockedChanged, Func<IReadOnlyList<string>>? customExecutableNames = null, Func<bool>? detectFullscreen = null)
        : this(blockedChanged, customExecutableNames, detectFullscreen, null) { }

    internal DesktopEnvironment(Action<bool> blockedChanged, Func<IReadOnlyList<string>>? customExecutableNames,
        Func<bool>? detectFullscreen, Func<DesktopEnvironmentObservation>? observe)
    {
        this.blockedChanged = blockedChanged ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(blockedChanged));
        this.customExecutableNames = customExecutableNames ?? (() => Array.Empty<string>());
        this.detectFullscreen = detectFullscreen ?? (() => true);
        this.observe = observe ?? (() => ObserveDesktop(this.detectFullscreen()));
        dispatcher = Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher;
        foregroundCallback = (_, _, _, _, _, _, _) =>
        {
            if (!disposed && !dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Background, new Action(Refresh));
        };
        callbackHandle = GCHandle.Alloc(foregroundCallback);
        if (observe is null) foregroundHook = NativeMethods.SetWinEventHook(3, 3, IntPtr.Zero, foregroundCallback, 0, 0, 0x0002);
        timer = new DispatcherTimer(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2), DispatcherPriority.Background, (_, _) => Refresh(), dispatcher);
        Refresh();
    }

    public void Refresh()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        var next = false;
        var reason = "";
        try
        {
            var observeFullscreen = detectFullscreen();
            var observation = observe();
            if (observation.NotificationState is int state && (state == 1 || observeFullscreen && state is 2 or 3 or 4))
            {
                next = true;
                reason = state == 1 ? "会话未激活" : state == 4 ? "演示模式" : "全屏应用";
            }
            if (observeFullscreen && !observation.IsOwnProcess)
            {
                if (MatchesExecutable(observation.Executable, customExecutableNames())) { next = true; reason = "自定义游戏名单"; }
                if (!next && !observation.ShellWindow && observation.Visible && !observation.Minimized && !observation.Captioned &&
                    !observation.WindowBounds.IsEmpty && !observation.MonitorBounds.IsEmpty &&
                    CoversMonitor(observation.WindowBounds, observation.MonitorBounds))
                {
                    next = true; reason = "无边框全屏";
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Desktop foreground observation: {0}", error.Message); }
        BlockReason = reason;
        if (next == IsBlocked) return;
        IsBlocked = next;
        try { blockedChanged(next); }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Desktop visibility observer: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private static DesktopEnvironmentObservation ObserveDesktop(bool inspectForeground)
    {
        int? notification = NativeMethods.SHQueryUserNotificationState(out var state) >= 0 ? state : null;
        if (!inspectForeground) return new(notification, false, null, false, false, false, false, Rect.Empty, Rect.Empty);
        var foreground = NativeMethods.GetForegroundWindow();
        if (foreground == IntPtr.Zero) return new(notification, false, null, false, false, false, false, Rect.Empty, Rect.Empty);
        NativeMethods.GetWindowThreadProcessId(foreground, out var pid);
        var own = pid == (uint)Environment.ProcessId;
        var monitor = NativeMethods.MonitorFromWindow(foreground, 2);
        var bounds = TryWindowRect(foreground, out var rectangle) ? rectangle : Rect.Empty;
        var monitorBounds = TryMonitor(monitor, out var information) ? information.Bounds : Rect.Empty;
        return new(notification, own, own ? null : ForegroundExecutable(pid), IsDesktopShell(foreground),
            NativeMethods.IsWindowVisible(foreground), NativeMethods.IsIconic(foreground),
            (NativeMethods.GetWindowLongPtr(foreground, -16).ToInt64() & 0x00C00000) != 0, bounds, monitorBounds);
    }

    internal static bool MatchesExecutable(string? executable, IReadOnlyList<string> names)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(executable)) return false;
        var name = Path.GetFileName(executable);
        return names.Any(item => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item) &&
            string.Equals(name, Path.GetFileName(item.Trim()), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    internal static bool CoversMonitor(Rect window, Rect monitor)
        => Math.Abs(window.Left - monitor.Left) <= 2 && Math.Abs(window.Top - monitor.Top) <= 2 &&
           Math.Abs(window.Right - monitor.Right) <= 2 && Math.Abs(window.Bottom - monitor.Bottom) <= 2;

    public static IReadOnlyList<DesktopMonitor> GetMonitors()
    {
        var result = new List<DesktopMonitor>();
        NativeMethods.MonitorEnumCallback callback = (monitor, _, _, _) =>
        { if (TryMonitor(monitor, out var item)) result.Add(item); return true; };
        NativeMethods.EnumDisplayMonitors(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, callback, IntPtr.Zero);
        return result;
    }

    public static DesktopMonitor GetWorkAreaForPosition(double physicalX, double physicalY)
    {
        var monitor = NativeMethods.MonitorFromPoint(new NativeMethods.NativePoint((int)Math.Round(physicalX), (int)Math.Round(physicalY)), 2);
        if (TryMonitor(monitor, out var item)) return item;
        var area = SystemParameters.WorkArea;
        return new DesktopMonitor("primary", area, area, 1, 1, true);
    }

    public static Point ClampPosition(Point physical, Size physicalSize, Rect workArea)
    {
        static double Safe(double value, double fallback) => double.IsFinite(value) ? value : fallback;
        var width = Math.Max(1, Safe(physicalSize.Width, 1));
        var height = Math.Max(1, Safe(physicalSize.Height, 1));
        return new Point(Math.Clamp(Safe(physical.X, workArea.Left), workArea.Left, Math.Max(workArea.Left, workArea.Right - width)),
            Math.Clamp(Safe(physical.Y, workArea.Top), workArea.Top, Math.Max(workArea.Top, workArea.Bottom - height)));
    }

    internal static bool TryWindowRect(IntPtr window, out Rect rectangle)
    {
        if (NativeMethods.DwmGetWindowAttribute(window, 9, out var native, Marshal.SizeOf<NativeMethods.NativeRect>()) != 0 &&
            !NativeMethods.GetWindowRect(window, out native)) { rectangle = Rect.Empty; return false; }
        rectangle = native.AsRect();
        return !rectangle.IsEmpty && rectangle.Width > 0 && rectangle.Height > 0;
    }

    private static bool TryMonitor(IntPtr monitor, out DesktopMonitor result)
    {
        var information = new NativeMethods.MonitorInformation { Size = Marshal.SizeOf<NativeMethods.MonitorInformation>() };
        if (monitor == IntPtr.Zero || !NativeMethods.GetMonitorInfo(monitor, ref information)) { result = null!; return false; }
        uint x = 96, y = 96;
        try { if (NativeMethods.GetDpiForMonitor(monitor, 0, out var dpiX, out var dpiY) == 0) { x = dpiX; y = dpiY; } }
        catch (DllNotFoundException) { }
        catch (EntryPointNotFoundException) { }
        result = new DesktopMonitor(information.DeviceName ?? "", information.Monitor.AsRect(), information.Work.AsRect(),
            Math.Max(1, x) / 96d, Math.Max(1, y) / 96d, (information.Flags & 1) != 0);
        return true;
    }

    private static bool IsDesktopShell(IntPtr window)
    {
        var className = new StringBuilder(128);
        NativeMethods.GetClassName(window, className, className.Capacity);
        return className.ToString() is "Progman" or "WorkerW" or "Shell_TrayWnd" or "Shell_SecondaryTrayWnd";
    }

    private static string? ForegroundExecutable(uint processId)
    {
        var process = NativeMethods.OpenProcess(0x1000, false, processId);
        if (process == IntPtr.Zero) return null;
        try
        {
            var size = 1024;
            var path = new StringBuilder(size);
            return NativeMethods.QueryFullProcessImageName(process, 0, path, ref size) ? path.ToString() : null;
        }
        finally { NativeMethods.CloseHandle(process); }
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        disposed = true;
        timer.Stop();
        if (foregroundHook != IntPtr.Zero) { NativeMethods.UnhookWinEvent(foregroundHook); foregroundHook = IntPtr.Zero; }
        if (callbackHandle.IsAllocated) callbackHandle.Free();
    }
}

internal static class NativeMethods
{
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] internal readonly struct NativePoint(int x, int y)
    { public readonly int X = x; public readonly int Y = y; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] internal struct NativeRect
    {
        public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom;
        public readonly Rect AsRect() => Right >= Left && Bottom >= Top ? new Rect(Left, Top, Right - Left, Bottom - Top) : Rect.Empty;
    }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] internal struct MonitorInformation
    {
        public int Size;
        public NativeRect Monitor, Work;
        public uint Flags;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 32)] public string? DeviceName;
    }
    internal delegate void WinEventCallback(IntPtr hook, uint eventType, IntPtr window, int objectId, int childId, uint threadId, uint time);
    internal delegate bool MonitorEnumCallback(IntPtr monitor, IntPtr hdc, IntPtr rectangle, IntPtr data);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] internal static extern IntPtr SetWinEventHook(uint minimum, uint maximum, IntPtr module, WinEventCallback callback, uint processId, uint threadId, uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool UnhookWinEvent(IntPtr hook);
    [DllImport("shell32.dll")] internal static extern int SHQueryUserNotificationState(out int state);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] internal static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] internal static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr window, out uint processId);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "GetWindowLongPtrW")] internal static extern IntPtr GetWindowLongPtr(IntPtr window, int index);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "SetWindowLongPtrW")] internal static extern IntPtr SetWindowLongPtr(IntPtr window, int index, IntPtr value);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr window, out NativeRect rectangle);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] internal static extern int DwmGetWindowAttribute(IntPtr window, int attribute, out NativeRect value, int size);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] internal static extern IntPtr MonitorFromWindow(IntPtr window, uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] internal static extern IntPtr MonitorFromPoint(NativePoint point, uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool GetMonitorInfo(IntPtr monitor, ref MonitorInformation information);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool EnumDisplayMonitors(IntPtr hdc, IntPtr clip, MonitorEnumCallback callback, IntPtr data);
    [DllImport("shcore.dll")] internal static extern int GetDpiForMonitor(IntPtr monitor, int type, out uint x, out uint y);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] internal static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr window, StringBuilder name, int maximum);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] internal static extern IntPtr OpenProcess(uint access, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] bool inherit, uint id);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool QueryFullProcessImageName(IntPtr process, uint flags, StringBuilder name, ref int size);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool GetCursorPos(out NativePoint point);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] internal static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr window, IntPtr after, int x, int y, int width, int height, uint flags);
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/DesktopPetWindow.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>One small alpha-shaped desktop window, independent of the main application's visibility.</summary>
public sealed class DesktopPetWindow : Window, IDisposable
{
    private readonly Func<bool> reduceMotion;
    private PetMotionMode motionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle;
    private readonly DispatcherTimer holdTimer = new(DispatcherPriority.Input) { Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(300) };
    private readonly DispatcherTimer walkTimer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background) { Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1000d / 60) };
    private readonly ContextMenu menu = new();
    private readonly PetMessageWindow messageWindow;
    private HwndSource? source;
    private bool disposed;
    private bool pointerDown;
    private bool dragging;
    private bool dragHover;
    private bool walking;
    private bool stopBeforeMoving;
    private bool environmentVisible;
    private Point pressPoint;
    private Point startPosition;
    private Point walkStart;
    private Point walkEnd;
    private long walkStarted;
    private WalkStage walkStage;
    private long walkGeneration;
    private double walkScale = 1;
    private double walkDirection = 1;
    private double walkSegmentStart;
    private double walkSegmentEnd;
    private double walkSegmentVelocity;
    private double walkSegmentSeconds;
    private double walkDistance;
    private double walkVelocity;
    private Action? turnCompleted;
    private long pressStarted;
    private string requestedAnimation = "idle";
    private int petSize = 160;
    public PetSpriteView SpriteView { get; } = new();
    public bool IsInteracting => pointerDown || dragging || dragHover || menu.IsOpen;
    public bool IsWalking => walking;
    public bool IsEnvironmentVisible => environmentVisible;
    internal int ActiveMovementTimerCount => walkTimer.IsEnabled ? 1 : 0;
    internal int ActiveHoldTimerCount => holdTimer.IsEnabled ? 1 : 0;
    internal ContextMenu InteractionMenu => menu;
    internal double WalkDistanceDips => walkDistance;
    internal double PlannedWalkDistanceDips => walkSegmentEnd;
    internal bool IsWalkDistanceBound => !SpriteView.UsesLegacyClips && walking && walkStage is WalkStage.Moving or WalkStage.Stopping or WalkStage.Exiting;
    internal event Action<double, bool>? WalkProgressChanged;
    public event Action<IReadOnlyList<string>>? FilesDropped;
    public event Action<string>? InteractionRequested;
    public event Action<PetPosition>? PositionChanged;
    public event EventHandler? PresentationChanged;
    private string publishedAnimation = "idle";
    private bool publishedMirrored;
    private enum WalkStage { None, Turning, Entering, Moving, Stopping, Exiting }
    public PetPosition Position
    {
        get
        {
            var position = PhysicalPosition;
            return new PetPosition(position.X, position.Y, DesktopEnvironment.GetWorkAreaForPosition(position.X + 1, position.Y + 1).Id);
        }
    }
    private Point PhysicalPosition
    {
        get
        {
            if (source is not null && DesktopEnvironment.TryWindowRect(source.Handle, out var bounds)) return bounds.TopLeft;
            return startPosition;
        }
    }

    public DesktopPetWindow(Func<bool>? reduceMotion = null)
    {
        this.reduceMotion = reduceMotion ?? (() => MotionPreferences.ShouldReducePetMotion(motionMode));
        Title = "你的猫咪桌宠";
        WindowStyle = WindowStyle.None;
        ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize;
        AllowsTransparency = true;
        Background = Brushes.Transparent;
        ShowInTaskbar = false;
        ShowActivated = false;
        Topmost = true;
        Focusable = false;
        AllowDrop = true;
        Width = Height = petSize;
        Content = SpriteView;
        // The native pet is created before being shown. Explicit lifetime ownership avoids WPF's shown-owner restriction.
        messageWindow = new PetMessageWindow();
        BuildMenu();
        MouseLeftButtonDown += PointerPressed;
        MouseLeftButtonUp += PointerReleased;
        MouseMove += PointerMoved;
        MouseRightButtonUp += (_, args) =>
        {
            CancelWalk();
            CancelPointer();
            SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation);
            menu.PlacementTarget = this;
            menu.IsOpen = true;
            args.Handled = true;
        };
        LostMouseCapture += (_, _) => { if (pointerDown) EndDrag(false); };
        DragOver += FilesDragOver;
        DragLeave += (_, _) => { dragHover = false; if (!pointerDown) SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation); };
        Drop += FilesDrop;
        holdTimer.Tick += (_, _) => BeginDrag();
        walkTimer.Tick += (_, _) => AdvanceWalk();
        SourceInitialized += (_, _) =>
        {
            source = HwndSource.FromHwnd(new WindowInteropHelper(this).Handle);
            if (source is null) return;
            var style = NativeMethods.GetWindowLongPtr(source.Handle, -20).ToInt64();
            NativeMethods.SetWindowLongPtr(source.Handle, -20, new IntPtr(style | 0x08000000 | 0x00000080));
            source.AddHook(WindowMessage);
        };
        MotionPreferences.Changed += MotionChanged;
        Closed += (_, _) => DisposeResources();
    }

    public void LoadSprites(string manifestPath) => SpriteView.LoadManifest(manifestPath);

    // Publish actual desktop poses (including carried/dragging/walking), rather than only
    // the controller's requested idle state. Other views reuse the same presentation.
    private void SetSpriteAnimation(string animation, bool restart = false)
    {
        SpriteView.SetAnimation(animation, restart);
        var mirrored = SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0;
        if (publishedAnimation == SpriteView.Animation && publishedMirrored == mirrored) return;
        publishedAnimation = SpriteView.Animation;
        publishedMirrored = mirrored;
        PresentationChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }

    public void SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode mode)
    {
        motionMode = Enum.IsDefined(mode) ? mode : PetMotionMode.Gentle;
        SpriteView.SetMotionMode(motionMode);
        MotionChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }

    public void SetAnimation(string state, bool restart = false)
    {
        requestedAnimation = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(state) ? "idle" : state;
        if (walking && requestedAnimation is not ("idle" or "walk")) StopWalk();
        if (!dragging && !dragHover && !walking) SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation, restart);
    }

    public void Configure(bool topmost)
    {
        Topmost = topmost;
        messageWindow.Topmost = topmost;
    }

    public void SetSize(int dip)
    {
        var position = PhysicalPosition;
        petSize = Math.Clamp(dip, 80, 240);
        Width = Height = petSize;
        SpriteView.Width = SpriteView.Height = petSize;
        SetPosition(position.X, position.Y);
    }

    public void SetPosition(double physicalX, double physicalY)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        var helper = new WindowInteropHelper(this);
        var handle = helper.EnsureHandle();
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetWorkAreaForPosition(physicalX + petSize / 2d, physicalY + petSize / 2d);
        var size = new Size(petSize * monitor.DpiScaleX, petSize * monitor.DpiScaleY);
        var position = DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(new Point(physicalX, physicalY), size, monitor.WorkArea);
        startPosition = position;
        NativeMethods.SetWindowPos(handle, IntPtr.Zero, (int)Math.Round(position.X), (int)Math.Round(position.Y),
            (int)Math.Round(size.Width), (int)Math.Round(size.Height), 0x0010 | 0x0004);
        if (messageWindow.IsVisible) PositionMessage();
    }

    public void SetEnvironmentVisible(bool visible)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        environmentVisible = visible;
        SpriteView.SetMotionEnabled(visible && !reduceMotion());
        if (visible)
        {
            if (!IsVisible) Show();
            var position = PhysicalPosition;
            SetPosition(position.X, position.Y);
        }
        else
        {
            menu.IsOpen = false;
            dragHover = false;
            CancelPointer();
            CancelWalk();
            SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation);
            messageWindow.CancelMessage();
            if (IsVisible) Hide();
        }
    }

    internal void DismissMessage()
    {
        if (!disposed) messageWindow.CancelMessage();
    }

    public void ShowMessage(string text, Action? resultAction = null, string? buttonLabel = null)
    {
        if (disposed || !environmentVisible || !IsVisible) return;
        messageWindow.SetMessage(text, resultAction, buttonLabel);
        PositionMessage();
        messageWindow.Show();
        PositionMessage();
    }

    private void PositionMessage()
    {
        var position = PhysicalPosition;
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetWorkAreaForPosition(position.X + 1, position.Y + 1);
        var size = new Size(messageWindow.Width * monitor.DpiScaleX, messageWindow.Height * monitor.DpiScaleY);
        var left = position.X + (petSize * monitor.DpiScaleX - size.Width) / 2;
        var top = position.Y - size.Height - 8 * monitor.DpiScaleY;
        if (top < monitor.WorkArea.Top) top = position.Y + petSize * monitor.DpiScaleY + 8 * monitor.DpiScaleY;
        var clamped = DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(new Point(left, top), size, monitor.WorkArea);
        NativeMethods.SetWindowPos(new WindowInteropHelper(messageWindow).EnsureHandle(), IntPtr.Zero, (int)Math.Round(clamped.X),
            (int)Math.Round(clamped.Y), (int)Math.Round(size.Width), (int)Math.Round(size.Height), 0x0010 | 0x0004);
    }

    public bool TryWalk(double distanceDip)
    {
        if (disposed || !environmentVisible || !IsVisible || IsInteracting || walking || reduceMotion() || requestedAnimation != "idle") return false;
        walkStart = PhysicalPosition;
        var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetWorkAreaForPosition(walkStart.X + 1, walkStart.Y + 1);
        var target = new Point(walkStart.X + Math.Clamp(distanceDip, -100, 100) * monitor.DpiScaleX, walkStart.Y);
        walkEnd = DesktopEnvironment.ClampPosition(target, new Size(petSize * monitor.DpiScaleX, petSize * monitor.DpiScaleY), monitor.WorkArea);
        if (Math.Abs(walkEnd.X - walkStart.X) < 8) return false;
        if (SpriteView.UsesLegacyClips)
        {
            // The restored original drawings keep their original frame clock
            // and the released 2.4-second desktop stroll.
            walking = true; stopBeforeMoving = false; ++walkGeneration;
            walkStage = WalkStage.Moving; walkDistance = walkVelocity = 0;
            walkStarted = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
            walkScale = monitor.DpiScaleX * petSize / 160d;
            walkSegmentEnd = Math.Abs(walkEnd.X - walkStart.X) / walkScale;
            SpriteView.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(.5, .5);
            SpriteView.RenderTransform = new ScaleTransform(walkEnd.X < walkStart.X ? -1 : 1, 1);
            SpriteView.ReleaseWalkPhase(); SetSpriteAnimation("walk"); walkTimer.Start();
            return true;
        }
        // Sprite stride is measured on the reference 160-DIP character. Scale the
        // travelled distance with the actual pet and monitor, rather than running
        // an unrelated animation clock that lets planted feet slide over the desk.
        walkScale = monitor.DpiScaleX * petSize / 160d;
        walkDirection = Math.Sign(walkEnd.X - walkStart.X);
        var available = Math.Abs(walkEnd.X - walkStart.X) / walkScale;
        var distance = PetWalkTrajectory.ContactAlignedDistance(available, SpriteView.WalkStrideLength, SpriteView.WalkContactPhases);
        if (distance * walkScale < 8) return false;
        walkEnd = new Point(walkStart.X + walkDirection * distance * walkScale, walkStart.Y);
        walking = true;
        stopBeforeMoving = false;
        var generation = ++walkGeneration;
        walkDistance = walkVelocity = 0;
        walkSegmentStart = 0;
        walkSegmentEnd = distance;
        walkSegmentVelocity = 0;
        walkSegmentSeconds = Math.Max(1, distance / 20d);
        var facesLeft = SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0;
        if (facesLeft != (walkDirection < 0) && SpriteView.HasAnimation("turn"))
        {
            walkStage = WalkStage.Turning;
            turnCompleted = () =>
            {
                if (generation != walkGeneration || !walking || walkStage != WalkStage.Turning) return;
                RemoveTurnCompletion();
                BeginWalkEntry();
            };
            SpriteView.AnimationCompleted += turnCompleted;
            SetSpriteAnimation("turn");
        }
        else BeginWalkEntry();
        walkTimer.Start();
        return true;
    }

    private void BeginWalkEntry()
    {
        SpriteView.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(.5, .5);
        SpriteView.RenderTransform = walkDirection < 0 ? new ScaleTransform(-1, 1) : Transform.Identity;
        walkStage = WalkStage.Entering;
        SetSpriteAnimation("walk");
    }

    private void AdvanceWalk()
    {
        if (!environmentVisible || reduceMotion() || IsInteracting) { CancelWalk(); return; }
        if (SpriteView.UsesLegacyClips)
        {
            var progress = Math.Clamp(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(walkStarted).TotalSeconds / 2.4, 0, 1);
            var eased = progress * progress * (3 - 2 * progress);
            SetPosition(walkStart.X + (walkEnd.X - walkStart.X) * eased, walkStart.Y);
            walkDistance = Math.Abs(PhysicalPosition.X - walkStart.X) / walkScale;
            if (progress >= 1) CancelWalk();
            return;
        }
        if (walkStage is WalkStage.Turning or WalkStage.Exiting) return;
        if (walkStage == WalkStage.Entering)
        {
            if (!SpriteView.IsWalkLoopReady) return;
            if (stopBeforeMoving)
            {
                SpriteView.UpdateWalkDistance(0);
                CompleteWalk();
                return;
            }
            walkStage = WalkStage.Moving;
            walkStarted = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        }
        var elapsed = Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(walkStarted).TotalSeconds;
        var sample = PetWalkTrajectory.Sample(walkSegmentStart, walkSegmentEnd, walkSegmentVelocity, walkSegmentSeconds, elapsed);
        walkDistance = sample.Distance;
        walkVelocity = sample.Velocity;
        SetPosition(walkStart.X + walkDirection * walkDistance * walkScale, walkStart.Y);
        var actualDistance = Math.Abs(PhysicalPosition.X - walkStart.X) / walkScale;
        SpriteView.UpdateWalkDistance(actualDistance);
        WalkProgressChanged?.Invoke(actualDistance, false);
        if (elapsed >= walkSegmentSeconds)
        {
            // Preserve the exact contact marker despite native integer pixel rounding.
            walkDistance = walkSegmentEnd;
            SpriteView.UpdateWalkDistance(walkDistance);
            WalkProgressChanged?.Invoke(walkDistance, false);
            CompleteWalk();
        }
    }

    public void StopWalk()
    {
        if (!walking) return;
        if (SpriteView.UsesLegacyClips) { CancelWalk(); return; }
        if (walkStage is WalkStage.Turning or WalkStage.Entering)
        {
            // Finish the drawn turn and reach the first planted pose before
            // taking its matching exit. A new interaction must not cut a
            // half-turned cat directly into a different whole-cat drawing.
            stopBeforeMoving = true;
            return;
        }
        if (walkStage is WalkStage.Stopping or WalkStage.Exiting) return;
        var contact = PetWalkTrajectory.NextContactDistance(walkDistance, walkSegmentEnd, SpriteView.WalkStrideLength, SpriteView.WalkContactPhases);
        if (contact - walkDistance < .01) { CompleteWalk(); return; }
        walkSegmentStart = walkDistance;
        walkSegmentEnd = contact;
        walkSegmentVelocity = walkVelocity;
        var remainder = contact - walkDistance;
        walkSegmentSeconds = walkVelocity > .01 ? Math.Min(.65, 2 * remainder / walkVelocity) : Math.Max(.2, remainder / 30);
        walkStarted = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        walkStage = WalkStage.Stopping;
    }

    private void CompleteWalk()
    {
        if (!walking || walkStage == WalkStage.Exiting) return;
        walkStage = WalkStage.Exiting;
        walkVelocity = 0;
        var generation = walkGeneration;
        WalkProgressChanged?.Invoke(walkDistance, true);
        SpriteView.CompleteWalk(() =>
        {
            if (generation != walkGeneration || !walking || walkStage != WalkStage.Exiting) return;
            FinishWalk();
        });
    }

    private void RemoveTurnCompletion()
    {
        if (turnCompleted is null) return;
        SpriteView.AnimationCompleted -= turnCompleted;
        turnCompleted = null;
    }

    private void CancelWalk()
    {
        if (!walking) return;
        ++walkGeneration;
        RemoveTurnCompletion();
        SpriteView.CancelWalkMotion();
        FinishWalk();
    }

    private void FinishWalk()
    {
        walking = false;
        stopBeforeMoving = false;
        walkStage = WalkStage.None;
        walkTimer.Stop();
        RemoveTurnCompletion();
        SpriteView.CancelWalkMotion();
        // Retain facing after landing. Returning the transform to identity here
        // used to flip the entire cat instantly as soon as a walk ended.
        SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation);
        PositionChanged?.Invoke(Position);
    }

    private void PointerPressed(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs args)
    {
        if (args.ChangedButton != MouseButton.Left || disposed) return;
        CancelWalk();
        pointerDown = true;
        pressStarted = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        pressPoint = CursorPosition();
        startPosition = PhysicalPosition;
        CaptureMouse();
        holdTimer.Start();
        args.Handled = true;
    }

    private void BeginDrag()
    {
        holdTimer.Stop();
        if (!pointerDown || Mouse.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed) return;
        dragging = true;
        SetSpriteAnimation("held");
        MoveWithPointer();
    }

    private void PointerMoved(object sender, MouseEventArgs args)
    {
        if (!pointerDown || !dragging) return;
        if (Mouse.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed) { EndDrag(false); return; }
        MoveWithPointer();
        args.Handled = true;
    }

    private void MoveWithPointer()
    {
        var current = CursorPosition();
        var target = new Point(startPosition.X + current.X - pressPoint.X, startPosition.Y + current.Y - pressPoint.Y);
        // SetPosition updates its remembered position; preserve the original press origin throughout the drag.
        var origin = startPosition;
        SetPosition(target.X, target.Y);
        startPosition = origin;
    }

    private void PointerReleased(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs args)
    {
        if (!pointerDown) return;
        var click = !dragging && IsShortClick(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(pressStarted));
        var point = args.GetPosition(SpriteView);
        EndDrag(true);
        if (click && SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(point)) InteractionRequested?.Invoke(SpriteView.IsHeadHit(point) ? "pet-head" : "pet-body");
        args.Handled = true;
    }

    private void EndDrag(bool savePosition)
    {
        var wasDragging = dragging;
        CancelPointer();
        SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation);
        if (wasDragging || savePosition) PositionChanged?.Invoke(Position);
    }

    private void CancelPointer()
    {
        holdTimer.Stop();
        pointerDown = dragging = false;
        if (IsMouseCaptured) ReleaseMouseCapture();
    }

    private static Point CursorPosition()
        => NativeMethods.GetCursorPos(out var point) ? new Point(point.X, point.Y) : new Point();

    internal static bool IsShortClick(TimeSpan elapsed) => elapsed >= TimeSpan.Zero && elapsed < TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(300);
    internal static bool IsHeadHit(Point local, double height) => height > 0 && local.Y >= 0 && local.Y < height * .58;

    private void FilesDragOver(object sender, DragEventArgs args)
    {
        var accepts = args.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop);
        dragHover = accepts;
        args.Effects = accepts ? DragDropEffects.Copy : DragDropEffects.None;
        if (accepts) { CancelWalk(); SetSpriteAnimation("drag"); }
        args.Handled = true;
    }

    private void FilesDrop(object sender, DragEventArgs args)
    {
        dragHover = false;
        SetSpriteAnimation(requestedAnimation);
        args.Handled = true;
        if (args.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop) is string[] paths)
            FilesDropped?.Invoke(paths.Where(path => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)).Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToArray());
    }

    private void BuildMenu()
    {
        MenuItem Item(string label, string action)
        {
            var item = new MenuItem { Header = label };
            item.Click += (_, args) => { args.Handled = true; menu.IsOpen = false; InteractionRequested?.Invoke(action); };
            return item;
        }
        var process = new PetSubmenuItem { Header = "处理文件" };
        process.Items.Add(Item("选择文件…", "choose-files"));
        process.Items.Add(Item("选择文件夹…", "choose-folder"));
        menu.Items.Add(process);
        var interact = new PetSubmenuItem { Header = "互动" };
        foreach (var option in new[] { ("摸摸头", "pet-head"), ("一起玩耍", "play"), ("喂食", "feed"), ("开始工作", "work"), ("停止工作", "stop-work"), ("喵喵商店", "shop") })
            interact.Items.Add(Item(option.Item1, option.Item2));
        menu.Items.Add(interact);
        menu.Items.Add(new Separator());
        menu.Items.Add(Item("打开软件", "open"));
        menu.Items.Add(Item("桌宠设置", "settings"));
        menu.Items.Add(Item("暂时隐藏", "hide"));
        menu.Items.Add(Item("退出软件", "exit"));
    }

    private IntPtr WindowMessage(IntPtr window, int message, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam, ref bool handled)
    {
        if (message == 0x0021) { handled = true; return new IntPtr(3); } // MA_NOACTIVATE
        if (message == 0x0084 && !dragging)
        {
            if (!SpriteView.IsLoaded) { handled = true; return new IntPtr(-1); }
            var packed = lParam.ToInt64();
            var screen = new Point((short)(packed & 0xFFFF), (short)((packed >> 16) & 0xFFFF));
            var local = SpriteView.PointFromScreen(screen);
            if (!SpriteView.ContainsVisiblePixel(local)) { handled = true; return new IntPtr(-1); } // HTTRANSPARENT
        }
        if (message is 0x02E0 or 0x007E) // WM_DPICHANGED / WM_DISPLAYCHANGE
            Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, new Action(() =>
            {
                if (disposed) return;
                var position = PhysicalPosition;
                SetPosition(position.X, position.Y);
                PositionChanged?.Invoke(Position);
            }));
        return IntPtr.Zero;
    }

    private void MotionChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args)
    {
        if (reduceMotion()) CancelWalk();
        SpriteView.SetMotionEnabled(environmentVisible && !reduceMotion());
    }

    private void DisposeResources()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        disposed = true;
        CancelPointer();
        ++walkGeneration;
        RemoveTurnCompletion();
        SpriteView.CancelWalkMotion();
        walkTimer.Stop();
        menu.IsOpen = false;
        MotionPreferences.Changed -= MotionChanged;
        source?.RemoveHook(WindowMessage);
        SpriteView.Dispose();
        messageWindow.Dispose();
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        DisposeResources();
        Close();
    }
}

internal sealed class PetMessageWindow : Window, IDisposable
{
    private readonly TextBlock text = new() { TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap };
    private readonly Button result = new() { HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, Margin = new Thickness(0, 7, 0, 0), Padding = new Thickness(9, 4, 9, 4), Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed };
    private readonly DispatcherTimer timer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background) { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(7) };
    private Action? resultAction;
    private bool disposed;
    private bool closed;
    public PetMessageWindow()
    {
        ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
        Title = "猫咪的提醒";
        WindowStyle = WindowStyle.None;
        ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize;
        AllowsTransparency = true;
        Background = Brushes.Transparent;
        ShowActivated = false;
        ShowInTaskbar = false;
        Topmost = true;
        Width = 268;
        Height = 104;
        SetResourceReference(FontFamilyProperty, "UiFont");
        SetResourceReference(FontSizeProperty, "BodySize");
        SetResourceReference(ForegroundProperty, "Text");
        text.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, "BodySize");
        text.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.LineHeightProperty, "BodyLineHeight");
        text.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "Text");
        result.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "SoftButton");
        var body = new StackPanel();
        body.Children.Add(text);
        body.Children.Add(result);
        var surface = new Border { BorderThickness = new Thickness(1), Padding = new Thickness(14, 11, 14, 11), Child = body };
        surface.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "Surface");
        surface.SetResourceReference(Border.BorderBrushProperty, "Border");
        surface.SetResourceReference(Border.CornerRadiusProperty, "CardRadius");
        Content = surface;
        result.Click += (_, _) => { var action = resultAction; CancelMessage(); action?.Invoke(); };
        timer.Tick += (_, _) => CancelMessage();
        MouseEnter += (_, _) => timer.Stop();
        MouseLeave += (_, _) => { if (IsVisible) timer.Start(); };
        SourceInitialized += (_, _) =>
        {
            var handle = new WindowInteropHelper(this).Handle;
            NativeMethods.SetWindowLongPtr(handle, -20, new IntPtr(NativeMethods.GetWindowLongPtr(handle, -20).ToInt64() | 0x08000000 | 0x00000080));
        };
        Closed += (_, _) => { closed = true; timer.Stop(); resultAction = null; };
    }
    public void SetMessage(string message, Action? action, string? button)
    {
        timer.Stop();
        text.Text = message.Length > 160 ? message[..157] + "…" : message;
        resultAction = action;
        result.Content = button ?? "查看结果";
        result.Visibility = action is null ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
        text.Measure(new Size(Width - 30, double.PositiveInfinity));
        result.Measure(new Size(Width - 30, double.PositiveInfinity));
        Height = Math.Clamp(text.DesiredSize.Height + 24 + (action is null ? 0 : result.DesiredSize.Height + 7), 82, 320);
        timer.Start();
    }
    public void CancelMessage() { timer.Stop(); resultAction = null; if (IsVisible) Hide(); }
    public void Dispose() { if (disposed) return; disposed = true; timer.Stop(); resultAction = null; if (!closed) Close(); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/HomeCompanionView.cs ---
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Automation;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Media;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>A home-only presentation of the existing pet. Ownership, rewards and work stay in PetController.</summary>
public sealed class HomeCompanionView : UserControl, IDisposable
{
    private readonly Grid stage = new();
    private readonly StackPanel conversation = new();
    private readonly TextBlock message = Label("", "Section", false);
    private readonly TextBlock status = Label("", "Caption", true);
    private readonly TextBlock balance = Label("", "Caption", true);
    private readonly Button cat;
    private Window? owner;
    private bool homeVisible, disposed;
    private bool? compact;
    public PetController Controller { get; }
    public PetSpriteView SpriteView { get; } = new();
    public bool IsRegistered { get; private set; }
    public string DisplayedMessage => message.Text;
    public string DisplayedStatus => status.Text;
    public event EventHandler? BecameActive;

    public HomeCompanionView(PetController controller)
    {
        Controller = controller;
        AutomationProperties.SetAutomationId(this, "HomeCompanion");
        AutomationProperties.SetName(this, "首页猫咪陪伴区");

        SetResourceReference(ForegroundProperty, "Text");
        Margin = new Thickness(0, 12, 0, 0);
        var body = new StackPanel();
