        body.Children.Add(heading);
        var hint = Label("忙的时候陪你，闲的时候一起玩。", "Caption", true); hint.Margin = new Thickness(0, 5, 0, 4); body.Children.Add(hint);
        stage.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(224) });
        stage.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition());
        stage.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        stage.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        cat = Button("摸摸猫咪", () => controller.Interact("pet-head"));
        cat.Content = SpriteView; cat.Padding = new Thickness(4); cat.Width = 196; cat.Height = 184;
        cat.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center; cat.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
        cat.ToolTip = "摸摸猫咪 · 也可以按 Enter 或空格";
        cat.SetResourceReference(BackgroundProperty, "Surface");
        AutomationProperties.SetAutomationId(cat, "HomePetInteract");
        SpriteView.Width = 180; SpriteView.Height = 170;
        SpriteView.SetMotionEnabled(false);
        stage.Children.Add(cat);
        var speech = new Border { Child = message, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(16), Padding = new Thickness(16, 12, 16, 12) };
        speech.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "SoftSurface");
        AutomationProperties.SetAutomationId(message, "HomePetMessage");
        conversation.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
        conversation.Children.Add(speech);
        status.Margin = new Thickness(2, 10, 0, 0); conversation.Children.Add(status);
        AutomationProperties.SetAutomationId(status, "HomePetStatus");
        var actions = new WrapPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 8, 0, 0) };
        actions.Children.Add(Button("摸摸头", () => controller.Interact("pet-head")));
        actions.Children.Add(Button("一起玩耍", () => controller.Interact("play")));
        actions.Children.Add(Button("喂食", () => controller.Interact("feed")));
        var shop = Button("商店与背包", () => controller.OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("shop"), editing: false); actions.Children.Add(shop);
        conversation.Children.Add(actions);
        balance.Margin = new Thickness(2, 2, 0, 0); conversation.Children.Add(balance);
        stage.Children.Add(conversation); Grid.SetColumn(conversation, 1);
        body.Children.Add(stage);
        var card = new Border { Child = body, Padding = new Thickness(20, 18, 20, 14) };
        card.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "Card");
        // Keep animated content out of a full-card blur surface. The shared surface,
        // outline and corner tokens provide the same hierarchy without a live shadow pass.
        card.Effect = null;
        Content = card;
        Loaded += LoadedView;
        Unloaded += UnloadedView;
        IsVisibleChanged += VisibilityChanged;
        SizeChanged += (_, _) => ApplyLayout();
        Refresh();
    }

    private Button Button(string label, Action action, bool editing = true)
    {
        var button = new Button { Content = label, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 8, 8), Padding = new Thickness(12, 8, 12, 8) };
        button.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "SoftButton");
        AutomationProperties.SetName(button, label);
        if (editing) button.SetBinding(IsEnabledProperty, new Binding("CanEdit") { Source = Controller.Model });
        button.Click += (_, _) => action();
        return button;
    }
    private static TextBlock Label(string text, string level, bool muted)
    {
        var label = new TextBlock { Text = text, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap };
        label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, level + "Size");
        label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.LineHeightProperty, level + "LineHeight");
        label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, muted ? "Muted" : "Text");
        return label;
    }
    private void ApplyLayout()
    {
        if (ActualWidth <= 0) return;
        var next = ActualWidth < 640;
        if (compact == next) return;
        compact = next;
        stage.ColumnDefinitions[0].Width = next ? new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) : new GridLength(224);
        stage.ColumnDefinitions[1].Width = next ? new GridLength(0) : new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star);
        Grid.SetRow(conversation, next ? 1 : 0); Grid.SetColumn(conversation, next ? 0 : 1);
        conversation.Margin = new Thickness(0, next ? 0 : 12, 0, 0);
    }
    public void SetHomeVisible(bool value)
    {
        homeVisible = value;
        RefreshPresence();
    }
    private void LoadedView(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
        if (owner is not null) { owner.StateChanged += OwnerStateChanged; owner.IsVisibleChanged += OwnerVisibilityChanged; }
        ApplyLayout();
        RefreshPresence();
    }
    private void UnloadedView(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        SetRegistered(false);
        if (owner is not null) { owner.StateChanged -= OwnerStateChanged; owner.IsVisibleChanged -= OwnerVisibilityChanged; owner = null; }
    }
    private void OwnerStateChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e) => RefreshPresence();
    private void OwnerVisibilityChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e) => RefreshPresence();
    private void VisibilityChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e) => RefreshPresence();
    private void RefreshPresence()
    {
        SetRegistered(!disposed && homeVisible && IsLoaded && IsVisible && owner is { IsVisible: true } && owner.WindowState != WindowState.Minimized);
    }
    private void SetRegistered(bool value)
    {
        if (value == IsRegistered) return;
        IsRegistered = value;
        if (value)
        {
            Controller.Updated += ControllerUpdated;
            Controller.Model.PropertyChanged += ModelChanged;
            Controller.RegisterPreview(SpriteView);
            Refresh();
            BecameActive?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
        }
        else
        {
            Controller.Updated -= ControllerUpdated;
            Controller.Model.PropertyChanged -= ModelChanged;
            Controller.UnregisterPreview(SpriteView);
            SpriteView.SetMotionEnabled(false);
        }
    }
    private void ControllerUpdated(object? sender, EventArgs e) => Refresh();
    private void ModelChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.PropertyName) || e.PropertyName is "Language" or "ActiveBatchStatus" or "HasActiveBatch" or "IsProcessing" or "IsImporting") Refresh();
    }
    private void Refresh()
    {
        var account = Controller.Account.Snapshot;
        var localizedMessage = Locale.TranslateText(Controller.LatestMessage);
        if (message.Text != localizedMessage)
        {
            message.Text = localizedMessage;
            message.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, message.Text.Length > 80 ? "BodySize" : "SectionSize");
            message.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.LineHeightProperty, message.Text.Length > 80 ? "BodyLineHeight" : "SectionLineHeight");
        }
        status.Text = Controller.Model.HasActiveBatch ? Controller.Model.ActiveBatchStatus : Controller.Model.IsImporting ? "正在接住文件，马上就好。" :
            account.WorkRequested ? Locale.F($"{(account.WorkRunning ? Locale.T("工作中") : Locale.T("已暂停"))} · 已累计 {PetController.WorkDurationText(account.WorkElapsed)} · 本次 {account.WorkEarned:0.##} 喵喵币") :
            Controller.CurrentAnimation switch { "sleep" => "猫咪正在小憩，摸摸它就会醒来。", "walk" => "猫咪正在散步，陪你活动一下。", "happy" or "play" or "play-wand" => "猫咪很开心，正在和你一起玩。", "groom" => "正在认真整理毛毛。", "stretch" => "伸个懒腰，再继续吧。", "work" => "正在陪你一起处理文件。", _ => "猫咪就在这里，随时陪你。" };
        balance.Text = Locale.F($"{account.Coins:0.##} 喵喵币 · 和桌面猫咪共享互动与装扮");
    }
    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        disposed = true;
        SetRegistered(false);
        if (owner is not null) { owner.StateChanged -= OwnerStateChanged; owner.IsVisibleChanged -= OwnerVisibilityChanged; owner = null; }
        Loaded -= LoadedView; Unloaded -= UnloadedView; IsVisibleChanged -= VisibilityChanged;
        SpriteView.Dispose();
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/HomePetGreeting.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Local-time, text-only welcome lines. No network or speech service is involved.</summary>
public static class HomePetGreeting
{
    public static IReadOnlyList<string> ChoicesForHour(int hour)
    {
        if (hour is < 0 or > 23) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(hour));
        var timed = hour switch
        {
            >= 5 and < 11 => new[] { "早上好喵！今天也一起慢慢开始吧。", "早安，见到你就精神起来啦！", "新的一天，先接住一份好心情喵。" },
            >= 11 and < 14 => new[] { "中午好喵！忙完记得好好吃饭。", "午间来见你啦，今天要做什么呢？", "中午好，留一点时间伸个懒腰吧。" },
            >= 14 and < 18 => new[] { "下午好喵！我来陪你把事情一件件完成。", "午后的猫咪已就位，今天要做什么呢？", "下午好，累了就和我一起歇一会儿。" },
            >= 18 and < 23 => new[] { "晚上好喵！今天辛苦啦。", "晚上好，剩下的事情我们一起慢慢来。", "夜晚也有猫咪陪你，欢迎回来喵！" },
            _ => new[] { "夜深啦喵，忙完也要早点休息。", "这么晚还在忙呀？我安静地陪着你。", "夜里好喵，慢慢来，别忘了照顾自己。" }
        };
        return new[] { timed[0], timed[1], timed[2], "你来啦，今天要做什么呢？", "欢迎回来喵！猫咪已经准备好啦。", "今天也陪你一起，把事情一件件完成。" };
    }

    public static string Choose(DateTime localTime, Random random)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(random);
        var choices = ChoicesForHour(localTime.Hour);
        return choices[random.Next(choices.Count)];
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/PetAccessoryView.cs ---
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

internal sealed record PetAccessoryPoseDiagnostics(Point StarAnchor, Point NeckAnchor, Matrix HeadTransform,
    Matrix NeckTransform, Rect StarBounds, Rect CollarBounds, Rect BellBounds);

/// <summary>
/// Vector accessories in the artwork's reference 160-DIP plane. The parent applies
/// the same uniform fit, breathing and mirroring as the cat; this view never caches pixels.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class PetAccessoryView : FrameworkElement
{
    private static readonly DrawingGroup collarDrawing = MakeCollar();
    private static readonly DrawingGroup bellDrawing = MakeBell();
    private static readonly DrawingGroup starDrawing = MakeStar();
    private readonly MatrixTransform headTransform = new();
    private readonly MatrixTransform neckTransform = new();
    private Point head;
    private Point neck;
    private bool wearsCollar;
    private bool wearsBell;
    private bool wearsStar;
    private Geometry? neckOcclusion;

    public PetAccessoryView()
    {
        IsHitTestVisible = false;
        UseLayoutRounding = true;
        HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
        VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
        Width = Height = 160;
    }

    public void SetEquipment(bool collar, bool bell, bool star)
    {
        if (wearsCollar == collar && wearsBell == bell && wearsStar == star) return;
        wearsCollar = collar; wearsBell = bell; wearsStar = star;
        InvalidateVisual();
    }

    /// <param name="head">Star attachment point on the fur at the ear base, in reference DIPs.</param>
    /// <param name="neck">Collar contact point immediately under the chin, in reference DIPs.</param>
    /// <param name="headAngle">Clockwise head rotation in degrees.</param>
    /// <param name="neckAngle">Clockwise neck rotation in degrees.</param>
    /// <param name="scale">Local accessory scale, before the parent's uniform fit.</param>
    public void SetPose(Point head, Point neck, double headAngle = 0, double neckAngle = 0, double scale = 1)
    {
        if (!Finite(head) || !Finite(neck)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(head), "Attachment points must be finite.");
        if (!double.IsFinite(headAngle) || !double.IsFinite(neckAngle)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(headAngle));
        if (!double.IsFinite(scale) || scale <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(scale));
        this.head = head; this.neck = neck;
        headTransform.Matrix = PoseMatrix(head, headAngle, scale);
        neckTransform.Matrix = PoseMatrix(neck, neckAngle, scale);
        InvalidateVisual();
    }

    /// <summary>Actual foreground paw outlines, in the same reference plane as the artwork.</summary>
    public void SetNeckOcclusion(Geometry? foreground)
    {
        if (ReferenceEquals(neckOcclusion, foreground)) return;
        if (foreground is { IsFrozen: false }) { foreground = foreground.CloneCurrentValue(); foreground.Freeze(); }
        neckOcclusion = foreground; InvalidateVisual();
    }

    internal PetAccessoryPoseDiagnostics PoseDiagnostics => new(head, neck, headTransform.Matrix, neckTransform.Matrix,
        headTransform.TransformBounds(starDrawing.Bounds), neckTransform.TransformBounds(collarDrawing.Bounds), neckTransform.TransformBounds(bellDrawing.Bounds));

    protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawing)
    {
        base.OnRender(drawing);
        if (wearsCollar || wearsBell)
        {
            if (neckOcclusion is not null)
            {
                var canvas = new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(0, 0, Math.Max(1, ActualWidth), Math.Max(1, ActualHeight)));
                drawing.PushClip(new CombinedGeometry(GeometryCombineMode.Exclude, canvas, neckOcclusion));
            }
            drawing.PushTransform(neckTransform);
            if (wearsCollar) drawing.DrawDrawing(collarDrawing);
            if (wearsBell) drawing.DrawDrawing(bellDrawing);
            drawing.Pop();
            if (neckOcclusion is not null) drawing.Pop();
        }
        if (wearsStar)
        {
            drawing.PushTransform(headTransform); drawing.DrawDrawing(starDrawing); drawing.Pop();
        }
    }

    private static Matrix PoseMatrix(Point anchor, double angle, double scale)
    {
        var matrix = Matrix.Identity;
        matrix.Scale(scale, scale); matrix.Rotate(angle); matrix.Translate(anchor.X, anchor.Y);
        return matrix;
    }
    private static bool Finite(Point point) => double.IsFinite(point.X) && double.IsFinite(point.Y);

    private static DrawingGroup MakeCollar()
    {
        var drawing = new DrawingGroup();
        using (var context = drawing.Open())
        {
            // A narrow lower arc follows the chin; an ellipse would paint across the face.
            var band = Shape("M-16.5,-1.8 C-9.5,1.2 9.5,1.2 16.5,-1.8 L15.7,1.7 C7.5,5.2 -7.5,5.2 -15.7,1.7 Z");
            context.DrawGeometry(Brush(133, 123, 190), Stroke(73, 64, 113, .85), band);
            context.DrawGeometry(null, Stroke(188, 178, 232, .85), Shape("M-13.5,.1 C-6.5,2.5 6.5,2.5 13.5,.1"));
        }
        drawing.Freeze(); return drawing;
    }

    private static DrawingGroup MakeBell()
    {
        var drawing = new DrawingGroup();
        using (var context = drawing.Open())
        {
            context.DrawEllipse(null, Stroke(132, 91, 38, .65), new Point(0, 3.7), 1, 1.4);
            context.DrawEllipse(Brush(244, 202, 103), Stroke(129, 88, 34, .75), new Point(0, 7), 2.8, 2.8);
            context.DrawEllipse(Brush(255, 239, 176), null, new Point(-.85, 6), .85, .9);
            context.DrawLine(Stroke(129, 88, 34, .65), new Point(-1.8, 8), new Point(1.8, 8));
            context.DrawEllipse(Brush(129, 88, 34), null, new Point(0, 8.25), .5, .65);
        }
        drawing.Freeze(); return drawing;
    }

    private static DrawingGroup MakeStar()
    {
        var drawing = new DrawingGroup();
        using (var context = drawing.Open())
        {
            // The short bar sits on the fur behind the star and reads as a hair clip.
            context.DrawLine(Stroke(83, 75, 123, 1.6), new Point(-4.5, 2), new Point(4.5, -1));
            var shape = new StreamGeometry();
            using (var path = shape.Open())
            {
                for (var index = 0; index < 10; index++)
                {
                    var angle = (-90 + index * 36) * Math.PI / 180;
                    var radius = index % 2 == 0 ? 5.2 : 2.45;
                    var point = new Point(Math.Cos(angle) * radius, Math.Sin(angle) * radius);
                    if (index == 0) path.BeginFigure(point, true, true); else path.LineTo(point, true, false);
                }
            }
            shape.Freeze();
            context.DrawGeometry(Brush(250, 213, 122), Stroke(137, 99, 46, .7), shape);
            context.DrawLine(Stroke(255, 244, 197, .75), new Point(-1.1, -.9), new Point(0, -3.1));
        }
        drawing.Freeze(); return drawing;
    }

    private static SolidColorBrush Brush(byte red, byte green, byte blue)
    {
        var brush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(red, green, blue)); brush.Freeze(); return brush;
    }
    private static Pen Stroke(byte red, byte green, byte blue, double thickness)
    {
        var pen = new Pen(Brush(red, green, blue), thickness) { StartLineCap = PenLineCap.Round, EndLineCap = PenLineCap.Round, LineJoin = PenLineJoin.Round };
        pen.Freeze(); return pen;
    }
    private static Geometry Shape(string data) { var geometry = Geometry.Parse(data); geometry.Freeze(); return geometry; }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/PetController.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Controls;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Owns companionship presentation; file execution remains in the shared model.</summary>
public sealed class PetController : IDisposable
{
    private readonly DispatcherTimer heartbeat = new() { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1) };
    private readonly HashSet<string> pending = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private readonly Dictionary<string,string> pendingReasons = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private readonly HashSet<Guid> notified = new();
    private readonly HashSet<PetSpriteView> previews = new();
    private readonly Random random = new();
    private DesktopEnvironment? environment;
    private bool manuallyHidden, blocked, disposed, choosing;
    private bool sessionLocked, suspended;
    private Guid lastCelebratedWork;
    private int lastWorkReply = -1;
    private int deferredCompleted, deferredFailed, deferredStopped, deferredNotFound;
    private DateTime nextRoam = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(100), nextIdle = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(20), nextWake = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(8);
    private DateTime transientUntil, lastInteraction = DateTime.UtcNow;
    private DateTime resultNoticeUntil;
    private string transient = "idle";
    private int lastReply = -1;
    private int lastHeadReply = -1;
    private static readonly string[] headReplies =
    {
        "呼噜呼噜……再摸一下吧。", "喵～摸摸头，烦恼都溜走啦。", "这个力度刚刚好，呼噜～",
        "好舒服呀，再陪我一会儿吧。", "今天的摸摸头，我收下啦！", "有你在身边，猫猫好安心。"
    };
    public MainViewModel Model { get; }
    public PetStateStore Account { get; }
    public DesktopPetWindow DesktopWindow { get; }
    public IReadOnlyList<string> PendingPaths => pending.ToArray();
    public IReadOnlyDictionary<string,string> PendingReasons => pendingReasons;
    public string LatestMessage { get; private set; } = "今天也陪你一起，把事情一件件完成。";
    public bool IsPetVisible => Model.PetSettings.Enabled && !manuallyHidden && !blocked && !sessionLocked && !suspended;
    public int ActivePreviewCount => previews.Count;
    public int ActiveHeartbeatCount => heartbeat.IsEnabled ? 1 : 0;
    public string CurrentAnimation => DesktopWindow.SpriteView.Animation;
    private bool HasVisibleCompanion => !sessionLocked && !suspended && (IsPetVisible || previews.Count > 0);
    private bool ReducePetMotion => MotionPreferences.ShouldReducePetMotion(Model.PetSettings.MotionMode);
    public event EventHandler? Updated;
    public Action? OpenMainRequested { get; set; }
    public Action<string>? OpenPetPageRequested { get; set; }
    public Func<Task>? ExitRequested { get; set; }
    public Func<Window?>? MainWindowProvider { get; set; }
    public PetController(MainViewModel model)
    {
        Model = model;
        Account = new PetStateStore(model.SettingsDirectory);
        DesktopWindow = new DesktopPetWindow(() => ReducePetMotion);
        nextIdle = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(random.Next(15, 31));
        var manifest = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json");
        if (File.Exists(manifest)) DesktopWindow.LoadSprites(manifest);
        DesktopWindow.FilesDropped += ReceiveFiles;
        DesktopWindow.InteractionRequested += Interact;
        DesktopWindow.PresentationChanged += DesktopPresentationChanged;
        DesktopWindow.WalkProgressChanged += DesktopWalkProgressChanged;
        DesktopWindow.PositionChanged += p =>
        {
            var saved=Model.PetSettings;
            if (saved.Left is { } x && saved.Top is { } y && Math.Abs(x-p.X)<.5 && Math.Abs(y-p.Y)<.5 && saved.MonitorDeviceName==p.MonitorId) return;
            saved.Left=p.X; saved.Top=p.Y; saved.MonitorDeviceName=p.MonitorId; Model.UpdatePetSettings(saved);
        };
        Model.PropertyChanged += ModelChanged;
        Model.ProcessingTaskChanged += TaskChanged;
        Model.ProcessingBatchFinished += BatchFinished;
        Account.StateChanged += AccountChanged;
        MotionPreferences.Changed += MotionChanged;
        heartbeat.Tick += Tick;
        SystemEvents.SessionSwitch += SessionChanged;
        SystemEvents.PowerModeChanged += PowerChanged;
        ApplySettings(true);
        AccountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    /// <summary>A visible in-app view joins the existing pet; it never owns an account or a second work timer.</summary>
    public void RegisterPreview(PetSpriteView preview)
    {
        if (disposed || !previews.Add(preview)) return;
        var manifest = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Assets", "Pet", "animations.json");
        if (!preview.HasSpriteAtlas && !preview.TryShareLoadedSpritesFrom(DesktopWindow.SpriteView) && File.Exists(manifest)) preview.LoadManifest(manifest);
        SyncPreviews(configureMotion: true);
        UpdateHeartbeat();
        SyncAvailability();
        SyncAnimation();
    }
    public void UnregisterPreview(PetSpriteView preview)
    {
        if (!previews.Remove(preview)) return;
        preview.SetMotionEnabled(false);
        UpdateHeartbeat();
        if (!disposed) SyncAvailability();
        if (!HasVisibleCompanion && !disposed) Account.Flush();
    }
    private void UpdateHeartbeat()
    {
        if (!disposed && (HasVisibleCompanion || Account.Snapshot.WorkRequested || Account.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards)) heartbeat.Start(); else heartbeat.Stop();
    }
    private void DesktopPresentationChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        SyncPreviews();
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    private void DesktopWalkProgressChanged(double distanceDips, bool completing)
    {
        foreach (var preview in previews)
        {
            if (preview.Animation != "walk") continue;
            preview.UpdateWalkDistance(distanceDips);
            if (completing) preview.CompleteWalk(() => { });
        }
    }
    private void SyncPreviews(bool configureMotion = false, bool restart = false)
    {
        var account = Account.Snapshot;
        foreach (var preview in previews)
        {
            // Updating frame-timer options on every account heartbeat can postpone a
            // long idle frame indefinitely. Configure clocks only when policy changes.
            if (configureMotion)
            {
                preview.SetMotionMode(Model.PetSettings.MotionMode);
                preview.SetMotionEnabled(!ReducePetMotion && !sessionLocked && !suspended);
            }
            preview.SetAccessories(account.EquippedAccessories.ContainsKey(PetAccessorySlot.Neck), account.EquippedAccessories.ContainsKey(PetAccessorySlot.Head));
            var mirrored = DesktopWindow.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0;
            if ((preview.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0) != mirrored)
            {
                preview.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(.5, .5);
                preview.RenderTransform = mirrored ? new ScaleTransform(-1, 1) : Transform.Identity;
            }
            preview.SetAnimation(CurrentAnimation, restart);
            if (CurrentAnimation == "walk" && DesktopWindow.IsWalkDistanceBound)
                preview.UpdateWalkDistance(DesktopWindow.WalkDistanceDips);
        }
    }
    /// <summary>Startup greetings must never displace active work, a result, or an existing interaction.</summary>
    public bool TryStartupGreeting(string message)
    {
        if (disposed || !Model.CanEdit || Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning || Account.Snapshot.LastError is not null ||
            DesktopWindow.IsInteracting || DateTime.UtcNow < transientUntil || DateTime.UtcNow < resultNoticeUntil) return false;
        Animate("happy", message);
        return true;
    }
    public void ChangeSettings(Action<PetSettings> change)
    {
        var wasEnabled=Model.PetSettings.Enabled;
        change(Model.PetSettings);
        Model.UpdatePetSettings(Model.PetSettings);
        if (!wasEnabled && Model.PetSettings.Enabled) manuallyHidden = false;
        ApplySettings();
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    private void ModelChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(MainViewModel.PetSettings)) ApplySettings();
        if (e.PropertyName is nameof(MainViewModel.IsProcessing) or nameof(MainViewModel.IsImporting))
        {
            SyncAvailability();
            SyncAnimation();
            if (Model.CanEdit && pending.Count > 0 && IsPetVisible && DateTime.UtcNow >= resultNoticeUntil)
                DesktopWindow.ShowMessage($"有 {pending.Count} 个文件等你选择处理方式。", () => ChoosePendingFiles(), "选择功能");
            Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
        }
    }
    private void MotionChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e) { SyncAnimation(); SyncPreviews(configureMotion: true); }
    private void AccountChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        UpdateHeartbeat();
        SyncAnimation();
        // The platform view owns accessory placement for each frame.
        DesktopWindow.SpriteView.SetAccessories(Account.Snapshot.EquippedAccessories.ContainsKey(PetAccessorySlot.Neck), Account.Snapshot.EquippedAccessories.ContainsKey(PetAccessorySlot.Head));
        SyncPreviews();
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    public void ApplySettings(bool initial = false)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        var settings = Model.PetSettings;
        DesktopWindow.SetSize(settings.Size);
        DesktopWindow.Configure(settings.Topmost);
        DesktopWindow.SetMotionMode(settings.MotionMode);
        if (!settings.Roaming || ReducePetMotion) DesktopWindow.StopWalk();
        if (initial)
        {
            if (settings.Left is { } x && settings.Top is { } y) DesktopWindow.SetPosition(x, y);
            else
            {
                var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().FirstOrDefault(m => m.Primary) ?? DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First();
                DesktopWindow.SetPosition(monitor.WorkArea.Right - settings.Size * monitor.DpiScaleX - 32, monitor.WorkArea.Bottom - settings.Size * monitor.DpiScaleY - 16);
            }
        }
        if (settings.Enabled && environment is null)
            environment = new DesktopEnvironment(SetBlocked, () => Model.PetSettings.GameExecutables, () => Model.PetSettings.HideForFullscreen);
        if (!settings.Enabled && environment is not null)
        { environment.Dispose(); environment = null; blocked = false; }
        environment?.Refresh();
        UpdateVisibility();
    }
    private void SetBlocked(bool value)
    {
        if (disposed || value == blocked) return;
        blocked = value;
        UpdateVisibility();
        if (!blocked && IsPetVisible) ShowDeferredSummary();
    }
    public void ToggleVisibility()
    {
        if (!Model.PetSettings.Enabled) { ChangeSettings(s => s.Enabled = true); return; }
        manuallyHidden = !manuallyHidden;
        UpdateVisibility();
        if (IsPetVisible) ShowDeferredSummary();
    }
    public void HideTemporarily() { manuallyHidden = true; UpdateVisibility(); }
    private void UpdateVisibility()
    {
        var visible = IsPetVisible;
        DesktopWindow.SetEnvironmentVisible(visible);
        UpdateHeartbeat();
        SyncAvailability();
        SyncAnimation();
        SyncPreviews(configureMotion: true);
        if (!HasVisibleCompanion) Account.Flush();
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    private void SyncAvailability() => Account.SetSystemPaused(sessionLocked || suspended);
    private void SessionChanged(object sender, SessionSwitchEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.Reason is SessionSwitchReason.SessionLock or SessionSwitchReason.ConsoleDisconnect or SessionSwitchReason.RemoteDisconnect)
            DispatchSystemState(() => sessionLocked = true);
        else if (e.Reason is SessionSwitchReason.SessionUnlock or SessionSwitchReason.ConsoleConnect or SessionSwitchReason.RemoteConnect)
            DispatchSystemState(() => sessionLocked = false);
    }
    private void PowerChanged(object sender, PowerModeChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.Mode == PowerModes.Suspend) DispatchSystemState(() => suspended = true);
        else if (e.Mode == PowerModes.Resume) DispatchSystemState(() => suspended = false);
    }
    private void DispatchSystemState(Action update)
    {
        if (disposed || DesktopWindow.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) return;
        DesktopWindow.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => { if (disposed) return; update(); SyncAvailability(); UpdateVisibility(); });
    }
    private void Tick(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        SyncAvailability();
        var settlement = Account.Tick();
        if (settlement.Succeeded && settlement.WorkSession is { } summary) CelebrateWork(summary);
        if (!HasVisibleCompanion) return;
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        if (Model.CanEdit && !DesktopWindow.IsInteracting && !Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && now >= nextWake)
        { lastInteraction=now; nextWake=now.AddMinutes(random.Next(7,13)); nextRoam=now.AddSeconds(random.Next(90,111)); nextIdle=now.AddSeconds(random.Next(15,31)); }
        if (Model.CanEdit && !DesktopWindow.IsInteracting && !Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning && !ReducePetMotion && now - lastInteraction < TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2))
        {
            if (IsPetVisible && Model.PetSettings.Roaming && now >= nextRoam && now - lastInteraction < TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2))
            { DesktopWindow.TryWalk(random.Next(40, 121) * (random.Next(2) == 0 ? -1 : 1)); nextRoam = now.AddSeconds(random.Next(90, 181)); }
            if (!DesktopWindow.IsWalking && now >= nextIdle && now >= transientUntil)
            { transient = random.Next(2) == 0 ? "groom" : "stretch"; transientUntil = now.AddSeconds(2); nextIdle = now.AddSeconds(random.Next(15, 31)); }
        }
        SyncAnimation();
    }
    private void SyncAnimation()
        => ApplyAnimation(restart: false);

    private void ApplyAnimation(bool restart)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        if (DesktopWindow.IsInteracting) { SyncPreviews(); return; }
        var state = !Model.CanEdit ? "work" : DateTime.UtcNow < transientUntil ? transient : Account.Snapshot.WorkRunning ? "work" : DateTime.UtcNow - lastInteraction > TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2) ? "sleep" : "idle";
        DesktopWindow.SetAnimation(state, restart);
        SyncPreviews(restart: restart);
    }
    private void TaskChanged(object? sender, ProcessingTaskSnapshot task)
    {
        if (task.Status != JobStatus.Processing) return;
        var percent = task.Percent.HasValue ? $" · {Math.Clamp(task.Percent.Value, 0, 99):0}%" : " · 正在处理";
        LatestMessage = $"{task.DisplayName}\n{task.Stage ?? "处理中"} · {task.Index}/{task.Count}{percent}";
        if (IsPetVisible) DesktopWindow.ShowMessage(LatestMessage);
        DesktopWindow.SetAnimation("work");
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    private void BatchFinished(object? sender, ProcessingBatchSummary summary)
    {
        var settlement = Account.SettleBatch(summary.BatchId, summary.SuccessfulRequestIds);
        if (!notified.Add(summary.BatchId)) return;
        transient = summary.FailedCount > 0 ? "error" : summary.SuccessfulRequestIds.Count > 0 ? "success" : "idle";
        transientUntil = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(3);
        resultNoticeUntil = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(10);
        var message = $"完成 {summary.SuccessfulRequestIds.Count} 项" + (summary.ExhaustedCount > 0 ? $" · {summary.ExhaustedCount} 项暂未找到密码" : "") + (summary.FailedCount > 0 ? $" · {summary.FailedCount} 项遇到问题" : "") + (summary.Cancelled ? " · 已停止" : "") + (settlement.Succeeded && settlement.CoinsChanged > 0 ? $"\n获得 {settlement.CoinsChanged} 喵喵币" : !settlement.Succeeded ? "\n奖励待保存，可在桌宠页重试。" : "");
        LatestMessage = message;
        SyncAnimation();
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
        // This preference controls desktop bubbles; the shared companion still
        // needs the real result so home cannot retain a stale processing message.
        if (!Model.PetSettings.CompletionNotifications)
        {
            DesktopWindow.DismissMessage();
            return;
        }
        if (!IsPetVisible)
        {
            deferredCompleted += summary.SuccessfulRequestIds.Count;
            deferredFailed += summary.FailedCount;
            deferredStopped += summary.Cancelled ? Math.Max(1,summary.CancelledCount) : 0;
            deferredNotFound += summary.ExhaustedCount;
            return;
        }
        var output = summary.Tasks.LastOrDefault(r => r.Status == JobStatus.Completed && r.OutputPath is not null);
        DesktopWindow.ShowMessage(message, output is not null ? () => OpenResult(output.OutputPath!, output.OutputIsDirectory) : () => OpenMainRequested?.Invoke(), output is not null ? "打开结果" : "查看任务");
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    private void ShowDeferredSummary()
    {
        if (!Model.PetSettings.CompletionNotifications)
        {
            deferredCompleted = deferredFailed = deferredStopped = deferredNotFound = 0;
            return;
        }
        if (deferredCompleted + deferredFailed + deferredStopped + deferredNotFound == 0) return;
        var message = $"刚才完成 {deferredCompleted} 项" + (deferredFailed > 0 ? $" · {deferredFailed} 项遇到问题" : "") + (deferredNotFound > 0 ? $" · {deferredNotFound} 项暂未找到密码" : "") + (deferredStopped > 0 ? " · 有任务已停止" : "");
        deferredCompleted = deferredFailed = deferredStopped = deferredNotFound = 0;
        resultNoticeUntil = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(10);
        LatestMessage = message;
        DesktopWindow.ShowMessage(message, () => OpenMainRequested?.Invoke(), "查看任务");
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    public void ReceiveFiles(IReadOnlyList<string> paths)
    {
        foreach (var path in paths)
        {
            try { var fullPath=Path.GetFullPath(path); pending.Add(fullPath); if(!File.Exists(fullPath)&&!Directory.Exists(fullPath)) pendingReasons[fullPath]="文件已移动或无法读取，请重新选择。"; } catch (Exception e) when (e is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or PathTooLongException) { }
        }
        lastInteraction = DateTime.UtcNow;
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
        if (Model.CanEdit && !choosing && IsPetVisible) ChoosePendingFiles();
        else if (IsPetVisible) DesktopWindow.ShowMessage($"已接住 {pending.Count} 个文件，当前任务结束后再选功能。", () => OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("files"), "查看待处理");
    }
    public void ClearPendingFiles() { pending.Clear(); pendingReasons.Clear(); Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty); }
    public void SelectFiles(bool folders)
    {
        OpenMainRequested?.Invoke();
        var owner = MainWindowProvider?.Invoke();
        if (folders)
        { var dialog = new OpenFolderDialog { Title = Locale.T("把文件夹交给猫咪"), Multiselect = true }; if (dialog.ShowDialog(owner) == true) ReceiveFiles(dialog.FolderNames); }
        else
        { var dialog = new OpenFileDialog { Title = Locale.T("把文件交给猫咪，再选择处理方式"), Multiselect = true, Filter = Locale.T("所有文件|*.*") }; if (dialog.ShowDialog(owner) == true) ReceiveFiles(dialog.FileNames); }
    }
    public async void ChoosePendingFiles()
    {
        if (choosing || pending.Count == 0) { if (pending.Count == 0) SelectFiles(false); return; }
        if (!Model.CanEdit) { OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("files"); return; }
        choosing = true;
        try
        {
            OpenMainRequested?.Invoke();
            var owner = MainWindowProvider?.Invoke();
            var choices = Model.GetFileActionChoices(pending);
            var selected = FileActionPicker.Show(MainWindowProvider?.Invoke(), choices);
            if (selected is null) return;
            var result = await Model.ImportForActionAsync(selected.Value, pending.ToArray());
            foreach (var path in result.AcceptedPaths) { pending.Remove(path); pendingReasons.Remove(path); }
            foreach (var rejected in result.RejectedPaths) pendingReasons[rejected.SourcePath]=rejected.Reason;
            // Existing queue duplicates count as accepted by the bridge; rejected files remain selectable.
            Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            if (pending.Count > 0) DesktopWindow.ShowMessage($"还有 {pending.Count} 项待选择或检查，可在桌宠页查看。", () => OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("files"), "查看");
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Model.HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
        finally { choosing = false; }
    }
    public void Interact(string action)
    {
        lastInteraction = DateTime.UtcNow;
        nextIdle = lastInteraction.AddSeconds(random.Next(15, 31));
        switch (action)
        {
            case "process": ChoosePendingFiles(); return;
            case "choose-files": SelectFiles(false); return;
            case "choose-folder": SelectFiles(true); return;
            case "open": OpenMainRequested?.Invoke(); return;
            case "settings": OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("settings"); return;
            case "shop": OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("shop"); return;
            case "hide": HideTemporarily(); return;
            case "exit": _ = ExitRequested?.Invoke(); return;
            case "feed": OpenPetPageRequested?.Invoke("inventory"); return;
            case "work": Explain(Account.StartWork()); return;
            case "stop-work": StopWork(); return;
            case "play": Animate("play", "陪你玩一小会儿，喵～"); return;
            case "pet-head":
                // Draw once per petting, excluding the last reply without a retry loop.
                var headReply = random.Next(headReplies.Length - (lastHeadReply < 0 ? 0 : 1));
                if (lastHeadReply >= 0 && headReply >= lastHeadReply) headReply++;
                lastHeadReply = headReply;
                Animate("happy", headReplies[headReply]); return;
            default:
                var replies = new[] { "今天也陪你一起把文件整理好。", "累了就休息一下，我在这里。", "喵～有文件需要我帮忙吗？", "接住今天的小小好心情！", "你来了，我的尾巴都开心起来啦。", "慢慢来，事情会一件件完成的。" };
                int next; do { next = random.Next(replies.Length); } while (next == lastReply); lastReply = next;
                Animate("happy", replies[next]); return;
        }
    }
    public void Animate(string state, string message)
    {
        LatestMessage=message;
        if (!Model.CanEdit) { DesktopWindow.ShowMessage("先陪你把文件处理好，稍后再玩～"); return; }
        // Only explicit interactions restart a pose; heartbeat/account synchronization remains idempotent.
        transient = state; transientUntil = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(3); SyncAvailability(); ApplyAnimation(restart: true);
        if (IsPetVisible) DesktopWindow.ShowMessage(message);
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    public static string WorkDurationText(TimeSpan duration) => $"{(long)duration.TotalHours:00}:{duration.Minutes:00}:{duration.Seconds:00}";
    public PetTransactionResult StopWork()
    {
        var active = Account.Snapshot.WorkRequested;
        var result = Account.StopWork();
        if (result.Succeeded && active && result.WorkSession is { } summary) CelebrateWork(summary);
        else if (!result.Succeeded) Explain(result);
        return result;
    }
    private void CelebrateWork(PetWorkSessionSummary summary)
    {
        if (summary.SessionId == lastCelebratedWork) return;
        lastCelebratedWork = summary.SessionId;
        var replies = new[] { "太棒了喵！这次赚了 {0} 喵喵币！", "辛苦啦喵！{0} 喵喵币收入囊中！", "收工啦喵！一起赚到了 {0} 喵喵币！" };
        int next; do { next = random.Next(replies.Length); } while (next == lastWorkReply);
        lastWorkReply = next;
        var celebration = string.Format(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Locale.T(replies[next]), summary.CoinsEarned.ToString("0.##"));
        LatestMessage = Locale.F($"本次工作了 {WorkDurationText(summary.Duration)}。") + "\n" + celebration;
        transient = "success"; transientUntil = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(4);
        SyncAnimation();
        if (IsPetVisible) DesktopWindow.ShowMessage(LatestMessage);
        Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    /// <summary>The application calls this before committing to a graceful exit.</summary>
    public PetTransactionResult SettleWorkForExit() => Account.StopWork();
    public void Explain(PetTransactionResult result)
    {
        if (result.Succeeded && result.WorkSession is { } summary && !Account.Snapshot.WorkRequested)
        { CelebrateWork(summary); return; }
        LatestMessage=Locale.TranslateText(result.Message); if (IsPetVisible) DesktopWindow.ShowMessage(LatestMessage); Updated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    public static void OpenResult(string path, bool directory)
    {
        try { var target = directory ? path : Path.GetDirectoryName(path); if (target is not null && Directory.Exists(target)) Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(target) { UseShellExecute = true }); } catch (Exception e) { Trace.TraceWarning("Open pet result: {0}", e.Message); }
    }
    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        Account.StopWork(); Account.Flush();
        disposed = true; heartbeat.Stop(); heartbeat.Tick -= Tick;
        foreach (var preview in previews) preview.SetMotionEnabled(false);
        previews.Clear();
        environment?.Dispose();
        SystemEvents.SessionSwitch -= SessionChanged;
        SystemEvents.PowerModeChanged -= PowerChanged;
        Model.PropertyChanged -= ModelChanged; Model.ProcessingTaskChanged -= TaskChanged; Model.ProcessingBatchFinished -= BatchFinished;
        Account.StateChanged -= AccountChanged; MotionPreferences.Changed -= MotionChanged;
        DesktopWindow.PresentationChanged -= DesktopPresentationChanged;
        DesktopWindow.WalkProgressChanged -= DesktopWalkProgressChanged;
        DesktopWindow.Dispose(); pending.Clear();
    }
}

using System;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>A separate companionship page, never changes the active file operation.</summary>
public sealed class PetPage : UserControl, IDisposable
{
    private readonly PetController controller;
    private readonly TextBlock balance = new(), workStatus = new(), pendingStatus = new(), errorStatus = new(), interactionStatus = new();
    private readonly StackPanel content = new();
    private readonly List<Action> refreshers = new();
    private string tab = "companion";
    private readonly Dictionary<string, Button> tabs = new();
    private readonly DockPanel root;
    private readonly TextBlock subtitle, greetingTitle, greetingHint;
    private readonly Grid hero;
    private readonly Image heroImage;
    private readonly StackPanel greeting;
    private readonly Border heroCard;
    private readonly WrapPanel navigation;
    private readonly List<Border> itemCards = new();
    private Grid? itemsGrid;
    private bool? compactHeader;
    public PetPage(PetController controller)
    {
        this.controller = controller;
        ThemeManager.EnsureInitialized();
        SetResourceReference(FontFamilyProperty, "UiFont");
        SetResourceReference(FontSizeProperty, "BodySize");
        SetResourceReference(ForegroundProperty, "Text");
        root = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(25,24,25,20) };
        var header = new StackPanel(); DockPanel.SetDock(header, Dock.Top);
        header.Children.Add(Label("桌面上的小小陪伴", 25, true));
        subtitle = Label("把文件交给我，也留一点时间给自己。", 12, muted: true);
        header.Children.Add(subtitle);
        hero = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(0,20,0,16) };
        hero.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(120) }); hero.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition());
        heroImage = new Image { Width = 112, Height = 112, Stretch = Stretch.Uniform };
        // The desktop player already owns a frozen idle drawing backed by the shared atlas.
        // A static header should not decode the full PNG again or allocate an animation player.
        heroImage.Source = controller.DesktopWindow.SpriteView.IdleImageSource;
        RenderOptions.SetBitmapScalingMode(heroImage, BitmapScalingMode.HighQuality);
        hero.Children.Add(heroImage);
        greeting = new StackPanel { VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, Margin = new Thickness(16,0,0,0) }; Grid.SetColumn(greeting,1);
        greetingTitle = Label("今天也一起慢慢完成吧。",18,true); greeting.Children.Add(greetingTitle);
        balance.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "NumericText"); balance.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "Accent"); balance.Margin = new Thickness(0,8,0,0); greeting.Children.Add(balance);
        greetingHint = Label("没有饥饿惩罚，离开多久都欢迎你回来。",12,muted:true); greeting.Children.Add(greetingHint);
        interactionStatus.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty,"CaptionText"); interactionStatus.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty,"Accent"); interactionStatus.TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap; greeting.Children.Add(interactionStatus);
        hero.Children.Add(greeting); heroCard = Card(hero); header.Children.Add(heroCard);
        navigation = new WrapPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0,0,0,16) };
        foreach (var (key,title) in new[] { ("companion","陪伴互动"),("files","待处理文件"),("shop","喵喵商店"),("inventory","我的背包"),("settings","桌宠设置") })
        { var b = Button(title,()=>SelectTab(key)); b.Margin = new Thickness(0,0,7,7); tabs.Add(key,b); navigation.Children.Add(b); }
        header.Children.Add(navigation); root.Children.Add(header);
        root.Children.Add(new ScrollViewer { Content = content, VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto, HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled });
        Content = root;
        Loaded += (_, _) => ApplyResponsiveLayout();
        SizeChanged += (_, _) => ApplyResponsiveLayout();
        controller.Updated += Changed;
        Locale.Changed += LanguageChanged;
        SelectTab("companion");
    }
    private void ApplyResponsiveLayout()
    {
        if (ActualWidth <= 0 || ActualHeight <= 0) return;
        var compact = ActualHeight < 620;
        if (compactHeader != compact)
        {
            compactHeader = compact;
            root.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(16,12,16,12) : new Thickness(25,24,25,20);
            subtitle.Visibility = greetingHint.Visibility = interactionStatus.Visibility = compact ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
            hero.ColumnDefinitions[0].Width = new GridLength(compact ? 48 : 120);
            heroImage.Width = heroImage.Height = compact ? 48 : 112;
            hero.Margin = compact ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0,20,0,16);
            greeting.Margin = new Thickness(compact ? 8 : 16,0,0,0);
            greetingTitle.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, compact ? "BodySize" : "SectionSize");
            greetingTitle.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.LineHeightProperty, compact ? "BodyLineHeight" : "SectionLineHeight");
            greetingTitle.Margin = new Thickness(0,0,0,compact ? 2 : 7);
            balance.Margin = new Thickness(0,compact ? 0 : 8,0,0);
            if (compact) heroCard.Padding = new Thickness(12);
            else heroCard.ClearValue(Border.PaddingProperty);
            heroCard.Margin = new Thickness(0,0,0,compact ? 8 : 13);
            navigation.Margin = new Thickness(0,0,0,compact ? 8 : 16);
        }
        ArrangeItems();
    }
    private void ArrangeItems()
    {
        if (itemsGrid is null) return;
        var availableWidth = ActualWidth - root.Margin.Left - root.Margin.Right - 10;
        var columns = availableWidth > 0 && availableWidth < 640 ? 1 : 2;
        if (itemsGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Count == columns && itemsGrid.RowDefinitions.Count > 0) return;
        itemsGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Clear(); itemsGrid.RowDefinitions.Clear();
        for (var column = 0; column < columns; column++) itemsGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition());
        for (var row = 0; row < (itemCards.Count + columns - 1) / columns; row++)
            itemsGrid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        for (var index = 0; index < itemCards.Count; index++)
        {
            var card = itemCards[index];
            Grid.SetRow(card, index / columns); Grid.SetColumn(card, index % columns);
            card.Margin = columns == 1 ? new Thickness(0,0,0,14) : new Thickness(index % 2 == 0 ? 0 : 7,0,index % 2 == 0 ? 7 : 0,14);
        }
    }
    private static TextBlock Label(string text,double size=14,bool bold=false,bool muted=false)
    {
        var label = new TextBlock { Text=text, FontWeight=bold?FontWeights.SemiBold:FontWeights.Normal,
            TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin=new Thickness(0,0,0,7) };
        var level = size >= 24 ? "Title" : size >= 17 ? "Section" : size < 14 ? "Caption" : "Body";
        label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, level+"Size");
        label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.LineHeightProperty, level+"LineHeight");
        label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, muted?"Muted":"Text");
        return label;
    }
    private static Border Card(UIElement child)
    {
        var card = new Border { Child=child, Margin=new Thickness(0,0,0,13) };
        card.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "Card");
        return card;
    }
    private static Button Button(string title,Action action,bool primary=false)
    { var button = new Button { Content=title, Tag=title }; button.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty,primary?"PrimaryButton":"SoftButton"); button.Click+=(_,_)=>action(); return button; }
    private void Changed(object? sender,EventArgs e) => Refresh();
    private void LanguageChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        if (!Dispatcher.CheckAccess()) { Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(() => LanguageChanged(sender, e))); return; }
        RefreshTabLabels(); Refresh();
    }
    private void RefreshTabLabels()
    {
        foreach (var pair in tabs)
        {
            var selected = pair.Key == tab;
            pair.Value.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, selected ? "PrimaryButton" : "SoftButton");
            pair.Value.Content = (selected ? "✓ " : "") + Locale.T(pair.Value.Tag?.ToString() ?? "");
        }
    }
    public void SelectTab(string name)
    {
        foreach (var text in new[] {workStatus,pendingStatus,errorStatus}) if (text.Parent is Panel oldParent) oldParent.Children.Remove(text);
        tab = tabs.ContainsKey(name)?name:"companion";
        content.Children.Clear(); refreshers.Clear(); itemsGrid = null; itemCards.Clear();
        RefreshTabLabels();
        switch(tab) { case "shop": BuildItems(false); break; case "inventory": BuildItems(true); break; case "settings": BuildSettings(); break; case "files": BuildFiles(); break; default: BuildCompanion(); break; }
        Refresh();
    }
    private void BuildCompanion()
    {
        var play = new StackPanel(); play.Children.Add(Label("和猫咪待一会儿",18,true)); play.Children.Add(Label("摸摸头，玩一下。每个小动作都有回应。",12,muted:true));
        var row = new WrapPanel();
        foreach(var (title,action) in new[] { ("摸摸头","pet-head"),("打个招呼","pet-body"),("一起玩耍","play"),("喂点好吃的","feed") }) { var button=Button(title,()=>controller.Interact(action)); button.Margin=new Thickness(0,6,8,0); row.Children.Add(button); }
        play.Children.Add(row); content.Children.Add(Card(play));
        var work=new StackPanel(); work.Children.Add(Label("猫咪的轻松工作",18,true)); work.Children.Add(Label("每分钟 4 喵喵币，按秒累计并保留两位小数。最小化、隐藏和处理文件时继续；锁屏或休眠时暂停。",12,muted:true));
        workStatus.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty,"BodySize"); workStatus.Margin=new Thickness(0,8,0,10); work.Children.Add(workStatus);
        var controls=new WrapPanel();

        work.Children.Add(controls); content.Children.Add(Card(work));

    }
    private void BuildFiles()
    {
        var files=new StackPanel(); files.Children.Add(Label("接住的文件",18,true)); pendingStatus.TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap; pendingStatus.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.LineHeightProperty,"BodyLineHeight"); files.Children.Add(pendingStatus);
        var row=new WrapPanel();
        foreach(var (title,action) in new (string,Action)[] { ("选择文件",()=>controller.SelectFiles(false)),("选择文件夹",()=>controller.SelectFiles(true)),("选择处理功能",()=>controller.ChoosePendingFiles()),("清空待选",()=>controller.ClearPendingFiles()) }) { var b=Button(title,action); b.Margin=new Thickness(0,12,8,0); row.Children.Add(b); }
        files.Children.Add(row); files.Children.Add(Label("文件内容不会保存到桌宠存档；正式退出时清空待选列表。",11,muted:true)); content.Children.Add(Card(files));
    }
    private void BuildItems(bool inventory)
    {
        var grid=new Grid();
        foreach(var item in PetShopCatalog.Items)
        {
            var current=item;
            var body=new StackPanel();
            var title=new StackPanel {Orientation=Orientation.Horizontal}; title.Children.Add(new PetShopIcon {Item=item.Id,Width=24,Height=24,Margin=new Thickness(0,0,8,5)}); title.Children.Add(Label(item.Title,17,true));body.Children.Add(title);
            body.Children.Add(Label(item.Description,11,muted:true));
            var stock=Label("",12); body.Children.Add(stock);
            var button=Button(inventory?"使用":"购买",()=>
            {
                PetTransactionResult result;
                if(!inventory) result=controller.Account.Buy(current.Id);
                else if(current.Consumable) { if(!controller.Model.CanEdit) { controller.Animate("feed",""); return; } result=controller.Account.Feed(current.Id); if(result.Succeeded)controller.Animate(current.Id==PetShopItemId.Fish?"feed":"feed-canned",current.Id==PetShopItemId.Fish?"小鱼干真香，喵～":"开罐头啦，今天好幸福。"); }
                else if(current.Kind==PetShopItemKind.Toy) { controller.Animate(current.Id==PetShopItemId.Yarn?"play":"play-wand",current.Id==PetShopItemId.Yarn?"追到毛线球啦！":"小羽毛，别跑～"); return; }
                else result=controller.Account.Snapshot.IsEquipped(current.Id)?controller.Account.Unequip(current.AccessorySlot!.Value):controller.Account.Equip(current.Id);
                controller.Explain(result);
            },!inventory);
            body.Children.Add(button);
            refreshers.Add(()=>
            {
                var state=controller.Account.Snapshot; var quantity=state.Quantity(current.Id);
                stock.Text=inventory?(quantity>0?$"已拥有{(current.Consumable?$" · {quantity} 份":"")}":"还没放进背包"):$"{current.Price} 喵喵币";
                button.Content=inventory?(current.Consumable?"喂食":current.Kind==PetShopItemKind.Toy?"一起玩":state.IsEquipped(current.Id)?"卸下":"装备") : !current.Consumable&&quantity>0?"已拥有":"购买";
                button.IsEnabled=inventory?quantity>0&&!state.HasPendingRewards:state.Coins>=current.Price&&!state.HasPendingRewards&&(current.Consumable||quantity==0);
            });
            var card=Card(body); itemCards.Add(card); grid.Children.Add(card);
        }
        itemsGrid = grid; ArrangeItems(); content.Children.Add(grid); BuildError();
    }
    private void BuildError()
    {
        errorStatus.TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap; errorStatus.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty,"Danger"); errorStatus.Margin=new Thickness(0,12,0,10); content.Children.Add(errorStatus);
        var retry=Button("重试保存",()=>controller.Explain(controller.Account.RetryPendingRewards())); content.Children.Add(retry); refreshers.Add(()=>retry.Visibility=controller.Account.Snapshot.HasPendingRewards||controller.Account.Snapshot.LastError is not null?Visibility.Visible:Visibility.Collapsed);
    }
    private void BuildSettings()
    {
        var settings=controller.Model.PetSettings;
        var body=new StackPanel();body.Children.Add(Label("桌宠设置",18,true));
        void Toggle(string title,bool value,Action<bool> change) { var box=new CheckBox { Content=title,IsChecked=value,Margin=new Thickness(0,7,0,10) }; box.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty,"Switch"); box.Click+=(_,_)=>controller.ChangeSettings(s=>change(box.IsChecked==true));body.Children.Add(box); }
        Toggle("启用桌面猫咪",settings.Enabled,v=>controller.Model.PetSettings.Enabled=v);
        Toggle("偶尔在桌面走动",settings.Roaming,v=>controller.Model.PetSettings.Roaming=v);
        Toggle("猫咪显示在其他窗口上方",settings.Topmost,v=>controller.Model.PetSettings.Topmost=v);
        Toggle("完成后显示安静气泡",settings.CompletionNotifications,v=>controller.Model.PetSettings.CompletionNotifications=v);
        Toggle("全屏、演示及指定游戏时自动隐藏",settings.HideForFullscreen,v=>controller.Model.PetSettings.HideForFullscreen=v);
        var size=new ComboBox { ItemsSource=new[] {120,160,200},SelectedItem=settings.Size,Width=140,HorizontalAlignment=HorizontalAlignment.Left,Margin=new Thickness(0,6,0,15) }; size.SelectionChanged+=(_,_)=> { if(size.SelectedItem is int value)controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.Size=value); };
        body.Children.Add(Label("猫咪大小",12));body.Children.Add(size);
        var motion=new ComboBox { ItemsSource=new[] { "持续轻柔","跟随 Windows","减少动效" },SelectedIndex=(int)settings.MotionMode,Width=180,HorizontalAlignment=HorizontalAlignment.Left };motion.SelectionChanged+=(_,_)=> { if(motion.SelectedIndex>=0)controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.MotionMode=(PetMotionMode)motion.SelectedIndex); };
        body.Children.Add(Label("桌宠动画偏好",12));body.Children.Add(motion);
        body.Children.Add(Label("持续轻柔会保持猫咪的呼吸与动作；跟随 Windows 按系统动画设置播放；减少动效保留静态表情与文字反馈。此选项只影响桌宠。",12,muted:true));content.Children.Add(Card(body));
        var games=new StackPanel();games.Children.Add(Label("窗口化游戏列表",18,true));games.Children.Add(Label("填写程序名（例如 game.exe），每行一个。该程序在前台时猫咪自动休息。",12,muted:true));
        var list=new TextBox { Text=string.Join(Environment.NewLine,settings.GameExecutables),AcceptsReturn=true,Height=90,VerticalScrollBarVisibility=ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,Padding=new Thickness(10),Margin=new Thickness(0,4,0,12) }; games.Children.Add(list);
        var row=new WrapPanel();row.Children.Add(Button("添加游戏程序",()=> { var picker=new OpenFileDialog { Filter=Locale.T("程序|*.exe"),Title=Locale.T("选择窗口化游戏") };if(picker.ShowDialog(Window.GetWindow(this))==true) list.Text+=(list.Text.Length>0?Environment.NewLine:"")+Path.GetFileName(picker.FileName); }));
        var save=Button("保存列表",()=>controller.ChangeSettings(s=>s.GameExecutables=list.Text.Split(new[] {'\r','\n'},StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Select(v=>v.Trim()).Where(v=>v.Length>0).ToList()),true);save.Margin=new Thickness(8,0,0,0);row.Children.Add(save);games.Children.Add(row);content.Children.Add(Card(games));
        content.Children.Add(Label("设置会自动保存在本机。关闭主窗口后继续陪伴；彻底退出请使用托盘菜单。",11,muted:true));
    }
    private void Refresh()
    {
        var state=controller.Account.Snapshot;
        balance.Text=Locale.F($"●  {state.Coins:0.##} 喵喵币");
        interactionStatus.Text=Locale.TranslateText(controller.LatestMessage);
        workStatus.Text=state.WorkRequested
            ? Locale.F($"{(state.WorkRunning ? Locale.T("工作中") : Locale.T("已暂停"))} · 已累计 {PetController.WorkDurationText(state.WorkElapsed)} · 本次 {state.WorkEarned:0.##} 喵喵币")
            : state.LastWorkSession is { } summary
                ? Locale.F($"上次工作 {PetController.WorkDurationText(summary.Duration)} · 获得 {summary.CoinsEarned:0.##} 喵喵币")
                : Locale.T("猫咪准备好了，随时开始一段轻松工作。");
        pendingStatus.Text=controller.PendingPaths.Count==0?"还没有待选文件，拖到猫咪身上或从这里添加。":string.Join(Environment.NewLine,controller.PendingPaths.Take(20).Select(path=>Path.GetFileName(path)+(controller.PendingReasons.TryGetValue(path,out var reason)?"\n  "+reason:"")))+ (controller.PendingPaths.Count>20?$"\n……共 {controller.PendingPaths.Count} 项":"")+(!controller.Model.CanEdit?"\n正在处理文件，结束后再选择功能。":"");
        errorStatus.Text=Locale.TranslateText(state.LastError??"");
        foreach(var refresh in refreshers)refresh();
    }
    public void Dispose() { controller.Updated-=Changed; Locale.Changed-=LanguageChanged; }
}
