using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Small local vector icons stay readable on Windows versions without newer emoji fonts.</summary>
internal sealed class PetShopIcon : FrameworkElement
{
    public PetShopItemId Item { get; set; }
    protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawing)
    {
        base.OnRender(drawing);
        var stroke=new Pen(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(99,102,180)),1.6) {StartLineCap=PenLineCap.Round,EndLineCap=PenLineCap.Round,LineJoin=PenLineJoin.Round};
        var fill=new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(229,228,252));
        var warm=new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(249,222,153));
        drawing.PushTransform(new ScaleTransform(ActualWidth/24,ActualHeight/24));
        switch(Item)
        {
            case PetShopItemId.Fish:
                drawing.DrawGeometry(fill,stroke,Geometry.Parse("M3,7 L8,12 L3,17 Z"));drawing.DrawEllipse(fill,stroke,new Point(14,12),7,5);drawing.DrawEllipse(stroke.Brush,null,new Point(17,11),1,1);break;
            case PetShopItemId.CannedFood:
                drawing.DrawRoundedRectangle(fill,stroke,new Rect(6,5,13,16),2,2);drawing.DrawEllipse(warm,stroke,new Point(12.5,5),6.5,2);drawing.DrawLine(stroke,new Point(7,10),new Point(18,10));drawing.DrawEllipse(warm,null,new Point(12,15),2.5,2.5);break;
            case PetShopItemId.Yarn:
                drawing.DrawEllipse(fill,stroke,new Point(11,11),8,8);drawing.DrawGeometry(null,stroke,Geometry.Parse("M5,7 Q10,12 17,13 M6,5 Q12,7 18,10 M4,11 Q9,15 16,17 M11,4 Q7,10 10,18 M18,16 Q22,22 17,22"));break;
            case PetShopItemId.Wand:
                drawing.DrawLine(stroke,new Point(4,22),new Point(14,4));drawing.DrawGeometry(warm,stroke,Geometry.Parse("M13,3 Q22,1 21,9 Q20,15 13,15 Q9,9 13,3 Z"));drawing.DrawLine(stroke,new Point(13,14),new Point(18,5));break;
            case PetShopItemId.Collar:
                drawing.DrawGeometry(warm,stroke,Geometry.Parse("M7,13 Q7,5 12,5 Q17,5 17,13 L20,17 L4,17 Z"));drawing.DrawEllipse(warm,stroke,new Point(12,20),2,2);break;
            case PetShopItemId.StarClip:
                drawing.DrawGeometry(warm,stroke,Geometry.Parse("M12,2 L15,8 L22,9 L17,14 L18,21 L12,18 L6,21 L7,14 L2,9 L9,8 Z"));break;
        }
        drawing.Pop();
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/PetSpriteAssets.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

// Clip definitions contain geometry only. Keeping a definition never keeps a decoded page alive.
internal sealed record PetAtlasPage(string Path, int Columns, int Rows, Size SourceSize, int Width, int Height, (int Top, int Height)[] RowBounds);
internal sealed record PetPose(PetAtlasPage Page, Int32Rect Bounds, Size Canvas, Rect Placement, Point Head, Point Neck, Geometry? Clip, double Milliseconds, string Contact, IReadOnlyList<Point> Feet, double HeadAngle = 0, double NeckAngle = 0, double AccessoryScale = 1, Geometry? AccessoryOcclusion = null);
internal sealed record PetSegment(IReadOnlyList<PetPose> Frames, bool Loop);
internal sealed record PetAction(string Name, PetSegment? Enter, PetSegment Loop, PetSegment? Exit, IReadOnlyList<int> ExitAt, double Baseline, double StrideLength, IReadOnlyList<double> ContactPhases)
{
    public IReadOnlyDictionary<int, PetSegment> ExitByMarker { get; init; } = new Dictionary<int, PetSegment>();
    public bool HasExit => Exit is not null || ExitByMarker.Count > 0;
    public PetSegment? ExitFor(int marker) => ExitByMarker.GetValueOrDefault(marker) ?? (ExitAt.Contains(marker) ? Exit : null);
    public IEnumerable<PetPose> AllFrames => (Enter?.Frames ?? Array.Empty<PetPose>()).Concat(Loop.Frames).Concat(Exit?.Frames ?? Array.Empty<PetPose>()).Concat(ExitByMarker.OrderBy(x => x.Key).SelectMany(x => x.Value.Frames));
}

internal sealed class PetSpriteAssets
{
    public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, PetAction> Actions { get; }
    public int Version { get; }
    private PetSpriteAssets(Dictionary<string, PetAction> actions, int version) { Actions = actions; Version = version; }

    public static PetSpriteAssets Load(string manifestPath)
    {
        var fullPath = Path.GetFullPath(manifestPath);
        using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(fullPath));
        var root = document.RootElement;
        var version = root.TryGetProperty("version", out var v) ? v.GetInt32() : 2;
        var pages = new List<PetAtlasPage>();
        foreach (var value in root.GetProperty("atlases").EnumerateArray())
        {
            var path = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(fullPath)!, value.GetProperty("file").GetString() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Missing sprite page.")));
            var columns = Math.Clamp(value.GetProperty("columns").GetInt32(), 1, 64);
            var rows = Math.Clamp(value.GetProperty("rows").GetInt32(), 1, 64);
            int width, height;
            using (var input = File.OpenRead(path))
            {
                var decoder = BitmapDecoder.Create(input, BitmapCreateOptions.DelayCreation, BitmapCacheOption.None);
                width = decoder.Frames[0].PixelWidth; height = decoder.Frames[0].PixelHeight;
            }
            if (width < columns || height < rows || width % columns != 0 || height % rows != 0) throw new InvalidDataException("Sprite page dimensions do not match its grid.");
            var source = SizeValue(value, "sourceSize", new Size(width, height));
            var ratio = Math.Min(1d, 256d / Math.Max(width / columns, height / rows));
            width = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(width / (double)columns * ratio)) * columns;
            height = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(height / (double)rows * ratio)) * rows;
            if (width * (long)height * 4 > PetTextureCache.LimitBytes) throw new InvalidDataException("A sprite page exceeds the decoded texture budget.");
            var boundaries = Enumerable.Range(0, rows).Select(row => (Top: row * height / rows, Height: height / rows)).ToArray();
            if (value.TryGetProperty("rowBounds", out var bounds))
            {
                if (bounds.GetArrayLength() != rows) throw new InvalidDataException("Each sprite row needs one boundary.");
                for (var i = 0; i < rows; i++)
                {
                    var top = bounds[i][0].GetDouble(); var size = bounds[i][1].GetDouble();
                    if (!double.IsFinite(top) || !double.IsFinite(size) || top < 0 || size <= 0 || top + size > 1.000001) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite row boundary.");
                    var start = Math.Clamp((int)Math.Round(top * height), 0, height - 1);
                    boundaries[i] = (start, Math.Clamp((int)Math.Round((top + size) * height), start + 1, height) - start);
                }
            }
            pages.Add(new PetAtlasPage(path, columns, rows, source, width, height, boundaries));
        }
        var actions = new Dictionary<string, PetAction>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var item in root.GetProperty("animations").EnumerateObject())
        {
            var definition = item.Value;
            var baseline = Number(definition, "baseline", .94);
            if (baseline < 0 || baseline > 1) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite baseline.");
            var defaults = DefaultAnchors(item.Name);
            var canvas = SizeValue(definition, "canvasSize", Size.Empty);
            PetSegment? enter = null, exit = null;
            var exits = new Dictionary<int, PetSegment>();
            PetSegment loop;
            if (definition.TryGetProperty("segments", out var segments))
            {
                if (segments.TryGetProperty("enter", out var e)) enter = ReadSegment(e, false);
                if (segments.TryGetProperty("exit", out var x)) exit = ReadSegment(x, false);
                if (segments.TryGetProperty("exitByMarker", out var variants))
                    foreach (var variant in variants.EnumerateObject())
                    {
                        if (!int.TryParse(variant.Name, out var marker) || marker < 0) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite exit marker.");
                        exits.Add(marker, ReadSegment(variant.Value, false));
                    }
                loop = segments.TryGetProperty("loop", out var l) ? ReadSegment(l, !definition.TryGetProperty("loop", out var loops) || loops.GetBoolean()) : throw new InvalidDataException("Missing sprite loop segment.");
            }
            else loop = ReadLegacy();
            var exitAt = definition.TryGetProperty("exitAt", out var markers) ? markers.EnumerateArray().Select(x => x.GetInt32()).Distinct().OrderBy(x => x).ToArray() : exits.Count > 0 ? exits.Keys.OrderBy(x => x).ToArray() : new[] { loop.Loop ? 0 : loop.Frames.Count - 1 };
            if (exitAt.Any(x => x < 0 || x >= loop.Frames.Count)) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid loop exit marker.");
            if (exits.Keys.Any(x => x >= loop.Frames.Count) || exit is null && exitAt.Any(x => !exits.ContainsKey(x)) && exits.Count > 0) throw new InvalidDataException("An exit marker needs a matching exit segment.");
            var contacts = definition.TryGetProperty("contactPhases", out var phases)
                ? phases.EnumerateArray().Select(x => x.GetDouble()).ToArray()
                : string.Equals(item.Name, "walk", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && (exit is not null || exits.Count > 0)
                ? exitAt.Select(x => x / (double)loop.Frames.Count).ToArray()
                : loop.Frames.Select((pose, i) => (pose, i)).Where(x => x.pose.Contact is "both" or "left" or "right").Select(x => x.i / (double)loop.Frames.Count).ToArray();
            if (contacts.Length == 0) contacts = new[] { 0d, .5d };
            if (contacts.Any(x => !double.IsFinite(x) || x < 0 || x >= 1)) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid gait contact phase.");
            if (string.Equals(item.Name, "walk", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && (exit is not null || exits.Count > 0) &&
                contacts.Any(x => Math.Abs(x * loop.Frames.Count - Math.Round(x * loop.Frames.Count)) > .00001 || !exitAt.Contains((int)Math.Round(x * loop.Frames.Count))))
                throw new InvalidDataException("Each walking stop contact needs its matching exit marker.");
            actions.Add(item.Name, new PetAction(item.Name, enter, loop, exit, exitAt, baseline, Math.Clamp(Number(definition, "strideLength", 48), 1, 2048), contacts.Distinct().OrderBy(x => x).ToArray()) { ExitByMarker = exits });

            PetSegment ReadSegment(JsonElement segment, bool defaultLoop)
            {
                var list = new List<PetPose>();
                var interval = Number(segment, "frameMs", Number(definition, "frameMs", 1000d / 30));
                foreach (var frame in segment.GetProperty("frames").EnumerateArray())
                {
                    var pageIndex = frame.TryGetProperty("atlas", out var index) ? index.GetInt32() : definition.TryGetProperty("atlas", out index) ? index.GetInt32() : 0;
                    if (pageIndex < 0 || pageIndex >= pages.Count) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite page index.");
                    var page = pages[pageIndex];
                    var cell = frame.TryGetProperty("cell", out var cellValue) ? cellValue.GetInt32() : 0;
                    if (cell < 0 || cell >= page.Columns * page.Rows) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite cell.");
                    var rect = frame.TryGetProperty("rect", out var rectangle) ? RectValue(rectangle) : new Rect(cell % page.Columns * page.SourceSize.Width / page.Columns, cell / page.Columns * page.SourceSize.Height / page.Rows, page.SourceSize.Width / page.Columns, page.SourceSize.Height / page.Rows);
                    var frameCanvas = SizeValue(frame, "canvasSize", canvas.IsEmpty ? new Size(rect.Width, rect.Height) : canvas);
                    var placement = frame.TryGetProperty("placement", out var p) ? RectValue(p) : new Rect(frameCanvas);
                    var feet = frame.TryGetProperty("feet", out var footArray) ? footArray.EnumerateArray().Select(p => new Point(p[0].GetDouble(), p[1].GetDouble())).ToArray() : Array.Empty<Point>();
                    if (feet.Any(p => !double.IsFinite(p.X) || !double.IsFinite(p.Y) || p.X < 0 || p.X > 1 || p.Y < 0 || p.Y > 1)) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid foot contact anchor.");
                    list.Add(Pose(page, rect, frameCanvas, placement, Anchor(frame, "head", defaults.Head), Anchor(frame, "neck", defaults.Neck), null, Number(frame, "ms", interval), frame.TryGetProperty("contact", out var contact) ? contact.GetString() ?? "none" : "none", feet, Number(frame, "headAngle", 0), Number(frame, "neckAngle", 0), Number(frame, "accessoryScale", 1), ReadOcclusion(frame, frameCanvas)));
                }
                if (list.Count == 0) throw new InvalidDataException("Empty sprite segment.");
                return new PetSegment(list.ToArray(), segment.TryGetProperty("loop", out var repeating) ? repeating.GetBoolean() : defaultLoop);
            }
            PetSegment ReadLegacy()
            {
                var pageIndex = definition.GetProperty("atlas").GetInt32();
                if (pageIndex < 0 || pageIndex >= pages.Count) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite page index.");
                var page = pages[pageIndex]; var row = definition.GetProperty("row").GetInt32();
                if (row < 0 || row >= page.Rows) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite row.");
                var frames = definition.GetProperty("frames");
                var columns = frames.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array ? frames.EnumerateArray().Select(x => x.GetInt32()).ToArray() : Enumerable.Range(0, Math.Clamp(frames.GetInt32(), 1, page.Columns)).ToArray();
                if (columns.Length == 0 || columns.Any(x => x < 0 || x >= page.Columns)) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite column.");
                var rects = FrameArray(definition, "frameRects", columns.Length);
                var placements = FrameArray(definition, "framePlacements", columns.Length);
                var polygons = FrameArray(definition, "frameClipPolygons", columns.Length);
                var anchors = definition.TryGetProperty("anchors", out var a) ? a.EnumerateArray().ToArray() : Array.Empty<JsonElement>();
                var durations = definition.TryGetProperty("frameDurationsMs", out var d) ? d.EnumerateArray().Select(x => x.GetDouble()).ToArray() : Enumerable.Repeat(Number(definition, "frameMs", 160), columns.Length).ToArray();
                if (durations.Length != columns.Length) throw new InvalidDataException("Each frame needs one duration.");
                var poses = new List<PetPose>();
                for (var i = 0; i < columns.Length; i++)
                {
                    var bounds = new Int32Rect(columns[i] * page.Width / page.Columns, page.RowBounds[row].Top, page.Width / page.Columns, page.RowBounds[row].Height);
                    var rect = rects.Length > 0 ? RectValue(rects[i]) : new Rect(bounds.X * page.SourceSize.Width / page.Width, bounds.Y * page.SourceSize.Height / page.Height, bounds.Width * page.SourceSize.Width / page.Width, bounds.Height * page.SourceSize.Height / page.Height);
                    var frameCanvas = canvas.IsEmpty ? new Size(bounds.Width, bounds.Height) : canvas;
                    var placement = placements.Length > 0 ? RectValue(placements[i]) : new Rect(frameCanvas);
                    var geometry = polygons.Length > 0 ? Polygon(polygons[i], page.Width / page.SourceSize.Width, page.Height / page.SourceSize.Height) : null;
                    var attachments = i < anchors.Length ? anchors[i] : definition;
                    poses.Add(Pose(page, rect, frameCanvas, placement,
                        Anchor(attachments, "head", defaults.Head), Anchor(attachments, "neck", defaults.Neck), geometry, durations[i], "none", Array.Empty<Point>(),
                        Number(attachments, "headAngle", 0), Number(attachments, "neckAngle", 0), Number(attachments, "accessoryScale", 1), ReadOcclusion(attachments, frameCanvas)));
                }
                return new PetSegment(poses.ToArray(), !definition.TryGetProperty("loop", out var repeating) || repeating.GetBoolean());
            }
        }
        if (!actions.ContainsKey("idle")) throw new InvalidDataException("The sprite manifest must contain idle.");
        return new PetSpriteAssets(actions, version);
    }

    private static PetPose Pose(PetAtlasPage page, Rect source, Size canvas, Rect placement, Point head, Point neck, Geometry? polygon, double duration, string contact, IReadOnlyList<Point> feet, double headAngle = 0, double neckAngle = 0, double accessoryScale = 1, Geometry? accessoryOcclusion = null)
    {
        if (source.Left < 0 || source.Top < 0 || source.Right > page.SourceSize.Width + .001 || source.Bottom > page.SourceSize.Height + .001) throw new InvalidDataException("Sprite rectangle must fit its page.");
        if (placement.Left < 0 || placement.Top < 0 || placement.Right > canvas.Width + .001 || placement.Bottom > canvas.Height + .001) throw new InvalidDataException("Sprite placement must fit its canvas.");
        var sx = page.Width / page.SourceSize.Width; var sy = page.Height / page.SourceSize.Height;
        var left = (int)Math.Round(source.X * sx); var top = (int)Math.Round(source.Y * sy);
        var bounds = new Int32Rect(left, top, Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(source.Right * sx) - left), Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(source.Bottom * sy) - top));
        Geometry clip = new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(bounds.X, bounds.Y, bounds.Width, bounds.Height));
        if (polygon is not null) clip = new CombinedGeometry(GeometryCombineMode.Intersect, clip, polygon);
        clip.Freeze();
        if (!double.IsFinite(duration) || duration < 1 || duration > 15000) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite duration.");
        if (Math.Abs(headAngle) > 360 || Math.Abs(neckAngle) > 360 || accessoryScale < .25 || accessoryScale > 3) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid accessory pose.");
        return new PetPose(page, bounds, canvas, placement, head, neck, clip, duration, contact, feet, headAngle, neckAngle, accessoryScale, accessoryOcclusion);
    }
    private static Geometry? ReadOcclusion(JsonElement frame, Size canvas)
    {
        if (!frame.TryGetProperty("accessoryOcclusion", out var polygons) || polygons.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Null) return null;
        if (polygons.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || polygons.GetArrayLength() > 16) throw new InvalidDataException("Accessory occlusion needs up to 16 foreground polygons.");
        if (polygons.GetArrayLength() == 0) return null;
        Geometry? union = null;
        foreach (var polygon in polygons.EnumerateArray())
        {
            if (polygon.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || polygon.GetArrayLength() < 3 || polygon.GetArrayLength() > 128) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid accessory foreground polygon.");
            var points = polygon.EnumerateArray().Select(point =>
            {
                if (point.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || point.GetArrayLength() != 2) throw new InvalidDataException("Foreground polygon points need x,y.");
                var x = point[0].GetDouble(); var y = point[1].GetDouble();
                if (!double.IsFinite(x) || !double.IsFinite(y) || x < 0 || x > 1 || y < 0 || y > 1) throw new InvalidDataException("Foreground polygon points must fit the normalized canvas.");
                return new Point(x * 160, y * 160 * canvas.Height / canvas.Width);
            }).ToArray();
            var geometry = new StreamGeometry();
            using (var drawing = geometry.Open()) { drawing.BeginFigure(points[0], true, true); drawing.PolyLineTo(points.Skip(1).ToArray(), true, false); }
            geometry.Freeze(); union = union is null ? geometry : new CombinedGeometry(GeometryCombineMode.Union, union, geometry);
        }
        union!.Freeze(); return union;
    }
    internal static (Point Head, Point Neck) DefaultAnchors(string name) => name.ToLowerInvariant() switch
    {
        "sleep" => (new Point(.68, .20), new Point(.44, .806)),
        "walk" => (new Point(.78, .20), new Point(.55, .69)),
        "feed" or "feed-canned" or "happy" or "play" or "play-wand" or "work" or "success" or "error" or "drag" or "held" or "sit" => (new Point(.73, .21), new Point(.54, .73)),
        _ => (new Point(.73, .192), new Point(.54, .667))
    };
    private static double Number(JsonElement value, string name, double fallback)
    {
        var result = value.TryGetProperty(name, out var number) ? number.GetDouble() : fallback;
        if (!double.IsFinite(result)) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite number.");
        return result;
    }
    private static Size SizeValue(JsonElement value, string name, Size fallback)
    {
        if (!value.TryGetProperty(name, out var size)) return fallback;
        if (size.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || size.GetArrayLength() != 2) throw new InvalidDataException("Sprite size needs width and height.");
        var width = size[0].GetDouble(); var height = size[1].GetDouble();
        if (!double.IsFinite(width) || !double.IsFinite(height) || width <= 0 || height <= 0 || width > 16384 || height > 16384) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite size.");
        return new Size(width, height);
    }
    private static Rect RectValue(JsonElement value)
    {
        if (value.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || value.GetArrayLength() != 4) throw new InvalidDataException("Sprite rectangle needs x, y, width and height.");
        var n = value.EnumerateArray().Select(x => x.GetDouble()).ToArray();
        if (n.Any(x => !double.IsFinite(x)) || n[2] <= 0 || n[3] <= 0) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite rectangle.");
        return new Rect(n[0], n[1], n[2], n[3]);
    }
    private static JsonElement[] FrameArray(JsonElement value, string name, int count)
    {
        if (!value.TryGetProperty(name, out var frames)) return Array.Empty<JsonElement>();
        if (frames.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || frames.GetArrayLength() != count) throw new InvalidDataException("Each frame needs one " + name + ".");
        return frames.EnumerateArray().ToArray();
    }
    private static Point Anchor(JsonElement value, string name, Point fallback)
    {
        if (!value.TryGetProperty(name, out var p) || p.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || p.GetArrayLength() != 2) return fallback;
        var x = p[0].GetDouble(); var y = p[1].GetDouble();
        return double.IsFinite(x) && double.IsFinite(y) ? new Point(Math.Clamp(x, 0, 1), Math.Clamp(y, 0, 1)) : fallback;
    }
    private static Geometry? Polygon(JsonElement value, double sx, double sy)
    {
        if (value.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Null) return null;
        if (value.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array || value.GetArrayLength() < 3 || value.GetArrayLength() > 128) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite polygon.");
        var points = value.EnumerateArray().Select(p => new Point(p[0].GetDouble() * sx, p[1].GetDouble() * sy)).ToArray();
        if (points.Any(p => !double.IsFinite(p.X) || !double.IsFinite(p.Y))) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid sprite polygon point.");
        var result = new StreamGeometry();
        using (var drawing = result.Open()) { drawing.BeginFigure(points[0], true, true); drawing.PolyLineTo(points.Skip(1).ToArray(), true, false); }
        result.Freeze(); return result;
    }
}

/// <summary>Shared pinned LRU. Its budget includes pages still displayed by a visible view.</summary>
internal static class PetTextureCache
{
    internal const long LimitBytes = 16 * 1024 * 1024;
    internal sealed class Lease : IDisposable
    {
        private Entry? entry;
        public BitmapSource Image => entry?.Image ?? throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(Lease));
        internal Lease(Entry value) { entry = value; value.Pins++; }
        internal ImageSource Drawing(PetPose pose)
        {
            var value = entry ?? throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(Lease));
            if (value.Drawings.TryGetValue(pose, out var image)) return image;
            var bounds = pose.Bounds; var placement = pose.Placement;
            var drawing = new DrawingGroup();
            drawing.Children.Add(new GeometryDrawing(Brushes.Transparent, null, new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(pose.Canvas))));
            var crop = new DrawingGroup { ClipGeometry = pose.Clip, Transform = new MatrixTransform(placement.Width / bounds.Width, 0, 0, placement.Height / bounds.Height, placement.X - bounds.X * placement.Width / bounds.Width, placement.Y - bounds.Y * placement.Height / bounds.Height) };
            crop.Children.Add(new ImageDrawing(value.Image, new Rect(0, 0, value.Image.PixelWidth, value.Image.PixelHeight)));
            drawing.Children.Add(crop); drawing.Freeze();
            image = new DrawingImage(drawing); image.Freeze(); value.Drawings.Add(pose, image); return image;
        }
        public void Dispose() { if (entry is { } value) { value.Pins--; entry = null; } }
    }
    internal sealed class Entry
    {
        internal required BitmapSource Image;
        internal required long Bytes;
        internal long Stamp;
        internal int Pins;
        internal readonly Dictionary<PetPose, ImageSource> Drawings = new();
    }
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, Entry> entries = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private static long stamp;
    internal static long Bytes { get; private set; }
    internal static Lease Acquire(PetAtlasPage page)
    {
        var key = page.Path + "|" + page.Width + "|" + page.Height + "|" + File.GetLastWriteTimeUtc(page.Path).Ticks;
        if (entries.TryGetValue(key, out var known)) { known.Stamp = ++stamp; return new Lease(known); }
        var bytes = page.Width * (long)page.Height * 4;
        while (Bytes + bytes > LimitBytes)
        {
            var victim = entries.Where(x => x.Value.Pins == 0).OrderBy(x => x.Value.Stamp).FirstOrDefault();
            if (victim.Key is null) throw new InvalidDataException("Visible sprite pages exceed the shared 16 MiB budget.");
            entries.Remove(victim.Key); Bytes -= victim.Value.Bytes;
        }
        var image = new BitmapImage(); image.BeginInit();
        image.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad; image.CreateOptions = BitmapCreateOptions.IgnoreImageCache;
        image.DecodePixelWidth = page.Width; image.DecodePixelHeight = page.Height;
        image.UriSource = new Uri(page.Path, UriKind.Absolute); image.EndInit(); image.Freeze();
        BitmapSource normalized = image;
        if (image.Format != PixelFormats.Bgra32 && image.Format != PixelFormats.Pbgra32) { normalized = new FormatConvertedBitmap(image, PixelFormats.Bgra32, null, 0); normalized.Freeze(); }
        var entry = new Entry { Image = normalized, Bytes = bytes, Stamp = ++stamp };
        entries.Add(key, entry); Bytes += bytes; return new Lease(entry);
    }
}

internal sealed record PetFrameDiagnostics(Int32Rect SourceRect, Size CanvasSize, Rect Placement, Point Head, Point Neck, double Baseline, IReadOnlyList<Point>? Feet = null, string Contact = "none", string? AtlasFile = null, double HeadAngle = 0, double NeckAngle = 0, double AccessoryScale = 1);

/// <summary>Metadata-only clips, real pose transitions, and one pinned page per visible view.</summary>
public sealed class PetSpriteView : Grid, IDisposable
{
    private static readonly ConditionalWeakTable<PetSpriteAssets, ImageSource> idleThumbnails = new();
    private readonly Grid visual = new() { IsHitTestVisible = false };
    private readonly ScaleTransform breath = new();
    private readonly RotateTransform weightShift = new();
    private readonly Image picture = new() { Stretch = Stretch.Uniform, IsHitTestVisible = false };
    private readonly PetAccessoryView accessories = new() { IsHitTestVisible = false, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top };
    private static readonly DependencyProperty AmbientTimeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AmbientTime", typeof(double), typeof(PetSpriteView), new PropertyMetadata(0d, (owner, args) => ((PetSpriteView)owner).UpdateAmbientTransform((double)args.NewValue)));
    private readonly DispatcherTimer timer = new(DispatcherPriority.Background) { Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1000d / 30) };
    private PetSpriteAssets? assets;
    private PetAction? activeAction;
    private PetSegment? activeSegment;
    private string segmentName = "loop";
    private PetPose? pose;
    private PetTextureCache.Lease? texture;
    private PetAtlasPage? texturePage;
    private ImageSource? fallback;
    private string state = "idle";
    private int frame;
    private bool motionEnabled = true;
    private PetMotionMode motionMode = PetMotionMode.Gentle;
    private bool ambientRunning;
    private double pausedAmbientTime;
    private double idleHoldMilliseconds;
    private bool disposed;
    private bool completed;
    private bool exitCompletesAction;
    private long exitRequestVersion;
    private long requestVersion;
    private long nextFrameTimestamp;
    private bool externalWalk;
    private double walkDistance;
    private Action? walkCompletion;
    private long walkCompletionVersion;
    private bool wearsCollar;
    private bool wearsStarClip;
    private readonly byte[] hitPixel = new byte[4];

    public string Animation => state;
    public bool HasActiveAnimation => timer.IsEnabled || ambientRunning;
    public bool HasSpriteAtlas => assets is { Actions.Count: > 1 };
    public bool HasAnimation(string animation) => assets?.Actions.ContainsKey(animation) == true;
    internal bool UsesLegacyClips => assets is { Version: < 3 };
    public int ActiveFrameTimerCount => timer.IsEnabled ? 1 : 0;
    public int ActiveTransitionClockCount => 0;
    public int ActiveAmbientClockCount => ambientRunning ? 1 : 0;
    public int RenderedFrameIndex => frame;
    internal static long CachedAtlasBytes => PetTextureCache.Bytes;
    internal double FrameIntervalMilliseconds => timer.Interval.TotalMilliseconds;
    internal Matrix AmbientTransform => visual.RenderTransform.Value;
    internal double AmbientTimeSeconds => (double)GetValue(AmbientTimeProperty);
    internal string ActiveSegmentName => segmentName;
    internal string? ActiveActionName => activeAction?.Name;
    internal bool HasTextureReference => texture is not null;
    internal Geometry? CurrentAccessoryOcclusion => pose?.AccessoryOcclusion;
    internal PetFrameDiagnostics? CurrentFrameDiagnostics => pose is null ? null : Describe(pose, activeAction?.Baseline ?? .94);
    public string? AssetError { get; private set; }
    public event Action? AnimationCompleted;
    public bool IsWalkLoopReady => activeAction?.Name == "walk" && segmentName == "loop" && !completed;
    public double WalkStrideLength => assets?.Actions.GetValueOrDefault("walk")?.StrideLength ?? 48;
    public IReadOnlyList<double> WalkContactPhases => assets?.Actions.GetValueOrDefault("walk")?.ContactPhases ?? new[] { 0d, .5d };
    /// <summary>A small independent frozen illustration; it never pins an animation page.</summary>
    public ImageSource? IdleImageSource => assets is null ? fallback : idleThumbnails.GetValue(assets, CreateIdleThumbnail);

    public PetSpriteView()
    {
        Background = Brushes.Transparent; IsHitTestVisible = true; UseLayoutRounding = true;
        var ambient = new TransformGroup(); ambient.Children.Add(breath); ambient.Children.Add(weightShift); visual.RenderTransform = ambient;
        Children.Add(visual); visual.Children.Add(picture); visual.Children.Add(accessories);
        SizeChanged += (_, _) => { PlaceAccessories(); UpdateAmbientTransform(AmbientTimeSeconds); };
        timer.Tick += (_, _) => Advance(); Loaded += (_, _) => RefreshPlayback(); Unloaded += (_, _) => SuspendView();
        IsVisibleChanged += (_, _) => RefreshPlayback(); MotionPreferences.Changed += MotionChanged;
        LoadFallback(); SetAccessories(false, false);
    }

    public void LoadManifest(string manifestPath)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        SuspendView();
        try { assets = PetSpriteAssets.Load(manifestPath); fallback = null; AssetError = null; ++requestVersion; StartAction(state, false); }
        catch (Exception error) { AssetError = error.Message; Trace.TraceWarning("Desktop pet sprites: {0}", error.Message); LoadFallback(); }
    }
    /// <summary>Only immutable definitions are shared. Each view owns one local texture lease and clock.</summary>
    public bool TryShareLoadedSpritesFrom(PetSpriteView source)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(source);
        if (disposed || source.disposed || !source.HasSpriteAtlas) return false;
        if (ReferenceEquals(this, source)) return true;
        SuspendView(); assets = source.assets; fallback = null; AssetError = source.AssetError;
        ++requestVersion; StartAction(state, false); return true;
    }
    private static ImageSource CreateIdleThumbnail(PetSpriteAssets source)
    {
        var idle = source.Actions["idle"].Loop.Frames[0]; using var page = PetTextureCache.Acquire(idle.Page);
        var visual = new DrawingVisual(); using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen()) drawing.DrawImage(page.Drawing(idle), new Rect(0, 0, 160, 160));
        var image = new RenderTargetBitmap(160, 160, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); image.Render(visual); image.Freeze(); return image;
    }
    private void LoadFallback()
    {
        ReleaseTexture(); assets = null; activeAction = null; activeSegment = null; pose = null;
        try
        {
            var image = new BitmapImage(); image.BeginInit(); image.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad; image.DecodePixelWidth = 256;
            image.UriSource = new Uri("pack://application:,,,/YourCatFileAssistant;component/Assets/Cat/cat2d-idle.png", UriKind.Absolute); image.EndInit(); image.Freeze(); fallback = image;
            if (IsLoaded && IsVisible) picture.Source = fallback;
        }
        catch (Exception error) { AssetError = error.Message; Trace.TraceWarning("Desktop pet fallback: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    public void SetAnimation(string animation, bool restart = false)
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        var next = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(animation) ? "idle" : animation;
        if (!restart && string.Equals(next, state, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && activeAction is not null) return;
        state = next; ++requestVersion; walkCompletion = null;
        if (!string.Equals(next, "walk", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) externalWalk = false;
        if (restart || !CanAnimate || activeAction is null || completed) { StartAction(next, CanAnimate); return; }
        if (segmentName == "exit") return;
        if (activeAction.HasExit)
        {
            if (segmentName == "loop" && AtExitPoint()) BeginExit(false); else RefreshPlayback();
            return;
        }
        StartAction(next, true);
    }
    public void SetMotionEnabled(bool enabled) { motionEnabled = enabled; RefreshPlayback(); }
    public void SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode mode) { motionMode = Enum.IsDefined(mode) ? mode : PetMotionMode.Gentle; RefreshPlayback(); }
    public void SetAccessories(bool collar, bool starClip) { wearsCollar = collar; wearsStarClip = starClip; PlaceAccessories(); }

    /// <summary>Distance is in logical DIPs at the reference 160 DIP pet size.</summary>
    public void UpdateWalkDistance(double distanceDips)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(distanceDips) || disposed) return;
        externalWalk = true; walkDistance = Math.Max(0, distanceDips);
        if (IsWalkLoopReady) { ApplyWalkPhase(); RefreshPlayback(); }
    }
    public void SetWalkPhase(double normalizedCyclePhase)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(normalizedCyclePhase)) return;
        UpdateWalkDistance(((normalizedCyclePhase % 1 + 1) % 1) * WalkStrideLength);
    }
    public void ReleaseWalkPhase() { externalWalk = false; RefreshPlayback(); }
    public void CompleteWalk(Action callback)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(callback); if (disposed) return;
        walkCompletion = callback; walkCompletionVersion = requestVersion; externalWalk = false;
        if (activeAction?.Name != "walk") { CompleteWalkCallback(); return; }
        if (!CanAnimate || !activeAction.HasExit) { completed = true; timer.Stop(); CompleteWalkCallback(); return; }
        if (AtExitPoint()) BeginExit(true); else RefreshPlayback();
    }
    public void CancelWalkMotion()
    {
        walkCompletion = null; externalWalk = false; walkDistance = 0;
        if (activeAction?.Name == "walk") { ++requestVersion; timer.Stop(); completed = true; }
    }
    private void ApplyWalkPhase()
    {
        if (!IsWalkLoopReady || activeSegment is null) return;
        var phase = (walkDistance / WalkStrideLength) % 1;
        if (phase > 1 - 1e-9) phase = 0;
        frame = Math.Min(activeSegment.Frames.Count - 1, (int)Math.Floor(phase * activeSegment.Frames.Count + 1e-9));
        pose = activeSegment.Frames[frame]; RenderPose(); PlaceAccessories();
    }

    private void StartAction(string requested, bool enter, long startTimestamp = 0)
    {
        timer.Stop(); nextFrameTimestamp = 0; completed = false; exitCompletesAction = false;
        activeAction = assets?.Actions.GetValueOrDefault(requested) ?? assets?.Actions.GetValueOrDefault("idle");
        if (activeAction is null) { activeSegment = null; pose = null; if (IsLoaded && IsVisible) picture.Source = fallback; RefreshPlayback(); return; }
        StartSegment(enter && activeAction.Enter is not null ? "enter" : "loop", startTimestamp: startTimestamp); RefreshPlayback();
    }
    private void StartSegment(string name, PetSegment? selected = null, long startTimestamp = 0)
    {
        if (activeAction is null) return;
        segmentName = name; activeSegment = selected ?? (name switch { "enter" => activeAction.Enter, "exit" => activeAction.Exit, _ => activeAction.Loop });
        if (activeSegment is null) return;
        frame = 0; pose = activeSegment.Frames[0]; idleHoldMilliseconds = PickIdleHold();
        nextFrameTimestamp = (startTimestamp > 0 ? startTimestamp : Stopwatch.GetTimestamp()) + DurationTicks(FrameDuration());
        if (externalWalk && IsWalkLoopReady) ApplyWalkPhase(); else RenderPose();
        PlaceAccessories(); UpdateAmbientTransform(AmbientTimeSeconds);
    }
    private void BeginExit(bool completing, long startTimestamp = 0)
    {
        var exit = activeAction?.ExitFor(frame);
        if (exit is null) { if (completing) FinishAction(); else StartAction(state, true, startTimestamp); return; }
        exitCompletesAction = completing; exitRequestVersion = requestVersion; StartSegment("exit", exit, startTimestamp); RefreshPlayback();
    }
    private bool AtExitPoint() => activeAction?.ExitFor(frame) is not null;
    private bool DestinationChanged => activeAction is not null && !string.Equals(activeAction.Name,
        assets?.Actions.ContainsKey(state) == true ? state : "idle", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private void RefreshPlayback()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        if (!IsLoaded || !IsVisible) { SuspendView(); return; }
        if (pose is not null && picture.Source is null) RenderPose(); else if (pose is null && picture.Source is null) picture.Source = fallback;
        if (!CanAnimate)
        {
            timer.Stop(); nextFrameTimestamp = 0; StopAmbientPlayback(true);
            if (DestinationChanged || segmentName is "enter" or "exit") StartAction(state, false);
            return;
        }
        RefreshAmbientPlayback();
        if (activeSegment is null || completed || externalWalk && IsWalkLoopReady && !DestinationChanged || activeSegment.Frames.Count <= 1 && activeSegment.Loop && !DestinationChanged) { timer.Stop(); nextFrameTimestamp = 0; return; }
        if (!timer.IsEnabled) { if (nextFrameTimestamp == 0) nextFrameTimestamp = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() + DurationTicks(FrameDuration()); timer.Start(); }
    }
    private void Advance()
    {
        if (!CanAnimate || activeSegment is null) { RefreshPlayback(); return; }
        var now = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp(); if (now < nextFrameTimestamp) return;
        var guard = 0;
        while (now >= nextFrameTimestamp && guard++ < 512)
        {
            if ((DestinationChanged || walkCompletion is not null) && segmentName == "loop" && AtExitPoint()) { BeginExit(!DestinationChanged, nextFrameTimestamp); continue; }
            if (frame + 1 < activeSegment.Frames.Count) frame++;
            else if (segmentName == "enter")
            {
                var boundary = nextFrameTimestamp;
                StartSegment("loop", startTimestamp: boundary);
                if ((DestinationChanged || walkCompletion is not null) && AtExitPoint()) BeginExit(!DestinationChanged, boundary);
                if (externalWalk && IsWalkLoopReady) { RefreshPlayback(); return; }
                continue;
            }
            else if (segmentName == "exit")
            {
                if (DestinationChanged || !exitCompletesAction || exitRequestVersion != requestVersion)
                {
                    StartAction(state, true, nextFrameTimestamp);
                    if (!timer.IsEnabled || activeSegment is null) return;
                    continue;
                }
                FinishAction(); return;
            }
            else if (!activeSegment.Loop)
            {
                if (activeAction?.ExitFor(frame) is not null) { BeginExit(true, nextFrameTimestamp); continue; }
                FinishAction(); return;
            }
            else { frame = 0; idleHoldMilliseconds = PickIdleHold(); }
            nextFrameTimestamp += DurationTicks(FrameDuration()); pose = activeSegment.Frames[frame];
            if ((DestinationChanged || walkCompletion is not null) && segmentName == "loop" && AtExitPoint()) { BeginExit(!DestinationChanged, nextFrameTimestamp - DurationTicks(FrameDuration())); continue; }
        }
        if (guard >= 512) nextFrameTimestamp = now + DurationTicks(FrameDuration());
        pose = activeSegment.Frames[frame]; RenderPose(); PlaceAccessories(); UpdateAmbientTransform(AmbientTimeSeconds);
    }
    private void FinishAction()
    {
        timer.Stop(); completed = true;
        if (activeSegment is not null) { frame = activeSegment.Frames.Count - 1; pose = activeSegment.Frames[frame]; RenderPose(); }
        RefreshAmbientPlayback(); var version = requestVersion; CompleteWalkCallback();
        if (version == requestVersion) AnimationCompleted?.Invoke();
    }
    private void CompleteWalkCallback()
    {
        var callback = walkCompletion; var version = walkCompletionVersion; walkCompletion = null;
        if (callback is not null && version == requestVersion) callback();
    }
    private static long DurationTicks(double milliseconds) => Math.Max(1, (long)(milliseconds * Stopwatch.Frequency / 1000));
    private double PickIdleHold() => activeAction?.Name == "idle" && segmentName == "loop" && activeSegment?.Frames[0].Milliseconds >= 1000 ? Random.Shared.Next(2800, 5501) : 0;
    private double FrameDuration() => frame == 0 && idleHoldMilliseconds > 0 ? idleHoldMilliseconds : activeSegment?.Frames[frame].Milliseconds ?? 1000d / 30;

    private void RenderPose(bool force = false)
    {
        if (pose is null || disposed || !force && (!IsLoaded || !IsVisible)) return;
        try
        {
            if (!ReferenceEquals(texturePage, pose.Page)) { ReleaseTexture(); texture = PetTextureCache.Acquire(pose.Page); texturePage = pose.Page; }
            picture.Source = texture!.Drawing(pose);
        }
        catch (Exception error) { AssetError = error.Message; Trace.TraceWarning("Desktop pet page: {0}", error.Message); ReleaseTexture(); }
    }
    private void ReleaseTexture() { picture.Source = null; texture?.Dispose(); texture = null; texturePage = null; }
    private bool CanAnimate => !disposed && IsLoaded && IsVisible && motionEnabled && !MotionPreferences.ShouldReducePetMotion(motionMode);
    private bool UsesAmbientMotion => activeAction is not null && (assets?.Version < 3 || activeAction.Name is "idle" or "blink" or "sit" or "sleep" || completed) && (activeAction.Name is not ("walk" or "drag" or "held") || activeSegment?.Frames.Count == 1 || completed);
    private void RefreshAmbientPlayback()
    {
        if (!CanAnimate || !UsesAmbientMotion) { StopAmbientPlayback(!UsesAmbientMotion || !motionEnabled || motionMode == PetMotionMode.Reduced); return; }
        if (ambientRunning) return;
        var animation = new DoubleAnimation(pausedAmbientTime, pausedAmbientTime + 48, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(48)) { RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop };
        Timeline.SetDesiredFrameRate(animation, 30); ambientRunning = true; BeginAnimation(AmbientTimeProperty, animation, HandoffBehavior.SnapshotAndReplace);
    }
    private void UpdateAmbientTransform(double seconds)
    {
        if (pose is null) return;
        var fit = FitCanvas(pose.Canvas); breath.CenterX = weightShift.CenterX = fit.X + fit.Width / 2; breath.CenterY = weightShift.CenterY = fit.Y + fit.Height * (activeAction?.Baseline ?? .94);
        if (!CanAnimate || !UsesAmbientMotion) { breath.ScaleY = 1; weightShift.Angle = 0; return; }
        var sleeping = activeAction?.Name == "sleep"; var cycle = seconds * Math.PI * 2 / (sleeping ? 4.8 : 3.2);
        breath.ScaleY = 1 + (sleeping ? .012 : .015) * (.5 - .5 * Math.Cos(cycle)); weightShift.Angle = sleeping ? 0 : .35 * Math.Sin(seconds * Math.PI * 2 / 8);
    }
    private void StopAmbientPlayback(bool reset = false)
    {
        if (ambientRunning) { pausedAmbientTime = AmbientTimeSeconds % 48; SetCurrentValue(AmbientTimeProperty, pausedAmbientTime); BeginAnimation(AmbientTimeProperty, null); ambientRunning = false; }
        if (reset) { breath.ScaleY = 1; weightShift.Angle = 0; }
    }
    private void PlaceAccessories()
    {
        var width = ActualWidth > 0 ? ActualWidth : 160; var height = ActualHeight > 0 ? ActualHeight : 160; var aspect = pose is null ? 1 : pose.Canvas.Width / pose.Canvas.Height;
        var fitWidth = Math.Min(width, height * aspect); var fitHeight = fitWidth / aspect; var scale = fitWidth / 160d;
        accessories.Width = 160; accessories.Height = fitHeight / scale;
        var transform = new TransformGroup(); transform.Children.Add(new ScaleTransform(scale, scale)); transform.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform((width - fitWidth) / 2, (height - fitHeight) / 2)); accessories.RenderTransform = transform;
        var anchors = pose is null ? PetSpriteAssets.DefaultAnchors(state) : (pose.Head, pose.Neck); var baseHeight = fitHeight / scale;
        accessories.SetPose(new Point(anchors.Item1.X * 160, anchors.Item1.Y * baseHeight),
            new Point(anchors.Item2.X * 160, anchors.Item2.Y * baseHeight), pose?.HeadAngle ?? 0, pose?.NeckAngle ?? 0, pose?.AccessoryScale ?? 1);
        accessories.SetEquipment(wearsCollar, wearsCollar, wearsStarClip);
        accessories.SetNeckOcclusion(pose?.AccessoryOcclusion);
    }
    private Rect FitCanvas(Size canvas)
    {
        var width = ActualWidth > 0 ? ActualWidth : 160; var height = ActualHeight > 0 ? ActualHeight : 160; var scale = Math.Min(width / canvas.Width, height / canvas.Height);
        return new Rect((width - canvas.Width * scale) / 2, (height - canvas.Height * scale) / 2, canvas.Width * scale, canvas.Height * scale);
    }
    private bool TryMapCanvas(Point local, out PetPose active, out Point canvasPoint)
    {
        active = pose!; canvasPoint = default; if (active is null || ActualWidth <= 0 || ActualHeight <= 0) return false;
        var matrix = AmbientTransform; if (!matrix.HasInverse) return false; matrix.Invert(); local = matrix.Transform(local); var fit = FitCanvas(active.Canvas);
        canvasPoint = new Point((local.X - fit.X) * active.Canvas.Width / fit.Width, (local.Y - fit.Y) * active.Canvas.Height / fit.Height);
        return canvasPoint.X >= 0 && canvasPoint.Y >= 0 && canvasPoint.X < active.Canvas.Width && canvasPoint.Y < active.Canvas.Height;
    }
    private bool TryMapPixel(Point local, out PetPose active, out int x, out int y)
    {
        x = y = -1; if (!TryMapCanvas(local, out active, out var point) || !active.Placement.Contains(point)) return false;
        x = (int)Math.Floor((point.X - active.Placement.X) * active.Bounds.Width / active.Placement.Width); y = (int)Math.Floor((point.Y - active.Placement.Y) * active.Bounds.Height / active.Placement.Height);
        return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < active.Bounds.Width && y < active.Bounds.Height;
    }
    internal bool ContainsVisiblePixel(Point local)
    {
        if (texture is null || !TryMapPixel(local, out var active, out var x, out var y)) return false;
        if (active.Clip is { } clip && !clip.FillContains(new Point(active.Bounds.X + x + .5, active.Bounds.Y + y + .5))) return false;
        texture.Image.CopyPixels(new Int32Rect(active.Bounds.X + x, active.Bounds.Y + y, 1, 1), hitPixel, 4, 0); return hitPixel[3] > 16;
    }
    internal bool IsHeadHit(Point local)
    {
        if (!TryMapCanvas(local, out var active, out var point)) return false;
        var headX = (active.Head.X + active.Neck.X) * .5 * active.Canvas.Width; var headY = active.Head.Y * active.Canvas.Height;
        return Math.Abs(point.X - headX) <= active.Canvas.Width * .30 && point.Y >= headY - active.Canvas.Height * .16 && point.Y <= headY + active.Canvas.Height * .32;
    }
    private static PetFrameDiagnostics Describe(PetPose current, double baseline) => new(current.Bounds, current.Canvas, current.Placement, current.Head, current.Neck, baseline, current.Feet, current.Contact, current.Page.Path, current.HeadAngle, current.NeckAngle, current.AccessoryScale);
    internal IReadOnlyList<PetFrameDiagnostics> GetFrameDiagnostics(string animation) => assets?.Actions.TryGetValue(animation, out var action) == true ? action.AllFrames.Select(p => Describe(p, action.Baseline)).ToArray() : Array.Empty<PetFrameDiagnostics>();
    internal void SetFrameForTesting(string animation, int index)
    {
        if (assets is null || !assets.Actions.TryGetValue(animation, out var action)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(animation));
        var poses = action.AllFrames.ToArray(); if (index < 0 || index >= poses.Length) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(index));
        timer.Stop(); StopAmbientPlayback(true); ++requestVersion; state = animation; activeAction = action; activeSegment = new PetSegment(poses, false); segmentName = "test"; frame = index; pose = poses[index]; completed = false;
        RenderPose(true); PlaceAccessories();
    }
    private void MotionChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) => RefreshPlayback();
    private void SuspendView() { timer.Stop(); nextFrameTimestamp = 0; StopAmbientPlayback(reset: true); ReleaseTexture(); }
    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (disposed) return;
        disposed = true; ++requestVersion; walkCompletion = null; SuspendView(); MotionPreferences.Changed -= MotionChanged;
        assets = null; activeAction = null; activeSegment = null; pose = null; fallback = null;
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/PetSubmenuItem.cs ---
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Keeps either side of a desktop pet submenu joined to its header's hit area.</summary>
internal sealed class PetSubmenuItem : MenuItem
{
    public PetSubmenuItem() => SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, typeof(MenuItem));

    public override void OnApplyTemplate()
    {
        base.OnApplyTemplate();
        if (GetTemplateChild("PART_Popup") is not Popup popup) return;
        // An offset intended for the right edge creates a gap when Popup mirrors it to the left.
        // Give both directions an explicit inward overlap instead. WPF retains menu capture,
        // outside-click dismissal, keyboard navigation and the submenu's normal lifetime.
        popup.PlacementTarget = this;
        popup.Placement = PlacementMode.Custom;
        popup.HorizontalOffset = popup.VerticalOffset = 0;
        popup.CustomPopupPlacementCallback = PlaceSubmenu;
    }

    private CustomPopupPlacement[] PlaceSubmenu(Size popupSize, Size targetSize, Point offset)
    {
        var workArea = Rect.Empty;
        if (IsVisible && PresentationSource.FromVisual(this) is not null)
        {
            var origin = PointToScreen(new Point());
            var monitor = DesktopEnvironment.GetWorkAreaForPosition(origin.X, origin.Y);
            workArea = new Rect(PointFromScreen(monitor.WorkArea.TopLeft), PointFromScreen(monitor.WorkArea.BottomRight));
        }
        return CreatePlacements(popupSize, targetSize, workArea);
    }

    internal static CustomPopupPlacement[] CreatePlacements(Size popupSize, Size targetSize, Rect workArea)
    {
        // The popup has a 1 DIP border and 5 DIP inner padding. Cover that transition with
        // another 2 DIP of shared hit area: a 1 DIP join can round onto the native HWND's
        // exclusive right edge and hand a slow leftward move back to the parent popup.
        const double overlap = 8;
        var right = new CustomPopupPlacement(new Point(Math.Max(0, targetSize.Width - overlap), -5), PopupPrimaryAxis.Horizontal);
        var left = new CustomPopupPlacement(new Point(overlap - popupSize.Width, -5), PopupPrimaryAxis.Horizontal);
        // Pick the side with enough room, with native Popup clipping as a fallback for small work areas.
        var preferLeft = !workArea.IsEmpty && right.Point.X + popupSize.Width > workArea.Right &&
            (left.Point.X >= workArea.Left || -workArea.Left > workArea.Right - targetSize.Width);
        return preferLeft ? [left, right] : [right, left];
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/PetWalkTrajectory.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Distance and contact calculations shared by native movement and its acceptance checks.</summary>
internal static class PetWalkTrajectory
{
    internal static double ContactAlignedDistance(double available, double stride, IReadOnlyList<double> contacts)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(available) || available <= 0 || !double.IsFinite(stride) || stride <= 0) return 0;
        var result = Math.Floor(available / stride) * stride;
        var cycle = Math.Floor(available / stride);
        foreach (var phase in contacts)
        {
            if (!double.IsFinite(phase) || phase < 0 || phase > 1) continue;
            var candidate = (cycle + phase) * stride;
            if (candidate <= available + .000001) result = Math.Max(result, candidate);
        }
        return result;
    }

    internal static double NextContactDistance(double current, double maximum, double stride, IReadOnlyList<double> contacts)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(current) || !double.IsFinite(maximum) || stride <= 0 || !double.IsFinite(stride)) return current;
        var result = maximum;
        var cycle = Math.Floor(current / stride);
        // A cycle boundary is always the shared first/last planted pose, even if
        // an imported clip omits the redundant zero marker in its metadata.
        var boundary = Math.Ceiling((current - .000001) / stride) * stride;
        if (boundary >= current - .000001) result = Math.Min(result, boundary);
        foreach (var phase in contacts)
        {
            if (!double.IsFinite(phase) || phase < 0 || phase > 1) continue;
            var candidate = (cycle + phase) * stride;
            if (candidate < current - .000001) candidate += stride;
            result = Math.Min(result, candidate);
        }
        return Math.Clamp(result, current, Math.Max(current, maximum));
    }

    internal static (double Distance, double Velocity) Sample(double start, double end, double initialVelocity, double duration, double elapsed)
    {
        if (duration <= 0 || elapsed >= duration) return (end, 0);
        var t = Math.Clamp(elapsed / duration, 0, 1);
        var distance = end - start;
        // Cubic Hermite interpolation retains the velocity when an in-progress
        // walk is asked to stop, and reaches the next foot contact at rest.
        var position = start + distance * (3 * t * t - 2 * t * t * t)
            + initialVelocity * duration * (t * t * t - 2 * t * t + t);
        var velocity = distance / duration * (6 * t - 6 * t * t)
            + initialVelocity * (3 * t * t - 4 * t + 1);
        return (Math.Clamp(position, start, end), Math.Max(0, velocity));
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Pet/TrayIcon.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Small native tray adapter, without another UI framework or external packages.</summary>
internal sealed class TrayIcon : IDisposable
{
    private readonly HwndSource source;
    private readonly Action open;
    private readonly Func<IEnumerable<(string Title, Action Execute)>> menu;
    private readonly uint taskbarCreated;
    private NotifyData data;
    private bool disposed;
    private const int CallbackMessage = 0x8000 + 37;
    public TrayIcon(Action open, Func<IEnumerable<(string Title, Action Execute)>> menu)
    {
        this.open = open; this.menu = menu;
        source = new HwndSource(new HwndSourceParameters("CatFileAssistant.Tray") { Width=0, Height=0, WindowStyle=unchecked((int)0x80000000), ExtendedWindowStyle=0x80 });
        source.AddHook(WndProc);
        taskbarCreated = RegisterWindowMessage("TaskbarCreated");
        var icons = new IntPtr[1];
        ExtractIconEx(Environment.ProcessPath!,0,icons,null,1);
        data = new NotifyData { Size=(uint)Marshal.SizeOf<NotifyData>(), Window=source.Handle, Id=1, Flags=1|2|4, Callback=CallbackMessage, Icon=icons[0], Tip=TipText(), Info="", InfoTitle="" };
        Shell_NotifyIcon(0,ref data);
        Locale.Changed += LanguageChanged;
    }
    private static string TipText() => Locale.T("你的猫咪文件助手") + " · " + AppVersion.Label + "\n" + Locale.T("双击打开，右键管理桌宠");
    private void LanguageChanged(object? sender, EventArgs args) { if (disposed) return; data.Tip = TipText(); Shell_NotifyIcon(1, ref data); }
    private IntPtr WndProc(IntPtr hwnd,int msg,IntPtr wParam,IntPtr lParam,ref bool handled)
    {
        if (disposed) return IntPtr.Zero;
        if ((uint)msg==taskbarCreated) Shell_NotifyIcon(0,ref data);
        if(msg==CallbackMessage)
        {
            var mouse=unchecked((int)lParam.ToInt64());
            if(mouse==0x203)open();
            else if(mouse==0x205)
            {
                SetForegroundWindow(source.Handle);
                var popup=new ContextMenu { Placement=PlacementMode.MousePoint };
                foreach(var (title,action) in menu()) { var item=new MenuItem { Header=Locale.TranslateText(title) }; item.Click+=(_,_)=>action(); popup.Items.Add(item); }
                popup.Closed+=(_,_)=>PostMessage(source.Handle,0,IntPtr.Zero,IntPtr.Zero);
                popup.IsOpen=true;
            }
            handled=true;
        }
        return IntPtr.Zero;
    }
    public void Dispose()
    {
        if(disposed)return;disposed=true;
        Locale.Changed -= LanguageChanged;
        Shell_NotifyIcon(2,ref data);
        if(data.Icon!=IntPtr.Zero)DestroyIcon(data.Icon);
        source.RemoveHook(WndProc);source.Dispose();
    }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential,CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] private struct NotifyData
    {
        public uint Size;public IntPtr Window;public uint Id,Flags,Callback;public IntPtr Icon;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr,SizeConst=128)]public string Tip;
        public uint State,StateMask;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr,SizeConst=256)]public string Info;
        public uint Timeout;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr,SizeConst=64)]public string InfoTitle;
        public uint InfoFlags;public Guid Guid;public IntPtr BalloonIcon;
    }
    [DllImport("shell32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] [return:MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] private static extern bool Shell_NotifyIcon(uint message,ref NotifyData data);
    [DllImport("shell32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] private static extern uint ExtractIconEx(string path,int index,IntPtr[] large,IntPtr[]? small,uint count);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool DestroyIcon(IntPtr icon);
    [DllImport("user32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)]private static extern uint RegisterWindowMessage(string message);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr hwnd,int message,IntPtr wParam,IntPtr lParam);
}
