--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Themes/ThemeManager.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Effects;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Motion;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Themes;

/// <summary>Application-wide presentation resources. It never changes processing settings.</summary>
public static class ThemeManager
{
    private static Application? initializedApplication;
    private static Dictionary<string, string> light = DefaultLight();
    private static Dictionary<string, string> dark = DefaultDark();
    private static bool subscriptionsActive;
    private static string effectiveTheme = "";
    public static string Preference { get; private set; } = "System";
    public static event EventHandler? Changed;

    public static void EnsureInitialized()
    {
        var app = Application.Current;
        if (app is null || app.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) return;
        if (!app.Dispatcher.CheckAccess()) { app.Dispatcher.Invoke(EnsureInitialized); return; }
        if (ReferenceEquals(initializedApplication, app)) return;
        if (initializedApplication is not null) { initializedApplication.Exit -= ApplicationExited; initializedApplication.Activated -= ApplicationActivated; }
        initializedApplication = app;
        if (!app.Resources.MergedDictionaries.Any(dictionary =>
            dictionary.Source?.OriginalString.EndsWith("Themes/AppTheme.xaml", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true))
            app.Resources.MergedDictionaries.Add(new ResourceDictionary
            { Source = new Uri("/YourCatFileAssistant;component/Themes/AppTheme.xaml", UriKind.Relative) });
        LoadTokens(app.Resources);
        app.Resources["UiFont"] = ChooseUiFont();
        app.Resources["NumericFont"] = app.Resources["UiFont"];
        app.Exit += ApplicationExited;
        app.Activated += ApplicationActivated;
        if (!subscriptionsActive)
        {
            try { SystemEvents.UserPreferenceChanged += SystemPreferenceChanged; }
            catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("System theme notifications: {0}", error.Message); }
            SystemParameters.StaticPropertyChanged += SystemParameterChanged;
            subscriptionsActive = true;
        }
        effectiveTheme = "";
        ApplyPalette();
    }

    public static void SetPreference(string preference)
    {
        var next = preference switch { "Light" => "Light", "Dark" => "Dark", _ => "System" };
        var app = Application.Current;
        if (app is not null && !app.Dispatcher.CheckAccess())
        { app.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => SetPreference(next)); return; }
        Preference = next;
        EnsureInitialized();
        ApplyPalette();
    }

    private static void LoadTokens(ResourceDictionary resources)
    {
        light = DefaultLight(); dark = DefaultDark();
        var metrics = new Dictionary<string, double>
        {
            ["BodySize"] = 14, ["BodyLineHeight"] = 22, ["CaptionSize"] = 12, ["CaptionLineHeight"] = 18,
            ["TitleSize"] = 24, ["TitleLineHeight"] = 32, ["SectionSize"] = 18, ["SectionLineHeight"] = 26,
            ["ButtonSize"] = 14, ["NumericSize"] = 14, ["ControlMinHeight"] = 36
        };
        var radius = new Dictionary<string, double> { ["ButtonRadius"] = 10, ["InputRadius"] = 8, ["CardRadius"] = 16, ["DropRadius"] = 18, ["DialogRadius"] = 18 };
        var motion = new Dictionary<string, int> { ["hoverDurationMs"] = 140, ["pressDurationMs"] = 100, ["releaseDurationMs"] = 140,
            ["menuDurationMs"] = 180, ["pageDurationMs"] = 220, ["dialogDurationMs"] = 200, ["progressDurationMs"] = 150,
            ["selectionDurationMs"] = 160, ["rowEntranceDurationMs"] = 160, ["successDurationMs"] = 600,
            ["idleBlinkMinMs"] = 4000, ["idleBlinkMaxMs"] = 7000, ["sleepAfterMs"] = 120000 };
        try
        {
            var path = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "shared", "design-tokens.json");
            if (File.Exists(path))
            {
                using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(path));
                var root = document.RootElement;
                if (root.TryGetProperty("themes", out var themes))
                {
                    if (themes.TryGetProperty("light", out var palette)) ReadPalette(palette, light);
                    if (themes.TryGetProperty("dark", out palette)) ReadPalette(palette, dark);
                }
                if (root.TryGetProperty("typography", out var typography))
                    foreach (var key in metrics.Keys.ToArray())
                    {
                        var jsonKey = char.ToLowerInvariant(key[0]) + key[1..];
                        if (typography.TryGetProperty(jsonKey, out var value) && value.TryGetDouble(out var number) && double.IsFinite(number))
                            metrics[key] = Math.Clamp(number, 10, 64);
                    }
                if (root.TryGetProperty("radius", out var corners))
                    foreach (var pair in new[] { ("button", "ButtonRadius"), ("input", "InputRadius"), ("card", "CardRadius"), ("dropArea", "DropRadius"), ("dialog", "DialogRadius") })
                        if (corners.TryGetProperty(pair.Item1, out var value) && value.TryGetDouble(out var number) && double.IsFinite(number))
                            radius[pair.Item2] = Math.Clamp(number, 0, 28);
                if (root.TryGetProperty("motion", out var timings))
                    foreach (var key in motion.Keys.ToArray())
                        if (timings.TryGetProperty(key, out var value) && value.TryGetInt32(out var number))
                            motion[key] = Math.Clamp(number, 1, key == "sleepAfterMs" ? 600000 : key.StartsWith("idleBlink", StringComparison.Ordinal) ? 60000 : 1000);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Design token fallback: {0}", error.Message); }
        foreach (var pair in metrics) resources[pair.Key] = pair.Value;
        foreach (var pair in radius) resources[pair.Key] = new CornerRadius(pair.Value);
        foreach (var value in new[] { 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32 }) resources["Space" + value] = (double)value;
        resources["PagePadding"] = new Thickness(24);
        resources["CardPadding"] = new Thickness(20);
        resources["ButtonPadding"] = new Thickness(14, 8, 14, 8);
        resources["InputPadding"] = new Thickness(10, 6, 10, 6);
        foreach (var pair in motion) resources["Motion" + char.ToUpperInvariant(pair.Key[0]) + pair.Key[1..]] = pair.Value;
        UiMotion.ConfigureDurations(motion["hoverDurationMs"], motion["pressDurationMs"], motion["releaseDurationMs"], motion["progressDurationMs"]);
    }

    private static void ReadPalette(JsonElement source, Dictionary<string, string> palette)
    {
        foreach (var pair in source.EnumerateObject())
            if (pair.Value.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String && pair.Value.GetString() is { } value)
            {
                try { _ = (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value); palette[char.ToUpperInvariant(pair.Name[0]) + pair.Name[1..]] = value; }
                catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Invalid theme color {0}: {1}", pair.Name, error.Message); }
            }
    }

    private static FontFamily ChooseUiFont()
    {
        var families = Fonts.SystemFontFamilies.Select(font => font.Source).ToHashSet(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var candidate in new[] { "Segoe UI Variable Text", "Segoe UI Variable" })
        {
            if (!families.Contains(candidate)) continue;
            var typeface = new Typeface(new FontFamily(candidate), FontStyles.Normal, FontWeights.Normal, FontStretches.Normal);
            if (typeface.TryGetGlyphTypeface(out var glyph) && glyph.Win32FamilyNames.Values.Any(name => name.StartsWith("Segoe UI Variable", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                return new FontFamily(candidate + ", Segoe UI, Microsoft YaHei UI, Microsoft YaHei, Global User Interface");
        }
        return new FontFamily("Segoe UI, Microsoft YaHei UI, Microsoft YaHei, Global User Interface");
    }

    private static void ApplyPalette()
    {
        var app = initializedApplication;
        if (app is null || app.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) return;
        var theme = SystemParameters.HighContrast ? "HighContrast" : Preference == "System" ? ReadSystemTheme() : Preference;
        if (theme == effectiveTheme) return;
        effectiveTheme = theme;
        foreach (var pair in theme == "Dark" ? dark : light)
        {
            var brush = new SolidColorBrush((Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(pair.Value));
            brush.Freeze(); app.Resources[pair.Key] = brush;
        }
        if (theme == "HighContrast") ApplyHighContrast(app.Resources);
        var shadow = new DropShadowEffect { BlurRadius = 8, ShadowDepth = 1, Direction = 270,
            Color = ((SolidColorBrush)app.Resources["Shadow"]).Color, Opacity = theme == "HighContrast" ? 0 : theme == "Dark" ? 0.12 : 0.045, RenderingBias = RenderingBias.Performance };
        shadow.Freeze(); app.Resources["CardShadow"] = shadow;
        app.Resources["IsDarkTheme"] = theme == "Dark";
        app.Resources["IsHighContrastTheme"] = theme == "HighContrast";
        if (Changed is not { } handlers) return;
        foreach (EventHandler handler in handlers.GetInvocationList())
            try { handler(null, EventArgs.Empty); }
            catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("Theme observer: {0}", error.Message); }
    }

    private static string ReadSystemTheme()
    {
        try { return Registry.GetValue(@"HKEY_CURRENT_USER\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize", "AppsUseLightTheme", 1) is int value && value == 0 ? "Dark" : "Light"; }
        catch (Exception error) { Trace.TraceWarning("System theme fallback: {0}", error.Message); return "Light"; }
    }

    private static void ApplyHighContrast(ResourceDictionary resources)
    {
        foreach (var key in new[] { "Background", "Surface", "SoftSurface", "HoverSurface", "PressedSurface", "AccentTint", "SuccessTint", "WarningTint", "DangerTint", "DangerHover", "DangerPressed" })
            resources[key] = SystemColors.WindowBrush;
        foreach (var key in new[] { "Text", "Muted", "Success", "Warning", "Danger", "OnDanger", "StrongBorder", "Border" }) resources[key] = SystemColors.WindowTextBrush;
        foreach (var key in new[] { "Accent", "AccentHover", "AccentPressed", "AccentBorder", "FocusBorder", "SelectionBackground" }) resources[key] = SystemColors.HighlightBrush;
        resources["OnAccent"] = resources["SelectionText"] = SystemColors.HighlightTextBrush;
        resources["DisabledText"] = SystemColors.GrayTextBrush;
    }

    private static void SystemPreferenceChanged(object sender, UserPreferenceChangedEventArgs args) => ScheduleRefresh();
    private static void ApplicationActivated(object? sender, EventArgs args) => ScheduleRefresh();
    private static void SystemParameterChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs args)
    { if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(args.PropertyName) || args.PropertyName == nameof(SystemParameters.HighContrast)) ScheduleRefresh(); }
    private static void ScheduleRefresh()
    {
        var app = initializedApplication;
        if (app is null || app.Dispatcher.HasShutdownStarted) return;
        app.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(ApplyPalette));
    }
    private static void ApplicationExited(object sender, ExitEventArgs args)
    {
        if (subscriptionsActive)
        {
            SystemEvents.UserPreferenceChanged -= SystemPreferenceChanged;
            SystemParameters.StaticPropertyChanged -= SystemParameterChanged;
            subscriptionsActive = false;
        }
        if (initializedApplication is not null) { initializedApplication.Exit -= ApplicationExited; initializedApplication.Activated -= ApplicationActivated; }
        initializedApplication = null;
    }

    private static Dictionary<string, string> DefaultLight() => new()
    {
        ["Background"] = "#F5F6F8", ["Surface"] = "#FFFFFF", ["Text"] = "#20242C", ["Muted"] = "#68717E",
        ["Accent"] = "#2864DC", ["OnAccent"] = "#FFFFFF", ["AccentHover"] = "#235ACA", ["AccentPressed"] = "#1E4EB0",
        ["SoftSurface"] = "#EDF0F5", ["HoverSurface"] = "#E7EDF8", ["PressedSurface"] = "#DDE6F6", ["Border"] = "#E2E6ED",
        ["StrongBorder"] = "#BBC4D1", ["AccentTint"] = "#EAF0FF", ["AccentBorder"] = "#B8CDF7", ["FocusBorder"] = "#2864DC",
        ["Success"] = "#27765A", ["SuccessTint"] = "#E8F4EE", ["Warning"] = "#966023", ["WarningTint"] = "#FCF1DF",
        ["Danger"] = "#B53E45", ["OnDanger"] = "#B53E45", ["DangerTint"] = "#FCECEE", ["DangerHover"] = "#F8E0E3", ["DangerPressed"] = "#F1D2D7",
        ["DisabledText"] = "#9199A6", ["SelectionBackground"] = "#EAF0FF", ["SelectionText"] = "#20242C", ["Shadow"] = "#0C172A", ["Scrim"] = "#300C172A"
    };
    private static Dictionary<string, string> DefaultDark() => new()
    {
        ["Background"] = "#17191E", ["Surface"] = "#23262D", ["Text"] = "#EEF0F4", ["Muted"] = "#A7B0BF",
        ["Accent"] = "#82AAFF", ["OnAccent"] = "#142037", ["AccentHover"] = "#96B8FF", ["AccentPressed"] = "#6C98F2",
        ["SoftSurface"] = "#2D313A", ["HoverSurface"] = "#343C4C", ["PressedSurface"] = "#3B465C", ["Border"] = "#343944",
        ["StrongBorder"] = "#596478", ["AccentTint"] = "#293B5D", ["AccentBorder"] = "#4A6592", ["FocusBorder"] = "#82AAFF",
        ["Success"] = "#85D0AD", ["SuccessTint"] = "#243D33", ["Warning"] = "#E6BF81", ["WarningTint"] = "#443724",
        ["Danger"] = "#F59A9F", ["OnDanger"] = "#F59A9F", ["DangerTint"] = "#442D34", ["DangerHover"] = "#50323B", ["DangerPressed"] = "#5F3843",
        ["DisabledText"] = "#758090", ["SelectionBackground"] = "#293B5D", ["SelectionText"] = "#EEF0F4", ["Shadow"] = "#000000", ["Scrim"] = "#80000000"
    };
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/ArchiveJobViewModel.cs ---
using System;
using System.IO;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed class ArchiveJobViewModel : ObservableObject
{
    public Guid Id { get; } = Guid.NewGuid();
    public string SourcePath { get; }
    public ArchiveOperation Operation { get; }
    public bool IsDirectory { get; }
    public long SourceBytes { get; }
    public string Name => Path.GetFileName(SourcePath.TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar));
    public bool RequiresPassword { get; }
    public bool IsExtraction => Operation == ArchiveOperation.Extract;
    public bool CanSetPassword => CanEdit && IsExtraction && Status is JobStatus.Pending or JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled;
    private string password = "";
    public string Password
    {
        get => password;
        set { if (CanEdit && Set(ref password, value ?? "")) { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasPassword)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PasswordHint)); } }
    }
    public bool HasPassword => password.Length > 0;
    public string PasswordHint => HasPassword ? Locale.T("修改密码") : Locale.T("设置密码");
    public void ClearPassword() { password = ""; OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasPassword)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PasswordHint)); }
    private bool canEdit = true;
    public bool CanEdit { get => canEdit; set { if (Set(ref canEdit, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanSetPassword)); } }
    private string plan = "";
    public string Plan { get => plan; set => Set(ref plan, value); }
    private JobStatus status = JobStatus.Pending;
    public JobStatus Status { get => status; set { if (Set(ref status, value)) NotifyStatus(); } }
    private double progress;
    public double Progress { get => progress; set { if (Set(ref progress, Math.Clamp(value, 0, 100))) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private bool isIndeterminate;
    public bool IsIndeterminate { get => isIndeterminate; set { if (Set(ref isIndeterminate, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private string stage = "";
    public string Stage { get => stage; set { if (Set(ref stage, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private string? outputPath;
    public string? OutputPath { get => outputPath; set { if (Set(ref outputPath, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasOutput)); } }
    public bool HasOutput => Status == JobStatus.Completed && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(OutputPath);
    public ArchiveOutputKind OutputKind { get; set; }
    private ConversionError? error;
    public ConversionError? Error { get => error; set { if (Set(ref error, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasError)); } }
    public bool HasError => Error is not null;
    private ArchiveStatistics? statistics;
    public ArchiveStatistics? Statistics { get => statistics; set { if (Set(ref statistics, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ResultSizeText)); } }
    private string SavingsText => Statistics is not { SourceBytes: > 0 } ? "" : Statistics.ReductionPercent >= 0
        ? Locale.F($" · 节省 {Statistics.ReductionPercent:0.#}%") : Locale.F($" · 增加 {-Statistics.ReductionPercent:0.#}%");
    public string ResultSizeText => Status == JobStatus.Completed && Statistics is not null
        ? Operation == ArchiveOperation.Create
            ? Locale.F($"{ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(Statistics.SourceBytes)} → {ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(Statistics.OutputBytes)}{SavingsText} · {Statistics.EntryCount} 个条目")
            : Locale.F($"已解压 {Statistics.EntryCount} 个条目 · {ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(Statistics.OutputBytes)}")
        : IsDirectory ? Locale.T("文件夹 · 包含子文件夹") : $"{ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(SourceBytes)}" + (RequiresPassword ? Locale.T(" · 需要密码") : "");
    public string StatusText => Status switch
    {
        JobStatus.Processing => IsIndeterminate ? StageText : $"{StageText} {Math.Min(99, Math.Floor(Progress)):0}%",
        JobStatus.Completed => Locale.T("已完成"),
        JobStatus.Failed => Locale.T("遇到问题"),
        JobStatus.Cancelled => Locale.T("已停止"),
        _ => Locale.T("待处理")
    };
    private string StageText => Stage switch
    {
        "snapshot" => Locale.T("准备文件"), "compressing" => Locale.T("正在打包"), "verifying" => Locale.T("正在校验"),
        "splitting" => Locale.T("生成分卷"), "extracting" => Locale.T("正在解压"), "decompressing" => Locale.T("读取压缩包"),
        "completed" => Locale.T("正在保存"), _ => Locale.T("处理中")
    };
    public ArchiveJobViewModel(string sourcePath, ArchiveOperation operation, bool isDirectory, long sourceBytes, bool requiresPassword = false)
    { SourcePath = sourcePath; Operation = operation; IsDirectory = isDirectory; SourceBytes = sourceBytes; RequiresPassword = requiresPassword; }
    private void NotifyStatus()
    { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ResultSizeText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasOutput)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanSetPassword)); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/ConversionJobViewModel.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed class ConversionJobViewModel : ObservableObject
{
    public Guid Id { get; } = Guid.NewGuid();
    public string SourcePath { get; }
    public string Name => Path.GetFileName(SourcePath);
    public string SourceType => Path.GetExtension(SourcePath).TrimStart('.').ToUpperInvariant() + (IsAnimated ? Locale.T(" · 动图") : "");
    public MediaInfo Media { get; }
    public long SourceBytes { get; }
    public string SizeText => FormatSize(SourceBytes);
    public bool IsCompression { get; }
    public bool IsGif => Preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Gif;
    public bool IsAnimated => Media.Animation?.IsAnimated == true;
    public ConversionMode MediaMode => Preset.Mode;
    public IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> CompressionPresets { get; } = Array.Empty<FormatPreset>();
    public FormatPreset? SourceCompressionPreset { get; }
    private bool compressionConfigured;
    private FormatPreset preset;
    public FormatPreset Preset
    {
        get => preset;
        set
        {
            if (!Set(ref preset, value)) return;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FormatName));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedCompressionPreset));
        }
    }
    public FormatPreset? SelectedCompressionPreset
    {
        get => IsCompression && compressionConfigured ? Preset : null;
        set
        {
            if (!CanConfigure || value is null || !CompressionPresets.Contains(value)) return;
            Preset = value;
            compressionConfigured = true;
            if (compression is not null)
                compression = compression with { PreserveSourceFormat = SourceCompressionPreset?.Id == value.Id };
            NotifyConfiguration();
        }
    }
    public bool IsConfigured => !IsCompression || compressionConfigured;
    public bool NeedsFormatSelection => IsCompression && !IsConfigured;
    public bool ShowFormatPicker => IsCompression && CanConfigure;
    public string FormatName => IsGif ? Locale.T("GIF · 动图") : IsCompression
        ? !IsConfigured ? Locale.T("请选择输出格式") : Preset.Extension.ToUpperInvariant() + (IsAnimated ? Locale.T(" · 保留动画") : SourceCompressionPreset?.Id == Preset.Id ? Locale.T(" · 保留格式") : "")
        : Locale.T(Preset.Name);
    public string FormatSelectionHint => NeedsFormatSelection ? CompressionPresets.Count == 0 ? Locale.T("没有可保留文件内容的压缩格式，请移除此任务。") : Locale.T("原格式不适合此压缩方式，请为文件选择输出格式。") : IsAnimated ? Locale.T("完整保留动画") : "";
    private CompressionOptions? compression;
    public CompressionOptions? Compression => compression;
    private GifOptions? gif;
    public GifOptions? Gif => gif;
    public bool HasTargetSummary => IsCompression || IsGif;
    public string TargetSummary => IsGif ? $"{GifQualityName(gif?.Quality ?? GifQualityLevel.Medium)} · GIF" : compression is null ? "" : compression.Mode == CompressionMode.TargetSize
        ? Locale.F($"每个文件 ≤ {FormatSize(compression.MaximumBytes ?? 0)}")
        : Locale.F($"{LevelName(compression.Level)} · ≤原体积 {compression.Ratio:P0}");
    public void ApplyCompression(CompressionOptions value)
    {
        if (!IsCompression || !CanConfigure) return;
        compression = value with { PreserveSourceFormat = compression?.PreserveSourceFormat ?? true };
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Compression));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TargetSummary));
    }
    public void ApplyGif(GifOptions value)
    {
        if (!IsGif || !CanConfigure) return;
        gif = value;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Gif));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TargetSummary));
    }
    private bool canEdit = true;
    public bool CanEdit
    {
        get => canEdit;
        set { if (Set(ref canEdit, value)) NotifyConfiguration(); }
    }
    public bool CanConfigure => (IsCompression || IsGif) && CanEdit && Status is JobStatus.Pending or JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled;
    private JobStatus status = JobStatus.Pending;
    public JobStatus Status
    {
        get => status;
        set
        {
            if (!Set(ref status, value)) return;
            if (value != JobStatus.Processing) IsIndeterminate = false;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ResultSizeText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasOutput)); NotifyConfiguration();
        }
    }
    private string? outputPath;
    public string? OutputPath { get => outputPath; set { if (Set(ref outputPath, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasOutput)); } }
    public bool HasOutput => Status == JobStatus.Completed && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(OutputPath);
    private ConversionError? error;
    public ConversionError? Error { get => error; set { if (Set(ref error, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasError)); } }
    public bool HasError => Error is not null;
    private double progress;
    public double Progress { get => progress; set { if (Set(ref progress, Math.Clamp(value, 0, 100))) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private bool isIndeterminate;
    public bool IsIndeterminate { get => isIndeterminate; set { if (Set(ref isIndeterminate, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private int attempt = 1;
    public int Attempt { get => attempt; set { if (Set(ref attempt, Math.Max(1, value))) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private CompressionStatistics? statistics;
    public CompressionStatistics? Statistics
    {
        get => statistics;
        set
        {
            if (!Set(ref statistics, value)) return;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(OutputBytes)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ResultSizeText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText));
        }
    }
    private GifStatistics? gifStatistics;
    public GifStatistics? GifStatistics
    {
        get => gifStatistics;
        set
        {
            if (!Set(ref gifStatistics, value)) return;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(OutputBytes)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ResultSizeText));
        }
    }
    public long? OutputBytes => GifStatistics?.OutputBytes ?? Statistics?.OutputBytes;
    public string ResultSizeText => IsGif && Status == JobStatus.Completed && GifStatistics is not null
        ? $"{FormatSize(GifStatistics.SourceBytes)} → {FormatSize(GifStatistics.OutputBytes)}"
        : IsCompression && Status == JobStatus.Completed && Statistics is not null
        ? $"{FormatSize(Statistics.SourceBytes)} → {FormatSize(Statistics.OutputBytes)} · " +
            (Statistics.ReductionPercent >= 0 ? Locale.F($"减少 {Statistics.ReductionPercent:0.#}%") : Locale.F($"增大 {-Statistics.ReductionPercent:0.#}%"))
        : $"{SourceType} · {SizeText}";
    public string StatusText => Status switch
    {
        JobStatus.Processing when Attempt > 1 => Locale.F($"校准大小 · 第{Attempt}轮"),
        JobStatus.Processing when IsIndeterminate => Locale.T("处理中"),
        JobStatus.Processing => Locale.F($"处理中 {Math.Min(99, Math.Floor(Progress)):0}%"),
        JobStatus.Completed when Statistics?.AlreadyWithinLimit == true => Locale.T("已符合上限"),
        JobStatus.Completed => Locale.T("已完成"),
        JobStatus.Failed => Locale.T("遇到问题"),
        JobStatus.Cancelled => Locale.T("已停止"),
        _ when NeedsFormatSelection => Locale.T("待选格式"),
        _ => Locale.T("待处理")
    };
    public ConversionJobViewModel(string path, MediaInfo media, FormatPreset preset, GifOptions? gif = null)
    {
        SourcePath = path; Media = media; this.preset = preset;
        this.gif = preset.Mode == ConversionMode.Gif ? gif ?? new GifOptions() : null;
        SourceBytes = media.SourceBytes > 0 ? media.SourceBytes : new FileInfo(path).Length;
    }
    public ConversionJobViewModel(string path, MediaInfo media, ConversionMode mediaMode, CompressionOptions options,
        IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset> candidates, FormatPreset? sourcePreset)
    {
        SourcePath = path; Media = media; SourceBytes = media.SourceBytes > 0 ? media.SourceBytes : new FileInfo(path).Length;
        IsCompression = true; compression = options;
        SourceCompressionPreset = IsAnimated && sourcePreset is not null ? AnimationLabel(sourcePreset) : sourcePreset;
        var formats = candidates.ToList();
        if (SourceCompressionPreset is not null && formats.All(p => p.Id != SourceCompressionPreset.Id)) formats.Insert(0, SourceCompressionPreset);
        if (IsAnimated) formats = formats.Select(AnimationLabel).ToList();
        CompressionPresets = formats;
        preset = SourceCompressionPreset ?? new FormatPreset("compression-unconfigured", Locale.T("请选择输出格式"), "", "", mediaMode, "", "");
        compressionConfigured = SourceCompressionPreset is not null;
    }
    private static FormatPreset AnimationLabel(FormatPreset value) => value with { Name = value.Codec == "gif" ? Locale.T("GIF · 保留动画") : Locale.T("动态 WebP · 保留动画") };
    private void NotifyConfiguration()
    {
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(IsConfigured), nameof(NeedsFormatSelection), nameof(CanConfigure), nameof(ShowFormatPicker), nameof(FormatSelectionHint), nameof(FormatName), nameof(SelectedCompressionPreset), nameof(StatusText) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
    }
    public static string LevelName(CompressionLevel value) => value switch
    { CompressionLevel.Large => Locale.T("大"), CompressionLevel.Small => Locale.T("小"), CompressionLevel.Tiny => Locale.T("超小"), _ => Locale.T("中") };
    public static string GifQualityName(GifQualityLevel value) => value switch
    { GifQualityLevel.Original => Locale.T("原画"), GifQualityLevel.High => Locale.T("高"), GifQualityLevel.Low => Locale.T("低"), GifQualityLevel.VeryLow => Locale.T("极低"), _ => Locale.T("中") };
    public static string FormatSize(long length) => length >= 1000000000 ? $"{length / 1000000000d:0.##} GB"
        : length >= 1000000 ? $"{length / 1000000d:0.##} MB" : $"{length / 1000d:0.##} KB";
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/ImageObfuscationJobViewModel.cs ---
using System;
using System.IO;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed class ImageObfuscationJobViewModel : ObservableObject
{
    public Guid Id { get; } = Guid.NewGuid();
    public string SourcePath { get; }
    public string Name => Path.GetFileName(SourcePath);
    public long SourceBytes { get; }
    public ImageObfuscationOperation Operation { get; }
    public ImageObfuscationInfo Info { get; }
    public bool UsesStoredOptions => Operation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate && Info.IsMarked;
    private ImageObfuscationOptions options;
    public ImageObfuscationOptions Options => options;
    public string Plan => Locale.F($"{MainViewModel.ImageObfuscationModeName(Options.Mode)} · {Options.Iterations} 次") + (UsesStoredOptions ? Locale.T(" · 自动识别") : "");
    public string InputDescription => $"{Statistics?.Width ?? Info.Width} × {Statistics?.Height ?? Info.Height}" + (Info.HasAlpha ? Locale.T(" · 含透明区域") : "");
    private JobStatus status = JobStatus.Pending;
    public JobStatus Status { get => status; private set { if (Set(ref status, value)) NotifyState(); } }
    private double progress;
    public double Progress { get => progress; set { if (Set(ref progress, Math.Clamp(value, 0, 100))) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private bool isIndeterminate;
    public bool IsIndeterminate { get => isIndeterminate; set { if (Set(ref isIndeterminate, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private string stage = "";
    public string Stage { get => stage; set { if (Set(ref stage, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } }
    private string? outputPath;
    public string? OutputPath { get => outputPath; private set { if (Set(ref outputPath, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasOutput)); } }
    public bool HasOutput => Status == JobStatus.Completed && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(OutputPath);
    private ConversionError? error;
    public ConversionError? Error { get => error; private set { if (Set(ref error, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasError)); } }
    public bool HasError => Error is not null;
    private ImageObfuscationStatistics? statistics;
    public ImageObfuscationStatistics? Statistics { get => statistics; private set { if (Set(ref statistics, value)) { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ResultSizeText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(InputDescription)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } } }
    public string ResultSizeText => Statistics is not null && Status == JobStatus.Completed
        ? $"{ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(Statistics.SourceBytes)} → {ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(Statistics.OutputBytes)}"
        : ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(SourceBytes);
    public string StatusText => Status switch
    {
        JobStatus.Completed => Statistics?.Verified == true ? Locale.T("已完成 · 校验通过") : Locale.T("已处理，未校验原图"),
        JobStatus.Failed => Locale.T("遇到问题"),
        JobStatus.Cancelled => Locale.T("已停止"),
        JobStatus.Processing => Stage == "cancelling" ? Locale.T("正在停止") : IsIndeterminate ? StageText : $"{StageText} {Math.Min(99, Math.Floor(Progress)):0}%",
        _ => Locale.T("待处理")
    };
    private string StageText => Stage switch
    {
        "checking" or "inspect" or "decoding" or "检查图片" => Locale.T("检查图片"),
        "读取像素" => Locale.T("读取像素"),
        "重排像素" => Locale.T("重排像素"),
        "verifying" or "校验输出像素" => Locale.T("正在校验"),
        "saving" or "encoding" or "committing" or "保存无损 PNG" => Locale.T("正在保存"),
        _ => Operation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate ? Locale.T("正在混淆") : Locale.T("正在解混淆")
    };
    public ImageObfuscationJobViewModel(string sourcePath, long sourceBytes, ImageObfuscationOperation operation,
        ImageObfuscationInfo info, ImageObfuscationOptions selectedOptions)
    {
        SourcePath = sourcePath; SourceBytes = sourceBytes; Operation = operation; Info = info;
        options = UsesStoredOptions && info.StoredMode.HasValue ? new(info.StoredMode.Value, info.StoredIterations) : selectedOptions;
    }
    public void ApplyOptions(ImageObfuscationOptions value)
    {
        if (UsesStoredOptions || Status is not (JobStatus.Pending or JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled)) return;
        options = value; OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Options)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Plan));
    }
    public void Begin()
    { OutputPath = null; Error = null; Statistics = null; Progress = 0; Stage = "checking"; IsIndeterminate = true; Status = JobStatus.Processing; }
    public void Apply(ImageObfuscationResult result)
    {
        // Store the full committed outcome before notifying presentation subscribers.
        outputPath = result.OutputPath; error = result.Error; statistics = result.Statistics;
        isIndeterminate = false; status = result.Status; if (status == JobStatus.Completed) progress = 100;
        if (result.Statistics is { } actual) options = new(actual.Mode, actual.Iterations);
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(OutputPath), nameof(Error), nameof(Statistics), nameof(IsIndeterminate), nameof(Status), nameof(Progress),
            nameof(Options), nameof(Plan), nameof(InputDescription), nameof(ResultSizeText), nameof(StatusText), nameof(HasError), nameof(HasOutput) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
    }
    public void Reset()
    { OutputPath = null; Error = null; Statistics = null; Progress = 0; Stage = ""; IsIndeterminate = false; Status = JobStatus.Pending; }
    private void NotifyState()
    { foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(StatusText), nameof(ResultSizeText), nameof(HasOutput) }) OnPropertyChanged(name); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.Archives.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Globalization;

using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed record ArchiveChoice<T>(T Value, string Name);

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    private IArchiveEngine archiveEngine = null!;
    private ArchiveImportService archiveImporter = null!;
    private bool injectedArchiveEngine;
    private ArchiveOperation? archiveOperation;
    public bool IsArchivePage => archiveOperation.HasValue;
    public bool IsMediaPage => !IsArchivePage && !IsPasswordRecoveryPage && !IsImageObfuscationPage;
    public bool IsArchiveCreatePage => archiveOperation == ArchiveOperation.Create;
    public bool IsArchiveExtractPage => archiveOperation == ArchiveOperation.Extract;
    public bool ArchiveOptionsEditable => CanEdit;
    public ObservableCollection<ArchiveJobViewModel> ArchiveQueue { get; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<ArchiveJobViewModel> VisibleArchiveQueue { get; } = new();
    public IReadOnlyList<string> ArchiveInputs => VisibleArchiveQueue.Select(j => j.SourcePath).ToArray();
    public IReadOnlyList<ArchiveChoice<ArchiveFormat>> ArchiveFormats { get; } = new[]
    { new ArchiveChoice<ArchiveFormat>(ArchiveFormat.Zip, Locale.T("ZIP · 通用兼容")), new(ArchiveFormat.SevenZip, Locale.T("7Z · 更高压缩")), new(ArchiveFormat.Tar, Locale.T("TAR · 仅打包")), new(ArchiveFormat.TarGZip, Locale.T("TAR.GZ · 压缩打包")) };
    public ObservableCollection<ArchiveChoice<ArchiveLevel>> ArchiveLevels { get; } = new();
    private ArchiveFormat selectedArchiveFormat;
    public ArchiveFormat SelectedArchiveFormat
    {
        get => selectedArchiveFormat;
        set
        {
            if (!CanEdit || !Enum.IsDefined(value) || !Set(ref selectedArchiveFormat, value)) return;
            settings.ArchiveFormat = value;
            if (!ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsPassword(value)) ClearArchivePassword();
            if (!ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsSplit(value)) archiveSplitEnabled = false;
            RefreshArchiveLevels();
            ArchiveSettingsChanged();
        }
    }
    private ArchiveLevel selectedArchiveLevel;
    public ArchiveLevel SelectedArchiveLevel
    {
        get => selectedArchiveLevel;
        set { if (CanEdit && ArchiveCapabilities.Levels(SelectedArchiveFormat).Contains(value) && Set(ref selectedArchiveLevel, value)) { settings.ArchiveLevel = value; ArchiveSettingsChanged(); } }
    }
    private ArchiveGrouping selectedArchiveGrouping;
    public ArchiveGrouping SelectedArchiveGrouping
    {
        get => selectedArchiveGrouping;
        set { if (CanEdit && Enum.IsDefined(value) && Set(ref selectedArchiveGrouping, value)) { settings.ArchiveGrouping = value; ArchiveSettingsChanged(); } }
    }
    public bool CombinedArchiveSelected => SelectedArchiveGrouping == ArchiveGrouping.Combined;
    public bool SeparateArchiveSelected => SelectedArchiveGrouping == ArchiveGrouping.Separate;
    private string archiveName = "文件压缩包";
    public string ArchiveName { get => archiveName; set { if (CanEdit && Set(ref archiveName, value ?? "")) ArchiveSettingsChanged(); } }
    private bool archiveSplitEnabled;
    public bool ArchiveSplitEnabled
    {
        get => archiveSplitEnabled;
        set { if (CanEdit && Set(ref archiveSplitEnabled, value && ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsSplit(SelectedArchiveFormat))) ArchiveSettingsChanged(); }
    }
    private string archiveSplitMiB = "100";
    public string ArchiveSplitMiB { get => archiveSplitMiB; set { if (CanEdit && Set(ref archiveSplitMiB, value ?? "")) ArchiveSettingsChanged(); } }
    public bool ArchiveSupportsSplit => ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsSplit(SelectedArchiveFormat);
    public bool ArchiveSupportsPassword => IsArchiveExtractPage || ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsPassword(SelectedArchiveFormat);
    private bool archivePasswordEnabled;
    public bool ArchivePasswordEnabled
    {
        get => archivePasswordEnabled;
        set
        {
            if (!CanEdit || !Set(ref archivePasswordEnabled, value && ArchiveSupportsPassword)) return;
            if (!archivePasswordEnabled) ClearArchivePassword();
            ArchiveSettingsChanged();
        }
    }
    private string archivePassword = "";
    public string ArchivePassword
    {
        get => archivePassword;
        set
        {
            if (!CanEdit) return;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && ArchiveSupportsPassword)
            { archivePasswordEnabled = true; OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchivePasswordEnabled)); }
            SetArchivePassword(value);
        }
    }
    public event EventHandler? ArchivePasswordCleared;
    public void SetArchivePassword(string value)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        archivePassword = value ?? "";
        Refresh();
    }
    public void ClearArchivePassword()
    {
        archivePassword = "";
        archivePasswordEnabled = false;
        foreach (var row in ArchiveQueue) row.ClearPassword();
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchivePasswordEnabled));
        ArchivePasswordCleared?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    private string? CurrentArchivePassword => ArchivePasswordEnabled && archivePassword.Length > 0 ? archivePassword : null;
    public string ArchiveEngineDllPath => settings.ArchiveEngineDllPath ?? Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "tools", "7zip", "bin", "7z.dll");
    public bool IsArchiveEngineReady => injectedArchiveEngine || archiveEngine is ArchiveEngine { IsReady: true };
    public string ArchiveEngineStatus => IsArchiveEngineReady ? Locale.T("压缩包引擎已就绪 · 7-Zip") : Locale.T("压缩包引擎不完整，请在设置中查看");
    public ArchiveLimits ArchiveExtractionLimits => new(settings.ArchiveMaximumEntries, settings.ArchiveMaximumDepth, settings.ArchiveMaximumBytes);
    public string ArchiveLimitsSummary => Locale.F($"解压保护：最多 {settings.ArchiveMaximumEntries:N0} 个条目 · {settings.ArchiveMaximumDepth} 层 · {settings.ArchiveMaximumBytes / (1024d * 1024 * 1024):0.##} GiB");
    private string ArchiveCreatePlan => $"{ArchiveCapabilities.Name(SelectedArchiveFormat)} · {ArchiveLevelName(SelectedArchiveLevel)} · {(CombinedArchiveSelected ? Locale.T("合并打包") : Locale.T("分别打包"))}";
    public string ArchiveFormatSummary => IsArchiveExtractPage ? Locale.T("每个压缩包解压到独立文件夹") : ArchiveCreatePlan;
    public string ArchiveDescription => SelectedArchiveFormat switch
    {
        ArchiveFormat.Zip => Locale.T("ZIP 无密码时适合常见解压工具；启用密码后使用 AES-256，Windows 内置解压可能无法打开，文件名仍可见。"),
        ArchiveFormat.SevenZip => Locale.T("7Z 使用 LZMA2；启用密码后同时加密文件名。分卷请保留全部 .001、.002 等文件。"),
        ArchiveFormat.Tar => Locale.T("TAR 只打包，不压缩；不支持归档密码和分卷。"),
        _ => Locale.T("先打包为 TAR，再使用 GZip 压缩；不支持归档密码和分卷。")
    };
    public string ArchiveInputError
    {
        get
        {
            if (!IsArchivePage) return "";
            if (!IsArchiveEngineReady) return Locale.T("请在设置中确认压缩包引擎已就绪。");
            if (ArchivePasswordEnabled && archivePassword.Length == 0) return Locale.T("请填写密码，或关闭密码选项。");
            if (ArchivePasswordEnabled && archivePassword.IndexOfAny(new[] { '\0', '\r', '\n' }) >= 0) return Locale.T("密码不能包含换行或空字符。");
            if (IsArchiveCreatePage && SelectedArchiveFormat == ArchiveFormat.Zip && ArchivePasswordEnabled && (archivePassword.Length > 99 || archivePassword.Any(c => c < 32 || c > 126))) return Locale.T("ZIP 密码限 1–99 个英文、数字或符号；中文或 emoji 密码请选择 7Z。");
            if (IsArchiveCreatePage && SelectedArchiveFormat == ArchiveFormat.SevenZip && ArchivePasswordEnabled && archivePassword.Length > 1024) return Locale.T("7Z 密码最多 1024 个字符。");
            if (IsArchiveExtractPage && VisibleArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending && j.RequiresPassword && !j.HasPassword) && CurrentArchivePassword is null) return Locale.T("队列包含加密压缩包，请填写解压密码或为该项设置密码。");
            if (IsArchiveExtractPage && VisibleArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending && j.Password.IndexOfAny(new[] { '\0', '\r', '\n' }) >= 0)) return Locale.T("密码不能包含换行或空字符。");
            if (IsArchiveCreatePage && CombinedArchiveSelected && (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ArchiveName) || ArchiveName.IndexOfAny(Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars()) >= 0 || ArchiveName.EndsWith('.') || ArchiveName.EndsWith(' '))) return Locale.T("请填写有效的压缩包名称（无需扩展名）。");
            if (IsArchiveCreatePage && CombinedArchiveSelected && (ArchiveName.Length > 240 || System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(ArchiveName.Trim(), @"^(CON|PRN|AUX|NUL|CLOCK\$|COM[1-9¹²³]|LPT[1-9¹²³])(?:\.|$)", System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.CultureInvariant))) return Locale.T("请使用不超过 240 个字符的普通名称，不能使用 CON、COM1 等系统保留名称。");
            if (IsArchiveCreatePage && ArchiveSplitEnabled && !TryArchiveSplitBytes(out _)) return Locale.T("每卷大小需为 1–102400 MiB（最多 100 GiB，可带小数）。");
            return "";
        }
    }
    private bool TryArchiveSplitBytes(out long bytes)
    {
        bytes = 0;
        if (!decimal.TryParse(ArchiveSplitMiB.Trim(), NumberStyles.AllowDecimalPoint, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var mib) || mib < 1 || mib > 102400m) return false;
        bytes = (long)decimal.Floor(mib * 1048576m);
        return bytes > 0;
    }
    public static string ArchiveLevelName(ArchiveLevel level) => level switch { ArchiveLevel.Store => Locale.T("存储"), ArchiveLevel.Fast => Locale.T("快速"), ArchiveLevel.Ultra => Locale.T("高压缩"), _ => Locale.T("标准") };
    private void InitializeArchives(IArchiveEngine? suppliedEngine)
    {
        injectedArchiveEngine = suppliedEngine is not null;
        archiveEngine = suppliedEngine ?? new ArchiveEngine(ArchiveEngineDllPath, log: log);
        archiveImporter = new ArchiveImportService(archiveEngine);
        selectedArchiveFormat = settings.ArchiveFormat;
        selectedArchiveLevel = settings.ArchiveLevel;
        selectedArchiveGrouping = settings.ArchiveGrouping;
        archiveSplitMiB = settings.ArchiveSplitMiB.ToString("0.############################", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        archiveSplitEnabled = settings.ArchiveSplitEnabled && ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsSplit(selectedArchiveFormat);
        RefreshArchiveLevels();
        ArchiveQueue.CollectionChanged += (_, _) => { RefreshVisibleArchives(); Refresh(); };
    }
    private void RefreshArchiveLevels()
    {
        var allowed = ArchiveCapabilities.Levels(SelectedArchiveFormat);
        if (!allowed.Contains(selectedArchiveLevel)) selectedArchiveLevel = allowed.Contains(ArchiveLevel.Standard) ? ArchiveLevel.Standard : allowed[0];
        ArchiveLevels.Clear();
        foreach (var level in allowed) ArchiveLevels.Add(new(level, ArchiveLevelName(level)));
        settings.ArchiveLevel = selectedArchiveLevel;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedArchiveLevel));
    }
    private void ArchiveSettingsChanged()
    {
        settings.ArchiveSplitEnabled = ArchiveSplitEnabled;
        if (TryArchiveSplitBytes(out _)) settings.ArchiveSplitMiB = decimal.Parse(ArchiveSplitMiB.Trim(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        foreach (var row in ArchiveQueue.Where(j => j.Operation == ArchiveOperation.Create && j.Status == JobStatus.Pending)) row.Plan = ArchiveCreatePlan;
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(CombinedArchiveSelected), nameof(SeparateArchiveSelected), nameof(ArchiveSupportsSplit), nameof(ArchiveSupportsPassword), nameof(ArchiveSplitEnabled), nameof(ArchiveDescription), nameof(ArchiveFormatSummary), nameof(FormatSummary), nameof(ArchiveInputError) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
        SaveSettings();
        RefreshTaskCenter();
        Refresh();
    }
    public void SetArchivePage(ArchiveOperation operation)
    {
        if (!CanNavigate || !Enum.IsDefined(operation)) return;
        if (!IsProcessing && archiveOperation != operation) ClearArchivePassword();
        archiveOperation = operation;
        imageObfuscationOperation = null;
        SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>();
        RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue();
        isPasswordRecoveryPage = false;
        isCompressionPage = false;
        SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>();
        RefreshVisibleQueue();
        RefreshVisibleArchives();
        NotifyPage();
        if (!IsProcessing) { ArchiveSettingsChanged(); RestorePageFeedback(); }
        else Refresh();
        NotifyViewedFeedback();
    }
    public void SetArchiveCreatePage() => SetArchivePage(ArchiveOperation.Create);
    public void SetArchiveExtractPage() => SetArchivePage(ArchiveOperation.Extract);
    private void RefreshVisibleArchives()
    {
        var visible = ArchiveQueue.Where(j => j.Operation == archiveOperation).ToArray();
        foreach (var row in VisibleArchiveQueue.Where(j => !visible.Contains(j)).ToArray()) VisibleArchiveQueue.Remove(row);
        foreach (var row in visible.Where(j => !VisibleArchiveQueue.Contains(j))) VisibleArchiveQueue.Add(row);
    }
    public Task AddArchiveFilesAsync(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        if (!CanEdit || !IsArchivePage) return Task.CompletedTask;
        activeImport = ImportArchivesAsync(paths.ToArray());
        return activeImport;
    }
    private async Task ImportArchivesAsync(IReadOnlyList<string> paths)
    {
        var operation = archiveOperation!.Value;
        IsImporting = true;
        importCancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var token = importCancellation.Token;
        var rejected = new List<string>();
        var added = 0;
        StatusMessage = Locale.T("正在检查归档输入……");
        try
        {
            var inputs = operation == ArchiveOperation.Extract ? ArchiveImportService.NormalizeExtractionSources(paths) : paths.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToArray();
            foreach (var path in inputs)
            {
                token.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                string source;
                long bytes;
                bool directory = false, requiresPassword = false;
                ConversionError? error;
                if (operation == ArchiveOperation.Create)
                {
                    var inspection = await archiveImporter.InspectCreateAsync(path, token);
                    source = inspection.SourcePath; bytes = inspection.SourceBytes; directory = inspection.IsDirectory; error = inspection.Error;
                }
                else
                {
                    var inspection = await archiveImporter.InspectExtractAsync(path, CurrentArchivePassword, token);
                    source = inspection.SourcePath; bytes = inspection.SourceBytes; requiresPassword = inspection.RequiresPassword; error = inspection.Error;
                }
                if (error is not null) { LastError = error; RejectPetImport(path, error.Message); rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(path)}：{error.Message}"); continue; }
                if (ArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.Operation == operation && j.Status != JobStatus.Completed && string.Equals(j.SourcePath, source, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                { AcceptPetImport(path); continue; }
                var row = new ArchiveJobViewModel(source, operation, directory, bytes, requiresPassword) { Plan = operation == ArchiveOperation.Create ? ArchiveFormatSummary : Locale.T("解压到独立文件夹") };
                row.PropertyChanged += (_, change) => { if (change.PropertyName is nameof(ArchiveJobViewModel.Status) or nameof(ArchiveJobViewModel.HasPassword)) Refresh(); };
                ArchiveQueue.Add(row); AcceptPetImport(path); added++;
            }
            if (rejected.Count > 0)
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这些输入好像遇到了一点问题。");
                LastError = new("archive.input-rejected", string.Join(Environment.NewLine, rejected.Take(8)), Locale.T("请确认输入存在、可以读取，分卷文件在同一文件夹。"), LastError?.DiagnosticId, LastError?.TechnicalDetails);
                StatusMessage = Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个，{rejected.Count} 个无法导入");
                ErrorsChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
            else { if (added > 0) ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = added > 0 ? Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个 · 选好方案即可开始") : Locale.T("这些输入已经在队列中了"); }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { StatusMessage = Locale.T("归档输入检查已停止"); }
        catch (Exception error) { HandleArchiveUnexpectedError(error); }
        finally { importCancellation.Dispose(); importCancellation = null; IsImporting = false; }
    }
    private async Task RunArchiveBatchAsync()
    {
        var batch = VisibleArchiveQueue.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending).ToArray();
        var sourceAction = IsArchiveCreatePage ? FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive : FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive;
        var password = CurrentArchivePassword;
        var options = new ArchiveCreateOptions(SelectedArchiveFormat, SelectedArchiveLevel, ArchiveCapabilities.SupportsPassword(SelectedArchiveFormat) ? password : null,
            ArchiveSplitEnabled && TryArchiveSplitBytes(out var splitBytes) ? splitBytes : null);
        var requests = new List<ArchiveRequest>();
        var rows = new Dictionary<Guid, ArchiveJobViewModel[]>();
        CreateArchiveRequest? combinedRequest = null;
        if (sourceAction == FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive && CombinedArchiveSelected)
        {
            combinedRequest = new CreateArchiveRequest(pendingCombinedTaskId, batch.Select(j => j.SourcePath).ToArray(), OutputDirectory, ArchiveName.Trim(), options);
            requests.Add(combinedRequest); rows[combinedRequest.Id] = batch;
        }
        else
        {
            foreach (var row in batch)
            {
                ArchiveRequest request = row.Operation == ArchiveOperation.Create
                    ? new CreateArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), new[] { row.SourcePath }, OutputDirectory, row.IsDirectory ? row.Name : Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(row.Name), options)
                    : new ExtractArchiveRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), row.SourcePath, OutputDirectory, new(row.HasPassword ? row.Password : password, ArchiveExtractionLimits));
                requests.Add(request); rows[request.Id] = new[] { row };
            }
        }
        IsProcessing = true;
        cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var token = cancellation.Token;
        var batchContext = BeginActiveBatch(sourceAction);
        var petBatch = BeginPetBatch(sourceAction, requests.Count);
        LastError = null;
        try
        {
            if (combinedRequest is not null) RegisterCombinedArchiveTask(combinedRequest.Id, combinedRequest.ArchiveName, batch);
            var outcome = await new SerialArchiveQueue(archiveEngine).RunAsync(requests,
                onStarted: request =>
                {
                    StartPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, request is CreateArchiveRequest create ? create.ArchiveName : rows[request.Id][0].Name, requests.IndexOf(request) + 1);
                    foreach (var row in rows[request.Id]) { row.Status = JobStatus.Processing; row.Progress = 0; row.IsIndeterminate = true; row.Stage = Locale.T("准备中"); row.Error = null; row.Statistics = null; row.OutputPath = null; }
                    AssistantState = AssistantState.PROCESSING;
                    AssistantMessage = request is CreateArchiveRequest ? Locale.T("正在帮你打包文件……") : Locale.T("正在帮你解压文件……");
                    StatusMessage = Locale.F($"处理中 · {requests.IndexOf(request) + 1}/{requests.Count}");
                },
                onCompleted: (request, result) =>
                {
                    CompletePetTask(petBatch, request.Id, result.Status, result.OutputPath, result.OutputKind == ArchiveOutputKind.Directory, result.Error?.Message, result.Status == JobStatus.Completed);
                    ApplyArchiveResult(rows[request.Id], result);
                },
                progressFactory: request => new Progress<ArchiveProgress>(p =>
                {
                    ReportPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, p.Percent, p.Stage);
                    foreach (var row in rows[request.Id].Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Processing))
                    {
                        row.IsIndeterminate = !p.Percent.HasValue || !double.IsFinite(p.Percent.Value);
                        row.Stage = p.Stage ?? Locale.T("处理中");
                        if (p.Percent.HasValue && double.IsFinite(p.Percent.Value)) row.Progress = Math.Max(row.Progress, Math.Min(99, p.Percent.Value));
                    }
                }),
                onObserverError: _ => log.Write("Archive queue view notification failed", Locale.T("归档界面通知失败；密码不写入诊断。")), cancellationToken: token);
            foreach (var entry in outcome.Results)
            {
                CompletePetTask(petBatch, entry.Request.Id, entry.Result.Status, entry.Result.OutputPath, entry.Result.OutputKind == ArchiveOutputKind.Directory, entry.Result.Error?.Message, entry.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed);
                ApplyArchiveResult(rows[entry.Request.Id], entry.Result);
            }
            if (outcome.Cancelled)
            { AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("已停止，准备好了可以继续。"); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已停止 · 尚未开始的任务保留，已停止任务可重试"); }
            else if (outcome.FailedCount > 0)
            { AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这个压缩包好像遇到了一点问题。"); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"本次完成 {outcome.CompletedCount} 个任务，{outcome.FailedCount} 个遇到问题"); }
            else
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.SUCCESS; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("搞定啦！"); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"本次 {outcome.CompletedCount} 个归档任务完成 · 原文件已保留");
                if (outcome.CompletedCount == requests.Count && requests.Count > 0) CelebrateBatchCompletion();
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { StatusMessage = Locale.T("已停止归档处理"); }
        catch (Exception error) { HandleArchiveUnexpectedError(error); foreach (var row in batch.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Processing)) { row.Error = BatchError; row.Status = JobStatus.Failed; } }
        finally
        {
            FinishPetBatch(petBatch, token.IsCancellationRequested);
            password = null;
            cancellation.Dispose(); cancellation = null;
            ClearArchivePassword();
            EndActiveBatch(batchContext);
            IsProcessing = false; Refresh();
        }
    }
    private void ApplyArchiveResult(IEnumerable<ArchiveJobViewModel> rows, ArchiveResult result)
    {
        foreach (var row in rows)
        {
            row.OutputKind = result.OutputKind; row.OutputPath = result.OutputPath; row.Statistics = result.Statistics; row.Error = result.Error; row.IsIndeterminate = false; row.Status = result.Status;
            if (result.Status == JobStatus.Completed) row.Progress = 100;
        }
        if (result.Error is not null) LastError = result.Error;
    }
    private void HandleArchiveUnexpectedError(Exception _)
    {
        var id = log.Write("Archive desktop operation failed", Locale.T("归档操作未完成；为保护密码，不记录异常参数。"));
        LastError = new("archive.desktop-operation", Locale.T("归档操作遇到了问题。"), Locale.T("请确认引擎、文件和保存位置可用，然后重试。"), id);
        AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这个压缩包好像遇到了一点问题。"); StatusMessage = Locale.T("归档操作未完成 · 可以查看原因");
    }
    private void RetryArchiveFailed()
    {
        var retry = VisibleArchiveQueue.Where(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled).ToArray();
        ForgetArchiveTaskGroups(retry);
        foreach (var row in retry) { row.Status = JobStatus.Pending; row.Progress = 0; row.Stage = ""; row.Error = null; row.Statistics = null; row.OutputPath = null; }
        RefreshTaskCenter();
        ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已重新加入待处理队列 · 加密压缩包请重新输入密码");
    }
    public void RemoveArchiveJobs(IEnumerable<ArchiveJobViewModel> rows)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        foreach (var row in rows.Where(j => j.Operation == archiveOperation).ToArray()) { row.ClearPassword(); ArchiveQueue.Remove(row); }
        if (!HasJobs) ResetAssistant();
    }
    private void ClearArchives()
    { ClearArchivePassword(); foreach (var row in VisibleArchiveQueue.ToArray()) ArchiveQueue.Remove(row); ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("准备就绪 · 添加输入以创建") + QueueTitle; }
    public void SetArchiveEngineDllPath(string? path)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        settings.ArchiveEngineDllPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path) ? null : Path.GetFullPath(path);
        if (!injectedArchiveEngine) { archiveEngine = new ArchiveEngine(ArchiveEngineDllPath, log: log); archiveImporter = new ArchiveImportService(archiveEngine); }
        if (!injectedRecoveryEngine) recoveryEngine = new PasswordRecoveryEngine(ArchiveEngineDllPath, log: log);
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsRecoveryEngineReady));
        SaveSettings(); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchiveEngineDllPath)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsArchiveEngineReady)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchiveEngineStatus)); Refresh();
    }
    public void SetArchiveLimits(ArchiveLimits limits)
    {
        if (!CanEdit || limits.MaximumEntries < 1 || limits.MaximumDepth < 1 || limits.MaximumBytes < 1) return;
        settings.ArchiveMaximumEntries = limits.MaximumEntries; settings.ArchiveMaximumDepth = limits.MaximumDepth; settings.ArchiveMaximumBytes = limits.MaximumBytes;
        SaveSettings(); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchiveExtractionLimits)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchiveLimitsSummary));
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.Batches.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    // Browsing a page never changes the feedback or cancellation target of a running batch.
    // Ordinary media conversions intentionally share their existing queue and feedback.
    private readonly Dictionary<FileProcessingAction, PageFeedback> pageFeedback = new();
    private ActiveBatchContext? activeBatch;
    private sealed class PageFeedback
    {
        public AssistantState AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE;
        public string AssistantMessage = Locale.T("把文件交给我吧！");
        public string StatusMessage = Locale.T("准备就绪");
        public ConversionError? LastError;
        public long CompletionCue;
        public bool HasContent;
    }
    private sealed class ActiveBatchContext(FileProcessingAction source, PageFeedback feedback, Action? onCancel)
    {
        public FileProcessingAction Source { get; } = source;
        public PageFeedback Feedback { get; } = feedback;
        public Action? OnCancel { get; } = onCancel;
    }

    public FileProcessingAction CurrentFileAction => IsImageObfuscatePage ? FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage
        : IsImageDeobfuscatePage ? FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage
        : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword
        : IsArchiveCreatePage ? FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive
        : IsArchiveExtractPage ? FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive
        : IsCompressionPage ? FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia
        : IsGifPage ? FileProcessingAction.Gif
        : Mode switch { ConversionMode.Audio => FileProcessingAction.Audio, ConversionMode.Image => FileProcessingAction.Image,
            ConversionMode.Extract => FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio, _ => FileProcessingAction.Video };

    private static FileProcessingAction FeedbackKey(FileProcessingAction action) => action is FileProcessingAction.Audio
        or FileProcessingAction.Image or FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio ? FileProcessingAction.Video : action;
    private PageFeedback FeedbackFor(FileProcessingAction action)
    {
        var key = FeedbackKey(action);
        if (!pageFeedback.TryGetValue(key, out var feedback)) pageFeedback[key] = feedback = new PageFeedback();
        return feedback;
    }
    private PageFeedback ViewedFeedback => FeedbackFor(CurrentFileAction);
    private PageFeedback WriteFeedback => activeBatch?.Feedback ?? ViewedFeedback;
    private ConversionError? BatchError => WriteFeedback.LastError;

    public bool HasActiveBatch => activeBatch is not null;
    public FileProcessingAction? ActiveBatchSource => activeBatch?.Source;
    public string ActiveBatchTitle => activeBatch is { } batch ? FileActionTitle(batch.Source) : "";
    public string ActiveBatchStatus => activeBatch is null ? "" : CurrentProcessingTask is { Status: JobStatus.Processing } task
        ? task.DisplayName + " · " + activeBatch.Feedback.StatusMessage + (ActiveBatchProgress is { } percent ? $" · {Math.Min(99, Math.Floor(percent)):0}%" : "")
        : activeBatch.Feedback.StatusMessage;
    // A nullable percentage represents the current real execution, never an invented timer.
    public double? ActiveBatchProgress => HasActiveBatch && cancellation?.IsCancellationRequested == false
        && CurrentProcessingTask is { Status: JobStatus.Processing } task ? task.Percent : null;
    public bool ActiveBatchIsIndeterminate => !ActiveBatchProgress.HasValue;
    public void NavigateToActiveBatch()
    {
        if (activeBatch is { } batch && CanNavigate) NavigateToFileAction(batch.Source);
    }
    private ActiveBatchContext BeginActiveBatch(FileProcessingAction source, Action? onCancel = null)
    {
        var context = new ActiveBatchContext(source, FeedbackFor(source), onCancel);
        activeBatch = context;
        NotifyActiveBatch();
        return context;
    }
    private void EndActiveBatch(ActiveBatchContext context)
    {
        if (!ReferenceEquals(activeBatch, context)) return;
        activeBatch = null;
        NotifyActiveBatch();
        NotifyViewedFeedback();
    }
    private void NotifyActiveBatch()
    {
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(HasActiveBatch), nameof(ActiveBatchSource), nameof(ActiveBatchTitle), nameof(ActiveBatchStatus), nameof(ActiveBatchProgress), nameof(ActiveBatchIsIndeterminate) })
            OnPropertyChanged(name);
    }
    private void NotifyActiveBatchProgress()
    {
        NotifySafely(() => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ActiveBatchProgress)));
        NotifySafely(() => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ActiveBatchIsIndeterminate)));
    }
    private void NotifyFeedbackChanged()
    {
        WriteFeedback.HasContent = true;
        if (ReferenceEquals(WriteFeedback, ViewedFeedback)) NotifyViewedFeedback();
        if (HasActiveBatch) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ActiveBatchStatus));
    }
    private void NotifyViewedFeedback()
    {
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(AssistantState), nameof(AssistantMessage), nameof(StatusMessage), nameof(FooterMessage),
            nameof(LastError), nameof(ShowError), nameof(IsDragging), nameof(CompletionCue), nameof(CurrentFileAction) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
    }
    private void CelebrateBatchCompletion()
    {
        WriteFeedback.CompletionCue++;
        if (ReferenceEquals(WriteFeedback, ViewedFeedback)) NotifySafely(() => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CompletionCue)));
    }
}

        : Locale.T("当前选择已完成 · 设置用于后续添加的文件");
    private bool TryMaximumBytes(out long bytes)
    {
        bytes = 0;
        if (!decimal.TryParse(MaximumSizeMb.Trim(), NumberStyles.AllowDecimalPoint, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var mb)
            || mb <= 0 || mb > long.MaxValue / 1000000m) return false;
        bytes = (long)decimal.Floor(mb * 1000000m);
        return bytes > 0;
    }
    private CompressionOptions? CurrentCompressionOptions() => StandardCompressionSelected
        ? new CompressionOptions(CompressionMode.Standard, SelectedCompressionLevel)
        : TryMaximumBytes(out var bytes) ? new CompressionOptions(CompressionMode.TargetSize, SelectedCompressionLevel, bytes) : null;
    private void CompressionSettingsChanged()
    {
        if (TryMaximumBytes(out _)) settings.CompressionMaximumMb = decimal.Parse(MaximumSizeMb.Trim(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        var options = CurrentCompressionOptions();
        if (options is not null)
        {
            var targets = SelectedJobs.Count > 0 ? SelectedJobs : (IEnumerable<ConversionJobViewModel>)VisibleQueue;
            foreach (var row in targets.Where(j => j.IsCompression)) row.ApplyCompression(options);
        }
        SaveSettings();
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(StandardCompressionSelected), nameof(TargetSizeCompressionSelected), nameof(LargeCompressionSelected), nameof(MediumCompressionSelected), nameof(SmallCompressionSelected), nameof(TinyCompressionSelected), nameof(CompressionInputError), nameof(HasCompressionInputError), nameof(CompressionDescription), nameof(FormatSummary) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
        Refresh();
    }

"" : Locale.F($" · {FailedCount} 个遇到问题")) : Locale.F($"{VisibleQueue.Count} 个文件 · {CompletedCount} 个完成") + (UnconfiguredCount == 0 ? "" : Locale.F($" · {UnconfiguredCount} 个待选格式")) + (FailedCount == 0 ? "" : Locale.F($" · {FailedCount} 个遇到问题"));
    public string FormatSummary => IsImageObfuscationPage ? ImageObfuscationPlan : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? RecoveryPlan : IsArchivePage ?     public string DropInstruction => IsImageObfuscationPage ? Locale.T("拖入静态图片到这里") : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? Locale.T("拖入加密 ZIP、7Z 或 RAR，分卷放在同一目录") : IsArchiveCreatePage ? Locale.T("任意文件、文件夹可以一起拖入") : IsArchiveExtractPage ? Locale.T("拖入压缩包，分卷请放在同一文件夹") : IsCompressionPage ? Locale.T("视频、图片、音频可以一起拖入") : Mode switch { ConversionMode.Audio => Locale.T("拖入音频到这里"), ConversionMode.Image => Locale.T("拖入图片到这里"), _ => Locale.T("拖入视频到这里") };
    public string SupportedFormats => IsImageObfuscationPage ? Locale.T("PNG · JPG · WebP · BMP · 输出 PNG") : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? Locale.T("ZIP · 7Z · RAR · 4 / 6 位纯数字") : IsArchiveCreatePage ? "ZIP · 7Z · TAR · TAR.GZ" : IsArchiveExtractPage ? "ZIP · 7Z · RAR · TAR · GZ · BZ2 · XZ" : IsCompressionPage ? Locale.T("视频 · 图片（含动图） · 音频") : Mode switch { ConversionMode.Audio => "MP3 · M4A · WAV · FLAC · OGG", ConversionMode.Image => "JPG · PNG · WebP · BMP · TIFF", _ => "MP4 · MOV · MKV · WebM · AVI" };
    public string QueueTitle => IsImageObfuscatePage ? Locale.T("图片混淆队列") : IsImageDeobfuscatePage ? Locale.T("图片解混淆队列") : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? Locale.T("密码恢复队列") : IsArchiveCreatePage ? Locale.T("打包队列") : IsArchiveExtractPage ? Locale.T("解压队列") : IsGifPage ? Locale.T("GIF 队列") : IsCompressionPage ? Locale.T("压缩队列") : Locale.T("转换队列");
    public string StartButtonText => IsImageObfuscatePage ? Locale.T("开始混淆") : IsImageDeobfuscatePage ? Locale.T("开始解混淆") : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? Locale.T("开始恢复") : IsArchiveCreatePage ? Locale.T("开始打包") : IsArchiveExtractPage ? Locale.T("开始解压") : IsGifPage ? Locale.T("开始生成 GIF") : IsCompressionPage ? Locale.T("开始压缩") : Locale.T("开始转换");
    public string SummaryLabel => IsImageObfuscationPage ? Locale.T("当前图片方案") : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? Locale.T("当前恢复方案") : IsArchivePage ? Locale.T("当前归档方案") : IsGifPage ? Locale.T("当前 GIF 档位") : IsCompressionPage ? Locale.T("当前压缩方案") : Locale.T("当前输出格式");
    public bool VideoSelected => IsConversionPage && Mode == ConversionMode.Video;
    public bool AudioSelected => IsConversionPage && Mode == ConversionMode.Audio;
    public bool ImageSelected => IsConversionPage && Mode == ConversionMode.Image;
    public bool ExtractSelected => IsConversionPage && Mode == ConversionMode.Extract;
    public AssistantState AssistantState { get => ViewedFeedback.AssistantState; private set { if (WriteFeedback.AssistantState == value) return; WriteFeedback.AssistantState = value; NotifyFeedbackChanged(); } }
    public bool IsDragging => AssistantState == AssistantState.DRAGGING;
    private AssistantState beforeDrag;
    private string beforeDragMessage = "";
    public string AssistantMessage { get => ViewedFeedback.AssistantMessage; private set { if (WriteFeedback.AssistantMessage == value) return; WriteFeedback.AssistantMessage = value; NotifyFeedbackChanged(); } }
    public string StatusMessage { get => ViewedFeedback.StatusMessage; private set { if (WriteFeedback.StatusMessage == value) return; WriteFeedback.StatusMessage = value; NotifyFeedbackChanged(); } }
    public string FooterMessage => HasStartHint ? StartHint : StatusMessage;
    public ConversionError? LastError { get => ViewedFeedback.LastError; private set { if (WriteFeedback.LastError == value) return; WriteFeedback.LastError = value; NotifyFeedbackChanged(); } }
    public bool ShowError => LastError is not null && AssistantState == AssistantState.ERROR;
    public event EventHandler? ErrorsChanged;

    public MainViewModel(IConversionEngine? engine = null, IReadOnlyList<FormatPreset>? presets = null, SettingsStore? settings = null, DiagnosticLog? log = null, IArchiveEngine? archiveEngine = null, IPasswordRecoveryEngine? recoveryEngine = null, IImageObfuscationEngine? imageObfuscationEngine = null)
    {
        var localRoot = CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Installation.InstallLifecycle.DataRoot;
        LogDirectory = Path.Combine(localRoot, "logs");
        this.log = log ?? new DiagnosticLog(LogDirectory);
        settingsStore = settings ?? new SettingsStore(localRoot);
        this.settings = settingsStore.Load();
        Locale.Language = this.settings.Language;
        ThemeManager.SetPreference(this.settings.ThemePreference);
        selectedCompressionMode = this.settings.CompressionMode;
        selectedCompressionLevel = this.settings.CompressionLevel;
        selectedGifQuality = this.settings.GifQuality;
        maximumSizeMb = this.settings.CompressionMaximumMb.ToString("0.############################", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        motionPreference = this.settings.MotionPreference;
        MotionPreferences.SetPreference(motionPreference);
        this.presets = presets ?? PresetCatalog.Load(Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "shared", "presets.json"));
        injectedEngine = engine is not null;
        this.engine = engine ?? new FfmpegConversionEngine(EngineDirectory, this.log);
        importer = new ImportService(this.engine);
        outputDirectory = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(this.settings.OutputDirectory)
            ? Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyVideos), "你的猫咪文件助手") : this.settings.OutputDirectory;
        StartCommand = new AsyncCommand(RunQueueAsync, HandleUnexpectedError, () => CanStart);
        CancelCommand = new RelayCommand(Cancel, () => IsProcessing);
        ClearCommand = new RelayCommand(Clear, () => CanEdit && HasJobs);
        RetryCommand = new RelayCommand(RetryFailed, () => CanEdit && (IsImageObfuscationPage ? VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Any(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled) : IsPasswordRecoveryPage ? RecoveryQueue.Any(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled) : IsArchivePage ? VisibleArchiveQueue.Any(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled) : VisibleQueue.Any(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled)));
        InitializeArchives(archiveEngine);
        InitializeRecovery(recoveryEngine);
        InitializeImageObfuscation(imageObfuscationEngine);
        InitializeTaskCenter();
        Queue.CollectionChanged += (_, _) => { RefreshVisibleQueue(); Refresh(); };
        SetMode(ConversionMode.Video);
    }

    public void SetMode(ConversionMode value)
    {
        if (!CanNavigate || !Enum.IsDefined(value)) return;
        // Re-selecting the current page must not empty the selector's collection: WPF
        // would clear SelectedItem while its unchanged source value remains cached.
        if (!IsArchivePage && !IsPasswordRecoveryPage && !IsImageObfuscationPage && !IsCompressionPage && mode == value && AvailablePresets.Count > 0)
        { NotifyViewedFeedback(); return; }
        var changingOperation = IsImageObfuscationPage || IsPasswordRecoveryPage || IsArchivePage || isCompressionPage || IsGifPage != (value == ConversionMode.Gif);
        if (!IsProcessing && IsArchivePage) ClearArchivePassword();
        archiveOperation = null;
        imageObfuscationOperation = null;
        SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>();
        RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue();
        isPasswordRecoveryPage = false;
        isCompressionPage = false;
        SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>();
        mode = value;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Mode));
        AvailablePresets.Clear();
        foreach (var preset in presets.Where(p => p.Mode == value)) AvailablePresets.Add(preset);
        settings.PresetIds.TryGetValue(value, out var remembered);
        selectedPreset = AvailablePresets.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Id == remembered) ?? AvailablePresets.FirstOrDefault();
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedPreset));
        RefreshVisibleQueue();
        RefreshVisibleArchives();
        NotifyPage();
        if (changingOperation && !IsProcessing) RestorePageFeedback();
        NotifyViewedFeedback();
        Refresh();
    }

    public void SetCompressionPage()
    {
        if (!CanNavigate || IsCompressionPage) return;
        if (!IsProcessing && IsArchivePage) ClearArchivePassword();
        archiveOperation = null;
        imageObfuscationOperation = null;
        SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>();
        RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue();
        isPasswordRecoveryPage = false;
        isCompressionPage = true;
        SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>();
        RefreshVisibleQueue(); RefreshVisibleArchives(); NotifyPage(); if (!IsProcessing) RestorePageFeedback(); NotifyViewedFeedback(); Refresh();
    }
    public void SetGifPage() => SetMode(ConversionMode.Gif);
    private void NotifyPage()
    {
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(IsImageObfuscationPage), nameof(IsImageObfuscatePage), nameof(IsImageDeobfuscatePage), nameof(ImageObfuscationDescription), nameof(ImageObfuscationScope), nameof(IsPasswordRecoveryPage), nameof(HasOutputLocation), nameof(IsArchivePage), nameof(IsMediaPage), nameof(IsArchiveCreatePage), nameof(IsArchiveExtractPage), nameof(IsCompressionPage), nameof(IsGifPage), nameof(IsConversionPage), nameof(PageTitle), nameof(PageSubtitle), nameof(DropInstruction), nameof(SupportedFormats), nameof(FormatSummary), nameof(VideoSelected), nameof(AudioSelected), nameof(ImageSelected), nameof(ExtractSelected), nameof(QueueTitle), nameof(StartButtonText), nameof(SummaryLabel), nameof(CompressionScope), nameof(GifScope) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
    }
    private bool BelongsToCurrentPage(ConversionJobViewModel row) => !IsImageObfuscationPage && !IsArchivePage && !IsPasswordRecoveryPage && (IsCompressionPage ? row.IsCompression : IsGifPage ? row.IsGif : !row.IsCompression && !row.IsGif);
    private void RefreshVisibleQueue()
    {
        var visible = Queue.Where(BelongsToCurrentPage).ToArray();
        foreach (var row in VisibleQueue.Where(j => !visible.Contains(j)).ToArray()) VisibleQueue.Remove(row);
        foreach (var row in visible.Where(j => !VisibleQueue.Contains(j))) VisibleQueue.Add(row);
    }
    private void RestorePageFeedback()
    {
        if (ViewedFeedback.HasContent) return;
        ResetAssistant();
        if (IsImageObfuscationPage && VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.LastOrDefault(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Failed && j.Error is not null) is { } imageFailed)
        { LastError = imageFailed.Error; AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这张图片好像遇到了一点问题。"); }
        var failed = VisibleQueue.LastOrDefault(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Failed && j.Error is not null);
        if (IsArchivePage && VisibleArchiveQueue.LastOrDefault(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Failed && j.Error is not null) is { } archiveFailed)
        {
            LastError = archiveFailed.Error; AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR;
            AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这个压缩包好像遇到了一点问题。");
        }
        if (failed is not null)
        {
            LastError = failed.Error; AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR;
            AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这个文件好像遇到了一点问题。");
        }
        StatusMessage = HasJobs ? Locale.T("准备就绪 · 当前页的任务会分别处理") : Locale.F($"准备就绪 · 添加文件以创建{QueueTitle}");
    }

    public Task AddFilesAsync(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        if (IsImageObfuscationPage) return AddImageObfuscationFilesAsync(paths);
        if (IsPasswordRecoveryPage) return AddRecoveryFilesAsync(paths);
        if (IsArchivePage) return AddArchiveFilesAsync(paths);
