        if (!CanEdit || (!IsCompressionPage && SelectedPreset is null)) return Task.CompletedTask;
        if (IsCompressionPage && CurrentCompressionOptions() is null) { StatusMessage = CompressionInputError; return Task.CompletedTask; }
        activeImport = ImportFilesAsync(paths);
        return activeImport;
    }
    private async Task ImportFilesAsync(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        var importMode = Mode;
        var importPreset = SelectedPreset!;
        var compressing = IsCompressionPage;
        var generatingGif = IsGifPage;
        var gifOptions = generatingGif ? new GifOptions(SelectedGifQuality) : null;
        var compressionOptions = CurrentCompressionOptions();
        IsImporting = true;
        importCancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var token = importCancellation.Token;
        StatusMessage = Locale.T("正在检查文件……");
        var rejected = new List<string>();
        var added = 0;
        try
        {
            foreach (var path in paths.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                token.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                var inspection = compressing ? await importer.InspectCompressionAsync(path, token) : await importer.InspectAsync(path, importMode, token);
                if (!inspection.Accepted || inspection.MediaInfo is null)
                {
                    var error = inspection.Error ?? new ConversionError("input.invalid", Locale.T("这个文件无法导入。"), Locale.T("请换一个文件重试。"));
                    LastError = error;
                    RejectPetImport(path, error.Message);
                    rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(path)}：{error.Message}");
                    continue;
                }
                var classifiedMode = compressing ? ImportService.Classify(inspection.SourcePath) ?? importMode : importMode;
                if (Queue.Any(j => j.Status != JobStatus.Completed && string.Equals(j.SourcePath, inspection.SourcePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && j.IsCompression == compressing && j.IsGif == generatingGif && (compressing || j.MediaMode == importMode)))
                { AcceptPetImport(path); continue; }
                var candidates = compressing ? CompressionCapabilities.Candidates(classifiedMode, inspection.MediaInfo, presets) : Array.Empty<FormatPreset>();
                var sourcePreset = compressing ? CompressionCapabilities.ResolveSourcePreset(inspection.SourcePath, classifiedMode, inspection.MediaInfo) : null;
                if (compressing && sourcePreset is null && candidates.Count == 0)
                {
                    LastError = new ConversionError("compression.no-format", Locale.T("没有可完整保留这个文件内容的压缩格式。"), Locale.T("请使用其他文件；原文件会保留。动画、透明区域、音轨和字幕不会静默丢弃。"));
                    RejectPetImport(path, LastError.Message);
                    rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(path)}：{LastError.Message}");
                    continue;
                }
                var row = compressing
                    ? new ConversionJobViewModel(inspection.SourcePath, inspection.MediaInfo, classifiedMode, compressionOptions!,
                        candidates, sourcePreset)
                    : new ConversionJobViewModel(inspection.SourcePath, inspection.MediaInfo, importPreset, gifOptions);
                row.PropertyChanged += (_, change) => { if (change.PropertyName is nameof(ConversionJobViewModel.Status) or nameof(ConversionJobViewModel.IsConfigured) or nameof(ConversionJobViewModel.Preset)) Refresh(); };
                Queue.Add(row);
                AcceptPetImport(path);
                added++;
            }
            if (rejected.Count > 0)
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR;
                AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这些文件好像遇到了一点问题。");
                StatusMessage = Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个文件，{rejected.Count} 个无法导入");
                LastError = new ConversionError("input.rejected", string.Join(Environment.NewLine, rejected.Take(8)), Locale.T("请确认文件类型适合当前功能，并且文件能够正常打开。"), LastError?.DiagnosticId, LastError?.TechnicalDetails);
                ErrorsChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
            else
            {
                if (added > 0) ResetAssistant();
                StatusMessage = added > 0 ? UnconfiguredCount > 0 ? Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个文件 · {UnconfiguredCount} 个需要选择输出格式") : Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个文件 · ") + (generatingGif ? Locale.T("选好档位即可生成 GIF") : compressing ? Locale.T("选好方案即可开始压缩") : Locale.T("选择格式后即可开始")) : Locale.T("这些文件已经在队列中了");
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { StatusMessage = Locale.T("文件检查已停止"); }
        catch (Exception error) { HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
        finally { importCancellation.Dispose(); importCancellation = null; IsImporting = false; }
    }

    public Task RunQueueAsync()
    {
        if (!CanStart) return activeRun;
        if (IsImageObfuscationPage) { activeRun = RunImageObfuscationBatchAsync(); return activeRun; }
        if (IsPasswordRecoveryPage) { activeRun = RunRecoveryBatchAsync(); return activeRun; }
        if (IsArchivePage) { activeRun = RunArchiveBatchAsync(); return activeRun; }
        activeRun = RunBatchAsync();
        return activeRun;
    }

    private async Task RunBatchAsync()
    {
        var batch = VisibleQueue.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending).ToArray();
        var petAction = IsGifPage ? FileProcessingAction.Gif : IsCompressionPage ? FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia : Mode switch
        { ConversionMode.Audio => FileProcessingAction.Audio, ConversionMode.Image => FileProcessingAction.Image, ConversionMode.Extract => FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio, _ => FileProcessingAction.Video };
        var requests = batch.Select(j => new ConversionRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), j.SourcePath, j.Preset, OutputDirectory, j.Compression, j.Gif)).ToArray();
        var jobs = requests.Zip(batch).ToDictionary(pair => pair.First.Id, pair => pair.Second);
        IsProcessing = true;
        cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var token = cancellation.Token;
        var batchContext = BeginActiveBatch(petAction);
        LastError = null;
        var petBatch = BeginPetBatch(petAction, batch.Length);
        try
        {
            var runner = new SerialConversionQueue(engine);
            var outcome = await runner.RunAsync(requests,
                onStarted: request =>
                {
                    var job = jobs[request.Id];
                    StartPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, job.Name, Array.IndexOf(batch, job) + 1,
                        job.IsGif ? FileProcessingAction.Gif : job.IsCompression ? FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia : job.MediaMode switch
                        { ConversionMode.Audio => FileProcessingAction.Audio, ConversionMode.Image => FileProcessingAction.Image, ConversionMode.Extract => FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio, _ => FileProcessingAction.Video });
                    AssistantState = AssistantState.PROCESSING;
                    AssistantMessage = Locale.F($"正在帮你{(job.IsGif ? Locale.T("生成 GIF ·") : job.IsCompression ? Locale.T("压缩") : Locale.T("处理"))} {job.Name}");
                    StatusMessage = Locale.F($"处理中 · {Array.IndexOf(batch, job) + 1}/{batch.Length}");
                    job.Status = JobStatus.Processing;
                    job.Progress = 0;
                    job.IsIndeterminate = true;
                    job.Attempt = 1;
                    job.Error = null;
                    job.OutputPath = null;
                    job.Statistics = null;
                    job.GifStatistics = null;
                },
                onCompleted: (request, result) =>
                {
                    CompletePetTask(petBatch, request.Id, result.Status, result.OutputPath, false, result.Error?.Message, result.Status == JobStatus.Completed);
                    var job = jobs[request.Id];
                    // The engine owns output commitment and cleanup. UI never deletes outputs.
                    job.OutputPath = result.OutputPath;
                    job.Statistics = result.Statistics;
                    job.GifStatistics = result.GifStatistics;
                    job.Error = result.Error;
                    job.Status = result.Status;
                    if (result.Status == JobStatus.Completed) job.Progress = 100;
                    if (result.Error is not null) LastError = result.Error;
                },
                progressFactory: request => new Progress<ConversionProgress>(p =>
                {
                    var job = jobs[request.Id];
                    if (job.Status != JobStatus.Processing) return;
                    ReportPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, p.Percent, p.Attempt > 1 ? Locale.F($"校准大小 · 第 {p.Attempt} 轮") : Locale.T("处理中"));
                    job.Attempt = p.Attempt;
                    job.IsIndeterminate = !p.Percent.HasValue || !double.IsFinite(p.Percent.Value);
                    if (p.Attempt > 1) StatusMessage = Locale.F($"校准大小 · 第 {p.Attempt} 轮 · {Array.IndexOf(batch, job) + 1}/{batch.Length}");
                    if (p.Percent.HasValue && double.IsFinite(p.Percent.Value))
                        job.Progress = Math.Max(job.Progress, Math.Min(99.5, p.Percent.Value));
                }),
                onObserverError: error => log.Write("Queue view notification failed", error.ToString()),
                cancellationToken: token);
            // Reconcile outcomes even if a view observer failed partway through a callback.
            foreach (var entry in outcome.Results)
            {
                CompletePetTask(petBatch, entry.Request.Id, entry.Result.Status, entry.Result.OutputPath, false, entry.Result.Error?.Message, entry.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed);
                var row = jobs[entry.Request.Id];
                NotifySafely(() => row.OutputPath = entry.Result.OutputPath);
                NotifySafely(() => row.Statistics = entry.Result.Statistics);
                NotifySafely(() => row.GifStatistics = entry.Result.GifStatistics);
                NotifySafely(() => row.Error = entry.Result.Error);
                NotifySafely(() => row.Status = entry.Result.Status);
                if (entry.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed) NotifySafely(() => row.Progress = 100);
                if (entry.Result.Error is not null) LastError = entry.Result.Error;
            }
            if (outcome.Cancelled)
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE;
                AssistantMessage = Locale.T("已停止，准备好了可以继续。");
                StatusMessage = Locale.T("已停止 · 尚未开始的文件仍在队列中");
            }
            else if (outcome.FailedCount > 0)
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR;
                AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这个文件好像遇到了一点问题。");
                StatusMessage = Locale.F($"本次完成 {outcome.CompletedCount} 个，{outcome.FailedCount} 个遇到问题");
            }
            else
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.SUCCESS;
                AssistantMessage = Locale.T("搞定啦！");
                StatusMessage = Locale.F($"本次 {batch.Length} 个文件已完成 · 原文件已保留");
                if (batch.Length > 0 && outcome.CompletedCount == batch.Length)
                {
                    // Presentation subscribers cannot change a committed conversion result.
                    CelebrateBatchCompletion();
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error) { HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
        finally
        {
            FinishPetBatch(petBatch, token.IsCancellationRequested);
            cancellation.Dispose();
            cancellation = null;
            EndActiveBatch(batchContext);
            IsProcessing = false;
            Refresh();
        }
    }

    public void Cancel()
    {
        cancellation?.Cancel();
        if (activeBatch is { } batch)
        {
            batch.OnCancel?.Invoke();
            StatusMessage = batch.Source switch
            {
                FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage or FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage => Locale.T("正在停止，等待图片处理完成清理……"),
                FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword => Locale.T("正在取消，等待恢复进程退出（最多约 5 秒）……"),
                _ => Locale.T("正在停止，等待处理进程退出……")
            };
            NotifyActiveBatchProgress();
        }
    }
    public void CancelImport() => importCancellation?.Cancel();
    public void RetryFailed()
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        if (IsImageObfuscationPage) { RetryImageObfuscationFailed(); return; }
        if (IsPasswordRecoveryPage) { RetryRecoveryFailed(); return; }
        if (IsArchivePage) { RetryArchiveFailed(); return; }
        foreach (var job in VisibleQueue.Where(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled)) { job.Status = JobStatus.Pending; job.Progress = 0; job.IsIndeterminate = false; job.Attempt = 1; job.Error = null; job.OutputPath = null; job.Statistics = null; job.GifStatistics = null; }
        ResetAssistant();
        StatusMessage = Locale.T("已重新加入待处理队列");
    }
    public void RemoveJobs(IEnumerable<ConversionJobViewModel> jobs)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        foreach (var job in jobs.Where(BelongsToCurrentPage).ToArray()) Queue.Remove(job);
        SelectedJobs = SelectedJobs.Where(Queue.Contains).ToArray();
        if (!HasJobs) ResetAssistant();
    }
    public void Clear()
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        if (IsImageObfuscationPage) { ClearImageObfuscationQueue(); return; }
        if (IsPasswordRecoveryPage) { ClearRecoveryQueue(); return; }
        if (IsArchivePage) { ClearArchives(); return; }
        foreach (var job in VisibleQueue.ToArray()) Queue.Remove(job);
        SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>(); ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"准备就绪 · 添加文件以创建{QueueTitle}");
    }
    public void SetDragging(bool value)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        if (value && !IsDragging) { beforeDrag = AssistantState; beforeDragMessage = AssistantMessage; AssistantState = AssistantState.DRAGGING; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("放这里就可以啦！"); }
        else if (!value && IsDragging) { AssistantState = beforeDrag; AssistantMessage = beforeDragMessage; }
    }
    public void SetEngineDirectory(string? directory)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        settings.EngineDirectory = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(directory) ? null : directory;
        if (!injectedEngine) { engine = new FfmpegConversionEngine(EngineDirectory, log); importer = new ImportService(engine); }
        if (!injectedImageObfuscationEngine) imageObfuscationEngine = new ImageObfuscationEngine(EngineDirectory, log);
        SaveSettings();
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(EngineDirectory)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsEngineReady)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(EngineStatus));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsImageObfuscationEngineReady)); Refresh();
    }
    public void HandleUnexpectedError(Exception error)
    {
        var id = log.Write("Desktop operation failed", error.ToString());
        LastError = new ConversionError("desktop.operation", Locale.T("这次操作遇到了问题。"), Locale.T("请重试。若问题持续，请打开高级详情并保存诊断信息。"), id, error.ToString());
        AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR;
        AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这个文件好像遇到了一点问题。");
        StatusMessage = Locale.T("操作未完成 · 可以查看原因");
    }
    private void ResetAssistant() { AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("把文件交给我吧！"); LastError = null; }
    private void NotifySafely(Action notification) { try { notification(); } catch (Exception error) { log.Write("Queue view reconciliation failed", error.ToString()); } }
    private void SaveSettings() { try { settingsStore.Save(settings); } catch (Exception error) { log.Write("Settings could not be saved", error.ToString()); } }
    private bool refreshing;
    private void Refresh()
    {
        if (refreshing) return;
        refreshing = true;
        try
        {
            foreach (var row in Queue) row.CanEdit = CanEdit;
            foreach (var row in ArchiveQueue) row.CanEdit = CanEdit;
            foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(CanEdit), nameof(CanNavigate), nameof(HasJobs), nameof(IsEmpty), nameof(PendingCount), nameof(CompletedCount), nameof(FailedCount), nameof(UnconfiguredCount), nameof(QueueSummary), nameof(RecoveryQueueSummary), nameof(CanStart), nameof(StartHint), nameof(HasStartHint), nameof(FooterMessage), nameof(CompressionScope), nameof(GifScope) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
            StartCommand?.Refresh(); CancelCommand?.Refresh(); ClearCommand?.Refresh(); RetryCommand?.Refresh();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ArchiveOptionsEditable));
        }
        finally { refreshing = false; }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.DesktopPet.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    public PetSettings PetSettings => settings.Pet;
    public string SettingsDirectory => Path.GetDirectoryName(settingsStore.FilePath)!;

    public void UpdatePetSettings(PetSettings value)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(value);
        settings.Pet = value;
        SaveSettings();
        NotifySafely(() => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PetSettings)));
    }

    public static string FileActionTitle(FileProcessingAction action) => Locale.T(action switch
    {
        FileProcessingAction.Video => "视频转换", FileProcessingAction.Audio => "音频转换",
        FileProcessingAction.Image => "图片转换", FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio => "提取音频",
        FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia => "文件压缩", FileProcessingAction.Gif => "生成 GIF",
        FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive => "文件打包", FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive => "解压文件",
        FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword => "密码恢复", FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage => "图片混淆",
        FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage => "图片解混淆", _ => "处理文件"
    });

    public IReadOnlyList<FileActionChoice> GetFileActionChoices(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        var inputs = NormalizePetPaths(paths);
        return Enum.GetValues<FileProcessingAction>().Select(action => new FileActionChoice(action,
            FileActionTitle(action), inputs.Where(path => IsCompatiblePetPath(action, path)).ToArray(), inputs.Count)).ToArray();
    }

    public async Task<FileImportResult> ImportForActionAsync(FileProcessingAction action, IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        var inputs = NormalizePetPaths(paths);
        if (!CanEdit) return new(inputs, Array.Empty<string>(), Array.Empty<FileImportRejection>(), Busy: true);
        if (!Enum.IsDefined(action)) return new(inputs, Array.Empty<string>(), inputs.Select(path => new FileImportRejection(path, "请选择有效的文件处理功能。 ")).ToArray());
        var compatible = CompatiblePetPaths(action, inputs);
        var rejections = inputs.Where(path => !IsCompatiblePetPath(action, path))
            .Select(path => new FileImportRejection(path, "这个文件或文件夹不适合所选功能，或已无法读取。 ")).ToList();
        if (compatible.Count == 0)
        {
            foreach (var path in inputs.Where(path => IsCompatiblePetPath(action, path)))
                rejections.Add(new(path, "无法读取压缩包首卷，请确认所有分卷位于同一个文件夹。"));
            return new(inputs, Array.Empty<string>(), rejections);
        }
        NavigateToFileAction(action);
        if (!CanEdit || CurrentFileAction != action)
            return new(inputs, Array.Empty<string>(), Array.Empty<FileImportRejection>(), Busy: true);
        // Capture this import's individual outcomes. An old completed row is not proof that a
        // newly dropped file still passes inspection (it may have moved or become damaged).
        var receipts = new Dictionary<string, string?>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        petImportReceipts = receipts;
        try { await AddFilesAsync(compatible); }
        finally { petImportReceipts = null; }
        var accepted = new List<string>();
        foreach (var path in inputs.Where(path => !rejections.Any(rejection => string.Equals(rejection.SourcePath, path, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))))
        {
            string canonical;
            try { canonical = action is FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive or FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword
                ? ArchiveImportService.NormalizeExtractionSources(new[] { path }).Single() : path; }
            catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
            { rejections.Add(new(path, "无法读取压缩包首卷，请确认分卷位于同一个文件夹。")); continue; }
            if (receipts.TryGetValue(canonical, out var reason) && reason is null) accepted.Add(path);
            else rejections.Add(new(path, reason ?? "这个文件未能加入队列，请在处理页面查看原因。"));
        }
        return new(inputs, accepted, rejections);
    }

    private Dictionary<string, string?>? petImportReceipts;
    private void AcceptPetImport(string path) { if (petImportReceipts is not null) petImportReceipts[path] = null; }
    private void RejectPetImport(string path, string reason) { if (petImportReceipts is not null) petImportReceipts[path] = reason; }

    private static IReadOnlyList<string> NormalizePetPaths(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        var result = new List<string>();
        var seen = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var input in paths)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input)) continue;
            string path;
            try { path = Path.GetFullPath(input); }
            catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or IOException)
            { path = input; }
            if (seen.Add(path)) result.Add(path);
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static bool IsCompatiblePetPath(FileProcessingAction action, string path)
    {
        try
        {
            if (action == FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive) return File.Exists(path) || Directory.Exists(path);
            if (!File.Exists(path)) return false;
            return action switch
            {
                FileProcessingAction.Video => ImportService.Classify(path) == ConversionMode.Video,
                FileProcessingAction.Audio => ImportService.Classify(path) == ConversionMode.Audio,
                FileProcessingAction.Image => ImportService.Classify(path) == ConversionMode.Image,
                FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio or FileProcessingAction.Gif => ImportService.Classify(path) == ConversionMode.Video,
                FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia => ImportService.Classify(path) is not null,
                FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive => ArchiveImportService.IsSupportedArchive(path),
                FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword => IsRecoverySource(path),
                FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage or FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage => IsImageObfuscationSource(path),
                _ => false
            };
        }
        catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException) { return false; }
    }

    private static IReadOnlyList<string> CompatiblePetPaths(FileProcessingAction action, IReadOnlyList<string> inputs)
    {
        var compatible = inputs.Where(path => IsCompatiblePetPath(action, path)).ToArray();
        if (action is not (FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive or FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword)) return compatible;
        // A later volume routes through the same canonical first-volume import used by the main window.
        try { return ArchiveImportService.NormalizeExtractionSources(compatible); }
        catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException or NotSupportedException or IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException) { return Array.Empty<string>(); }
    }

    public void NavigateToFileAction(FileProcessingAction action)
    {
        switch (action)
        {
            case FileProcessingAction.Video: SetMode(ConversionMode.Video); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.Audio: SetMode(ConversionMode.Audio); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.Image: SetMode(ConversionMode.Image); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio: SetMode(ConversionMode.Extract); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia: SetCompressionPage(); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.Gif: SetGifPage(); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive: SetArchiveCreatePage(); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive: SetArchiveExtractPage(); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword: SetPasswordRecoveryPage(); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage: SetImageObfuscatePage(); break;
            case FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage: SetImageDeobfuscatePage(); break;
        }
    }

    private ProcessingTaskSnapshot? currentProcessingTask;
    public ProcessingTaskSnapshot? CurrentProcessingTask => currentProcessingTask;
    public event EventHandler<ProcessingTaskSnapshot>? ProcessingTaskChanged;
    public event EventHandler<ProcessingBatchSummary>? ProcessingBatchFinished;
    private PetBatchContext? activePetBatch;

    private sealed class PetBatchContext(FileProcessingAction action, int count)
    {
        public Guid Id { get; } = Guid.NewGuid();
        public FileProcessingAction Action { get; } = action;
        public int Count { get; set; } = count;
        public Dictionary<Guid, ProcessingTaskSnapshot> Tasks { get; } = new();
        public HashSet<Guid> Successful { get; } = new();
        public HashSet<Guid> Exhausted { get; } = new();
        public long LastProgressTimestamp { get; set; }
    }

    private PetBatchContext BeginPetBatch(FileProcessingAction action, int count)
    {
        activePetBatch = new(action, count);
        currentProcessingTask = null;
        NotifySafely(() => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CurrentProcessingTask)));
        NotifyActiveBatchProgress();
        return activePetBatch;
    }

    private void StartPetTask(PetBatchContext batch, Guid operationId, string name, int index, FileProcessingAction? action = null)
    {
        if (!ReferenceEquals(batch, activePetBatch)) return;
        batch.LastProgressTimestamp = 0;
        PublishPetTask(batch, new(operationId, batch.Id, action ?? batch.Action, name, JobStatus.Processing, null,
            "准备中", index, batch.Count));
    }

    private void ReportPetTask(PetBatchContext batch, Guid operationId, double? percent, string? stage)
    {
        if (!ReferenceEquals(batch, activePetBatch) || !batch.Tasks.TryGetValue(operationId, out var task)
            || task.Status != JobStatus.Processing) return;
        var safePercent = percent is { } number && double.IsFinite(number) ? Math.Max(task.Percent ?? 0, Math.Clamp(number, 0, 99.5)) : (double?)null;
        // Recovery stage names come from the worker protocol. Never echo arbitrary worker
        // text (or a candidate/password) into the desktop bubble.
        var safeStage = task.Action == FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword ? stage switch
        {
            "preparing" or "checking" => "检查压缩包", "verifying" => "正在完整验证",
            "cancelling" => "正在取消", _ => "正在恢复数字密码"
        } : stage ?? "处理中";
        var timestamp = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        // Stage changes and transitions to unknown progress must be visible immediately.
        // Only repeated numeric updates are rate limited for the desktop pet.
        if (batch.LastProgressTimestamp != 0 && task.Stage == safeStage && task.Percent.HasValue == safePercent.HasValue
            && Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(batch.LastProgressTimestamp, timestamp).TotalMilliseconds < 250) return;
        batch.LastProgressTimestamp = timestamp;
        PublishPetTask(batch, task with { Percent = safePercent, Stage = safeStage });
    }

    private void CompletePetTask(PetBatchContext batch, Guid operationId, JobStatus status, string? outputPath,
        bool outputIsDirectory, string? error, bool successful, bool exhausted = false)
    {
        if (!ReferenceEquals(batch, activePetBatch) || !batch.Tasks.TryGetValue(operationId, out var task)) return;
        if (task.Status != JobStatus.Processing) return;
        if (successful) batch.Successful.Add(operationId);
        if (exhausted) batch.Exhausted.Add(operationId);
        PublishPetTask(batch, task with { Status = status, Percent = status == JobStatus.Completed ? 100 : task.Percent,
            Stage = exhausted ? "已尝试，暂未找到密码" : status switch { JobStatus.Completed => "已完成", JobStatus.Failed => "遇到问题", JobStatus.Cancelled => "已取消", _ => "处理中" },
            OutputPath = outputPath, OutputIsDirectory = outputIsDirectory, ErrorMessage = error });
    }

    private void CompleteRecoveryPetTask(PetBatchContext batch, Guid id, RecoveryResult result) =>
        CompletePetTask(batch, id, result.Status switch
        {
            RecoveryStatus.Cancelled => JobStatus.Cancelled, RecoveryStatus.Failed => JobStatus.Failed, _ => JobStatus.Completed
        }, null, false, result.Error is null ? null : "密码恢复遇到了问题，请打开软件查看原因。",
            result.Status == RecoveryStatus.Found, result.Status == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted);

    private void PublishPetTask(PetBatchContext batch, ProcessingTaskSnapshot task)
    {
        batch.Tasks[task.OperationId] = task;
        currentProcessingTask = task;
        NotifySafely(() => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CurrentProcessingTask)));
        NotifyActiveBatchProgress();
        RaisePetEvent(ProcessingTaskChanged, task);
    }

    private void FinishPetBatch(PetBatchContext batch, bool cancelled)
    {
        if (!ReferenceEquals(batch, activePetBatch)) return;
        foreach (var task in batch.Tasks.Values.Where(task => task.Status == JobStatus.Processing).ToArray())
            CompletePetTask(batch, task.OperationId, cancelled ? JobStatus.Cancelled : JobStatus.Failed, null, false,
                cancelled ? null : BatchError?.Message ?? "处理未完成。", false);
        var tasks = batch.Tasks.Values.OrderBy(task => task.Index).ToArray();
        var summary = new ProcessingBatchSummary(batch.Id, batch.Action,
            tasks.Count(task => task.Status == JobStatus.Completed), tasks.Count(task => task.Status == JobStatus.Failed),
            tasks.Count(task => task.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled), batch.Exhausted.Count, Math.Max(0, batch.Count - tasks.Length),
            batch.Successful.ToArray(), tasks, cancelled || tasks.Any(task => task.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled));
        activePetBatch = null;
        RaisePetEvent(ProcessingBatchFinished, summary);
    }

    private void RaisePetEvent<T>(EventHandler<T>? handlers, T value)
    {
        if (handlers is null) return;
        foreach (EventHandler<T> handler in handlers.GetInvocationList())
        {
            try { handler(this, value); }
            catch (Exception) { log.Write("Desktop pet task observer failed", "桌宠通知未能显示；文件结果保持不变。"); }
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.ImageObfuscation.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    private IImageObfuscationEngine imageObfuscationEngine = null!;
    private bool injectedImageObfuscationEngine;
    private ImageObfuscationOperation? imageObfuscationOperation;
    public bool IsImageObfuscationPage => imageObfuscationOperation.HasValue;
    public bool IsImageObfuscatePage => imageObfuscationOperation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate;
    public bool IsImageDeobfuscatePage => imageObfuscationOperation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate;
    public ObservableCollection<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel> ImageObfuscationQueue { get; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel> VisibleImageObfuscationQueue { get; } = new();
    private IReadOnlyList<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel> selectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>();
    public IReadOnlyList<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel> SelectedImageObfuscationJobs
    {
        get => selectedImageObfuscationJobs;
        set { selectedImageObfuscationJobs = value; OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ImageObfuscationScope)); }
    }
    public static IReadOnlyList<string> ImageObfuscationExtensions { get; } = new[] { ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp", ".bmp" };
    public static bool IsImageObfuscationSource(string path) => ImageObfuscationExtensions.Contains(Path.GetExtension(path), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private ImageObfuscationMode selectedImageObfuscationMode;
    public ImageObfuscationMode SelectedImageObfuscationMode
    {
        get => selectedImageObfuscationMode;
        set
        {
            if (!CanEdit || !Enum.IsDefined(value)) return;
            Set(ref selectedImageObfuscationMode, value);
            settings.ImageObfuscationMode = value; ImageObfuscationSettingsChanged();
        }
    }
    private int imageObfuscationIterations;
    public int ImageObfuscationIterations
    {
        get => imageObfuscationIterations;
        set
        {
            if (!CanEdit || value is < 1 or > 10) return;
            Set(ref imageObfuscationIterations, value);
            settings.ImageObfuscationIterations = value; ImageObfuscationSettingsChanged();
        }
    }
    public IReadOnlyList<int> ImageObfuscationIterationChoices { get; } = Enumerable.Range(1, 10).ToArray();
    public bool ImageGilbertSelected => SelectedImageObfuscationMode == ImageObfuscationMode.Gilbert;
    public bool ImageBlockSelected => SelectedImageObfuscationMode == ImageObfuscationMode.Block;
    public bool ImagePixelSelected => SelectedImageObfuscationMode == ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel;
    public bool IsImageObfuscationEngineReady => injectedImageObfuscationEngine || imageObfuscationEngine is ImageObfuscationEngine { IsAvailable: true };
    public string ImageObfuscationInputError => IsImageObfuscationEngineReady ? "" : Locale.T("请在设置中确认内置图片处理引擎完整。");
    public string ImageObfuscationPlan => Locale.F($"{ImageObfuscationModeName(SelectedImageObfuscationMode)} · {ImageObfuscationIterations} 次 · PNG");
    public static string ImageObfuscationModeName(ImageObfuscationMode value) => value switch { ImageObfuscationMode.Block => Locale.T("方块"), ImageObfuscationMode.Pixel => Locale.T("全像素"), _ => "Gilbert" };
    public string ImageObfuscationDescription => IsImageDeobfuscatePage
        ? Locale.T("本软件生成的图片会自动识别模式和次数并校验；其他图片按所选参数尝试，结果需自行确认。")
        : Locale.T("无密码的可逆图片排列；输出 PNG 并记录恢复参数。请保留原图，仅处理有权使用的图片。");
    public string ImageObfuscationScope => Locale.T("参数用于") + (SelectedImageObfuscationJobs.Count > 0 ? Locale.T("已选未开始的任务") : Locale.T("当前页未开始的任务")) + Locale.T("及后续添加；自动识别的恢复参数保持不变。");
    private ImageObfuscationOptions CurrentImageObfuscationOptions => new(SelectedImageObfuscationMode, ImageObfuscationIterations);
    private void InitializeImageObfuscation(IImageObfuscationEngine? suppliedEngine)
    {
        injectedImageObfuscationEngine = suppliedEngine is not null;
        imageObfuscationEngine = suppliedEngine ?? new ImageObfuscationEngine(EngineDirectory, log);
        selectedImageObfuscationMode = settings.ImageObfuscationMode;
        imageObfuscationIterations = settings.ImageObfuscationIterations;
        ImageObfuscationQueue.CollectionChanged += (_, _) => { RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue(); Refresh(); };
    }
    private void ImageObfuscationSettingsChanged()
    {
        var targets = SelectedImageObfuscationJobs.Count > 0 ? SelectedImageObfuscationJobs : (IEnumerable<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>)VisibleImageObfuscationQueue;
        foreach (var row in targets) row.ApplyOptions(CurrentImageObfuscationOptions);
        SaveSettings();
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(ImageGilbertSelected), nameof(ImageBlockSelected), nameof(ImagePixelSelected), nameof(ImageObfuscationPlan), nameof(FormatSummary) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
        Refresh();
    }
    public void SetImageObfuscationPage(ImageObfuscationOperation operation)
    {
        if (!CanNavigate || !Enum.IsDefined(operation)) return;
        if (!IsProcessing) ClearArchivePassword(); archiveOperation = null; isPasswordRecoveryPage = false; isCompressionPage = false;
        imageObfuscationOperation = operation; SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>();
        SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>();
        RefreshVisibleQueue(); RefreshVisibleArchives(); RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue();
        NotifyPage(); if (!IsProcessing) RestorePageFeedback(); NotifyViewedFeedback(); Refresh();
    }
    public void SetImageObfuscatePage() => SetImageObfuscationPage(ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate);
    public void SetImageDeobfuscatePage() => SetImageObfuscationPage(ImageObfuscationOperation.Deobfuscate);
    private void RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue()
    {
        var visible = ImageObfuscationQueue.Where(j => j.Operation == imageObfuscationOperation).ToArray();
        foreach (var row in VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Where(j => !visible.Contains(j)).ToArray()) VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Remove(row);
        foreach (var row in visible.Where(j => !VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Contains(j))) VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Add(row);
    }
    public Task AddImageObfuscationFilesAsync(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        if (!CanEdit || !IsImageObfuscationPage) return Task.CompletedTask;
        activeImport = ImportImageObfuscationFilesAsync(paths.ToArray()); return activeImport;
    }
    private async Task ImportImageObfuscationFilesAsync(IReadOnlyList<string> paths)
    {
        var operation = imageObfuscationOperation!.Value; var options = CurrentImageObfuscationOptions;
        IsImporting = true; importCancellation = new CancellationTokenSource(); var token = importCancellation.Token;
        var rejected = new List<string>(); var added = 0; StatusMessage = Locale.T("正在检查图片……");
        try
        {
            foreach (var candidate in paths.Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                token.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                try
                {
                    if (!File.Exists(candidate) || !IsImageObfuscationSource(candidate)) { RejectPetImport(candidate, Locale.T("请选择支持的静态图片。")); rejected.Add(Locale.F($"{Path.GetFileName(candidate)}：请选择支持的静态图片。")); continue; }
                    var path = Path.GetFullPath(candidate);
                    if (ImageObfuscationQueue.Any(j => j.Status != JobStatus.Completed && j.Operation == operation && string.Equals(j.SourcePath, path, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                    { AcceptPetImport(path); continue; }
                    var info = await imageObfuscationEngine.InspectAsync(path, token);
                    if (operation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate && info.IsMarked)
                    {
                        LastError = new("image.already_marked", Locale.T("这张图片已带有本软件的混淆标记。"), Locale.T("请先在图片解混淆页恢复，再对恢复后的图片重新混淆。"));
                        RejectPetImport(candidate, LastError.Message);
                        rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(candidate)}：{LastError.Message}"); continue;
                    }
                    var row = new ImageObfuscationJobViewModel(path, new FileInfo(path).Length, operation, info, options);
                    row.PropertyChanged += (_, change) => { if (change.PropertyName == nameof(ImageObfuscationJobViewModel.Status)) Refresh(); };
                    ImageObfuscationQueue.Add(row); AcceptPetImport(path); added++;
                }
                catch (OperationCanceledException) when (token.IsCancellationRequested) { throw; }
                catch (ConversionEngineException error) { LastError = error.Error; RejectPetImport(candidate, error.Error.Message); rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(candidate)}：{error.Error.Message}"); }
                catch (Exception error) when (error is not OutOfMemoryException)
                { RejectPetImport(candidate, Locale.T("这张图片暂时无法读取，请检查格式和文件是否完整。")); rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(candidate)}：{error.Message}"); }
            }
            if (rejected.Count > 0)
            {
                LastError = new("image-obfuscation.input-rejected", string.Join(Environment.NewLine, rejected.Take(8)), Locale.T("请确认图片可读取、格式受支持且未损坏。动图不支持；原文件保持不变。"), LastError?.DiagnosticId, LastError?.TechnicalDetails);
                AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这些图片暂时无法处理。");
                StatusMessage = Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个，{rejected.Count} 个无法导入"); ErrorsChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
            else { if (added > 0) ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = added > 0 ? Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 张图片 · 选好参数即可开始") : Locale.T("这些图片已经在当前队列中了"); }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { StatusMessage = Locale.T("图片检查已停止"); }
        catch (Exception error) { HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
        finally { importCancellation.Dispose(); importCancellation = null; IsImporting = false; }
    }
    private async Task RunImageObfuscationBatchAsync()
    {
        var batch = VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending).ToArray();
        var sourceAction = IsImageObfuscatePage ? FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage : FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage;
        var requests = batch.Select(j => new ImageObfuscationRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), j.SourcePath, OutputDirectory, j.Operation, j.Options)).ToArray();
        var rows = requests.Zip(batch).ToDictionary(pair => pair.First.Id, pair => pair.Second);
        IsProcessing = true; cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource(); var token = cancellation.Token;
        var batchContext = BeginActiveBatch(sourceAction,
            () => { foreach (var row in batch.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Processing)) row.Stage = "cancelling"; });
        LastError = null;
        var petBatch = BeginPetBatch(sourceAction, batch.Length);
        try
        {
            var outcome = await new SerialImageObfuscationQueue(imageObfuscationEngine).RunAsync(requests,
                onStarted: request => { StartPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, rows[request.Id].Name, Array.IndexOf(requests, request) + 1); rows[request.Id].Begin(); AssistantState = AssistantState.PROCESSING; AssistantMessage = request.Operation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate ? Locale.T("正在帮你排列图片……") : Locale.T("正在帮你恢复图片……"); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"处理中 · {Array.IndexOf(requests, request) + 1}/{batch.Length}"); },
                onCompleted: (request, result) => { CompletePetTask(petBatch, request.Id, result.Status, result.OutputPath, false, result.Error?.Message, result.Status == JobStatus.Completed); rows[request.Id].Apply(result); if (result.Error is not null) LastError = result.Error; },
                progressFactory: request => new Progress<ImageObfuscationProgress>(p =>
                {
                    var row = rows[request.Id]; if (row.Status != JobStatus.Processing || token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                    ReportPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, p.Percent, p.Stage);
                    row.Stage = p.Stage; row.IsIndeterminate = !p.Percent.HasValue || !double.IsFinite(p.Percent.Value);
                    if (p.Percent.HasValue && double.IsFinite(p.Percent.Value)) row.Progress = Math.Max(row.Progress, Math.Min(99.5, p.Percent.Value));
                }), onObserverError: error => log.Write("Image queue view notification failed", error.ToString()), cancellationToken: token);
            foreach (var entry in outcome.Results)
            {
                CompletePetTask(petBatch, entry.Request.Id, entry.Result.Status, entry.Result.OutputPath, false, entry.Result.Error?.Message, entry.Result.Status == JobStatus.Completed);
                NotifySafely(() => rows[entry.Request.Id].Apply(entry.Result));
            }
            if (outcome.Cancelled) { AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("已停止，准备好了可以继续。"); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已停止 · 尚未开始的图片仍在队列中"); }
            else if (batch.Any(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Failed)) { AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("部分图片遇到了问题。"); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"本次完成 {batch.Count(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Completed)} 个，{batch.Count(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Failed)} 个遇到问题"); }
            else
            {
                AssistantState = AssistantState.SUCCESS; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("搞定啦！");
                StatusMessage = batch.Any(j => j.Statistics?.Verified != true) ? Locale.T("本次图片已处理 · 无原图摘要的结果请自行确认") : Locale.F($"本次 {batch.Length} 张图片已完成 · 原文件已保留");
                if (batch.Length > 0 && batch.All(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Completed)) CelebrateBatchCompletion();
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error) { HandleUnexpectedError(error); }
        finally { FinishPetBatch(petBatch, token.IsCancellationRequested); cancellation.Dispose(); cancellation = null; EndActiveBatch(batchContext); IsProcessing = false; Refresh(); }
    }
    private void RetryImageObfuscationFailed()
    { foreach (var row in VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.Where(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled)) row.Reset(); ResetAssistant(); Refresh(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已重新加入待处理队列"); }
    public void RemoveImageObfuscationJobs(IEnumerable<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel> jobs)
    {
        if (!CanEdit) return;
        foreach (var row in jobs.Where(j => j.Operation == imageObfuscationOperation).ToArray()) ImageObfuscationQueue.Remove(row);
        SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = SelectedImageObfuscationJobs.Where(ImageObfuscationQueue.Contains).ToArray();
        if (!HasJobs) ResetAssistant();
    }
    private void ClearImageObfuscationQueue()
    { foreach (var row in VisibleImageObfuscationQueue.ToArray()) ImageObfuscationQueue.Remove(row); SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>(); ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("准备就绪 · 添加图片以创建") + QueueTitle; }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.Language.cs ---
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    public string Language
    {
        get => settings.Language;
        set
        {
            var language = LanguageSetting.Normalize(value);
            if (settings.Language == language && Locale.Language == language) return;
            settings.Language = language;
            SaveSettings();
            Locale.Language = language;
            RefreshLocalizedProperties();
            foreach (var item in Queue) item.RefreshLocalizedProperties();
            foreach (var item in ArchiveQueue) item.RefreshLocalizedProperties();
            foreach (var item in RecoveryQueue) item.RefreshLocalizedProperties();
            foreach (var item in ImageObfuscationQueue) item.RefreshLocalizedProperties();
            foreach (var item in TaskCenterItems) item.RefreshLocalizedProperties();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Language));
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.Recovery.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    private bool isPasswordRecoveryPage;
    public bool IsPasswordRecoveryPage => isPasswordRecoveryPage;
    public bool HasOutputLocation => !IsPasswordRecoveryPage;
    private IPasswordRecoveryEngine recoveryEngine = null!;
    private bool injectedRecoveryEngine;
    public ObservableCollection<RecoveryJobViewModel> RecoveryQueue { get; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<string> CommonRecoveryNumbers { get; } = new();
    private RecoveryMode selectedRecoveryMode;
    public RecoveryMode SelectedRecoveryMode
    {
        get => selectedRecoveryMode;
        set { if (CanEdit && Enum.IsDefined(value) && Set(ref selectedRecoveryMode, value)) { settings.RecoveryMode = value; RecoverySettingsChanged(); } }
    }
    private int recoveryDigits;
    public int RecoveryDigits
    {
        get => recoveryDigits;
        set { if (CanEdit && value is 4 or 6 && Set(ref recoveryDigits, value)) { settings.RecoveryDigits = value; RecoverySettingsChanged(); } }
    }
    private RecoveryIntensity selectedRecoveryIntensity;
    public RecoveryIntensity SelectedRecoveryIntensity
    {
        get => selectedRecoveryIntensity;
        set { if (CanEdit && Enum.IsDefined(value) && Set(ref selectedRecoveryIntensity, value)) { settings.RecoveryIntensity = value; RecoverySettingsChanged(); } }
    }
    public bool CommonRecoverySelected => SelectedRecoveryMode == RecoveryMode.CommonPasswords;
    public bool CpuRecoverySelected => SelectedRecoveryMode == RecoveryMode.Cpu;
    public bool RecoveryFourDigitsSelected => RecoveryDigits == 4;
    public bool RecoverySixDigitsSelected => RecoveryDigits == 6;
    public bool RecoveryLowSelected => SelectedRecoveryIntensity == RecoveryIntensity.Low;
    public bool RecoveryMediumSelected => SelectedRecoveryIntensity == RecoveryIntensity.Medium;
    public bool RecoveryHighSelected => SelectedRecoveryIntensity == RecoveryIntensity.High;
    public string CommonRecoverySummary => Locale.F($"已添加 {CommonRecoveryNumbers.Count} / 100 条常用数字 · 仅本次会话");
    public bool IsRecoveryEngineReady => injectedRecoveryEngine || recoveryEngine is PasswordRecoveryEngine { IsReady: true };
    public string RecoveryInputError => IsRecoveryEngineReady ? "" : Locale.T("请在设置中确认压缩包引擎已就绪。");
    public string RecoveryModeDescription => CommonRecoverySelected
        ? Locale.T("优先尝试等长数字；较长数字取前后段，较短数字重复或补零，再尝试内置规则候选；耗尽后停止。")
        : Locale.F($"逐一测试全部 {RecoveryDigits} 位纯数字密码，包含前导零。共 {(RecoveryDigits == 4 ? 10_000 : 1_000_000):N0} 个组合。");
    public string RecoveryIntensityDescription => SelectedRecoveryIntensity switch
    {
        RecoveryIntensity.High => Locale.T("高 · CPU 占用上限约 80%；实际占用可能更低。"),
        RecoveryIntensity.Low => Locale.T("低 · CPU 占用上限约 20%；实际占用可能更低。"),
        _ => Locale.T("中 · CPU 占用上限约 50%；实际占用可能更低。")
    };
    public string RecoveryCandidateSummary => Locale.F($"本方案共 {RecoveryCandidates.Total(CurrentRecoveryOptions, CommonRecoveryNumbers):N0} 个候选") + (CommonRecoverySelected ? $" · {CommonRecoverySummary}" : "");
    public string RecoveryPlan => Locale.F($"{(CommonRecoverySelected ? Locale.T("常用密码推测") : Locale.T("CPU 破解"))} · {RecoveryDigits} 位数字 · {RecoveryIntensityName(SelectedRecoveryIntensity)}");
    public string RecoveryQueueSummary => Locale.F($"{RecoveryQueue.Count} 个压缩包 · {RecoveryQueue.Count(j => j.ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Found)} 个找到密码") + (RecoveryQueue.Any(j => j.ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted) ? Locale.F($" · {RecoveryQueue.Count(j => j.ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted)} 个未找到") : "");
    public static string RecoveryIntensityName(RecoveryIntensity value) => value switch { RecoveryIntensity.High => Locale.T("高强度"), RecoveryIntensity.Low => Locale.T("低强度"), _ => Locale.T("中强度") };
    private RecoveryOptions CurrentRecoveryOptions => new(SelectedRecoveryMode, RecoveryDigits, SelectedRecoveryIntensity, ArchiveExtractionLimits);
    private void InitializeRecovery(IPasswordRecoveryEngine? suppliedEngine)
    {
        injectedRecoveryEngine = suppliedEngine is not null;
        recoveryEngine = suppliedEngine ?? new PasswordRecoveryEngine(ArchiveEngineDllPath, log: log);
        selectedRecoveryMode = settings.RecoveryMode; recoveryDigits = settings.RecoveryDigits; selectedRecoveryIntensity = settings.RecoveryIntensity;
        CommonRecoveryNumbers.CollectionChanged += (_, _) => { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CommonRecoverySummary)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RecoveryCandidateSummary)); RefreshRecoveryPlans(); };
        RecoveryQueue.CollectionChanged += (_, _) => Refresh();
    }
    private void RecoverySettingsChanged()
    {
        foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(CommonRecoverySelected), nameof(CpuRecoverySelected), nameof(RecoveryFourDigitsSelected), nameof(RecoverySixDigitsSelected), nameof(RecoveryLowSelected), nameof(RecoveryMediumSelected), nameof(RecoveryHighSelected), nameof(RecoveryModeDescription), nameof(RecoveryIntensityDescription), nameof(RecoveryCandidateSummary), nameof(RecoveryPlan), nameof(FormatSummary) }) OnPropertyChanged(name);
        RefreshRecoveryPlans(); SaveSettings(); Refresh();
    }
    private void RefreshRecoveryPlans()
    {
        foreach (var row in RecoveryQueue.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending)) { row.Plan = RecoveryPlan; row.TotalCandidates = RecoveryCandidates.Total(CurrentRecoveryOptions, CommonRecoveryNumbers.ToArray()); }
    }
    public void SetPasswordRecoveryPage()
    {
        if (!CanNavigate) return;
        if (!IsProcessing) ClearArchivePassword(); archiveOperation = null; isPasswordRecoveryPage = true; isCompressionPage = false;
        imageObfuscationOperation = null; SelectedImageObfuscationJobs = Array.Empty<ImageObfuscationJobViewModel>(); RefreshVisibleImageObfuscationQueue();
        SelectedJobs = Array.Empty<ConversionJobViewModel>(); RefreshVisibleQueue(); RefreshVisibleArchives(); NotifyPage(); if (!IsProcessing) RestorePageFeedback(); NotifyViewedFeedback(); Refresh();
    }
    public bool AddCommonRecoveryNumber(string? number, out string error)
    {
        error = "";
        if (!CanEdit) { error = Locale.T("请先停止当前任务。"); return false; }
        var value = number?.Trim() ?? "";
        if (value.Length is < 1 or > 20 || value.Any(c => c is < '0' or > '9')) { error = Locale.T("请输入 1–20 位纯数字，前导零会保留。"); return false; }
        if (CommonRecoveryNumbers.Contains(value, StringComparer.Ordinal)) return true;
        if (CommonRecoveryNumbers.Count >= 100) { error = Locale.T("最多添加 100 条常用数字，请先删除不需要的条目。"); return false; }
        CommonRecoveryNumbers.Add(value); return true;
    }
    public bool AddCommonRecoveryNumbersText(string? text, out string error)
    {
        error = "";
        if (!CanEdit) { error = Locale.T("请先停止当前任务。"); return false; }
        var lines = (text ?? "").Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Select(line => line.Trim()).Where(line => line.Length > 0).ToArray();
        if (lines.Length == 0) { error = Locale.T("请输入数字；多条数字请每行一条。"); return false; }
        try
        {
            var merged = RecoveryCandidates.NormalizeNumbers(CommonRecoveryNumbers.Concat(lines));
            foreach (var value in merged.Where(value => !CommonRecoveryNumbers.Contains(value, StringComparer.Ordinal))) CommonRecoveryNumbers.Add(value);
            return true;
        }
        catch (ArgumentException) { error = Locale.T("每条须为 1–20 个数字（0–9），合并重复项后最多 100 条。"); return false; }
    }
    public void RemoveCommonRecoveryNumbers(IEnumerable<string> values)
    { if (CanEdit) foreach (var value in values.ToArray()) CommonRecoveryNumbers.Remove(value); }
    public void ClearCommonRecoveryNumbers() { if (CanEdit) CommonRecoveryNumbers.Clear(); }
    public static bool IsRecoverySource(string path)
    {
        return System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(Path.GetFileName(path), @"\.(?:zip|7z|rar)(?:\.\d{3,})?$|\.(?:r|z)\d{2,}$", System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
    }
    public Task AddRecoveryFilesAsync(IEnumerable<string> paths)
    {
        if (!CanEdit || !IsPasswordRecoveryPage) return Task.CompletedTask;
        activeImport = ImportRecoveryAsync(paths.ToArray()); return activeImport;
    }
    private async Task ImportRecoveryAsync(IReadOnlyList<string> paths)
    {
        IsImporting = true; importCancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var rejected = new List<string>(); var added = 0; StatusMessage = Locale.T("正在检查待恢复压缩包……");
        try
        {
            foreach (var path in ArchiveImportService.NormalizeExtractionSources(paths))
            {
                importCancellation.Token.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                if (!IsRecoverySource(path)) { RejectPetImport(path, Locale.T("请选择 ZIP、7Z 或 RAR 压缩包。")); rejected.Add(Locale.F($"{Path.GetFileName(path)}：请选择 ZIP、7Z 或 RAR 压缩包。")); continue; }
                var inspection = await archiveImporter.InspectExtractAsync(path, cancellationToken: importCancellation.Token);
                if (inspection.Error is not null) { RejectPetImport(path, inspection.Error.Message); rejected.Add($"{Path.GetFileName(path)}：{inspection.Error.Message}"); continue; }
                if (!inspection.RequiresPassword) { RejectPetImport(path, Locale.T("压缩包无需密码。")); rejected.Add(Locale.F($"{Path.GetFileName(path)}：压缩包无需密码。")); continue; }
                if (RecoveryQueue.Any(j => j.Status != JobStatus.Completed && string.Equals(j.SourcePath, inspection.SourcePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                { AcceptPetImport(path); continue; }
                var row = new RecoveryJobViewModel(inspection.SourcePath, inspection.SourceBytes) { Plan = RecoveryPlan, TotalCandidates = RecoveryCandidates.Total(CurrentRecoveryOptions, CommonRecoveryNumbers.ToArray()) };
                row.PropertyChanged += (_, change) => { if (change.PropertyName is nameof(RecoveryJobViewModel.Status) or nameof(RecoveryJobViewModel.ResultStatus)) Refresh(); };
                RecoveryQueue.Add(row); AcceptPetImport(path); added++;
            }
            if (rejected.Count > 0)
            {
                LastError = new("recovery.input-rejected", string.Join(Environment.NewLine, rejected.Take(8)), Locale.T("请确认压缩包已加密且可读取，分卷文件位于同一目录。"));
                AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("这些压缩包暂时无法恢复。"); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个，{rejected.Count} 个无法导入"); ErrorsChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
            else { if (added > 0) ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = added > 0 ? Locale.F($"已添加 {added} 个压缩包 · 选择方案后开始恢复") : Locale.T("这些压缩包已在队列中"); }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { StatusMessage = Locale.T("压缩包检查已取消"); }
        catch (Exception) { HandleRecoveryUnexpectedError(); }
        finally { importCancellation.Dispose(); importCancellation = null; IsImporting = false; }
    }
    private async Task RunRecoveryBatchAsync()
    {
        var batch = RecoveryQueue.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Pending).ToArray();
        var numbers = CommonRecoveryNumbers.ToArray(); var options = CurrentRecoveryOptions;
        var requests = batch.Select(row => new RecoveryRequest(Guid.NewGuid(), row.SourcePath, options, numbers)).ToArray();
        var rows = requests.Zip(batch).ToDictionary(pair => pair.First.Id, pair => pair.Second);
        IsProcessing = true; cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource(); var token = cancellation.Token;
        var batchContext = BeginActiveBatch(FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword,
            () => { foreach (var row in batch.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Processing)) row.Stage = "cancelling"; });
        var petBatch = BeginPetBatch(FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword, batch.Length);
        LastError = null;
        try
        {
            var outcome = await new SerialRecoveryQueue(recoveryEngine).RunAsync(requests,
                onStarted: request =>
                {
                    StartPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, rows[request.Id].Name, Array.IndexOf(requests, request) + 1);
                    rows[request.Id].Begin(RecoveryCandidates.Total(options, numbers), options.Mode == RecoveryMode.CommonPasswords);
                    AssistantState = AssistantState.PROCESSING; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("正在尝试恢复数字密码……"); StatusMessage = Locale.F($"正在恢复 · {Array.IndexOf(requests, request) + 1}/{batch.Length}");
                },
                onCompleted: (request, result) => { CompleteRecoveryPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, result); rows[request.Id].Apply(result); if (result.Error is not null) LastError = result.Error; },
                progressFactory: request => new Progress<RecoveryProgress>(p =>
                    {
                        var row = rows[request.Id];
                        if (row.Status != JobStatus.Processing) return;
                        ReportPetTask(petBatch, request.Id, p.TotalCandidates > 0 && p.Stage == "testing" ? p.Percent : null, p.Stage);
                        row.CompletedCandidates = p.CompletedCandidates; row.TotalCandidates = p.TotalCandidates;
                        if (!token.IsCancellationRequested) row.Stage = p.Stage;
                        row.CpuLimitApproximate = p.CpuLimitApproximate;
                    }),
                onObserverError: _ => log.Write("Recovery queue view notification failed", Locale.T("恢复界面通知失败；候选和密码不写入诊断。")), cancellationToken: token);
            foreach (var entry in outcome.Results) { CompleteRecoveryPetTask(petBatch, entry.Request.Id, entry.Result); rows[entry.Request.Id].Apply(entry.Result); }
            if (outcome.Cancelled) { AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("密码恢复已取消。"); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已取消 · 未开始的任务保留，已取消任务可从头重试"); }
            else if (batch.Any(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Failed)) { AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("部分压缩包遇到了问题。"); StatusMessage = RecoveryQueueSummary; }
            else if (batch.Any(j => j.ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Found)) { AssistantState = AssistantState.SUCCESS; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("找到密码啦！可以复制，或转入解压。"); StatusMessage = RecoveryQueueSummary; CelebrateBatchCompletion(); }
            else { AssistantState = AssistantState.IDLE; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("候选已测试完成，暂时没有找到密码。");
                StatusMessage = options.Mode == RecoveryMode.CommonPasswords ? Locale.T("候选已耗尽 · 可改用 CPU 破解") : Locale.T("所选位数的全部数字组合已测试完 · 未找到密码"); }
        }
        catch (Exception) { HandleRecoveryUnexpectedError(); foreach (var row in batch.Where(j => j.Status == JobStatus.Processing)) row.Apply(new(RecoveryStatus.Failed, Error: BatchError)); }
        finally { FinishPetBatch(petBatch, token.IsCancellationRequested); Array.Clear(numbers); cancellation.Dispose(); cancellation = null; EndActiveBatch(batchContext); IsProcessing = false; Refresh(); }
    }
    private ConversionError RecoveryUnexpectedError()
    { var id = log.Write("Password recovery operation failed", Locale.T("密码恢复未完成；候选和恢复结果不写入诊断。")); return new("recovery.desktop-operation", Locale.T("密码恢复遇到了问题。"), Locale.T("请确认引擎和压缩包可读取，然后重试。"), id); }
    public void HandleRecoveryUnexpectedError()
    { LastError = RecoveryUnexpectedError(); AssistantState = AssistantState.ERROR; AssistantMessage = Locale.T("密码恢复遇到了问题。"); StatusMessage = Locale.T("恢复未完成 · 可以查看原因"); }
    private void RetryRecoveryFailed()
    {
        foreach (var row in RecoveryQueue.Where(j => j.Status is JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled)) row.Reset();
        RefreshRecoveryPlans(); ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已重新加入队列 · 从第一个候选开始恢复");
    }
    public void TryRecoveryWithCpu(RecoveryJobViewModel row)
    {
        if (!CanEdit || !RecoveryQueue.Contains(row) || !row.CanTryCpu) return;
        SelectedRecoveryMode = RecoveryMode.Cpu; row.Reset(); RefreshRecoveryPlans(); ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("已切换 CPU 破解 · 点击开始恢复测试全部数字组合");
    }
    public void RemoveRecoveryJobs(IEnumerable<RecoveryJobViewModel> rows)
    { if (CanEdit) foreach (var row in rows.ToArray()) { row.ClearSecret(); RecoveryQueue.Remove(row); } }
    private void ClearRecoveryQueue()
    { foreach (var row in RecoveryQueue) row.ClearSecret(); RecoveryQueue.Clear(); ResetAssistant(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("准备就绪 · 添加压缩包以创建恢复队列"); }
    public void ClearRecoverySecrets()
    { foreach (var row in RecoveryQueue) row.ClearSecret(); CommonRecoveryNumbers.Clear(); }
    public async Task TransferRecoveryToExtractionAsync(RecoveryJobViewModel row)
    {
        if (!CanEdit || !row.HasPassword || !RecoveryQueue.Contains(row)) return;
        var password = row.Password!;
        SetArchiveExtractPage();
        await AddArchiveFilesAsync(new[] { row.SourcePath });
        var target = ArchiveQueue.LastOrDefault(j => j.Operation == ArchiveOperation.Extract && (j.Status is JobStatus.Pending or JobStatus.Failed or JobStatus.Cancelled) && string.Equals(j.SourcePath, row.SourcePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        if (target is not null)
        {
            target.Status = JobStatus.Pending; target.Error = null; target.Progress = 0; target.Stage = ""; target.IsIndeterminate = false;
            target.Statistics = null; target.OutputPath = null; target.Password = password;
            ResetAssistant(); Refresh(); StatusMessage = Locale.T("密码已用于本次解压 · 点击开始解压");
        }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/MainViewModel.TaskCenter.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Collections.Specialized;
using System.Linq;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class MainViewModel
{
    public ObservableCollection<TaskCenterItem> TaskCenterItems { get; } = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<Guid, TaskCenterItem> taskCenterRows = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<Guid, CombinedArchiveTask> combinedArchiveTasks = new();
    private Guid pendingCombinedTaskId = Guid.NewGuid();
    private bool reconcilingTaskCenter;
    private sealed record CombinedArchiveTask(Guid Id, string Name, IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> Inputs);

    private void InitializeTaskCenter()
    {
        Queue.CollectionChanged += TaskCenterQueueChanged;
        ArchiveQueue.CollectionChanged += TaskCenterQueueChanged;
        RecoveryQueue.CollectionChanged += TaskCenterQueueChanged;
        ImageObfuscationQueue.CollectionChanged += TaskCenterQueueChanged;
        RefreshTaskCenter();
    }
    private void TaskCenterQueueChanged(object? sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args) => RefreshTaskCenter();
    private void RegisterCombinedArchiveTask(Guid id, string name, IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> inputs)
    {
        ForgetArchiveTaskGroups(inputs);
        combinedArchiveTasks[id] = new(id, name, inputs.ToArray());
        pendingCombinedTaskId = Guid.NewGuid();
        RefreshTaskCenter();
    }
    private void ForgetArchiveTaskGroups(IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> inputs)
    {
        foreach (var pair in combinedArchiveTasks.Where(pair => pair.Value.Inputs.Any(inputs.Contains)).ToArray()) combinedArchiveTasks.Remove(pair.Key);
    }
    private void RefreshTaskCenter()
    {
        if (reconcilingTaskCenter) return;
        reconcilingTaskCenter = true;
        try
        {
            var desired = new List<TaskCenterItem>();
            TaskCenterItem Get(Guid id, Func<TaskCenterItem> create)
            {
                if (!taskCenterRows.TryGetValue(id, out var item)) taskCenterRows[id] = item = create();
                desired.Add(item); return item;
            }
            void Group(Guid id, IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> rows, Func<string> name)
            {
                if (taskCenterRows.TryGetValue(id, out var old) && !old.Represents(rows))
                { TaskCenterItems.Remove(old); old.Dispose(); taskCenterRows.Remove(id); }
                var item = Get(id, () => new TaskCenterItem(id, rows, name));
                item.UpdateName(name); item.Refresh();
            }
            foreach (var row in Queue) Get(row.Id, () => new TaskCenterItem(row));
            foreach (var row in RecoveryQueue) Get(row.Id, () => new TaskCenterItem(row));
            foreach (var row in ImageObfuscationQueue) Get(row.Id, () => new TaskCenterItem(row));
            var grouped = new HashSet<ArchiveJobViewModel>();
            foreach (var pair in combinedArchiveTasks.ToArray())
            {
                var inputs = pair.Value.Inputs.Where(ArchiveQueue.Contains).ToArray();
                if (inputs.Length == 0) { combinedArchiveTasks.Remove(pair.Key); continue; }
                Group(pair.Key, inputs, () => pair.Value.Name);
                foreach (var row in inputs) grouped.Add(row);
            }
            var combinedPending = ArchiveQueue.Where(row => !grouped.Contains(row) && row.Operation == Core.ArchiveOperation.Create && row.Status == Core.JobStatus.Pending).ToArray();
            if (CombinedArchiveSelected && combinedPending.Length > 0)
            {
                Group(pendingCombinedTaskId, combinedPending, () => ArchiveName);
                foreach (var row in combinedPending) grouped.Add(row);
            }
            foreach (var row in ArchiveQueue.Where(row => !grouped.Contains(row))) Get(row.Id, () => new TaskCenterItem(row));
            foreach (var pair in taskCenterRows.Where(pair => !desired.Contains(pair.Value)).ToArray())
            { TaskCenterItems.Remove(pair.Value); pair.Value.Dispose(); taskCenterRows.Remove(pair.Key); }
            for (var index = 0; index < desired.Count; index++)
            {
                var item = desired[index];
                var oldIndex = TaskCenterItems.IndexOf(item);
                if (oldIndex < 0) TaskCenterItems.Insert(index, item);
                else if (oldIndex != index) TaskCenterItems.Move(oldIndex, index);
            }
        }
        finally { reconcilingTaskCenter = false; }
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/RecoveryJobViewModel.cs ---
using System;
using System.IO;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

public sealed class RecoveryJobViewModel : ObservableObject
{
    public Guid Id { get; } = Guid.NewGuid();
    public string SourcePath { get; }
    public string Name => Path.GetFileName(SourcePath);
    public long SourceBytes { get; }
    public string SourceSizeText => ConversionJobViewModel.FormatSize(SourceBytes);
    public RecoveryJobViewModel(string sourcePath, long sourceBytes) { SourcePath = sourcePath; SourceBytes = sourceBytes; }
    private JobStatus status = JobStatus.Pending;
    public JobStatus Status { get => status; set { if (Set(ref status, value)) NotifyState(); } }
    private RecoveryStatus? resultStatus;
    public RecoveryStatus? ResultStatus { get => resultStatus; set { if (Set(ref resultStatus, value)) NotifyState(); } }
    private string plan = "";
    public string Plan { get => plan; set => Set(ref plan, value); }
    private long completedCandidates;
    public long CompletedCandidates { get => completedCandidates; set { if (Set(ref completedCandidates, Math.Max(0, value))) NotifyProgress(); } }
    private long totalCandidates;
    public long TotalCandidates { get => totalCandidates; set { if (Set(ref totalCandidates, Math.Max(0, value))) NotifyProgress(); } }
    public long RemainingCandidates => Math.Max(0, TotalCandidates - CompletedCandidates);
    public double Progress => TotalCandidates > 0 ? Math.Clamp(CompletedCandidates * 100d / TotalCandidates, 0, 100) : 0;
    public bool IsIndeterminate => Status == JobStatus.Processing
        && (TotalCandidates <= 0 || Stage is "preparing" or "checking" or "verifying" or "cancelling");
    public string PercentageText => IsIndeterminate ? "—" : $"{Progress:0.00}%";
    public string CountsText => Locale.F($"已测试 {CompletedCandidates:N0} / {TotalCandidates:N0} · 剩余 {RemainingCandidates:N0}");
    private string stage = "preparing";
    public string Stage { get => stage; set { if (Set(ref stage, value)) { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsIndeterminate)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PercentageText)); } } }
    private bool cpuLimitApproximate;
    public bool CpuLimitApproximate { get => cpuLimitApproximate; set => Set(ref cpuLimitApproximate, value); }
    private string? password;
    public string? Password { get => password; private set { if (Set(ref password, value)) { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasPassword)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PasswordText)); } } }
    public bool HasPassword => ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Found && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Password);
    private bool possiblePassword;
    public bool PossiblePassword { get => possiblePassword; private set { if (Set(ref possiblePassword, value)) { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowPossiblePasswordWarning)); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText)); } } }
    public bool ShowPossiblePasswordWarning => ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted && PossiblePassword;
    public string PasswordText => HasPassword ? Locale.F($"密码：{Password}") : "";
    public bool CanTryCpu => ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Exhausted && CommonMode;
    public bool CommonMode { get; set; }
    private ConversionError? error;
    public ConversionError? Error { get => error; set { if (Set(ref error, value)) OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasError)); } }
    public bool HasError => Error is not null;
    public string StatusText => Status switch
    {
        JobStatus.Processing => Stage switch { "preparing" or "checking" => Locale.T("检查压缩包"), "verifying" => Locale.T("正在完整验证"), "cancelling" => Locale.T("正在取消 · 等待进程退出"), _ => Locale.T("正在恢复") },
        JobStatus.Cancelled => Locale.T("已取消"),
        JobStatus.Failed => Locale.T("遇到问题"),
        JobStatus.Completed => ResultStatus == RecoveryStatus.Found ? Locale.T("恢复成功 · 全包校验通过") : Locale.T("候选已耗尽 · 未找到"),
        _ => Locale.T("待恢复")
    };
    public void Begin(long total, bool commonMode)
    {
        ClearSecret(); Error = null; ResultStatus = null; CommonMode = commonMode; CpuLimitApproximate = false;
        CompletedCandidates = 0; TotalCandidates = total; Stage = "preparing"; Status = JobStatus.Processing;
    }
    public void Apply(RecoveryResult result)
    {
        if (result.TotalCandidates > 0) { CompletedCandidates = result.CompletedCandidates; TotalCandidates = result.TotalCandidates; }
        ResultStatus = result.Status; Password = result.Password; PossiblePassword = result.PossiblePassword; Error = result.Error;
        Status = result.Status switch { RecoveryStatus.Cancelled => JobStatus.Cancelled, RecoveryStatus.Failed => JobStatus.Failed, _ => JobStatus.Completed };
        NotifyState();
    }
    public void Reset()
    {
        ClearSecret(); ResultStatus = null; Error = null; CompletedCandidates = 0; CpuLimitApproximate = false; Stage = "preparing"; Status = JobStatus.Pending;
    }
    public void ClearSecret() { Password = null; PossiblePassword = false; }
    private void NotifyProgress()
    { foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(RemainingCandidates), nameof(Progress), nameof(IsIndeterminate), nameof(PercentageText), nameof(CountsText), nameof(StatusText) }) OnPropertyChanged(name); }
    private void NotifyState()
    { foreach (var name in new[] { nameof(StatusText), nameof(IsIndeterminate), nameof(PercentageText), nameof(HasPassword), nameof(PasswordText), nameof(CanTryCpu), nameof(ShowPossiblePasswordWarning) }) OnPropertyChanged(name); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/ViewModels/TaskCenterItem.cs ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Mvvm;

namespace CatFileAssistant.Desktop.ViewModels;

/// <summary>A live presentation adapter. It never schedules work or stores passwords.</summary>
public sealed class TaskCenterItem : ObservableObject, IDisposable
{
    private readonly object source;
    private readonly IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel>? archiveInputs;
    private Func<string>? archiveName;
    private readonly IReadOnlyList<INotifyPropertyChanged> observed;
    // A combined archive is one request. While its input rows reconcile one by one,
    // present the row matching the request's aggregate state instead of an arbitrary first input.
    private object PresentationSource => archiveInputs is { } rows
        ? rows.FirstOrDefault(row => row.Status == Status) ?? source : source;
    public Guid Id { get; }
    public IReadOnlyList<string> Inputs { get; }

    internal TaskCenterItem(ConversionJobViewModel row)
    { source = row; Id = row.Id; Inputs = new[] { row.SourcePath }; observed = new[] { row }; Subscribe(); }
    internal TaskCenterItem(RecoveryJobViewModel row)
    { source = row; Id = row.Id; Inputs = new[] { row.SourcePath }; observed = new[] { row }; Subscribe(); }
    internal TaskCenterItem(ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row)
    { source = row; Id = row.Id; Inputs = new[] { row.SourcePath }; observed = new[] { row }; Subscribe(); }
    internal TaskCenterItem(ArchiveJobViewModel row)
    { source = row; Id = row.Id; Inputs = new[] { row.SourcePath }; observed = new[] { row }; Subscribe(); }
    internal TaskCenterItem(Guid id, IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> rows, Func<string> name)
    {
        source = rows[0]; Id = id; archiveInputs = rows; archiveName = name;
        Inputs = rows.Select(row => row.SourcePath).ToArray(); observed = rows.Cast<INotifyPropertyChanged>().ToArray(); Subscribe();
    }
    private void Subscribe() { foreach (var row in observed) row.PropertyChanged += SourceChanged; }
    private void SourceChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs args) => Refresh();
    internal bool Represents(IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> rows) => archiveInputs is not null && archiveInputs.SequenceEqual(rows);
    internal void UpdateName(Func<string> name) => archiveName = name;
    internal void Refresh()
    {
        foreach (var property in new[] { nameof(Name), nameof(SourceAction), nameof(SourceTitle), nameof(Status), nameof(StatusText), nameof(Progress),
            nameof(IsIndeterminate), nameof(Information), nameof(Format), nameof(OutputPath), nameof(OutputIsDirectory), nameof(Error),
            nameof(HasOutput), nameof(HasError), nameof(InputSummary) }) OnPropertyChanged(property);
    }
    public string Name => archiveName?.Invoke() ?? (PresentationSource switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.Name, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.Name, RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.Name, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.Name, _ => "" });
    public FileProcessingAction SourceAction => PresentationSource switch
    {
        ConversionJobViewModel row when row.IsCompression => FileProcessingAction.CompressMedia,
        ConversionJobViewModel row when row.IsGif => FileProcessingAction.Gif,
        ConversionJobViewModel row => row.MediaMode switch { ConversionMode.Audio => FileProcessingAction.Audio,
            ConversionMode.Image => FileProcessingAction.Image, ConversionMode.Extract => FileProcessingAction.ExtractAudio, _ => FileProcessingAction.Video },
        ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.IsExtraction ? FileProcessingAction.ExtractArchive : FileProcessingAction.CreateArchive,
        RecoveryJobViewModel => FileProcessingAction.RecoverPassword,
        ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.Operation == ImageObfuscationOperation.Obfuscate ? FileProcessingAction.ObfuscateImage : FileProcessingAction.DeobfuscateImage,
        _ => FileProcessingAction.Video
    };
    public string SourceTitle => MainViewModel.FileActionTitle(SourceAction);
    public JobStatus Status => archiveInputs is { } rows ? GroupStatus(rows) : source switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.Status, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.Status, RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.Status, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.Status, _ => JobStatus.Pending };
    private static JobStatus GroupStatus(IReadOnlyList<ArchiveJobViewModel> rows) => rows.Any(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Processing) ? JobStatus.Processing
        : rows.Any(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Pending) ? JobStatus.Pending
        : rows.Any(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Failed) ? JobStatus.Failed
        : rows.Any(row => row.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled) ? JobStatus.Cancelled : JobStatus.Completed;
    public string StatusText => PresentationSource switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.StatusText, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.StatusText, RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.StatusText, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.StatusText, _ => Locale.T("待处理") };
    public double Progress => PresentationSource switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.Progress, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.Progress, RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.Progress, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.Progress, _ => 0 };
    public bool IsIndeterminate => Status == JobStatus.Processing && (PresentationSource switch
    {
        ConversionJobViewModel row => row.IsIndeterminate,
        ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.IsIndeterminate,
        RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.IsIndeterminate,
        ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.IsIndeterminate,
        _ => true
    });
    public string Information => archiveInputs is { Count: > 1 } rows
        ? Locale.F($"{rows.Count} 个输入 · ") + (Status == JobStatus.Completed && PresentationSource is ArchiveJobViewModel completed ? completed.ResultSizeText : Locale.T("合并为一个压缩包"))
        : PresentationSource switch { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.ResultSizeText, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.ResultSizeText,
            RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.SourceSizeText + " · " + row.CountsText, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.InputDescription + " · " + row.ResultSizeText, _ => "" };
    public string Format => PresentationSource switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.FormatName + (row.HasTargetSummary ? " · " + row.TargetSummary : ""), ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.Plan,
        RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.Plan, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => "PNG · " + row.Plan, _ => "" };
    public string? OutputPath => PresentationSource switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.OutputPath, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.OutputPath, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.OutputPath, _ => null };
    public bool OutputIsDirectory => PresentationSource is ArchiveJobViewModel { OutputKind: ArchiveOutputKind.Directory };
    public ConversionError? Error => PresentationSource switch
    { ConversionJobViewModel row => row.Error, ArchiveJobViewModel row => row.Error, RecoveryJobViewModel row => row.Error, ImageObfuscationJobViewModel row => row.Error, _ => null };
    public bool HasOutput => Status == JobStatus.Completed && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(OutputPath);
    public bool HasError => Error is not null;
    public string InputSummary => Inputs.Count == 1 ? Inputs[0] : Locale.F($"{Inputs.Count} 个输入合并打包");
    public void Dispose() { foreach (var row in observed) row.PropertyChanged -= SourceChanged; }
}
